## Abstract

Smithian Fold Theory V3 is a clean-room, machine-auditable reconstruction of a
single dependency-ordered scientific system. It begins with the operational
root theorem *there is no nothing*: a purported counterexample either presents
no statement, denial, record or proof object and therefore supplies no
counterexample, or it presents an occurrence and is not nothing. The admitted
occurrence forces its structural self-whole, the One; exact positive count,
part, distinction, return and the minimal reversible Fold are then generated
without importing arithmetic, geometry, probability, physical constants or a
continuum as premises. Every later result is separately registered. No later
law is permitted to become a retroactive assumption of an earlier proof.

The V3 corpus reported here contains **2,751 model-admitted claims** generated
from **892,246 registered candidates**, with **2,751 unique survivors** and
**11,004 passed controls**. All 2,751 receipt identities match their registered
claim identities; all 2,751 candidate censuses reconstruct; every admitted
claim traces to the single root; and the current ledger contains no missing
declared dependency. These totals cover the premise-free methods and
Foundation results; current full-field Mathematics, Information Science,
Classical Computation, Reversible and Quantum Computation, Physics, Chemistry
and Materials Science; completed foundations and dated return families in
Biology, Medicine, Consciousness and Cognitive Science, Earth and
Environmental Sciences, Astronomy and Cosmology, Social and Collective
Systems, and Engineering Translation; and the current twelve-claim
Cross-Branch Synthesis.

The work separates exact derivation from implementation, measurement,
reconstruction, correspondence, compatibility, support, confirmation and
adverse evidence. Candidate generation and elimination establish formal
status only at their declared boundaries. Natural-science claims additionally
retain source identity, measurement method, uncertainty, preregistration,
chronology, target custody, falsification conditions and every favourable,
adverse, null, failed, missing or unavailable result. Current branch status is
therefore non-uniform by design: seven field-wide branches are complete to
their frozen current-evidence scopes; Biology is at 82 of 424 registered
full-field obligations; later natural, cognitive, collective and engineering
branches possess complete foundations but do not yet claim frozen full-field
closure.

The preliminary monograph also reports the computational-proof programme.
Protein Fold V3 has an exact sequence representation, complete interaction
custody, a six-axis joint relation, a verified 42,173,082-candidate parent
search and a separately closed 583-state high-attraction slice, while the
remaining attraction bands, global fixed-geometry frontier, complete
whole-chain recurrence and registered 100-target AlphaFold comparison remain
open. Chess Fold and Go Fold retain measured V2 implementation results and
exact historical receipts, while their clean V3 recreations have not yet
started in the current repository. Unison Fold AI retains its V2 implementation
record and a V3 frontier scaffold; mathematical closure is open, empirical
validation is `NOT_RUN`, and deployment is `NOT_AUTHORIZED`.

This publication is deliberately preliminary because the computational
programmes, Biology continuation, downstream full-field censuses, final
one-owner lineage merge, canonical publication reconciliation and final heavy
verification remain active. Preliminary denotes a versioned scientific state,
not a short summary. This monograph presents the complete current dependency
argument at scale, binds it to a complete claim inventory, scientific audit and
machine archive, and is designed to be amended by later versions without
erasing its chronology.

**Keywords:** Smithian Fold Theory; theory of everything; premise-free
foundation; parameter-free derivation; computational proof; exact arithmetic;
physical constants; open science; machine-verifiable science; protein folding;
AlphaFold; reversible computation; quantum computation; knowledge tree.

## Headline findings

1. **One operational root.** The current corpus is a directed acyclic
   dependency system rooted in `SFT-ROOT-THERE-IS-NO-NOTHING`. Every one of the
   2,751 admitted claims possesses a complete route to that root.

2. **The Fold is generated rather than assumed.** Occurrence, the structural
   One, exact positive count, exact part and the reversible distinguished
   partition are admitted in separate enumerated stages. The minimal Fold is
   the unique survivor of its registered grammar; it is not an imported binary
   convention.

3. **The laws are exposed as forced selections.** Each admitted claim retains
   its candidate grammar, exact boundary, candidate count, one decision per
   candidate, survivor count, uniqueness account, controls, independent
   reconstruction and receipt identity. A prose statement alone cannot enter
   the model.

4. **The current corpus is large and fully enumerated at claim level.** The
   authoritative ledger contains 2,751 admitted claims, 892,246 candidate
   structures, 2,751 unique survivors and 11,004 passed controls. These are
   exact repository counts, not estimates or rounded publicity figures.

5. **Seven branches have current full-field closure.** Mathematics contains
   323 admitted claims; Information Science 262; Classical Computation 369;
   Reversible and Quantum Computation 288; Physics 368; Chemistry 281 live
   claims, including 272 registered discipline obligations and nine separately
   returned claims; and Materials Science 289. Together these seven papers
   comprise 2,180 live claims, 744,944 candidates and 8,720 controls.

6. **Later branches have complete foundations without being relabelled as
   complete fields.** Biology contains 82 admitted claims against a frozen
   424-obligation full-field census. Medicine contains 76 current foundation
   and return claims; Consciousness 77; Earth and Environment 75; Astronomy and
   Cosmology 77; Social and Collective Systems 76 across its two current
   ledgers; and Engineering Translation 80. Their exact current boundaries are
   retained in the branch chapters and status tables.

7. **Constants are treated as one dependency object.** Physics reconstructs
   the current registered constants, dimensional relations and measurement
   interfaces from the Fold dependency spine. Exact structural forms precede
   measured comparisons. No measured decimal is permitted to become a proof
   premise, and no post-derivation numerical correspondence is relabelled as a
   blind prediction.

8. **Adverse evidence remains part of the theory.** The corpus preserves
   failed transports, adverse rankings, null and unavailable records,
   corrected grammars, superseded implementation claims and chronology-bound
   development results. Later work may supersede an obstacle at a wider or
   stronger boundary, but it may not delete the original record.

9. **The open computational-proof programme is restarted under V3 custody.**
   Protein Fold is active and exact at progressively larger boundaries. Chess
   Fold, Go Fold and Unison Fold AI retain their V2 measured history while
   clean V3 recreations are separately identified. Their results test and
   translate the admitted corpus; they do not select its foundational laws.

10. **The repository is the public scientific instrument.** Human-readable
    papers provide the derivational narrative; claim packages provide the
    audit layer; machine receipts, source captures, manifests and executable
    traces provide the archive. Every access level is retained in the release
    rather than forcing either a conceptual reader or a verifier to use the
    wrong representation.

### What “preliminary” means here

Preliminary is a chronology and version classification. It does not excuse
omission, compression into an overview, weak proofreading or a missing audit
layer. This edition must be complete for the repository state it freezes. A
later version may add a closed Biology family, a new full-field census, a
completed computational proof or a corrected source transport, but it must
preserve the earlier claim, evidence and publication history. The final version
will be issued only when Maria Smith determines that the registered
computational programmes, remaining full-field work, global lineage merge,
publication reconciliation and final verification satisfy the final ToE
protocol.

## 1. Scope, ownership and publication authority

### 1.1 Scientific scope

This monograph covers the complete current SFT V3 dependency spine from the
operational root through Cross-Branch Synthesis and the computational-proof
frontier. It reports both field-wide and foundational branches at their exact
current boundaries. It preserves the scientific difference between a branch
whose registered current-evidence field census is closed and a branch whose
foundation is closed while wider census work remains open.

The domain includes formal derivation, executable implementation, empirical
interfaces, historical reconciliation and engineering translation. Those
domains are related but not interchangeable. Mathematical uniqueness does not
by itself measure nature. A successful program does not retroactively derive
its premises. A measured correspondence does not become a prediction unless
the target and chronology satisfy the registered prediction protocol.

### 1.2 One-owner branch boundaries

Each result has one scientific owner. Foundation owns the generation of the
Fold grammar. Mathematics owns the exact generated mathematical structures.
Information Science owns record, encoding, channel and inference structures.
Classical Computation owns machine execution and computational-resource
relations. Reversible and Quantum Computation owns reversible carriers,
quantum-state and process structures, and the formal classical–quantum
boundary. Physics owns quantities, dimensions, constants, fields, matter and
physical measurement. Chemistry owns chemical identity, composition, bonding,
reaction and chemical evidence. Materials owns structure–property relations
across scale. Biology and subsequent branches own the domain-specific laws and
measurements that consume these upstream structures.

Cross-Branch Synthesis owns no primitive law. It may prove a typed identity
between already admitted branch objects and may assemble a dependency ledger,
but it cannot repair an absent branch derivation or move a result into a branch
where its falsification and evidence are not owned.

### 1.3 Authorship and authority

Maria Smith is the author, copyright holder and sole publication authority for
this paper. Ernos Labs is the open-science movement, verification platform and
public knowledge tree through which the evidence is made inspectable. Agent
contributions are editorial, computational and verification assistance. An
agent cannot admit a model claim, declare Maria Smith's empirical conclusion,
change branch ownership, authorise an application campaign or publish this
paper.

Publication authority and scientific evidence are intentionally separate.
Maria Smith's authority controls whether a release occurs and how her work is
represented; it does not substitute for a derivation, source, control or
measurement. Conversely, an engine receipt establishes only the bounded
machine status encoded by its package. It does not erase authorship or grant an
agent publication authority.

### 1.4 Open-science mission

Ernos Labs exists to make scientific authority inspectable. It rejects the
substitution of credentials, institutional prestige, funding success,
consensus or predictive opacity for an auditable evidence route. The remedy is
not a lower standard. It is a public and stricter one: state the claim; expose
its dependencies; generate the alternatives; record every decision; preserve
the failed rows; bind the sources and artefacts; make the computation
replayable; distinguish proof from measurement; and permit any person to test
the result without first accepting the author's status.

The project is anti-institutional where institutions make access, prestige,
capital or professional permission prerequisites for scientific audibility.
It is not anti-expertise, anti-measurement or anti-criticism. Specialists are
invited to attack the derivations, candidate grammars, sources, controls,
measurements and code. Criticism is unrestricted; admission remains governed
by the public constitution. CC BY 4.0 and Apache-2.0 permit copying, inspection,
forking and lawful reuse while preserving Maria Smith's authorship and the
licence terms.

## 2. The scientific constitution

### 2.1 The root restriction

The V3 derivation begins without an imported scientific axiom. It does not
begin by assuming space, time, number, probability, matter, energy, logic as a
formal calculus or a computational machine. Its initial question is
operational: what would count as a presented counterexample to the statement
that there is no nothing? The registered two-class census is exhaustive at
that boundary:

1. no statement, denial, record or proof object is presented, so no
   counterexample is supplied; or
2. something is presented, and the presentation is an occurrence rather than
   nothing.

The result is not a conventional numerical assertion that a quantity differs
from zero. Numerical zero has not yet been admitted. It is an operational
closure statement about the impossibility of presenting nothing as a
counterexample.

### 2.2 Clean-room sequencing

V3 is a clean-room reconstruction. Earlier SFT papers, application programs
and observations may be compared only after the relevant V3 result has sealed.
They remain historical evidence and can establish agreement, disagreement,
stronger closure, weaker closure, an implementation result or a question that
the clean reconstruction must eventually address. They cannot become hidden
premises or answer keys.

The sequencing rule is global:

1. register the obligation and admissible dependencies;
2. freeze the candidate grammar and boundary;
3. execute the complete census or a certified depth-independent closure;
4. retain one decision for every candidate;
5. identify the unique survivor or preserve the adverse no-survivor or
   multiple-survivor result;
6. run named controls and an implementation-distinct reconstruction;
7. seal the result and receipt;
8. only then open historical comparison or external target evidence when the
   protocol permits it.

### 2.3 No-free-parameter rule

A fundamental SFT law cannot be selected by a fitted, trained, discretionary,
target-derived or silently imported numerical parameter. Exact positive
rational values may appear after their part and ratio construction has been
derived. Measured values, uncertainties, calibration factors and conventional
units remain legitimate empirical records, but they do not select the prior
formal law.

This rule does not pretend that all software has no configuration or that all
experiments lack apparatus choices. It distinguishes an implementation or
measurement condition from a free parameter that chooses the purported
fundamental result. Conditions and interfaces must be declared, source-bound
and retained in the evidence record.

### 2.4 Candidate closure and uniqueness

Every admitted claim states a grammar of admissible forms. The grammar may be
finite and exhaustively enumerated or it may possess a separately proved
depth-independent certificate. The package records:

- the exact claim and why the next object is required;
- admitted dependencies and branch owner;
- carrier, boundary and relation;
- provenance and evidence class;
- candidate grammar and cardinality;
- one decision for every candidate;
- survivor count and uniqueness explanation;
- falsification condition;
- adverse controls and their results;
- source identities, source transport and chronology; and
- receipt and artefact identities.

One survivor establishes uniqueness only within the registered grammar and
boundary. A bounded census without a generality certificate remains bounded.
A foundational closure does not silently become completion of every possible
future scientific application.

### 2.5 Host/computation quarantine

The implementation necessarily uses host-language integers, indices, empty
containers, Boolean values, file paths, process codes and finite machine
resources. These implement a proof or audit; they do not become SFT proof
objects merely by appearing in code. Floating-point computation may be used
for a declared measured comparison or rendering task. Exact formal decisions
use the exact representation declared by their package.

---

## 3. Mathematical constitution

The mathematical constitution is generated in dependency order. The
structural One is a complete self-whole, not a numeral assumed in advance.
Positive finite count records generated occurrences without omission or
addition. Exact parts arise from complete, disjoint and equal-fibred
partitions. The Fold is the minimal reversible distinguished partition with a
complete return relation. Ordered Fold words then supply finite sequences,
relations and recurrences from which later mathematical objects are generated.

At the foundational boundary the permitted proof domain is characterised by:

- positive generated occurrence and exact positive count;
- exact rational part after part construction;
- held structural labels rather than imported signed magnitudes;
- finite generated carriers and complete finite traces;
- reversible relations and explicit return;
- conditional bounded statements where no depth-independent certificate is
  available;
- no completed infinity as a premise;
- no ungenerated continuum as a premise;
- no floating-point value as a formal proof value; and
- no imported physical equation, constant or taxonomy selecting a
  mathematical law.

Mathematics extends this constitution into exact arithmetic, discrete
structure, graphs, algebra, order, finite geometry and topology, probability
and statistics, optimisation, dynamical systems, logic and proof, category and
type structure, exact relations, number theory, refinement and continuum
boundaries, algebraic-balance certificates, bounded many-body recurrence,
floored-fluid regularity, prime-pair censuses, Riemann correspondence, bounded
Collatz results, self-similarity and convergent-series laws, and the current
SFT-native calculator and verification surfaces. Each chapter states whether a
result is an exact theorem, a bounded census, a correspondence or an open
extension.

## 4. Empirical constitution and chronology

### 4.1 Evidence classes

The corpus uses the following terms consistently.

| Term | Meaning in this monograph |
|---|---|
| **Theorem** | An exact formal result closed by its registered derivation and proof boundary |
| **Law** | A model-admitted relation with one owner, declared dependencies, candidate closure and a surviving form |
| **Claim** | A registered proposition with a complete package; admission and empirical status are separately stated |
| **Constitution** | The rules governing what objects, dependencies, evidence and transitions may enter a stated layer |
| **Derivation** | A generated dependency route from admitted objects to a candidate grammar and surviving result |
| **Prediction** | A consequence sealed before access to the registered target or outcome |
| **Observation** | A source-bound external record |
| **Measurement** | A value bound to an instrument or method, unit, calibration, uncertainty and source |
| **Reconstruction** | An inferred history, latent state or reproduced structure derived from retained evidence |
| **Exact numerical correspondence** | Equality of exact derived and external numerical objects established after the derivation boundary |
| **Structural correspondence** | A relation between forms that does not assert exact numerical prediction |
| **Boundary correspondence** | Agreement restricted to a declared carrier, scale, case or interface |
| **Compatibility** | Evidence that is non-adverse but does not discriminate the claim uniquely |
| **Support** | Relevant evidence that favours a claim but is not uniquely probative |
| **Confirmation** | Status permitted only by the current registered evidence protocol |
| **Validation** | Successful execution of the specific formal, computational or empirical validation named in context |
| **Adverse result** | A registered test whose retained outcome conflicts with the tested claim or selector |
| **Unresolved result** | A result for which required evidence is unavailable, incomplete, censored, disputed or not yet executed |
| **Implementation identity** | The hash-bound source, environment and artefact state that produced an executable result |
| **Formal status** | The state of the derivation and candidate closure |
| **Empirical status** | The state of external observation, measurement or test |
| **Publication status** | Whether and where an artefact has been released under Maria Smith's authority |
| **Foundational closure** | Completion of the registered foundation of a branch |
| **Field-wide closure** | Completion of the branch's frozen current-evidence full-field census |
| **Current-evidence closure** | Closure against the frozen present inventory, not permanent closure of science |
| **Extension openness** | The obligation to admit later discoveries, corrections or failures through the same evidence constitution |

### 4.2 Proof and evidence verbs

This paper reserves *proves* for exact formal closure, *derives* for generated
structures and laws, *implements* for executable constructions, *predicts* for
properly sealed prospective consequences, *observes* for source-bound records,
*measures* for method-bound values, *reconstructs* for inferred histories or
latent states, *corresponds* for post-derivation relations, *is compatible
with* for non-adverse but non-discriminating evidence, *supports* for relevant
but non-unique evidence, *confirms* only where the registered protocol permits
that class, *adverse* where the registered outcome conflicts, and *unresolved*
where the required evidence remains missing or incomplete.

Test success verifies what the tests cover. It does not by itself empirically
confirm the theory. A source's reputation does not prove the claim. A later
successful result does not become an earlier blind prediction unless the
sealed chronology establishes that fact.

### 4.3 Empirical sequence

For a natural-science prediction the lawful sequence is:

1. establish capability closure without target access;
2. register the exact claim, target class, selection rule, metrics, failure
   treatment and falsification criterion;
3. place target custody outside the predicting process;
4. seal the executable predictor, environment and output identity;
5. open the target or comparator output only after the seal;
6. evaluate every row, including failed, invalid and unavailable rows;
7. publish the complete chronology and outcome without selective deletion.

Development-observed, post-target and retrospective results remain useful but
retain those labels. A failed source transport is not absence of evidence; it
is a transport result. Missing evidence is not a null result. A null result is
a completed observation whose registered outcome is null.

## 5. Dependency spine

The corpus is read in the order in which each branch receives the carriers and
relations required for its own work. The order is scientific rather than
prestige-based. A later branch may consume an upstream law but cannot rewrite
its ownership or become its premise.

| 02 | Mathematics | 323 claims | 323/323 full-field current-evidence complete | Exact mathematical carriers, relations, proofs and bounds |
| 03 | Information Science | 262 claims | 262/262 full-field current-evidence complete | Distinction, record, code, channel, inference and information custody |
| 04 | Classical Computation | 369 claims | 369/369 full-field current-evidence complete | Fold machines, algorithms, resources, programs and verification |
| 05 | Reversible and Quantum Computation | 288 claims | 288/288 full-field current-evidence complete | Reversible processes, quantum carriers, measurement and simulation handoffs |
| 06 | Physics | 368 claims | 368/368 current categorical scope complete | Quantity, dimension, constants, fields, matter and physical evidence |
| 07 | Chemistry | 281 live claims | 272/272 registered obligations plus nine returned claims | Chemical identity, composition, reaction, molecular and biochemical interfaces |
| 08 | Materials Science | 289 claims | 289/289 full-field current-evidence complete | Structure–property, defect, phase, interface and device-scale relations |
| 09 | Biology and Life Sciences | 82 claims | Foundation and current returned families complete; 82/424 full-field obligations | Living organisation, inheritance, development, ecology and protein interfaces |
| 10 | Medicine and Health Sciences | 76 claims | Foundation plus current return family complete; full-field census not frozen | Health, disease, intervention, evidence and clinical ownership |
| 11 | Consciousness and Cognitive Science | 77 claims | Foundation plus current return family complete; full-field census not frozen | Perception, memory, self, agency and cognitive measurement |
| 12 | Earth and Environmental Sciences | 75 claims | Foundation plus current return claim complete; full-field census not frozen | Earth systems, climate, environment and observation |

### 5.1 Why each layer follows the preceding one

Foundation supplies the first exact generated objects. Mathematics is required
because later branches need exact relations, composition, proof and bounded
generality. Information Science follows because a scientific system must
distinguish a state from its record, code, channel and inference. Computation
follows because registered transformations, algorithms and resource traces
require executable machines. Reversible and Quantum Computation follow once
process reversal, state composition and measurement interfaces can be typed
without importing physical apparatus as a mathematical premise.

Physics then binds formal structures to quantities, dimensions, sources,
responses and measurements. Chemistry requires physical carriers while adding
chemical identity, composition, bond, reaction and source-specific evidence.
Materials requires chemistry and physics but owns the collective
structure–property relations that neither upstream branch may silently absorb.
Biology consumes chemical and material organisation while adding living
identity, heredity, regulation and evolutionary and ecological relations.

Medicine depends on living systems but owns health, pathology, intervention
and clinical evidence. Consciousness and Cognitive Science require living
organisation, information and computation while owning perception, self and
cognitive evidence. Earth, astronomy and collective sciences consume physical,
chemical, biological, informational and dynamical structures at their own
scales. Engineering comes last among the domain branches because it translates
admitted laws into artefacts while owning safety, reliability and deployment.
Cross-Branch Synthesis can operate only after the relevant owners have spoken.

## 6. Registered source surface and three access levels

### 6.1 Conceptual paper

The conceptual layer is this dependency-ordered monograph. It explains why
each branch is required, what it derives, what evidence currently establishes,
which adverse and corrected records remain, and what the next branch receives.
Its purpose is a continuous scientific argument readable by specialists who
did not participate in the repository's construction.

### 6.2 Scientific audit layer

The audit layer contains the complete branch status tables, claim inventories,
candidate and survivor counts, controls, evidence chronology, source transport,
corrections, unresolved records, ownership and historical reconciliation. The
complete 2,751-row claim inventory and the seventeen dependency-ordered branch
audit volumes accompany this paper. Repetition required for evidential custody
belongs there rather than being allowed to destroy the narrative.

### 6.3 Machine archive

The machine layer contains the claim registrations, candidate censuses,
decisions, controls, certificates, evidence records, source captures, receipts,
hashes, executable traces and verification reports. Its full identity map is
bound by the release manifest and immutable repository revision. Large source
assets that exceed ordinary GitHub object limits retain their hashes and
authoritative archival routes; absence from an ordinary Git object is not
silently reported as absence of evidence.

### 6.4 Authority at release freeze

The authoritative release state is determined from current files, not from
memory or earlier prose. Before the authorised version may be published, its
builder and verifier must:

1. reconstruct the active-paper inventory and compare every SHA-256 identity;
2. reconstruct the global claim, candidate, survivor and control totals;
3. verify one root route and no missing dependency for every admitted claim;
4. inventory current branch closure and publication status;
5. preserve all adverse, corrected, failed-transport and unresolved records;
6. bind the current computational-programme gates without opening protected
   targets or comparator outputs;
7. render and inspect the complete paper and every public audit volume; and
8. record all remaining discrepancies for Maria Smith rather than harmonising
   them silently.

## 7. Reading this monograph

The chapters that follow move through the dependency spine rather than through
a conventional division between “fundamental” and “applied” disciplines. Each
major branch begins by naming the upstream objects already admitted, the next
unresolved problem and the new evidential boundary. Each ends by naming the
objects handed downstream and the limits that remain with its owner.

The narrative reports the principal derivations and current results. The
complete claim inventory gives every admitted claim ID, title, branch, status,
dependencies, candidate count, survivor count, controls and receipt route.
The branch audit volumes retain the complete claim-level fields required by the
publication guidance. Readers should therefore use the conceptual chapter to
understand the argument, the audit volume to inspect a result's custody and the
machine package to reproduce its acceptance or rejection.

This hierarchy is not a concession to opacity. It is what permits both human
comprehension and evidential completeness. No claim is removed from the audit
or machine archive merely because its full receipt is too repetitive for the
main argument.
