## Historical trajectory and V3 reconciliation

Smithian Fold Theory did not appear as a finished monograph with a settled
branch architecture. It developed during a concentrated sequence of
foundational writing, derivation, implementation, correction, public release
and clean-room reconstruction. The first public lineage asked whether
*nothing* could ever be presented as a counterexample to occurrence. The
following synthesis extended that root towards mathematics and physics.
Specialist papers separated particular constants, quantum relations,
mathematical boundaries and proposed physical consequences. Protein folding,
Chess Fold, Go Fold and Unison Fold AI then tested whether Fold relations could
be made executable outside the proof papers. The V3 programme finally rebuilt
the growing corpus as a dependency-ordered, one-owner knowledge tree with
claim-level candidate censuses, controls, evidence classes and machine
receipts.

That development is scientifically relevant, but history is not current
authority. This section therefore performs two tasks at once. It preserves
what each earlier version actually recorded, including favourable results,
adverse results, defects, failed transports and open objectives. It also
identifies the current record that now governs each result. An old publication
may establish that a statement, computation or prediction existed at a
particular point in the programme. It cannot overrule a later V3 claim package,
source capture, correction, evidence classification or verification report.
Likewise, the creation of V3 does not erase a prior result merely because its
paper is no longer the active scientific owner. The historical record remains
available so that the route from the first formulation to the present one can
be inspected rather than reconstructed from memory.

The authoritative freeze used here is dated 31 July 2026. Its direct-source
inventory identifies Maria Smith as author and publication authority and Ernos
Labs as the organisation and open-science platform. It binds the current
papers, the live claim ledger, the final seven complete-field publication
record, the current programme ledger, the checksum-bound v6 synthesis, the
historical computational manifests, the current Protein gate and the Protein
publication receipt. The inventory classifies this monograph as a preliminary
synthesis. The final computational proofs, the remaining full-field
programme, the global one-owner merge and the final heavy verification are not
complete. Those boundaries govern every historical statement below.

### How historical evidence is read

Four rules prevent the chronology from becoming either an advertisement or a
retrospective demolition.

First, a version label names a document in a lineage; it is not an evidence
class. The pre-V3 catalogue contains local version sequences such as 1.0,
2.0, 3.0, 3.9-final-status, 6.3 and 6.5. Those numbers were assigned within
different papers and applications. They do not form one common scale of
scientific maturity. A Protein release numbered 3.7, a ToE release numbered
6.0 and an Unison release numbered 6.3 cannot be compared by their version
numbers alone.

Second, formal closure and empirical success are separate. A candidate census
may prove that one relation survives a registered grammar without proving that
the relation predicts an external measurement. Conversely, a programme can
record a strong measured outcome without showing that its selector was
derived under the later V3 admission constitution. Both facts remain useful,
but neither may be substituted for the other.

Third, chronology is retained at the level at which the evidence was obtained.
A development-observed relation does not become blind because a later paper
describes it more elegantly. A target-isolated output remains target-isolated
even if its global score is adverse. A post-target diagnosis may motivate a
new prospective holdout, but it is not itself a prospective result. A later
successful construction may supersede an earlier implementation while leaving
the earlier adverse measurement historically intact.

Fourth, “complete” always names a boundary. The current record distinguishes a
complete operational Foundation, a complete registered family, a complete
field-wide current-evidence census, a completed implementation and a completed
empirical protocol. None of those expressions means that the discipline, the
theory or science itself has ended. This distinction becomes especially
important when comparing the compact pre-V3 synthesis with the much larger
V3 claim graph.

### The first operational root

The first scientific question was not which physical equation should be
treated as fundamental. Any ordinary equation already presupposes symbols,
objects, relations, rules of combination and a domain in which a distinction
can be made. SFT instead began by asking what could count as an operational
counterexample to the statement *there is no nothing*. If no statement,
denial, record or proof object is presented, no counterexample has been
supplied. If a purported counterexample is presented, an occurrence has been
supplied and the presentation is not nothing. The result is deliberately
operational. It does not import numerical zero, Boolean negation,
set-theoretic emptiness or a prior universe of discourse as an SFT premise.

This boundary first supplied the programme with a starting point and later
became the root theorem identified as
SFT-ROOT-THERE-IS-NO-NOTHING. The historical foundation paper explicitly
records an important internal development: the One began chronologically as
the project’s operational axiom and was subsequently derived beneath a
machine-checked self-proof. The current V3 description therefore does not
pretend that the final zero-axiom formulation was present unchanged at the
beginning. It preserves the earlier role while assigning current authority to
the reconstructed theorem and its receipt.

From the admitted occurrence, the project sought the minimum structure that
did not add an arbitrary choice. The occurrence’s complete self-whole became
the structural One. Positive finite count, exact part, distinction, retained
orientation, reversible return and the minimal Fold were separated into
successive obligations. This sequence matters. “One”, “part” and “Fold” are
not three names for the same intuition, and a later physical use of doubling
cannot be imported backwards as the reason the Fold was selected. The V3
Foundation now owns the generated objects, while the V3 Methods paper owns the
admission protocol by which they may enter the theory.

The early paper lineage *There Is No Nothing* retains concept DOI
10.5281/zenodo.21035460 and published versions 1.0, 2.0 and 3.0 in the
catalogue. It is an historical lineage rather than the current owner. The
catalogue marks every pre-V3 version with the exact public notice
“[Depreciated and replaced by V3]” and routes this concept to two successors:
the V3 Methods paper, version 0.3.0, DOI
10.5281/zenodo.21627646, and *From Nothing to Fold*, version 1.3.0, DOI
10.5281/zenodo.21627656. The literal word “Depreciated” is retained here
because it is the public catalogue label; it is not silently corrected inside
a quoted metadata identity.

The division between those two successors is substantive. Methods owns the
premise-free operational boundary, candidate-registration rules, fail-closed
admission engine, evidence vocabulary, publication authority and open
verification platform. Foundation owns the sixteen current root and
foundation claims that generate the One, count, part, distinction, Fold and
their immediate closure structure. Under this division, the constitution does
not admit itself as though it were simply another downstream physical object,
and a branch claim cannot alter the method that judged it.

### From a root paper to a theory-wide synthesis

The next phase asked how far the root construction could be carried. The
historical repository begins its visible clean-room recreation on 30 June
2026. During the following days the work expanded from the root and Fold into
exact arithmetic, orbit structure, physical constants, mass and mixing,
neutrinos, cosmology, nuclear and chemical structure, quantum relations,
classical dynamics, life, mind, mathematics and computation. The first large
ToE paper was assembled and publicly identified in early July. Successive
versions then incorporated the origin narrative, further derivations,
computational evidence, corrections and publication mapping.

The deprecated pre-V3 concept DOI for *The Smithian Fold Theory of Everything*
is 10.5281/zenodo.21182468. The historical catalogue preserves eleven version
labels in their actual public sequence: unnumbered; 2; 2.1; 2.0; 43.0; 44.0;
2; 3; 4; 5; and 6.0. The repeated and non-monotonic labels are part of the
record. They are not silently renumbered into a cleaner history because doing
so would break the relationship between the published objects and the files
that produced them. V3 treats those eleven deposits as one historical V2
publication lineage, not as eleven independent theories. The present V3
rebuild does not continue that deprecated lineage as another version.

Specialised papers emerged as particular questions became large enough to
require their own argument and evidence surface. *The Constants Are One
Object* pursued the common dependency of the constants. *The Continuum Was
the Bug* isolated the generated-finite mathematical boundary and its physical
consequences. *No Dice*, *Why There Is Uncertainty* and *Entanglement Without
Spookiness* treated deterministic support, observation classes and quantum
relation. *Smithium* developed the proposed periodic endpoint and extended
element programme. Further lineages treated the fine-structure constant, the
Hubble calibration ratio and cosmological constant, discrete-fluid
regularity, the Fold Decode measurement campaign, selfhood, free will and the
freedom of knowledge.

These papers did more than duplicate the large synthesis. They exposed
dependency questions that a single continuous narrative could hide. A
fine-structure comparison needed a different evidence surface from a
mathematical uniqueness proof. A proposed element needed nuclear, electronic
and chemical ownership. A claim about uncertainty needed to distinguish
formal many-to-one observation from an empirical statement about a physical
instrument. A statement about selfhood could not simply inherit its status
from an information-processing implementation. V3 later converted those
implicit separations into explicit branch ownership.

The pre-V3 catalogue now supplies a successor route for each specialised
concept. Constants route to Physics. The continuum routes to Mathematics and
Physics. The quantum papers route to Reversible and Quantum Computation.
Smithium routes to Chemistry and Physics. The Hubble paper routes to Physics
and Astronomy and Cosmology. Discrete-fluid regularity routes to Physics and
Earth and Environmental Sciences. The freedom-of-knowledge paper routes to
Methods. The self and free-will papers route to Consciousness and Cognitive
Science. Fold Decode routes to Information Science and Classical Computation,
with its application rebuild still pending. This routing preserves the useful
content of the earlier papers while refusing to let an historical paragraph
own several scientific fields at once.

### The checksum-bound v6 synthesis

Version 6.0 was the last large pre-V3 synthesis and is the principal historical
comparison surface for this monograph. The direct-source inventory binds the
file THE_SMITHIAN_FOLD_THEORY_OF_EVERYTHING.md at 110,926 bytes and SHA-256
a6bc294b76f5409c912149d91ba59e68bfcbe1e5f57f0995f57e122aaca64da8.
The bound source contains 1,405 lines and approximately 17,505 words. Its
Zenodo metadata names version v6.0, publication date 22 July 2026, CC BY 4.0
licensing and the same historical pre-V3 concept DOI. The preserved catalogue
audit identifies the public v6 version record as DOI
10.5281/zenodo.21482731 within that deprecated lineage.

The v6 paper stated the programme at the altitude then available. It opened
with the origin of the project, the no-nothing foundation and the exact
positive-rational domain. It then traversed the machinery of proof, the
fine-structure constant, mass, mixing and CP, neutrinos, cosmology, nuclear
and chemical matter, proposed forces and a dark sector, structural laws,
classical physics, matter, Earth and sky, life and mind, mathematics,
computational engines, a Grand Lock, a prediction ledger and reproduction
methods. Its machine surface reported 326 proof suites, 2,002 forced checks
and zero failures. Its publication map treated the ToE paper as the
navigational hub for the contemporary catalogue.

That document is valuable historical evidence for three reasons. It preserves
the scientific range that motivated the V3 branch programme. It binds the
pre-V3 equations, values, implementation descriptions and predictions to a
specific public version. It also shows why a more exact status constitution
became necessary. In v6, formal derivations, machine checks, measured
correspondences, application benchmarks, structural interpretations and open
predictions could occur close together in one prose stream. The paper often
used the vocabulary of closure at the scale of the synthesis. V3 does not use
that confidence as evidence; it routes each proposition to its present owner
and evidence record.

The checksum-bound source also contains an internal catalogue discrepancy that
must not be editorially erased. Part XVII and the Appendix A heading describe
the master inventory as Steps 1–303. The list itself contains Steps 1–303 and
then continues with Step 304 through Step 324, including later Protein, Go,
fluid, federated-mesh and Unison entries. The accurate historical statement is
therefore not simply “v6 had 303 steps” or “v6 had 324 uniformly indexed
steps”. It had a 303-step titled catalogue with appended entries through 324
in the bound source. V3 replaces that prose-step surface with separately
registered claims and retains this mismatch as a version-history fact.

The v6 paper also recorded application results at different maturity levels.
Its Protein discussion included a favourable material-architecture
reconstruction and multi-structure panel, alongside a distinct adverse
sequence-only whole-chain result and open propagation problem. Its Chess
discussion distinguished secured lower benchmarks from an active 2100
campaign. Its Go discussion distinguished exact small-board surfaces and
replayed 9-by-9 matches from a recovered 19-by-19 point-at-cut-off record with
a protocol defect. Its Unison discussion distinguished implemented counted
structures from runtime integration and general conversational objectives.
Those distinctions are preserved more precisely in the versioned manifests
described below.

V6 is therefore neither discarded nor treated as current. Its exact values
remain the authoritative statement of what that version said. Where a V3 owner
has reconstructed the corresponding result, the V3 package determines current
formal and empirical status. Where V3 has corrected or narrowed a result, the
correction governs. Where no V3 successor has completed the relevant test, the
old record may motivate the open programme but cannot be presented as if it
already satisfied the new protocol.

### The pre-V3 computational-proof programme

The computational projects were created to test whether the Fold could do
more than organise written derivations. Their scientific question was
constructive: could the theory produce exact state representations, executable
selectors, complete custody of decisions and measurable behaviour in domains
whose search spaces are large and whose outcomes are independently
checkable? The applications were Protein folding, Chess Fold, Go Fold and
Unison Fold AI. They are often discussed together as the V2 computational
programme, but the preserved application releases use their own local version
numbers. “V2” in this monograph denotes the pre-V3 model and implementation
era, not a claim that every application’s last release was literally numbered
2.

The 31 July inventory preserves four historical manifests as
historical-versioned implementation records and explicitly marks each
current_v3_proof_authority as false. That classification is not a rejection of
their measurements. It means that the manifests establish what the earlier
programmes executed, not that they satisfy a V3 proof gate written later.

| Programme record | Preserved boundary | Strongest relevant recorded evidence | Current V3 standing |
|---|---|---|---|
| Chess release 2.2, 18 July 2026 | Zero-trained-parameter counted engine with exact legality anchors | 54.2% at 1700; 62.5% at 1900; 41.7% in the latest completed 2100 batch | Historical measured implementation evidence; clean V3 recreation not started |
| Go release 2.2, 18 July 2026 | Exact small-board census and applied engine | Two independently replayed 2–0 GNU Go 9-by-9 batches; recovered 19-by-19 harness-reported 2–0 point-at-cut-off record with disclosed Round-1 defect | Historical exact and measured evidence; clean V3 recreation not started |
| Protein historical releases | Material reconstruction, local geometry and sequence-only blind development | Strong local windows and a favourable restricted material panel; adverse whole-chain and diverse-transfer results also preserved | Historical mixed evidence; V3 programme active under a new exact joint relation |
| Unison release 6.3, 18 July 2026 | Counted transformer-like implementation and law checks | 326 suites and 2,002 main-corpus checks; 21/21 current omni verification; mixed standalone generation and MMLU measurements | Historical implementation evidence; V3 scaffold present, formal and empirical gates open |

The table is intentionally compact. Each programme requires a fuller
reconciliation because its favourable and adverse records answer different
questions.

### Chess Fold: exact rules and measured rank campaigns

The preserved Chess 2.2 manifest describes a zero-trained-parameter counted
chess engine. Its formal implementation checks include 22 of 22 Fold chess-bot
tests and 8 of 8 endgame-oracle tests. Its perft anchors retain the registered
counts for the starting position, Kiwipete, an en-passant-pin position and a
promotion position. These records establish an executable legal substrate and
reproducible implementation checks.

The measured campaign is separately stated. At the 1700 boundary the engine
recorded 3 wins, 7 draws and 2 losses, scoring 54.2%. At 1900 it recorded 6
wins, 3 draws and 3 losses, scoring 62.5%. The latest completed 2100 batch
recorded 2 wins, 5 draws, 1 draw caused by the cap and 4 losses, scored as
41.7% in the manifest. Maria Smith’s registered victory criterion was strictly
over 50% at the point of victory, and the manifest reserves to her the
authority to decide when a development build deserves a real match.

An auxiliary v20 probe examined 36 positions, changed 9 choices, matched the
Stockfish-best move in 4 named cases and changed none of the 4 named
death-slide cases. The manifest explicitly classifies that probe as
Codex-authored and without governance authority. It therefore cannot be used
to rewrite Maria’s campaign conclusion or to declare a benchmark result. The
current V3 inventory says that no clean Chess recreation has started in the
current repository. The exact V2 checks and measured matches remain real
historical evidence; they are not a completed V3 Chess Fold proof.

### Go Fold: exact censuses, replayed wins and a preserved protocol defect

Go 2.2 contains both an exact finite surface and competitive measurements. The
recorded state censuses are 1 for 1-by-1, 5 for 1-by-2, 57 for 2-by-2, 489 for
2-by-3, 12,675 for 3-by-3 and 24,318,165 for 4-by-4. It records exact
empty-board values of 0 for 1-by-1, 0 for 1-by-2 and 1 for 2-by-2, with
17,038,501 nodes in the 2-by-2 solve. These are exact implementation surfaces,
not estimates of playing strength.

The applied record contains two separate 2–0 batches against GNU Go 3.8 on a
9-by-9 board at ceiling 3. Their game scores and transcript hashes are
preserved, and the release audit reports exact independent replay. A recovered
19-by-19 KataGo run is more narrowly classified. The historical harness
reported 2–0 at a 128-ply cut-off, with internal Tromp-style area scoring and
integer komi 7. In Round 1 the log contains two rejected play responses and
the old harness continued; Round 2 contains no logged rejected response. The
record is therefore a harness-reported point-at-cut-off measurement, not an
unqualified completed-match result.

This example became an important model for V3 evidence language. Preserving
the defect does not annul the exact censuses, the small-board solves or the
separate replayed GNU Go matches. Preserving the two reported scores does not
permit the rejected responses to be omitted. The current inventory reports no
clean V3 Go workspace. A future recreation must begin from the current
Classical Computation owner and register its complete state, history,
transposition, symmetry and match protocol without converting V2 evidence
into a new blind result.

### Protein before V3: strong restricted results and an adverse generalisation boundary

Protein developed through several public lineages rather than one uniform
sequence. The catalogue preserves the Fold Protein sequence-only lineage under
concept DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21276950, the Super Parity lineage under
10.5281/zenodo.21368944 and *Blind Protein Structure* under
10.5281/zenodo.21482127. Each is now routed to Biology with an
application-specific V3 rebuild identified as pending in the catalogue. Their
version labels must not be merged into one synthetic numbering scheme.

The favourable V2 material record is a real but bounded reconstruction result.
The current Protein paper records a published 24-structure panel with median
tm_repo 0.9255486262. The panel was mostly drawn from the ubiquitin family,
and its material relation had been derived from target-observed information.
It is therefore retained as a strong restricted reconstruction rather than
generalised blind evidence. A fresh diverse four-target development control
then gave mean TM 0.0742934939, median 0.0781022432, maximum 0.0987934258 and
median RMSD95 of 14.117 angstroms. That result is adverse for the V2
generalisation claim and remains visible.

A separately preserved blind-76 manifest, released on 19 July 2026, sealed a
76-residue ubiquitin sequence output before comparison. The complete output
matched 76 C-alpha atoms but scored TM 0.0269927379497572 and C-alpha dRMSD
52.89314678065376 angstroms. It nevertheless preserved all 74 same-index
three-residue windows and contained three very strong local windows, with
local TM scores approximately 0.99146, 0.96568 and 0.90596. The exact
historical conclusion is consequently mixed: the local geometry was strong,
while the sequence-only whole-chain assembly was adverse.

The historical protected lattice construction also contained a complete
76-residue ubiquitin trace reported at TM 0.9891211351 and C-alpha dRMSD
0.2608575408 angstroms, and the engine forced canonical alpha-helix and
beta-sheet coordinates. That existence and reconstruction evidence showed
that the local state space was not itself an obvious theoretical wall. It did
not supply the missing target-independent rule that propagates local
orientations into a general whole chain. The current V3 programme was designed
around precisely that distinction.

This is why neither a purely favourable nor a purely adverse summary is
acceptable. “Protein succeeded in V2” hides the restricted, target-derived
and family-heavy boundary. “Protein failed in V2” hides the exact local
geometry, protected full-chain witness and strong bounded reconstructions.
The accurate record is that V2 generated notable local and restricted
material evidence, while its fresh generalisation and sequence-only
whole-chain evidence were adverse. V3 retains both and tests a stronger,
target-free joint relation.

### Unison Fold AI: large counted implementations and open generalisation

The Unison lineage is the largest pre-V3 application version sequence in the
catalogue: 0.2; 1.1–1.9; 2.0–2.2; 3.0–3.4; 4.0–4.3; 4.6; 4.7; 5.0; and
6.0–6.5 under concept DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21217278. It is routed to Classical
Computation and Consciousness and Cognitive Science, while the
application-specific rebuild remains pending.

The preserved 6.3 manifest records a substantial executable surface. The main
corpus passed 2,002 checks across 326 suites with zero failures and 326 of 326
source-certificate identities. The Unison laws recorded 8 of 8 coherence
checks, 14 of 14 contextual-integration checks and 13 of 13
generation-selection checks. The current omni verifier passed 21 of 21. These
figures establish implementation and verification results within the
historical system.

The standalone empirical record is mixed and must be kept distinct from those
checks. Historical verify_unison passed 43 of 47. Free generation F3 measured
1 of 12. Two agent-selected MMLU development benchmarks measured 9 of 128 and
0 of 128. A Codex-authored auxiliary generation-quality comparison recorded
0 of 12 for the baseline, F1 and F3 and explicitly carried no governance
authority. These outcomes do not nullify the implemented structures, but the
passing structural checks do not turn the weak generation measurements into
validation.

The current V3 workspace is therefore classified as a frontier scaffold.
Mathematical closure remains open, empirical validation has not been run and
deployment is not authorised. The historical programme supplies engineering
experience, exact identities and tests for the future rebuild. It does not
authorize a present claim that Unison Fold AI has completed its V3
generalisation or parity objectives.

### Why the V3 clean-room reconstruction was required

By late July the project had accumulated a large number of equations, claims,
papers, receipts and application outputs in a very short period. Speed created
real scientific reach, but it also created a custody problem. A single paper
could place an exact derivation, a successful test suite, an observed
correspondence, a post-target reconstruction, an application benchmark and a
future prediction in adjacent paragraphs. A reader could not always tell
which statement selected which, which branch owned the result or which record
would prevail if a later source contradicted the prose.

The V3 repository was established on 23 July 2026 with an explicit clean-room
constitution and knowledge tree. The next recorded operations built the
fail-closed portable admission engine, admitted the root theorem and
structural One, and then derived positive count, exact parts and the minimal
Fold. This order prohibited the old synthesis from acting as an answer key.
Prior work could register an obligation, identify a promising carrier or
supply historical evidence, but a V3 result had to survive the new grammar
and evidence procedure.

The clean-room rule addresses contamination in both directions. Earlier
measured constants cannot choose a formal candidate. Later successful
applications cannot become premises of the theory that generated them.
External scientific convention can define a comparison or measurement
interface without being smuggled into the SFT derivation. Historical
application failures can test an implementation without being treated as
refutations of a broader relation that had not yet been implemented. Each
status is attached to the boundary that produced it.

Under V3, a claim begins as a registered obligation. Its dependency route and
candidate grammar are declared. The finite candidate surface is generated and
every candidate is retained with a decision. Admission requires one unique
survivor at the stated boundary, controls and an implementation-distinct
reconstruction. A receipt seals the result. Only then is external evidence
opened according to its own chronology and source custody. The terms theorem,
law, derivation, implementation, prediction, observation, measurement,
reconstruction, correspondence, compatibility, confirmation, adverse and
unresolved are no longer interchangeable rhetorical choices.

V3 also instituted one-owner branch boundaries. Mathematics owns generated
mathematical structures. Information Science owns representation, coding and
record relations. Classical Computation owns executable machine structures.
Reversible and Quantum Computation owns reversible and quantum carriers and
their formal boundary. Physics owns dimensional and measurement interfaces.
Chemistry owns chemical identity, bonding and reaction. Materials owns
structure–property relations across scale. Biology owns living organisation,
heredity and protein function. Medicine owns clinical and health
consequences. Consciousness owns cognitive and phenomenal claims. Earth,
Astronomy, Social and Engineering own their respective domain-specific
relations. A downstream branch can consume an upstream carrier; it cannot
retroactively validate or rewrite its source branch.

This reconstruction did not reduce the ambition of SFT. It transformed a
large flat synthesis into a directed scientific graph. The gain was not merely
administrative. The graph made non-uniform completion visible. A branch could
close its foundation without claiming a complete field. A dated return family
could be complete while a wider census remained unfrozen. A machine test could
pass while an empirical outcome remained adverse or unavailable. The
programme could now say exactly where work had reached without treating every
open boundary as a failure or every local success as total completion.

### The sixteen-paper V3 knowledge tree

The first publication architecture of V3 is a dependency-ordered set of
sixteen papers: one Methods paper and fifteen branch papers. The current
catalogue dated 29 July 2026 gives the following public versions. The order is
scientific, not merely bibliographic.

| Order | V3 owner paper | Public version and DOI at the freeze |
|---:|---|---|
| 00 | *There Is No Nothing: A Premise-Free Operational Foundation and an Open Verification Platform for Smithian Fold Theory* | 0.3.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21627646 |
| 01 | *From Nothing to Fold* | 1.3.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21627656 |
| 02 | *From Fold to Mathematics* | 1.5.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21688766 |
| 03 | *From Distinction to Information* | 1.4.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21688817 |
| 04 | *After Turing: The Fold Machine* | 1.4.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21688837 |
| 05 | *The Quantum Fold Machine* | 1.4.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21688860 |
| 06 | *From Fold to Physics* | 1.3.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21688879 |
| 07 | *From Fold to Chemistry* | 1.3.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21688899 |
| 08 | *From Fold to Materials* | 1.3.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21688923 |
| 09 | *From Fold to Life* | 1.0.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21630203 |
| 10 | *From Fold to Medicine* | 1.0.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21630785 |
| 11 | *From Fold to Consciousness* | 1.0.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21636397 |
| 12 | *From One World to Earth* | 1.0.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21640810 |
| 13 | *From One Sky to Cosmos* | 1.0.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21640812 |
| 14 | *From One Relation to Society* | 1.0.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21640814 |
| 15 | *From One Law to a Working World* | 1.0.0 — 10.5281/zenodo.21640816 |

The tree supports three modes of reading. A conceptual reader can follow the
derivational spine from the operational root to increasingly specialised
domains. A specialist can enter at a branch and trace every imported object to
its upstream owner. A verifier can bypass the narrative and reconstruct the
candidate, decision, control and receipt surfaces from the machine packages.
The representations differ because they serve different readers; their
scientific content must reconcile.

Methods is order 00 because it defines how anything is admitted. Foundation is
order 01 because it supplies the objects consumed by every branch. Mathematics
then exposes the exact generated structures used by Information and
Computation. Classical Computation precedes the reversible and quantum branch
because the latter must state exactly which operations and information
relations are being made reversible or quantised. Physics owns the entry into
quantity, dimension and external physical comparison. Chemistry consumes
physical carriers but owns chemical identity and reaction. Materials consumes
physics and chemistry but owns scale-linked structure and property. Biology
then consumes materials and chemistry without being reduced to either.
Medicine, Consciousness, Earth, Astronomy, Social and Engineering follow
their actual dependency routes.

Cross-Branch Synthesis is present in the current claim ledger but is not a
seventeenth paper in this sixteen-paper publication tree. Its twelve current
claims compose already admitted branch outputs. It owns no primitive law and
does not repair an unfinished branch. Protein Fold is likewise present as a
separate current preliminary application paper, not as a replacement for the
Biology owner paper. These distinctions explain why the active-source
inventory can contain additional paper and ledger surfaces while the V3
knowledge tree remains a sixteen-paper set.

### Seven full-field successor publications

On 29 July 2026 Maria Smith approved seven exact publication candidates. Each
was deposited through Zenodo’s new-version action on its existing branch
record. No independent replacement paper or new concept DOI was created. The
public verifier confirmed all seven records as public and latest in their
lineages, checked their parent relations and matched every public file size and
MD5 identity to the approved local releases.

| Branch | Version DOI | Live claims or obligations | Candidates | Controls | Rendered pages |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Mathematics 1.5.0 | 10.5281/zenodo.21688766 | 323 | 97,280 | 1,292 | 272 |
| Information Science 1.4.0 | 10.5281/zenodo.21688817 | 262 | 75,776 | 1,048 | 498 |
| Classical Computation 1.4.0 | 10.5281/zenodo.21688837 | 369 | 94,464 | 1,476 | 719 |
| Reversible and Quantum Computation 1.4.0 | 10.5281/zenodo.21688860 | 288 | 73,728 | 1,152 | 334 |
| Physics 1.3.0 | 10.5281/zenodo.21688879 | 368 | 257,776 | 1,472 | 886 |
| Chemistry 1.3.0 | 10.5281/zenodo.21688899 | 281 live claims; 272 registered obligations | 71,936 | 1,124 | 1,796 |
| Materials Science 1.3.0 | 10.5281/zenodo.21688923 | 289 | 73,984 | 1,156 | 790 |

Together the seven successor papers contain 2,180 live claims, 744,944
generated candidates, 8,720 controls and 5,295 rendered pages. Chemistry’s
count is deliberately expressed in two ways: 272 registered discipline
obligations are complete and nine separately returned claims remain in the
live branch, giving 281 live claims. Converting either number into the other
would erase a real distinction in the branch ledger.

“Full-field” in these publications means complete to the frozen or registered
current-evidence boundary stated by the branch. Mathematics, Information,
Classical Computation, Reversible and Quantum Computation and Materials are
recorded as full-field complete at their frozen censuses. Physics is complete
at its current categorical scope. Chemistry is complete at its 272 registered
discipline obligations while retaining the nine returned claims. The public
verification establishes deposit identity and custody. It does not convert
test success into empirical confirmation or prevent a later correction,
adverse result or lawful extension.

The version operation also matters historically. These papers were not
published as newly branded replacements that abandoned their earlier records.
Each new version retained its concept DOI and explicit parent. A reader can
therefore inspect how a branch moved from its earlier foundation or successor
edition to the complete-field version without relying on an external
narrative about what changed.

### Why current branch completion is non-uniform

The seven complete-field papers do not imply that every discipline has reached
the same boundary. All fifteen branch foundations are published and complete
at their declared foundational scopes. The dated V1/V2 novel-return programme
is complete across all fourteen registered subcategories. Beyond that shared
base, branch progress is intentionally non-uniform.

| Branch after Materials | Current admitted position | Exact current boundary |
|---|---:|---|
| Biology and Life Sciences | 82 | 82 of 424 frozen full-field obligations closed; 342 open |
| Medicine and Health Sciences | 76 | 72-law foundation plus four-claim prior-return family complete; full-field census not frozen |
| Consciousness and Cognitive Science | 77 | 72-law foundation plus five-claim prior-return family complete; full-field census not frozen |
| Earth and Environmental Sciences | 75 | 74-law foundation plus one-claim prior-return extension complete; full-field census not frozen |
| Astronomy and Cosmology | 77 | 72-law foundation plus five-claim prior-return family complete; full-field census not frozen |
| Social and Collective Systems | 76 | 72-law foundation plus four returned claims across the current ledgers; full-field census not frozen |
| Engineering Translation | 80 | 72-law foundation plus eight-claim prior-return family complete; full-field census not frozen |
| Cross-Branch Synthesis | 12 | Twelve inherited-return claims complete; final global synthesis open |

Biology is the active full-field branch. Its denominator of 424 is frozen, so
82 of 424 and 342 remaining are meaningful programme counts rather than an
estimate of an expanding list. The exact continuation recorded on 29 July is
the fourteen-obligation living-biochemistry-and-molecular-processes family,
beginning at its frozen target and formal-prediction boundary. A scaffold,
draft, test or source capture cannot close one of those obligations by itself.

For Medicine through Engineering, the absence of a frozen field-wide census is
not the same thing as 0% completion, nor is a complete foundation equivalent
to 100% field completion. The present papers own their foundational laws and
dated return families. Their broader field inventories have not yet been
frozen, so a percentage would manufacture a denominator. The correct status is
therefore qualitative but exact: foundation and registered returns complete;
field-wide census not frozen.

This non-uniformity is one of the principal gains of V3. V6 could describe a
theory-wide result in one section without exposing whether the surrounding
discipline had been fully inventoried. V3 records the achieved result and the
state of the containing branch separately. A strong astronomical
reconstruction can remain admitted while the complete Astronomy census stays
open. A protein result can be scientifically significant without being used
to close Biology. An engineering consequence can be derived while deployment
and safety validation remain separate.

### From the v6 step list to the current 2,751-claim graph

The current corpus contains 2,751 registered, model-admitted claims. They were
selected from 892,246 registered candidates, with 2,751 survivors and 11,004
passed controls. All 2,751 candidate censuses match their packages. All 2,751
receipt identities match the registered claim identities. All 2,751 claims
trace to the root, the ledger reports zero missing declared dependencies, and
it contains no unclassified obligations.

These figures cannot be compared to the historical 303-step heading by simple
division. The unit changed. A v6 step could combine a carrier, exact relation,
measured comparison, interpretation and open prediction in one paragraph.
V3 splits those functions where they require separate owners, grammars,
controls or evidence status. It also adds discipline-wide obligation families,
returned prior-work claims and explicit cross-branch compositions. The
increase is therefore a reconstruction of granularity and custody, not merely
an expansion of prose.

The direct-source content matrix adds a further publication audit boundary.
It finds candidate censuses, certificates, controls, registrations and status
records for all 2,751 claims. It finds elimination-receipt files for 2,671.
The remaining 80 are concentrated in the Chemistry representation, where 201
of 281 live claims expose the machine-extracted survivor-decision surface in
the inventory. All 281 Chemistry claims remain model-admitted, receipt-matched,
candidate-census-matched and root-traced in the authoritative ledger. This
paper must not turn that representational difference into either a fabricated
2,751-of-2,751 elimination-file count or an assertion that the 80 claims are
scientifically rejected. It is an audit-layer packaging boundary to retain in
the preliminary monograph and final reconciliation.

The content matrix also shows why this monograph cannot be a short overview.
It groups the live corpus into 432 derived families. It finds 2,705 claims
appearing somewhere in an active paper, 1,889 with an extracted active-paper
claim section, 46 absent from active papers and 861 lacking both an extracted
active-paper claim section and a WHY record. Every live claim is nevertheless
retained in the JSON matrix with its statement, dependencies, grammar, counts,
survivor, controls, evidence, chronology, receipt identity and package hashes.
The conceptual paper must supply a readable dependency argument while the
scientific audit and machine archive preserve what cannot sensibly be repeated
in prose.

The current ledger is strongly reconciled at claim level, but it is not the
final Grand Lock. The lightweight validator passes at 2,751 claims. The
canonical engine seal verifies all sixteen runtime files and the
verification-authority seal verifies all thirty-three protected files. The
last complete heavy invocation was run on 28 July 2026 at the earlier
all-foundations boundary: 1,319 of 1,319 derivation replays and 969 of 969
tests passed. It has not been rerun after the full-field expansion. The final
heavy run remains an explicit global obligation.

The global census/lineage_reconciliation record is likewise older than the
current 14-of-14 return completion and still reports a blocking row-to-claim
merge. The content families are closed at their dated boundaries, but the
final one-owner V1/V2 row and step merge has not been completed. A preliminary
ToE can accurately synthesise the current state; the final ToE protocol cannot
be declared satisfied until that merge, the canonical publication
reconciliation and the heavy verification are complete.

### Public catalogue reconciliation

The V3 publication operation did not delete the pre-V3 archive. On 28 July
2026 an unauthenticated read of the Zenodo catalogue returned 159 Ernos Labs
version records: 38 versions within the sixteen V3 concepts and 121 versions
within twenty-four pre-V3 concepts. All 121 pre-V3 version records carried the
exact replacement label, and no V3 record carried it. On 29 July the
complete-field verifier separately confirmed the seven new branch versions.

The twenty-four pre-V3 concepts include the historical ToE and foundation
lineages, the specialist mathematics and physics papers, the computational
applications and the earlier software record. Their DOIs and files remain
public. The operation added a status notice and successor route rather than
changing the past. This allows a reader to follow, for example, an early
fine-structure paper into current Physics, an early quantum paper into Quantum
Computation, or an early Protein result into Biology and the active Protein V3
application.

The earlier software concept, DOI 10.5281/zenodo.20775538, preserves versions
1.1.0, 1.1.5 and 1.1.6. It routes to the V3 Methods and platform paper and the
current public repository. Software remains a scientific instrument rather
than an independent theorem. Passing an old executable does not assign current
claim status, but preserving its source and version is necessary for
reproducing historical outputs.

Supersession therefore has a precise meaning in this corpus. It changes the
record that is allowed to speak for current science. It does not delete the
superseded bytes, DOI, adverse outcome or chronology. If a present V3 claim is
later corrected, the same rule will apply: the corrected successor will govern
current status, while the earlier version remains available as the evidence
that a correction occurred.

### The V3 Protein restart and its first standalone preliminary publication

Protein is the first computational programme to receive an active V3
recreation in the current repository. Its purpose is not to rerun the
favourable V2 material panel with new labels. The programme replaces the
target-derived selector with an exact sequence representation, explicit
source-bound coordinate emission, complete atom-pair custody, a registered
six-axis joint physical relation and an exact frontier solver. The intended
empirical endpoint is a prospectively sealed 100-target comparison against a
frozen AlphaFold 3 comparator.

The current programme has already passed several formal and computational
boundaries. A 256-form joint-microstate grammar was completely decided and one
relation preserving all six registered axes survived. The fixed-geometry
compiler and generic solver execute at the declared standard AGA boundary.
Successive parent investigations closed 215,940 and 3,347,070 candidate
prefixes. The current largest parent evaluates 42,173,082 candidates across
11,012 shards, comprising 375,014 completed leaves, 41,798,068 certified
prunes and 205 frontier states. An implementation-distinct reconstruction
matched the result with zero symbolic-comparison fallbacks.

The registered high-attraction slice is also closed. It contains 583 states;
573 are dominated and 10 retained, with 8 new nondominated incumbents. This is
a complete result for that slice, not for the remaining attraction bands or
the whole fixed-geometry sector. Two registered operations were active at the
31 July freeze: the remaining attraction-subband midpoint census and the
seeded-parent high-band reconciliation. No unfinished count from either is
imported into the paper.

Protein scientific gate v20 records 893 tests, zero failures and zero errors.
The publication-successor full-suite record v21 contains 898 tests, again with
zero failures and zero errors. Those results verify their respective
implementation and audit surfaces. They do not close an unexecuted frontier,
create a blind target or convert the pending AlphaFold comparison into
empirical evidence.

The V3 development evidence remains mixed. A no-extra-rule transition probe
retained one recurrent class containing all 84 glycine words and is adverse
for discrimination. An early 4APD atomistic carrier produced seven
sub-0.5-angstrom carbonyl-related pairs; a corrected carbonyl-plane
constructor removed every sub-1-angstrom pair, but the corrected structural
comparison remained adverse. The later joint relation superseded the old
zero-of-six executable-coordinate-family census at its fixed-geometry
boundary, while the adverse 4APD comparison remains intact.

Fresh sequence tests preserve the same discipline. The prospectively sealed
4B19 V2-generalised baseline was adverse at official median TM-score 0.17500.
A post-target alpha-helical diagnosis motivated an unseen 8HJC holdout, which
was adverse at 0.18242. A narrower cysteine-bounded rule transferred
positively to unseen 1JDM at 0.45609 within its registered class. The later
target-free partial recurrence closed computationally on all three current
development sequences, improved 4B19 to 0.23779, but regressed 8HJC to
0.139675 and 1JDM to 0.245185. The positive narrow transfer remains positive;
the two later regressions remain adverse. Neither is promoted to a complete
protein-fold result.

The official TM-align evaluator and AlphaFold 3 source environment are frozen.
The local AlphaFold data tests pass 7 of 7, Apple MPS is visible and the
inference suite passes 1 of 17. Restricted weights and the complete registered
databases are unavailable. This is an unavailable comparator dependency, not
an adverse AlphaFold result. At the freeze there are zero primary blind
targets, zero AlphaFold campaign output accesses and no parity result.

The first standalone V3 Protein preliminary paper was published on 31 July
2026 as version 0.9.3. Its version DOI is
10.5281/zenodo.21713537 and its retained concept DOI is
10.5281/zenodo.21713536. The verified public record contains eleven files,
names Maria Smith as creator, uses CC BY 4.0 and describes itself as the first
standalone preliminary-results release. Its version policy requires the
completed investigation to be published through Zenodo’s new-version action
on the same concept rather than by creating another standalone record.

Version 0.9.4 then lawfully superseded version 0.9.3 as the current publication
on that same concept. Its version DOI is 10.5281/zenodo.21717581. It adds the
complete 21-record claim audit, identity reconciliation and 898-test full-suite
record while preserving scientific gate v20 and the earlier version's
historical identity.

The paper’s current scientific gate is exact:
`verified_42173082_candidate_parent__closed_583_state_high_attraction_slice__remaining_subbands_and_recurrence_open__full_joint_predictor_halt`.
In readable terms, the exact representation, six-axis relation, largest parent
and high-attraction slice are real completed results. The lower bands,
complete fixed-geometry global frontier, arbitrary-sequence whole-chain
recurrence, certified full joint predictor and 100-target AlphaFold comparison
remain open. The preliminary paper predicts success at the final blind-parity
boundary, but prediction is not a parity result.

### The standalone V3 preliminary ToE publication

This monograph is the first theory-wide publication of the rebuilt V3 corpus.
Its human-readable version is 0.1.0 because it is a preliminary first
release in a new standalone V3 Zenodo lineage. It is not version 7 of the
deprecated pre-V3 ToE, and concept DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21182468 remains a
historical V2 identity only. Maria Smith authorised creation of a new
standalone Zenodo record. Draft 21717584 reserves version DOI
10.5281/zenodo.21717584 and concept record 21717583; Zenodo assigns the concept
DOI when the record becomes public. The authorised transaction binds the
rendered paper, audit layer, machine archive and checksums.

The word *preliminary* does not mean a sketch. It names the current programme
chronology. This version must give a complete and publication-grade account of
the repository state it freezes: the root, the branch derivations, the
field-wide papers, the claim graph, the evidence classifications, historical
corrections, current adverse results, open censuses, computational programmes
and machine identities. It is preliminary because several registered
scientific and integration operations remain open, not because those records
may be omitted.

The standalone V3 publication differs from v6 in scientific organisation as
well as size.
V6 was a compact synthesis organised around a prose step catalogue and a
single main proof-suite surface. The standalone V3 publication reconciles a
sixteen-paper
owner tree, seven complete-field successor papers, later branch foundations,
2,751 live claims, application records, source manifests and receipts. It must
explain the dependency relation among those objects without repeating every
machine record in its conceptual narrative. Full candidate members, decisions,
hashes and traces remain in the audit and archive layers.

The candidate also freezes open work without pre-empting it. A completed
Protein predictor and matched AlphaFold campaign will be later evidence. Clean
V3 Chess and Go recreations, when started, will produce their own registered
chronologies rather than inheriting V2 benchmark status. A closed Unison V3
architecture and empirical campaign will not be backdated into the historical
6.3 release. Biology’s remaining 342 obligations and the downstream
full-field censuses remain future version content until their own owner records
close them.

### Chronological status ledger

The principal stages can now be stated without treating every commit or
published version as a separate theory.

This ledger does not imply that every event began or ended on the day shown.
It identifies the dated records that freeze the relevant status. Application
development overlapped paper preparation, branch derivation overlapped
publication reconciliation and Protein work continues after its preliminary
deposit. The table is a custody map, not a simplified story of one task ending
before the next began.

### What is preserved, what is superseded and what remains open

The root question, the One, the Fold and the project’s open-science purpose are
continuous through the publication history. Their current formulations are
the V3 formulations. The early papers retain priority and development context,
but their language is not used to bypass the current candidate grammars.

The breadth of v6 is also continuous. Mathematics, physics, chemistry, life,
mind and computation remain within the programme. What changed is their
custody. A v6 proposition that now has an admitted V3 successor is read through
that successor. A proposition that was narrowed remains present at the
narrower scope. A proposition with an adverse or unavailable current record is
reported as adverse or unavailable. A proposition not yet reconstructed
remains historical or open; it is not restored merely because it appeared in
the v6 abstract.

The V2 computational programmes are preserved as empirical and engineering
history. Chess retains its exact rules checks and measured 1700, 1900 and 2100
batches. Go retains its exact censuses, solved values, replayed GNU Go games
and defective historical KataGo harness record. Protein retains strong local
and restricted material results together with adverse whole-chain and
generalisation tests. Unison retains its large counted implementation surfaces
and mixed benchmarks. None is silently relabelled as a completed V3
application.

The seven full-field branch publications are current. Their deposit identities
and current-evidence closures are established at their declared scopes.
Biology and the later branches are current at their foundations and returned
families, not at an invented universal full-field percentage. The 2,751-claim
ledger is current at claim, candidate-census, control, receipt-identity,
dependency and root-trace levels. The final heavy run, the global one-owner
historical merge and the final ToE protocol remain open.

Protein V3 is current through its 42,173,082-candidate parent and 583-state
high-attraction slice. Its adverse controls and positive narrow transfer are
also current at their own boundaries. General whole-chain recurrence and
AlphaFold parity remain unresolved because the registered decisive campaign
has not been run. Chess V3 and Go V3 have not started in the current
repository. Unison V3 is a scaffold with mathematical closure and empirical
validation open.

Finally, the V3 ToE 0.1.0 is a local preliminary candidate until the exact
release is approved and deposited as a new standalone record. Its future
concept and version DOIs are not assigned. The publication process must not
reuse the deprecated V2 concept DOI, invent a DOI or claim that an unapproved
local artefact is public. After this first V3 publication, later V3 ToE updates
must use Zenodo’s version action on the new V3 concept lineage.

### Reconciliation as a scientific result

The movement from the first no-nothing paper to this monograph is not simply a
sequence of increasingly long documents. It is a change in the unit of
scientific accountability. The first papers asked whether a premise-free
starting point and its consequences could be articulated. The large v6
synthesis demonstrated the reach of that programme and brought formal,
physical and computational work into one visible object. The applications
showed that Fold relations could generate serious executable systems and also
exposed where restricted success failed to generalise. V3 turned those
lessons into explicit rules of ownership, chronology, candidate custody and
evidence.

That reconstruction makes the project more persuasive because it makes
disagreement local. A reader need not accept an entire theory to inspect one
claim package. A failed Protein transfer does not become a vague verdict on
the root theorem; it remains an adverse result for a named selector. A passing
proof suite does not become empirical confirmation; it remains implementation
evidence. A measured physical correspondence cannot select its own exact form;
the derivation and comparison routes remain separate. A later correction does
not require forgetting what was previously published.

The resulting corpus is both larger and more bounded than v6. It is larger in
its 2,751 claims, 892,246 candidates, 11,004 controls, sixteen-paper tree and
seven field-wide publications. It is more bounded because every use of
completion, proof, prediction and validation must name its scope. The open
frontier is no longer a paragraph appended after a totalising claim. It is
part of the dependency graph itself.

The present trajectory therefore closes with a precise publication position.
The repository contains a current V3 scientific system whose root, branch
owners, live claims and public successor papers can be audited directly. It
also contains historical papers and computational records whose favourable
and adverse results remain discoverable. The proposed standalone V3 monograph
is the first theory-wide publication designed to reconcile those two surfaces at the
scale of the current corpus. It is complete for the state it freezes and
preliminary for the operations it identifies as open. Any later version must
extend or correct this record without rewriting its chronology. That is the
end of this project-version investigation, not the end of science.

## Part I — From Occurrence to Quantum Computation

*The root, Foundation, Mathematics, Information Science, Classical Computation, and Reversible and Quantum Computation*

This part follows the first continuous dependency route in Smithian Fold Theory
V3. It begins before arithmetic, probability, computation or physical law is
available. It ends at the exact interface through which Physics receives
generated states, processes and resource identities while retaining ownership
of preparation, units, apparatus, uncertainty and measurement.

The sequence is not a conventional classification imposed on a finished
theory. Each branch exists because the preceding branch leaves a precise
problem unresolved. The operational root establishes that an admissible proof
object occurs, but not yet how an occurrence is represented as a complete
whole. Foundation derives that whole and its minimal reversible distinction,
but not a general mathematical science. Mathematics supplies exact carriers,
relations, proofs and bounds, but it does not yet distinguish a state from a
record of that state. Information Science supplies that custody, but not a
machine that executes a registered process. Classical Computation derives the
machine and its limits, but hands reversible and quantum operations to their
own branch. Reversible and Quantum Computation then derives complete branch
support, phase, interference, entanglement, measurement semantics, gates,
circuits, correction and limits before any physical device is allowed to
select the formal law.

At the repository freeze used for this monograph, the five unique scientific
ledgers from Foundation through Quantum contain **1,258 admitted claims**,
**346,470 generated candidates**, **1,258 unique survivors** and **5,032 passed
controls**. The two inaugural results reproduced by the Methods paper are the
root and structural One already counted inside the sixteen-claim Foundation
ledger; they are not counted twice. The current claim matrix classifies 194 of
the 1,258 claims as `independently_replicated` and 1,064 as
`empirically_tested_and_independently_replicated`. In the formal branches,
“empirically tested” refers to registered post-seal execution and observation
of the declared computational objects. It is not silently enlarged into a
measurement of nature or hardware.

| Layer | Active version | Current publication identity | Claims | Candidates | Controls | Current boundary |
|---|---:|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| Methods, *There Is No Nothing* | 0.3.0 | `10.5281/zenodo.21627646` | 2 inaugural results, included in Foundation | 8 across those two registrations | 8 | Admission constitution and founding operational results |
| Foundation, *From Nothing to Fold* | 1.3.0 | `10.5281/zenodo.21627656` | 16 | 5,222 | 64 | Foundation complete |
| Mathematics, *From Fold to Mathematics* | 1.5.0 | `10.5281/zenodo.21688766` | 323 | 97,280 | 1,292 | Full-field current-evidence complete |
| Information Science, *From Distinction to Information* | 1.4.0 | `10.5281/zenodo.21688817` | 262 | 75,776 | 1,048 | Full-field current-evidence complete |
| Classical Computation, *After Turing: The Fold Machine* | 1.4.0 | `10.5281/zenodo.21688837` | 369 | 94,464 | 1,476 | Full-field current-evidence complete |
| Reversible and Quantum Computation, *The Quantum Fold Machine* | 1.4.0 | `10.5281/zenodo.21688860` | 288 | 73,728 | 1,152 | Full-field current-evidence complete |
| **Unique total, Foundation through Quantum** | — | — | **1,258** | **346,470** | **5,032** | Dated and extension-open |

The public release verification of 29 July 2026 is controlling for the four
complete-field version DOIs. Pre-deposit wording embedded in their frozen
