## Method and the operational root

### The problem before the first premise

A premise-free programme cannot begin by assuming a conventional logical
system, a number, a set, a space or a physical object. Any such starting point
would already contain the structure the programme claims to derive. The first
question must therefore be narrower: what can be said about an object that has
entered an operational statement, denial, record, proof or derivation?

The registered root theorem is `SFT-ROOT-THERE-IS-NO-NOTHING`:

> No admissible operational statement, denial, record, proof object or
> derivational object is nothing: absence presents no counterexample, while
> every presented counterexample is an occurrence.

Its generator partitions every purported counterexample by one relation:
whether it presents an identifiable object at the proof boundary. The complete
declared grammar has two classes. `unpresented-absence` supplies no statement,
denial, trace or identity and therefore supplies no counterexample. A
`presented-occurrence` supplies an identifiable presentation; that presentation
is itself an occurrence and therefore is not nothing. Of the two generated
classes, exactly one is executable as a purported counterexample, and its
execution defeats the purported counterexample.

The claim has two generated candidates, one unique survivor, four passed
controls and depth-independent closure inside its stated operational boundary.
The controls reject a false premise with no counterexample identity, changed
source custody, a tampered two-survivor artefact and an attempted expansion
into an unexpressed metaphysical domain. An implementation-distinct Python
reconstruction regenerates the partition without importing an SFT module. The
result is a formal operational theorem. It does not claim to describe an
unpresented metaphysical domain; it establishes the root required for a public,
machine-checkable derivation.

### From occurrence to the structural One

Occurrence alone does not yet supply a number. The next unresolved question is
how the admitted occurrence can be represented completely without omission or
unforced addition. `SFT-FOUNDATION-ONE-001` generates six coverage-and-addition
forms. The unique survivor retains the occurrence as its complete self-whole,
adds nothing not supplied by the occurrence and omits none of it. This is the
structural **One**.

The One is not imported as the conventional numeral 1. It is the exact
self-whole relation forced by complete coverage. Its six candidates, one
survivor and four controls are included in the Foundation totals. The Methods
paper owns and reproduces this inaugural result because the result completes
the minimal starting interface; later count, part and Fold laws remain
separately registered Foundation claims and cannot become retroactive premises
of the root or One.

### The admission constitution

The two founding results establish more than vocabulary. They demonstrate the
route every later SFT claim must follow. A registered claim records its exact
statement, dependencies, exclusions, provenance, candidate generator,
candidate boundary and intended generality certificate before it can acquire
model authority. The admission sequence is:

1. register the claim and its dependency identities;
2. generate the complete declared candidate grammar;
3. retain one decision for every candidate;
4. require exactly one survivor and prove its minimality and named-shape
   uniqueness;
5. establish finite, finite-successor or depth-independent closure at the
   declared boundary;
6. execute the required false-premise, changed-source, tampered-artifact and
   boundary controls;
7. seal the derivation and source identities;
8. reconstruct the result through an implementation-distinct route;
9. where nature is claimed, register and seal the consequence before target
   access, then preserve the complete observation and comparison record; and
10. issue an unchanged-engine receipt only after every applicable gate passes.

This is a fail-closed route. A successful example, conventional name,
institutional endorsement, benchmark score or attractive application cannot
replace the candidate census. A failed control, missing dependency, changed
source or duplicate survivor halts admission. An application may consume an
admitted law and may later test it, but it cannot tune the earlier grammar or
select the law that the application is then said to support.

The mathematical constitution excludes imported axioms, fitted, trained, free
or target-derived parameters, semantic numerical zero, negative proof
magnitudes, irrational or imaginary proof scalars, floating-point proof
equality, completed infinity and an ungenerated continuum. These exclusions
do not deny that host programs use indices, empty containers, Boolean values
and process return codes. Those objects are quarantined implementation
mechanics: they may count or execute evidence but cannot become SFT proof
objects. Likewise, conventional mathematical and scientific descriptions may
enter later as declared correspondences or measurements; they may not flow
backwards into the derivation.

### The empirical constitution

Formal forcing and empirical testing answer different questions. Candidate
generation asks which structure survives a declared grammar. Empirical testing
asks what a source-bound observation establishes after the formal consequence
has been sealed. For a natural-science claim, capability closure precedes a
value-free target registry; target custody remains separate from the predicting
process; the executable predictor and environment are sealed; target or
comparator output opens only afterwards; and every favourable, adverse, null,
failed, missing and unavailable row remains in the record.

A post-target calculation remains post-target. A development-observed result
remains development-observed. A failed transport is a failed transport rather
than an absence in nature. An unresolved source is not a null result. Later
evidence may lawfully change a current classification when the registered
protocol permits it, but it cannot become an earlier blind prediction by
editorial declaration.

The engine's complete executable-line coverage verifies which implementation
lines the tests executed. It does not prove the absence of every software
defect, complete an omitted candidate grammar or empirically confirm a natural
law. Cryptographic hashes identify the exact artefacts that were checked; they
do not make those artefacts immune from scientific criticism.

### What an admitted claim actually contains

The admission route is easiest to misunderstand when it is reduced to the
phrase “the computer checked it”. The computer does not receive a sentence and
decide whether the sentence sounds true. A claim package is a typed collection
of objects whose identities and relations are fixed before the final decision.
The human-readable statement names the scientific proposition, but model
authority belongs to the registered package rather than to persuasive prose.

The registration names the claim ID, title, owner, dependencies, excluded
inputs, candidate boundary, generation rule, intended certificate and required
controls. Each dependency must already possess a model-admitted receipt, and
the registered route must lead to `SFT-ROOT-THERE-IS-NO-NOTHING`. A narrative
citation to an upstream paper is not enough. The engine resolves the actual
receipt identities, so a missing dependency or a reference to a merely proposed
claim halts the route before enumeration.

Candidate generation then produces the literal declared product. A coordinate
may describe coverage, order, labelling, overlap, source custody, empirical
chronology or any other distinction that the claim says exhausts its boundary.
Every product member receives a stable candidate identity. The decision ledger
must contain one and only one decision for each identity. This requirement
prevents three common forms of retrospective selection: generating only the
favourable alternatives, silently dropping an awkward case, or adding a new
alternative after seeing which structure appears to work.

Elimination is ordered and explicit. A rejected candidate records its first
decisive failure, while the survivor records the complete set of preserving
properties. “Unique” means that the complete registered product contains one
survivor, not that the prose happens to discuss only one preferred object.
“Minimal” means that no less structured candidate within the grammar preserves
the requirements. “Named-shape uniqueness” checks that the surviving identity
and exact form are the ones declared by the theorem rather than an accidental
isomorphic or mislabelled result.

Closure is a separate question. A finite census can establish only its exact
finite boundary unless the package also supplies a lawful successor or
depth-independent certificate. A base-and-successor proof may cover every
supplied positive finite depth without constructing a completed infinite
object. A bounded search remains bounded when no such certificate exists.
This distinction is why a 10,000-value prime census and an unrestricted
positive-finite Fold recurrence can both be exact without having the same
generality class.

The certificate binds the claim, grammar, decisions, survivor, controls,
source manifest, derivation seal, independent implementation and closure
scope. The final receipt is not a free-standing badge. It is an identity map
to those underlying artefacts. If a source changes, the old hash does not
bless the new source. If a candidate is added, the earlier completeness
certificate does not cover it. If a law is extended, the extension receives a
new claim and receipt rather than inheriting authority by similarity.

### Why four controls recur

The repeated four-control structure is methodological rather than decorative.
Each control attacks a different route by which an invalid result could appear
to pass.

The **false-premise control** supplies an object that should fail the scientific
requirement. For the root it attempts to use unpresented absence as a
counterexample; for Fold it proposes the unchanged identity as a non-trivial
distinction; for an error-correction claim it may supply an ambiguous or
exceeding-budget image. A system that accepts both the result and its explicit
false premise has not discriminated the law.

The **changed-source control** alters a registered source or dependency
identity. Its purpose is not to show that cryptography proves the scientific
claim. It shows that a different input cannot silently reuse the old decision
and evidence chain. Source identity is scientifically important wherever
changing a source could change the result, and chronologically important where
the source must remain inaccessible until a seal.

The **tampered-artifact control** changes a candidate set, decision vector,
survivor, certificate or output. It tests whether the implementation notices a
mechanically plausible but unauthorised result. A missing decision, duplicate
survivor or altered terminal value must halt rather than being repaired by a
reader's charitable interpretation.

The **boundary control** tries to import something the claim excludes or to
expand the conclusion beyond its registered domain. Depending on the branch,
that may be a numerical zero, a signed or floating proof value, a stochastic
cause, an external answer table, an application result, a physical measurement
inside a formal theorem, or an unrestricted conventional conclusion from a
native bounded result. Passing the boundary control verifies that the
implementation rejected the prohibited expansion. It does not establish that no
other boundary criticism is possible.

The controls are called adverse because they are designed to make the route
fail in known ways. A passed adverse control means the expected rejection
occurred. That must not be confused with an adverse scientific observation,
which is a registered empirical result conflicting with the tested claim.
Both remain visible, but their evidence classes differ.

### Independent reconstruction and model authority

The main derivation and its validator cannot be the same executable path under
two filenames. An implementation-distinct reconstruction must regenerate the
declared object without importing the scientific module whose result it is
checking. This reduces the risk that one local coding error is merely repeated
by the validator. It does not make the two programs logically independent in
every possible sense; they may still share a mistaken specification. That is
why candidate completeness and specialist criticism remain necessary.

The engine is intentionally narrower than Maria Smith's scientific and
publication authority. It can establish that a registered package passed its
encoded gates under the protected engine and verifier identities. It cannot
decide whether Maria Smith approves publication, whether a conventional term
is rhetorically helpful, whether a newly proposed empirical interpretation
belongs to the claimed owner, or whether an unregistered specialist objection
has scientific force. Conversely, publication does not cause model admission.
An attractively edited or publicly deposited paper cannot turn a halted
package into an accepted claim.

This separation also applies to agent assistance. An agent may generate prose,
run a verifier, compare hashes or identify a discrepancy. It cannot alter
Maria Smith's authorship, approve a release on her behalf, grant a new claim
scientific status or delete an adverse record because it complicates the
narrative. The public repository makes that limitation inspectable by keeping
the machine authority, publication receipt and human authorship records
separate.

### Formal, computational and natural evidence

The same word can obscure very different evidential acts, so this monograph
reserves its verbs. A theorem is **proved** only at its formal grammar and
dependency boundary. A structure is **derived** when its alternatives are
generated and eliminated to the surviving form. An executable program
**implements** or **demonstrates** a derivation; successful execution does not
by itself measure the world. A consequence is a **prediction** only when its
target identity and chronology were correctly sealed before target access. A
source-bound external row is an **observation**, and a value tied to instrument,
method, condition and uncertainty is a **measurement**.

A post-derivation relation between an SFT form and a conventional form is a
**correspondence**. Structural correspondence concerns form; exact numerical
correspondence concerns equality or an exact interval relation; boundary
correspondence is confined to a named interface or limit. Evidence is
**compatible** when it is non-adverse but non-discriminating, and **supporting**
when relevant but non-unique. **Confirmation** is used only where the current
registered evidence protocol grants that classification. A conflicting
registered outcome is **adverse**. Evidence remains **unresolved** when the
required source is unavailable, incomplete, disputed or not yet sufficiently
classified.

These distinctions are not stylistic modesty. They preserve chronology and
falsifiability. A mathematical identity can correspond exactly to an observed
number without having predicted that number. A program can validate its own
trace without validating a physical theory. A later successful transport can
resolve a current access problem without erasing the historical failed
transport. A current adverse row can remain historically visible after a
lawful new claim at a stronger boundary succeeds.

### Threat model and limitations of the platform

The repository architecture addresses identifiable failure modes: source
drift, missing candidates, duplicate or missing decisions, hidden survivors,
changed controls, altered receipts, dependency substitution, post-seal target
access, suppressed rows and silent authority changes. Cross-platform commands
and standard-library implementations reduce environmental opacity. Source
manifests and hashes permit a reproducer to identify the exact object whose
status is being discussed.

It does not eliminate every threat. A registered candidate grammar may omit a
scientifically necessary coordinate that no current test anticipates. Two
implementations may share the same conceptual error. A source may be authentic
but inappropriate for the claim. A measurement protocol may preserve its rows
and still be poorly designed. A hash may bind a mistaken artefact perfectly.
No finite test suite proves the absence of all defects. Domain experts must
therefore inspect the definitions, boundaries, eliminations, controls and
empirical methods rather than treating the receipt as an oracle.

The clean-room rule controls answer flow from V1 and V2. Earlier work may
identify questions to reconstruct and may be compared after the V3 result is
sealed, but earlier certificates, executables and answer tables cannot enter
the new derivation runtime. The reconciliation layer then asks whether the V3
result preserves, corrects, narrows or leaves open each prior owned atomic
obligation. A clean room without reconciliation could omit history; a
reconciliation without a clean room could copy the answer. Both stages are
required.

### Repository as scientific publication

The platform's three access levels serve different readers. The conceptual
paper provides the dependency argument and major results. The scientific audit
layer exposes complete claim status, sources, controls, chronology,
corrections, ownership and reconciliation. The machine archive retains every
candidate, decision, trace, source snapshot, certificate and receipt. Moving a
full hash table out of a conceptual paragraph is an editorial change; deleting
the underlying hash or receipt is a scientific loss.

This architecture is also the practical anti-institutional claim of Ernos
Labs. The project does not ask readers to exchange trust in an established
institution for trust in a new one. It asks them to inspect the exact route by
which a claim acquired its current status. Maria Smith's independent position
is not evidence for the theory, and institutional prestige is not evidence
against it. The same public grammar, control and source questions apply to an
independent author, a professor, a corporation and an automated system.

Papers and documentation are licensed CC BY 4.0; repository code is licensed
under Apache 2.0. Those licences permit inspection, criticism, reproduction and
lawful reuse with attribution. The Ernos Labs name is separately a revocable
conformance designation: a fork may use the open work under its licences but
may represent itself as Ernos Labs only while it preserves the public evidence
constitution, adverse record, authorship and unchanged admission route.

### Open authority and falsification

The Ernos Labs platform separates authorship, publication authority,
criticism and scientific admission. Maria Smith is the author, copyright holder
and publication authority. Anyone may criticise, reproduce or attempt to
invalidate a claim. The narrower model authority is the public receipt issued
after the registered gates pass. Neither credential nor lack of credential can
override a failed gate.

The most direct formal falsifications are an omitted same-boundary candidate, a
second survivor, a simpler preserving form, a failure of the base or successor
certificate, or a counterexample inside the declared domain. Mechanical
falsifications include a dependency mismatch, changed source, missing decision,
failed control, non-reproducible trace or receipt mismatch. Natural-science
falsification additionally follows the claim-specific measurement and
chronology protocol. The method is therefore complete as an admission
constitution at its current versioned boundary while remaining open to a
lawful correction, a stronger test and a new registered question.

---

## 8. Foundation: from One to Fold

### 8.1 Why Foundation follows the root

The root and One establish occurrence and complete self-wholeness. They do not
yet provide a positive count, a proper part, a distinction, a transformation,
a composed word, a proof trace or an empirical boundary. Foundation owns those
objects because every later branch requires them. It does not own general
mathematics, a physical quantity, an observational dataset or the behaviour of
an application.

The current Foundation is complete at sixteen claims. It generated 5,222
candidates, retained sixteen unique survivors, passed sixty-four controls and
was independently reconstructed sixteen times. Its prior-work audit read all
763 registered V1/V2 entries—356 V1 and 407 V2—identified twenty-four
Foundation-relevant source entries, decomposed them into thirty-two
Foundation-owned atomic obligations and closed all thirty-two. The remaining
739 entries were recorded as reviewed non-Foundation ownership rather than
silently omitted.

### 8.2 Complete Foundation theorem ledger

| Dependency order | Claim | Candidates | Exact contribution |
|---:|---|---:|---|
| 1 | `SFT-ROOT-THERE-IS-NO-NOTHING` | 2 | Presented occurrence is the unique admissible operational class. |
| 2 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-ONE-001` | 6 | The occurrence's complete self-whole is the structural One. |
| 3 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-COUNT-001` | 10 | A non-empty finite succession has one exact positive count: its complete generation trace. |
| 4 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-PART-001` | 192 | A held selection in a complete, disjoint, equal-fibred partition has an exact positive held/whole coordinate. |
| 5 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-FOLD-001` | 288 | The minimal non-trivial reversible distinction is two equal disjoint held fibres with a complete return to One. |
| 6 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-PART-EQUIVALENCE-001` | 1,024 | Parts from different partitions are equal exactly through complete common refinement and bijective lifted selection. |
| 7 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-FOLD-ASSEMBLY-001` | 192 | Every finite Fold succession has complete held-label word support and retained transition and return traces. |
| 8 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-EXACT-OPERATIONS-001` | 128 | Exact positive junction, product, guarded Take, complete-One cast, unison and Fold form one root-bound interface. |
| 9 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-HALF-ONE-001` | 128 | One held fibre of the first Fold is the unique proper one-of-two ground. |
| 10 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-FOLD-DYNAMICS-001` | 128 | Fold is closed, two-to-one, phase-pair identifying and recurrent at the first one-third/two-thirds cycle. |
| 11 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-PRIMITIVE-MAP-UNIQUENESS-001` | 84 | Fold is the unique least-size generator in the declared four-map composition grammar. |
| 12 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-FORM-GRAMMAR-001` | 288 | Foundational forms are generated by a One leaf or a finite two-child Fold node with held edges and return. |
| 13 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-FORM-ENFORCEMENT-001` | 192 | A form's identity is its complete canonical recursive construction trace. |
| 14 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-DERIVATION-TRACE-001` | 256 | Every admitted finite derivation retains immutable sources, ordered operations, intermediates and root lineage. |
| 15 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-MEASURED-VALUE-BOUNDARY-001` | 256 | Derivation-to-measurement flow is one-way and every target row is retained. |
| 16 | `SFT-FOUNDATION-ADMISSION-ENFORCEMENT-001` | 2,048 | Exactly one generated admission route retains every required formal, control, validation, empirical and receipt gate. |
|  | **Total** | **5,222** | **Sixteen depth-independent, model-admitted Foundation claims** |

Every row has exactly one survivor and four passed controls. Candidate counts
are not publicity estimates: they are the exact sizes of the registered
products. The unequal sizes reflect different grammars. The root requires a
two-class operational partition, whereas admission enforcement must decide a
2,048-member product of custody and gate choices.

### 8.3 Count, part and exact equality

`SFT-FOUNDATION-COUNT-001` resolves the first question left by One: how can
plural occurrence be represented without assuming arithmetic? A generated
non-empty finite succession retains every generated occurrence exactly once
and adds none. Its ordered generation trace is its positive count. The theorem
does not begin with a numeral; the familiar numeral is a later interface label
for the already generated trace.

A count alone does not identify a portion of a whole. `SFT-FOUNDATION-PART-001`
therefore starts from a complete, disjoint, equal-fibred partition of One and a
non-empty held selection. The exact coordinate is the positive count of held
fibres relative to the positive count of the complete partition. Equality
across different partitions cannot be imported as fraction reduction.
`SFT-FOUNDATION-PART-EQUIVALENCE-001` generates the pair-cell common refinement
of two partitions, lifts both selections completely and requires a one-to-one
and onto pairing of the lifted held cells. The resulting equivalence is
reflexive, symmetric, transitive and stable under uniform refinement without
requiring an external scale.

This establishes the exact positive part carrier used throughout the corpus.
Empty selection remains structural empty One rather than a numerical proof
value. Opposition is retained by a held label identifying which side owns an
unmatched positive remainder. The constitution can therefore represent
absence, order and orientation without importing signed arithmetic at the
foundation.

### 8.4 The minimal Fold

The next unresolved problem is change. Identity leaves One unchanged, while an
arbitrary partition would introduce a selector or scale not present in the
unqualified whole. `SFT-FOUNDATION-FOLD-001` exhausts 288 combinations of whole
coverage, fibre overlap, fibre equality, extension class, label relation,
return relation and extra data. The unique survivor is complete, disjoint and
equal; uses the first positive-count extension; retains two distinct held
labels; includes an exact return to One; and adds no free datum.

That survivor is the minimal **Fold**. Its two labels are structural identities,
not positive and negative numerical quantities. The return relation matters:
without it, the partition is not established as a reversible distinction of
the source whole. The candidate eliminations also establish minimality. The
identity has no non-trivial distinction; every later extension is non-minimal
because the first extension already supplies one; overlapping or unequal
fibres fail exact partition; unheld or identical labels fail operational
distinguishability.

Repeated Fold must then be generated rather than presumed to be binary
notation. `SFT-FOUNDATION-FOLD-ASSEMBLY-001` begins from the structural empty
One word. At each finite successor, it appends each of the two held labels once
to every preceding word, retaining predecessor, transition and return traces.
The first five generated support sizes are conventionally displayed as 2, 4,
8, 16 and 32, but the generality certificate is the base-and-successor
construction, not extrapolation from those examples.

### 8.5 Exact operations, half-One and dynamics

The exact operational interface acts on positive rational parts through One.
Complete turns cast to One rather than to numerical absence. Junction combines
positive traces; product is generated pair-cell support; `Take(a,b)` is
admissible only when `a` is strictly larger than `b`; unison is the exact One
relation. Operational Fold is self-junction followed by complete-One cast:

\[
\operatorname{Fold}(x)=\operatorname{cast}(x\mathbin{\mathrm{joined}}x).
\]

Thus `cast(2)=One`, `Fold(3/4)=1/2`, and
`Take(One,1/4)=3/4` in the exact registered examples. Exact forms precede
their conventional notation; the notation does not select the operation.

The first Fold forces the unique proper one-of-two ground, **half-One**. Either
singleton held fibre self-junctions to One, has an equal-coordinate complement
and Folds to One; One itself is Fold-fixed. Version 1.3 explicitly corrects a
terminology collision retained from earlier work. Complement and phase
translation are different operations:

\[
C(x)=\operatorname{Take}(One,x),
\qquad
A(x)=\operatorname{cast}(x+\text{half-One}).
\]

Half-One is self-complementary, so `C(half-One)=half-One`; its half-One phase
translation casts to One, so `A(half-One)=One`. Both prior structural results
remain preserved, but the names are no longer conflated.

Fold dynamics then follows. For each image `y`, one preimage is `y/2` and the
other is its half-One phase partner. The phase map is involutive and Fold
identifies each phase pair:

\[
\operatorname{Fold}(A(x))=\operatorname{Fold}(x).
\]

For every generated even partition, phase-paired positions have the same Fold
image and the distinct images form the next uniform partition. The first
non-static recurrent orbit is exact:

\[
\frac{1}{3}\longmapsto\frac{2}{3}\longmapsto\frac{1}{3}.
\]

The primitive-map theorem scopes its stronger uniqueness claim carefully. Its
normalised zero-parameter grammar contains identity, square, constant One and
Fold. Base-four enumeration executes 4 one-operation words, 16 two-operation
words and 64 three-operation words, 84 in total. Identity is static; square is
injective and contracts every proper part; constant One collapses; Fold alone
is both non-injective and non-statically recurrent at size one. Positive
construction-size induction prevents any larger composition from displacing
that already qualifying size-one survivor. This is uniqueness within the
registered grammar and generator predicate, not an unrestricted theorem over
every imaginable expression language.

### 8.6 Form, proof and evidence boundaries

Fold words provide support, but downstream branches also require a canonical
object language. The foundational form grammar therefore has two productions:
a structural One leaf and a finite Fold node with two recursively generated
children on distinct held-labelled edges and an explicit return to the parent
whole. Form enforcement identifies a form by its complete canonical
construction trace; equivalent forms reproduce the same trace.

The derivation-trace theorem extends that custody to a whole proof. A valid
record retains immutable source and dependency identities, every operation in
order, the exact identity of each input and output, every intermediate and the
terminal result. Deterministic replay must reproduce the record and its route
to the root. The final admission theorem exhausts 2,048 possible combinations
of root custody, source custody, parameter status, enumeration, uniqueness,
form closure, controls, distinct validation, empirical order, disposition
retention and dependency use. Only the route retaining every gate survives.

The measured-value boundary is deliberately one-way. A formal law may seal a
consequence that a later source-bound measurement tests. A measurement may
support, conflict with or leave the consequence unresolved, but no value,
score or target may travel backwards to alter the law. Foundation itself owns
no physical measurements. Its sixteen empirical classifications are
independent formal reproductions, not claims that nature has been measured.

### 8.7 How the Foundation grammars force rather than name the result

The theorem ledger can look deceptively familiar after its objects have names.
“One”, “part”, “Fold” and “half” evoke conventional mathematics, but the
candidate products precede those translations. The scientific burden is to
show why the survivor is forced by the admitted dependencies and why nearby
forms fail without relying on the familiar name.

For count, the generator distinguishes complete versus incomplete generation
trace, duplicate versus single custody of an occurrence, ordered succession
versus an untraceable total, and extra data versus no addition. A conventional
integer label cannot repair a trace that omits or duplicates an occurrence.
The survivor is therefore not “the numeral that equals the length”; it is the
complete positive succession itself. The numeral is a readable correspondence
to that retained construction.

For an exact part, the whole must be completely partitioned, cells must be
disjoint, fibres must be equal and the held selection must be non-empty and
contained within the partition. Dropping completeness makes the denominator
unknown. Permitting overlap double-counts shared structure. Unequal fibres
introduce a weight not supplied by One. An unheld selection has no operational
identity. The 192 candidates exhaust these distinctions and leave the positive
held-count/whole-count coordinate. This order is important: a fraction is not
assumed and then used to justify equal fibres; equal-fibre generation and held
selection force the coordinate later written as a fraction.

The Fold product makes the same discipline visible at greater scale. Its 288
candidates classify whole coverage, overlap, equality, extension class, label
relation, return and added data. Exactly 144 lose part of One and fail at whole
coverage. Of the remaining forms, 72 repeat structure through overlapping
fibres and 36 introduce an unsupported allocation through unequal fibres.
Twelve identity-count forms never become non-trivial, while twelve later
extensions are non-minimal because the first extension already distinguishes.
Four forms omit labels, four reuse one label for both fibres, two omit the
return and one adds an external datum. The one remaining form has every
property in the theorem statement. This elimination census is the reason for
the result; the word Fold is assigned after the form survives.

Cross-partition equivalence must then solve a problem that naive coordinates
cannot. One-of-two and two-of-four have different construction traces but may
represent the same part. One-of-two and one-of-three do not. The 1,024-member
equivalence grammar generates common pair-cell refinement and asks whether it
covers both wholes, remains disjoint and equal-fibred, refines both source
partitions, lifts both held selections completely and pairs the lifted cells
bijectively without a new scale. Incomplete coverage eliminates 512 forms;
overlap eliminates 256; unequal cells eliminate 128; failures of the two
refinement and two lifting conditions successively remove 64, 32, 16 and 8.
Non-bijective, unmatched and absent pairing classes remove the remaining
non-preserving forms, and an extra-scale form is rejected last. The generated
one-of-two/two-of-four example passes; the one-of-two/one-of-three control does
not.

Fold-word assembly similarly avoids importing exponentiation. Its 192 forms
classify step coverage, word-length consistency, support completeness, word
uniqueness, transition records, return records and added data. A proposal that
retains no Fold step or omits one loses the recursive support. A word with the
wrong length loses one-label-per-step custody. Missing or duplicate words
destroy completeness. Missing transitions prevent replay; missing returns
break the relation to One. The survivor is therefore the complete recursive
word tree, from which the conventional support size `2^k` may later be read.

The form grammar then compresses that recursively generated support without
discarding its origin. A One leaf is terminal. A Fold node has two children on
distinct held edges and an explicit return to the parent whole. A candidate
with one child omits a forced fibre; a node with indistinguishable edges
collapses the distinction; a node without a return ceases to represent a Fold
of its parent. Form enforcement serialises the complete recursive construction
canonically, so equality is replayable trace identity rather than a visual or
authority judgement.

### 8.8 Exact operation design and the prohibition on silent absence

The operational interface is designed to remain closed on the exact positive
carrier. An unguarded subtraction-like operation could produce absence or a
signed magnitude not yet derived. `Take` therefore requires a strictly larger
source part and returns only the retained positive remainder. When two forms
coincide, the system records the unison relation rather than assigning a
negative or zero distance. When self-junction completes a whole turn, cast
returns One rather than deleting the completed cycle.

This distinction is functional, not cosmetic. If a completed turn were stored
as numerical zero, the same literal could mean an empty support, an index
origin, a process status, a vanished quantity or a complete return. Those
meanings have different dependencies and different reconstruction rights. The
Foundation preserves them as typed structural records. Empty One names a
structural absence at an interface; One names the complete self-whole and the
completed return; held orientation names which positive side retains an
unpaired part.

Fold's two-preimage law follows exactly from this cast. For an image `y`, the
lower preimage self-junctions directly to `y`. Adding half-One before
self-junction adds one complete One after doubling, which cast removes as a
complete turn without losing the retained phase label. The two preimages are
therefore distinct in the source and identical under Fold. A third preimage
would require an added phase class not generated by the first Fold.

Uniform even partitions expose the same law globally. In a partition with
`2n` positions, position `i` and position `i+n` are half-One phase partners.
Fold maps them to the same member of the `n`-position image partition. After
duplicate images are identified, every image position occurs with the same
generated multiplicity. This is a general finite-successor result over every
generated positive `n`, not a pattern inferred from the six executed control
partitions.

The recurrent one-third/two-thirds orbit supplies the least non-static witness
for the primitive-map predicate. Identity has no movement. Square retains
distinct inputs and strictly contracts a proper positive part. Constant One
has a fixed collapsed image. Fold has both a two-member fibre and a non-static
return cycle. The grammar's construction-size order then makes Fold the first
qualifying map before any composition. Enumerating the 84 words through size
three demonstrates the ranking implementation; the positive-size induction
excludes every longer word from being *least*, regardless of whether a longer
composition also has recurrence.

### 8.9 The proof trace as a mathematical object

An informal derivation can conceal where a symbol changed meaning, where an
unavailable premise entered or where a later result was treated as earlier.
The Foundation trace theorem makes those failures mechanically addressable.
A trace begins with immutable source and dependency receipt identities. Each
operation record names its exact input identities, operation identity and
output identity. Intermediate records are retained rather than reconstructed
from the desired conclusion. The terminal identity is accepted only when
deterministic replay reproduces every stage.

Root lineage is transitive. A Mathematics claim may depend on Foundation form
enforcement rather than cite the root directly, but the engine follows the
Foundation receipt's own dependencies until it reaches the root. A dependency
graph with a missing node, an unresolved cycle or an unadmitted leaf cannot be
repaired by placing “there is no nothing” in the abstract. The actual receipt
path must exist.

The trace also protects historical status. If an early attempt halted, its
source and terminal failure remain reproducible. A corrected claim uses a new
registration and does not change the earlier output identity. If a later
observation changes a current evidence classification, the later source and
date are appended; they do not appear inside the earlier derivation trace.

### 8.10 Why one admission path survives 2,048 alternatives

The admission theorem turns the Methods constitution into a Foundation result.
Its candidate grammar combines whether a proposed route is root-bound,
source-bound, axiom-free and zero-parameter; whether its declared grammar is
complete; whether it leaves one survivor and closes its form; whether controls
and implementation-distinct validation pass; whether an empirical target opens
only after sealing; whether every disposition is preserved; and whether only
admitted dependencies are used. These binary and multi-class choices generate
2,048 possible routes.

A route that keeps the right result but loses the source fails. A route that
keeps the source but samples candidates fails. A unique survivor without
closure fails. Closure without adverse controls fails. Main-path agreement
without distinct reconstruction fails. An empirical match whose target opened
before sealing fails the prediction class even if the value agrees. A route
that suppresses a rejected row fails evidence custody. A route that allows a
proposed but unadmitted dependency fails model authority. Only the route that
retains every gate survives.

This theorem explains why the later papers can share an admission vocabulary
without repeatedly redefining it. The generic gate logic belongs here; each
downstream claim still owns its exact grammar, eliminations, controls, sources
and chronology. The conceptual paper may therefore summarise the common route
while the audit and machine layers preserve the complete claim-specific
record.

### 8.11 Historical reconstruction and non-ownership

The Foundation audit does not treat every earlier SFT statement as a
Foundation theorem. It identifies the atomic obligation, asks which branch
owns its scientific falsification condition and records non-ownership
explicitly. A statement about a physical constant may depend on Fold, but its
value and measurement belong to Physics. A statement about a protein may use
Foundation recurrence, but sequence, structure and biological evidence belong
to Biology and the computational-proof programme. Dependency is not ownership.

This one-owner rule prevents an upstream formal result from acquiring
empirical authority merely because many downstream applications cite it. It
also prevents a later adverse application result from silently deleting the
formal theorem. The application may falsify a claimed handoff, parameterisation
or domain law; it does not alter the upstream candidate census unless its
counterexample lies inside that census's declared boundary.

### 8.12 Foundation status and handoff

The Foundation has no open registered row at its sixteen-claim boundary. The
half-One terminology correction is current and the earlier usages remain
visible in the reconciliation history. No adverse natural result exists here
because no natural measurement is owned. Open extension remains real: a new
foundational object, an omitted candidate or a counterexample inside a stated
boundary must enter through a new registered package rather than by revising a
receipt in prose.

Foundation hands Mathematics the structural One, positive finite count,
positive parts, exact common refinement, Fold, held labels, finite word and
form grammars, exact primitive operations, dynamics, proof traces and the
one-way empirical boundary. Mathematics must now derive general structure from
those objects without treating the names of conventional disciplines as
axioms.

---

## 9. Mathematics: the exact generated-finite language

### 9.1 Why Mathematics follows Foundation

Foundation can generate and compare Fold forms, but Physics, information and
computation require a much wider exact language: relations, graphs, algebraic
operations, order, geometry, probability, optimisation, dynamics, logic,
composition, bounds and certified approximation. Mathematics owns those
formal structures. It does not own the physical interpretation of a rational
threshold, a biological function, the semantics of a lived observation or the
performance of a device.

The current branch contains 323 admitted claims across twenty-four families.
It generated 97,280 candidates, retained 323 unique survivors, passed 1,292
controls and has 323 implementation-distinct reconstructions. Twenty-seven
claims—the twenty-two foundational mathematical claims and five scientific
calculator lineage claims—are classified as independently replicated. The
other 296 carry registered post-registry execution observations and are
classified as empirically tested and independently replicated within their
formal computational boundaries.

### 9.2 Mathematical constitution

The primitive numerical carrier is an exact positive part of One with its
construction and comparison trace. Addition is a disjoint junction; product is
complete pair-cell generation; quotient is common refinement; order retains a
positive unmatched remainder together with its held orientation. Structural
empty One expresses an absent selection or identity path without becoming a
semantic numerical zero. A held orientation replaces an imported negative
proof magnitude. Irrational, imaginary and floating descriptions may appear
only through exact enclosures, finite constructions, conventional
correspondences or measured values after the relevant law seals.

The continuum boundary is potential rather than completed. Every supplied
positive finite refinement depth can be generated, compared and extended, and
there is no greatest finite depth. That successor statement does not construct
a completed infinite carrier. A conventional continuum object therefore
requires a separately registered correspondence or approximation certificate;
it cannot be smuggled into an earlier proof because its notation is familiar.

The twelve validation claims partition all 305 pre-validation receipts across
the twenty-two scientific families before six handoffs complete the 323-row
ledger. The Grand Lock is a dated reconciliation: it preserves favourable,
adverse, absent, unresolved and boundary rows. It does not transform every row
into confirmation or prohibit a later mathematical question.

### 9.4 The first mathematical dependency chain

The first twelve mathematical claims establish the general generated-finite
language. Their candidate counts differ because each claim exhausts its own
registered product.

| Claim | Candidates | Principal result and exact boundary |
|---|---:|---|
| `SFT-MATH-EXACT-ARITHMETIC-001` | 256 | Positive traces and parts; disjoint junction, pair-cell product, common-refinement quotient, comparison and held remainder, without semantic zero or signed proof magnitude. |
| `SFT-MATH-DISCRETE-001` | 256 | Canonical finite collections, selections, relations, maps and base-successor induction. |
| `SFT-MATH-COMBINATORICS-001` | 256 | Complete finite recurrence, selection and canonical deduplication. |
| `SFT-MATH-GRAPH-NETWORK-001` | 512 | Nodes from generated forms, pair relations, paths, reachability, cycles, trees, cuts and conservation. |
| `SFT-MATH-ALGEBRA-001` | 512 | Complete finite carriers with total closed operation relations and exhaustive property witnesses. |
| `SFT-MATH-ORDER-LATTICE-001` | 512 | Witnessed order; totality only when certified; meet and join only when unique witnesses exist. |
| `SFT-MATH-GEOMETRY-TOPOLOGY-001` | 1,024 | Finite cells, boundary incidence and path distance; generated open families and inverse-image continuity. |
| `SFT-MATH-PROBABILITY-STATISTICS-001` | 512 | Exact held-support parts over deterministic microstates; uncertainty as observation closure rather than stochastic cause. |
| `SFT-MATH-OPTIMIZATION-001` | 512 | Complete candidates, feasibility and preference with all ties, undominated alternatives and Pareto classes retained. |
| `SFT-MATH-DYNAMICAL-SYSTEMS-001` | 1,024 | Carrier, transition, trajectories, recurrence, predecessor custody and an empty-One identity trajectory. |
| `SFT-MATH-LOGIC-PROOF-001` | 1,024 | Propositions, held orientation and proof traces; consistency and completeness statements remain relative to the registered finite grammar. |
| `SFT-MATH-CATEGORY-TYPE-COMPOSITION-001` | 1,024 | Objects, typed transitions, empty-One identity return, interface-preserving composition, functors and naturality. |

Arithmetic comes first because every later structure must count its support and
retain exact comparison. Discrete structure then separates carriers from held
selections and relations. Combinatorics exhausts finite choices; graph theory
turns pair relations into paths and networks. Algebra asks when operation
relations close and compose. Order refuses to manufacture an extremum where no
unique witness exists. Geometry and topology are generated from cells,
incidence, paths, open-family closure and inverse images rather than assumed as
a continuum.

Probability is downstream of complete deterministic support. A microstate is a
generated form; an observation is a total classification relation; states with
the same observation form a class. An event is a held support selection, and
its non-empty probability is its exact positive count relative to complete
support. Uncertainty is the ledger of distinctions closed by observation. It
does not cause an ungenerated transition. Optimisation then retains every
feasible undominated object and every tie. Dynamics iterates an admitted
transition while keeping terminal, recurrent and predecessor classes. Logic
and composition arrive only after those carriers exist, so their familiar
names cannot be retroactive premises of arithmetic.

### 9.5 Exact extensions and bounded famous-problem results

Ten further foundational claims reconstruct important V1/V2 lineages without
inflating bounded or structural results into unrestricted conventional
solutions.

`SFT-MATH-EXACT-RELATIONS-002` derives exact ratios, separations, reciprocals,
multi-fold complements, Fold transport, telescoping and least-common-return
cycles. `SFT-MATH-ORBIT-NUMBER-THEORY-002` separates power-of-two transients
from odd-denominator recurrence: the recurrent orbit length is the
multiplicative order of the binary generator modulo the odd denominator. The
statement is exact at the admitted rational Fold carrier.

`SFT-MATH-LIMIT-CONTINUUM-002` supplies exact finite rungs, the absence of a
greatest finite depth and rational convergence certificates while refusing a
completed infinity as a premise. `SFT-MATH-ALGEBRAIC-BALANCE-002` retains
positive polynomial sides, bracket and order records, and bisection
certificates for algebraic magnitudes without inserting an irrational root as
a foundational proof value.

`SFT-MATH-BOUNDED-N-BODY-002` proves recurrence for every finite tuple of
rational Fold states after any transient, with joint period given by the
least-common return of the odd-core periods. `SFT-MATH-FLOORED-FLUID-REGULARITY-002`
uses a depth-`k` grid whose minimum separation is `1/b^k`, bounds velocity
separation through One, and bounds discrete gradient and vorticity by `b^k`.
At depth five the exact bound is 32. This is a generated finite-depth
regularity result, not a continuum-fluid theorem.

`SFT-MATH-PRIME-PAIR-CENSUS-002` exhausts every even integer from 4 through
10,000: all 4,999 registered even values possess a prime complementary pair,
and the same bounded source contains 205 twin-prime pairs with upper member at
most 10,000. The claim is the complete bounded census, not an unbounded
Goldbach or twin-prime proof.

`SFT-MATH-RIEMANN-MIRROR-002` derives the complement involution and its unique
self-partner at half-One, then records a structural correspondence to the
`1/2` reflection of the zeta functional relation. Symmetry alone does not
locate every complex zero, and unrestricted complex-zero location lies outside
the current proof language.

`SFT-MATH-COLLATZ-FINITE-CENSUS-002` executes every starting value from 1
through 100,000 and records that each reaches the `1–4–2` cycle; the start 27
takes 111 steps. The claim also preserves an adverse control against the older
constant-three-quarter contraction shortcut: an odd macrostep can rise, so the
shortcut is not a proof even though the complete bounded census succeeds.

Finally, `SFT-MATH-SELF-SIMILAR-CONVERGENCE-002` derives binary local
separation, one distinction per step, the unit exponent under rank doubling,
`m^d` finite support and exact rational convergence records. Together these
claims show the intended discipline: an exact bounded result remains bounded,
a correspondence remains a correspondence and a failed shortcut remains
visible.

### 9.6 Arithmetic and algebraic extension across the full field

The complete-field extension asks whether the first exact arithmetic kernel is
sufficient to reconstruct the working relations used throughout later
mathematics. Eighteen `ARITH` claims answer that question in dependency order.
Generated succession supplies exact induction. Disjoint junction and pair-cell
generation supply addition and multiplication. Divisibility is witnessed by a
complete equal-cell decomposition; common divisors and multiples retain all
candidate witnesses before selecting a greatest or least class. Quotient and
remainder retain a positive quotient trace and a held orientation for the
unmatched part, so a conventional signed remainder is not a primitive.

Prime structure is irreducibility under the admitted positive product grammar.
Unique factorisation is not assumed from its conventional name: the certificate
retains the complete factor tree, canonical factor order and reconstruction of
the original whole. Canonical fractions reduce through common refinement and
pairing rather than an unrecorded division rule. Finite continued fractions
retain each reciprocal and remainder stage. Congruence is equality of held
remainders at a declared modulus, and compatible congruences compose only when
the registered compatibility witness exists. Prime-power valuation records
divisibility depth as a positive succession.

The later arithmetic claims keep Diophantine solution support, recurrence
sequences, finite generating-function correspondences, partitions,
compositions, divisor ledgers and prime-distribution enclosures. A generating
function is a post-derivation representation of a finite recurrence, not an
infinite series premise. A prime-distribution claim is a finite support and
enclosure result at its declared boundary; it does not inherit an asymptotic
theorem merely because conventional notation can display one.

Ten `ALEXT` claims then distinguish exact algebraic construction from imported
irrational values. Polynomial identity is checked by exact coefficient and
evaluation traces. A root is isolated by positive rational brackets and
orientation changes; the proof object is the bracket and refinement
certificate, not an ungenerated irrational scalar. Finite extension towers
retain each adjoining relation, dependency and reconstruction map. Finite
field, Galois-orbit and cyclotomic structures enter as explicit finite
correspondences after their carriers and operations have been forced.

The held-pair complex correspondence represents the two components and their
orientation as exact records. It permits finite algebraic manipulation and
enclosure without making an imaginary scalar a foundational magnitude.
Real-algebraic ordering compares certified brackets; prime-adic correspondence
retains valuation depth and declared completion boundary. The final
transcendental claim is a non-representability boundary: a failure to construct
an object in the admitted algebraic grammar is not reworded as a proof about
every broader conventional number system.

Sixteen algebraic-system claims build the hierarchy from a complete carrier
and total operation relation. A magma has closure. A semigroup adds an
associativity witness over complete triples. A monoid adds an identity relation.
A group adds held reversal with exact composition back to identity. Permutation
actions, normal substructures and quotients retain source and equivalence-class
custody. Ring, integral-domain and field correspondences add their registered
distributive, non-collapse and exact-division conditions without importing a
pre-existing field axiom.

Modules and algebras then combine scalar action with compatible products;
ideals and quotient structures retain the classes closed by the projection.
Representation and action decomposition preserve component and reconstruction
maps. Exact-sequence and homological correspondences record image/kernel
incidence using structural empty support where a conventional display uses
zero. Universal identities range over the complete declared finite term
grammar. Operadic composition closes the algebraic interfaces while retaining
slot, order and source identity. Each familiar structure is therefore a
successive organisation of admitted relations, not a word that imports its
textbook axioms.

### 9.7 Combinatorics, graphs and linear structure

The twelve combinatorial extensions begin with product, sum and bijection
counting, then generate permutations and combinations from complete held
selections. Inclusion–exclusion retains every overlap class and corrects each
repeated cell exactly once rather than using a memorised alternating formula as
a premise. Pigeonhole forcing follows because a map from a larger generated
support into fewer held target classes must produce a non-singleton fibre.
Recurrence and generating correspondences retain the construction order.

Partition incidence, extremal finite set systems, block designs, coding and
packing then use complete support and exact relation ledgers. The
probabilistic-method claim is explicitly a correspondence: deterministic
support is completely generated, and an exact positive part can witness that a
desired class is non-empty. No ontic random cause selects an object. Finite
Ramsey forcing retains the exact registered support and boundary rather than
claiming a completed infinite colouring universe. Species-like composition
records how labelled structures assemble and reconstruct.

Fourteen graph claims turn complete pair relations into a broad network
language. Graph identity retains nodes, adjacency and canonical isomorphism
witnesses. Paths and walks retain ordered edge traces; reachability is the
existence of such a trace; cycles retain return. Connectivity, cuts and flow
support keep source, sink and every crossing relation. Trees and forests are
acyclic connected or componentwise structures with explicit spanning custody.
Planarity is an embedding correspondence at the registered finite carrier,
not an assumed continuous plane.

Colouring, matching, covering and packing are complete finite constraint
problems in which all ties and alternative optima remain available. Directed
graphs add held orientation and causal reachability. Weighted networks attach
exact positive parts whose units and physical meaning remain downstream.
Hypergraphs generalise pair incidence to higher generated cells. Matroids
retain hereditary and exchange witnesses for independence. Reliability
records every registered failure class rather than replacing it with an
unidentified probability. Spectral graph results use exact rational
enclosures, and temporal networks retain versioned edge and causal records.

The fourteen `LINEAR` claims make coordinate structure similarly explicit. A
vector is a generated coordinate carrier with each position and value trace.
Linear maps preserve the declared junction and scalar-action relations. Matrix
notation is a representation of that map, and row operations retain an exact
inverse or equivalence certificate. Rank counts retained independent image
distinctions; nullity is the closed predecessor class at the map boundary,
expressed structurally rather than as a primitive zero dimension.

Determinants retain orientation and complete permutation terms. Linear systems
preserve exact solution, inconsistency or non-unique predecessor classes.
Basis, dimension and change of basis require complete spanning and independent
reconstruction. Inner product and metric are correspondences built from exact
positive forms. Eigen and invariant support retain exact equations or rational
enclosures rather than floating approximations. Multilinear maps, tensor
products and contractions retain every component identity and contraction
pair. Exterior and symmetric composition record antisymmetric held orientation
or permutation equivalence without importing signed scalars as primitive.
Finite-dimensional operator decomposition closes only where complete
reconstruction is certified.

### 9.8 Order, geometry and topology

The order family begins with a preorder and then quotients observationally
indistinguishable elements before asserting antisymmetry. A partial order
therefore retains witnessed comparability rather than presuming every pair can
be ranked. Totality is conditional on a complete pairwise comparison
certificate. Meet and join exist only where unique greatest-lower or
least-upper witnesses survive. Distributive, modular and Boolean-like
correspondences remain relative to the generated lattice and its complement
law.

Closure operators retain extensivity, monotonicity and idempotent return.
Galois connections preserve the two order maps and their exact adjunction
relation. Domain approximation is a generated finite correspondence; monotone
maps preserve order; finite fixed points are found through complete iteration
and recurrence. A conventional complete lattice enters only at an explicit
boundary because the current proof language does not install an ungenerated
completed carrier.

Sixteen geometry claims begin from point identity, incidence and exact
coordinates. Finite Euclidean distance is a correspondence derived from exact
coordinate relations and certified enclosures. Affine and projective
structures retain combinations, incidence and perspective maps. Convex hulls
are complete generated combinations, and separation requires an exact witness.
Discrete polytopes retain lattice incidence; Euler-type results retain the
complete face ledger rather than rely on a memorised characteristic.

Computational predicates make orientation and intersection executable on exact
inputs. Algebraic-geometry solution sets retain the registered equations and
complete finite solution or enclosure boundary. Differential geometry is a
finite-chart correspondence with explicit transition records. Curvature is
retained through exact finite transport; metric geodesics are shortest
generated path classes. Fractal and self-similar geometry retains its
construction recurrence and exact scaling relation. Packing, covering,
tessellation and transformation groups close through complete finite supports
and action ledgers.

Fourteen topology claims then separate neighbourhood structure from metric
assumption. An open-family correspondence is generated over complete support;
continuity is inverse-image preservation of that family. Compactness is a
finite-subcover correspondence at the declared carrier. Connected components
are reachability classes, and separation properties are observation-relative
distinction conditions. Product, quotient and subspace constructions retain
their source maps and closed distinctions.

Simplicial complexes turn cells and boundary incidence into composable finite
objects. Homotopy is a registered path-deformation correspondence.
Fundamental-cycle, homology and cohomology claims retain cycles, boundaries,
dual observations and exact composition; structural empty support replaces an
untyped zero group at the SFT boundary. Manifold claims require a finite atlas
and compatible chart records. Knot and link results apply to finite diagrams
with registered invariants. Persistent topology retains the filtration,
feature birth, feature closure and source custody rather than reporting an
untraceable barcode alone.

### 9.9 Calculus, analysis and equations without a hidden continuum

The calculus family starts with exact finite difference. Higher differences
record polynomial degree on generated support, while accumulation is a complete
finite sum. Their fundamental correspondence is an exact cancellation and
boundary ledger. Product and composition difference laws retain every term and
interface. Rational enclosure sequences then permit a derivative
correspondence through shrinking exact parts and an integral correspondence
through refinement sums.

These are correspondences because no completed continuum is admitted as a
premise. Multivariable and directional change use generated coordinate
directions. Divergence and flux are exact cell-boundary balances. Variational
stationarity is absence of an admissible oriented improvement at the declared
finite perturbation support, not a primitive derivative equal to numerical
zero. The final calculus claim records precisely when a continuum-limit
description is admissible and what finite certificate supports it.

Sixteen analysis claims extend the same rule. A sequence converges when a
registered rational enclosure and successor relation force every later term
inside the required exact band. Cauchy and completeness language remains a
correspondence over generated support, never an assertion that the construction
has produced a completed continuum. Series and power-series claims preserve
finite truncations and exact remainder bounds. Functional spaces are finite
representation classes with explicit evaluation and comparison interfaces.

Norm, seminorm and metric relations are exact positive or held-orientation
correspondences. Bounded and compact operator claims retain their finite
witness boundary. Harmonic, Fourier, transform, convolution and correlation
claims operate on complete generated finite support and preserve inversion or
reconstruction where certified. Orthogonal expansions retain basis and
coefficient custody. Distributional and weak observations are explicit
test-record correspondences. Nonlinear contraction states its exact shrinking
condition. Complex analysis uses held-pair representation and rational
enclosures. Spectral measure remains an operator and observation
correspondence, not an imported continuum object.

The equation families keep model syntax distinct from solution evidence.
Ordinary and partial difference equations are exact transition relations.
Differential counterparts are registered correspondences supported by finite
enclosures. Boundary and initial conditions are source records; well-posedness
separates existence, uniqueness and stable dependence. Integral, functional
and recurrence equations retain their complete relation and solution-space
support. Green-response forms are finite response correspondences. Weak
conservation keeps boundary and residual ledgers. Stability retains the
declared perturbation support. Existence, uniqueness and blow-up claims report
which of those statuses the exact boundary establishes rather than inferring
all three from one successful computation.

### 9.10 Measure, probability, optimisation and dynamics

Ten measure claims begin with exact positive weight on finite support and
disjoint additivity. Outer covering, measurable boundary and integration enter
as generated correspondences. Refinement-sum integration keeps each partition
and enclosure. Product and conditional support retain the exact whole against
which a part is formed. A signed-measure display is replaced at proof level by
positive magnitude plus held orientation. Generalised observations and
convergence of measures remain finite-witness boundaries.

The sixteen probability extensions then reconstruct conditional and Bayesian
relations from exact support restriction. Independence requires complete
product factorisation; expectation is exact support accumulation; variance
retains positive separation or paired differences without a negative primitive.
Finite distributions are complete part ledgers. Large-count and central-limit
claims are enclosure correspondences under their registered deterministic
enumerations, not ontic stochastic laws.

Estimation retains the estimator map and sufficient record. Confidence and
credible regions preserve their distinct construction and source assumptions.
Hypothesis testing keeps false-positive, false-negative and unavailable rows.
Likelihood and evidence ratios are exact positive relations at a registered
support. Bayesian update is conditional restriction, and a finite stochastic
process is a description over unresolved deterministic transition support.
Conditional conservation and statistical identifiability are stated through
complete observation classes; observationally equivalent sources remain
unresolved rather than being separated by a prior that the evidence did not
supply.

Sixteen optimisation claims distinguish feasibility, objective and preference.
Exact extrema are retained only after complete candidate evaluation. Convex,
linear, integer, combinatorial, network, dynamic, variational and
multi-objective forms each expose their carrier and admissible moves. Duality
and certificates retain the primal and comparison witnesses. Pareto fronts and
ties remain complete. Approximation records the exact objective distinction
from the optimum and the resource used to obtain it. Robust and uncertainty
claims preserve the complete registered scenario support. No optimiser may
erase an alternative merely because a software routine returned one member of
a tied class.

The twelve dynamical extensions retain state, transition, orbit, recurrence,
basin, invariant, stability, bifurcation, symbolic coding and multiscale
records. Reversibility is singleton predecessor custody; irreversible dynamics
retain the merged predecessor class. Apparent chaos is represented by exact
separation and observation sensitivity over generated finite successors. A
physical dynamical interpretation requires a later owner to supply units,
source law and measurements.

Logic and category then bind the whole language. Sixteen logic claims cover
syntax, semantics, consequence, proof, consistency, completeness boundaries,
decidability, model classes, induction, fixed points and constructive
witnesses relative to explicit grammars. Twelve category claims preserve
objects, arrows, identity, composition, functors, natural relations, products,
coproducts, limits, adjunction-like interfaces and type composition. These
families organise already admitted mathematics; they do not reach backwards
to become its axioms.

### 9.11 Numerical, symbolic and cross-branch interfaces

The numerical family provides exact rounding custody, interval propagation,
conditioning, stability, convergence, root isolation, linear and nonlinear
solvers, quadrature, recurrence evaluation and finite error certificates. A
floating result may be displayed at an interface only when its exact source or
enclosure remains available. The symbolic family retains canonical syntax,
rewrite provenance, identity checks, transforms and constructive
certificates. Neither a numerical library nor a computer-algebra output is an
answer oracle.

Eight cross-disciplinary interfaces then specify what Information Science,
Computation, Physics, Chemistry, Biology, Social Science and Engineering may
consume. The one-owner identity permits the same mathematical object to appear
in several branches without duplicating ownership of the law. A downstream
branch owns the new scientific interpretation, source and falsification
condition; Mathematics retains the underlying formal structure.

The validation family audits the complete predecessor vector, while the six
handoffs preserve measurement custody, conventional correspondence and lawful
extension. This field-wide layer adds breadth without weakening the original
constraints: each extension has 256 registered candidates, one survivor, four
controls, a root route, an independent reconstruction and a post-registry
observation record. Their shared shape permits compact exposition here, but
the individual machine records remain separate and complete.

### 9.12 The scientific calculator and preserved adverse evidence

The calculator lineage is a scientific result because it exposes how exact
proof objects become usable without becoming a shadow admission engine. It
contains five claims, `SFT-MATH-SCIENTIFIC-CALCULATOR-003` through `-007`, with
11,520 candidates in total.

Claim 003 passed its declared grammar, but a later operational audit found
reversed odd/even endpoints in one alternating arctangent enclosure. Its
artefacts and receipt remain preserved as **superseded adverse evidence**.
Claim 004 re-derived the core with corrected endpoint parity and an exact
tangent-composition check. Claim 005 extended the corrected core to the
declared calculator language and stateful application. Claim 006 completed the
calculator-first interface, law explorer and resource controls. Direct manual
testing then exposed a blank deprecated macOS desktop widget and phone
usability failures. Claim 007 kept the admitted evaluator immutable and forced
an accessible standards-rendered local web adapter.

The current calculator returns exact fractions or certified enclosures,
records its expression and complete operation trace, treats displayed `0` as
structural absence and halts on a prohibited negative result. A calculator
receipt establishes that an admitted operation evaluated an expression. It
does not admit the expression as a new theorem. A proposed new law still needs
its own registration, candidate grammar, controls, reconstruction and engine
receipt.

### 9.13 Reconciliation, evidence and open boundary

The V1/V2 Mathematics audit reviewed 356 V1 rows and 407 V2 numbered results,
identified seventy-one Mathematics-owned atomic obligations and closed all
seventy-one. Four corrections or invalidations remain prominent:

1. signed and zero host storage is implementation history, while the admitted
   proof boundary uses structural empty One and held orientation;
2. the prime skeleton and half-One reflection axis do not prove unrestricted
   complex-zero location;
3. the Collatz three-quarter shortcut fails even though the bounded
   100,000-start census succeeds; and
4. the depth-five fluid value 32 is derived from the reciprocal floor rather
   than installed as a damping parameter.

The branch is complete only to its frozen generated-finite current-evidence
census. It does not assert every named theorem over a completed continuum or
every object of conventional real and complex analysis. No natural dataset is
used to admit the formal mathematics. A downstream branch must separately own
the meaning and measurement of a mathematical structure.

Mathematics now hands Information Science canonical forms, exact relations,
finite probability support, maps, graphs, order, proof, dynamics and typed
composition. The unresolved problem is not whether those objects can be
represented, but how a scientific system records which distinctions are
retained, lost, communicated or unavailable.

---

## 10. Information Science: distinction under custody

### 10.1 Why information is not merely mathematics

A mathematical relation may map one carrier to another, but it does not by
itself say which object is the source, which is the record, what an observer
can distinguish, what a channel merges or what evidence is required to reverse
that merge. Information Science owns those questions. Its central object is
not an imported scalar. It is complete generated support together with exact
records of distinctions retained, closed, transformed and required for
reconstruction.

The branch contains 262 admitted claims across twenty families, 75,776
generated candidates, 262 unique survivors, 1,048 passed controls and 262
implementation-distinct reconstructions. The twelve foundational information
claims are independently replicated. The 250 field extensions add
post-registry observation packages and are classified as empirically tested
and independently replicated at their declared computational boundaries.

### 10.2 Information constitution and complete census

An information carrier is a complete generated support. A symbol is a held
distinction with its observation class. A record binds source identity,
transformation, context and retained output. An absent support cell is empty
One, not a zero-probability scalar. Entropy is the full ledger of observation
classes and unresolved within-class distinctions. Probability is an exact
part of deterministic support. Noise is a registered transformation, not an
uncaused event. Loss is a non-singleton predecessor fibre whose closed source
distinctions remain recorded.
