| `CORR` | 16 | 4,096 | Classical, probabilistic and quantum correspondences |
| `SEM` | 12 | 3,072 | Symbol, reference and context |
| `VALID` | 12 | 3,072 | Dated validation and Grand Lock |
| `HAND` | 6 | 1,536 | One-owner downstream handoffs |
| **Total** | **262** | **75,776** | **Current full-field census** |

The Grand Lock binds all 244 pre-validation receipts and 976 controls before
the twelve validation claims; six handoffs then complete the ledger. As in
Mathematics, this is a reconciliation of every frozen row, not a conversion of
every evidence type into one undifferentiated success label.

### 10.3 Twelve foundational information laws

| Claim | Principal derivation |
|---|---|
| `SFT-INFO-SYMBOL-DISTINCTION-001` | A symbol is a canonical held form plus its observation class; distinguishability is a retained label relation. |
| `SFT-INFO-ENCODING-DECODING-001` | Encoding is a total source-to-code map; exact decoding holds precisely on used codewords with a singleton predecessor or left inverse; many-to-one mapping is declared loss. |
| `SFT-INFO-QUANTITY-001` | Information quantity is complete support plus retained and closed distinction ledgers; the first Fold supplies one unit and positions supply support. |
| `SFT-INFO-ENTROPY-UNCERTAINTY-001` | Entropy retains every observation class, exact support part and unresolved deterministic pair; refinement cannot increase closed distinctions. |
| `SFT-INFO-COMPRESSION-REDUNDANCY-001` | A lossless description retains dictionary, token trace, unique parse and decoder; shortening beyond support injection is impossible. |
| `SFT-INFO-CHANNEL-CAPACITY-001` | A channel is a complete total relation; confusability is output-support overlap; zero-error codes have disjoint output supports; all greatest-cardinality ties are retained. |
| `SFT-INFO-NOISE-ERROR-001` | Noise is a registered transformation; error is an exact changed-position trace; correction is permitted only for a singleton registered predecessor. |
| `SFT-INFO-CODING-001` | A code retains word support and complete error images; correction is equivalent to pairwise-disjoint error-image supports, with redundancy witnessed by separation. |
| `SFT-INFO-MUTUAL-CONDITIONAL-001` | Conditioning is exact support restriction; dependence is joint incidence against exact marginal product; absent cells remain empty One. |
| `SFT-INFO-CONSERVATION-LOSS-001` | A transformation either retains a distinction or closes it inside a predecessor fibre; reversibility holds exactly for singleton fibres with complete custody. |
| `SFT-INFO-CLASSICAL-PROBABILISTIC-001` | Classical information is one held deterministic state; probabilistic information is the unresolved observation-class ledger over the same support. |
| `SFT-INFO-QUANTUM-CORRESPONDENCE-001` | Fold words supply complete quantum basis support at a correspondence boundary; phase, amplitudes, gates, entanglement and dynamics remain quantum-owned. |

The sequence matters. A distinction must exist before it can be encoded. An
encoding must preserve a source relation before quantity can count retained
and closed alternatives. Entropy then records which alternatives observation
fails to distinguish. Compression cannot be assessed before reconstruction is
defined. A channel cannot have capacity before its source-to-output relation
and confusability classes are complete. Noise and coding follow because error
must name the transformation and every predecessor before correction can be
unique.

The resulting coding law is deliberately stronger in custody and narrower in
assumption than a nearest-codeword rule. A metric may tie and an error rate may
be unknown. The exact decoder therefore enumerates complete registered error
images. If one received form belongs to one source image family, correction is
forced. If it belongs to several, the complete predecessor class is returned
and the machine refuses to guess. Width-three repetition is an executed
single-label example; it does not install a stochastic error model.

### 10.4 Relation, inference, privacy and thermodynamic meaning

The field extension applies the same custody to joint records, coarse-graining,
retrieval, estimators, privacy and thermodynamic correspondences. A conditional
record restricts both the held event and the reference whole. Coarse-graining
groups source forms while retaining the closed within-class distinctions.
Retrieval and ranking keep the query, source corpus, matching relation, ties
and exclusions. Inference retains the hypothesis support, observation relation,
compatible alternatives and any reconstruction assumption; a terminal score
cannot hide its source surface.

Privacy is exact access to distinctions. A released observation leaks the
source distinctions it separates and preserves privacy only for distinctions
that remain in a non-singleton observation class at the declared adversary
boundary. This structural statement does not make a practical security claim
without the Classical Computation branch's resource-bounded adversary and a
domain-specific implementation test.

The thermodynamic correspondence is equally disciplined. An irreversible
merge closes predecessor distinctions. Reversal requires a retained
predecessor record. That relation supplies an exact information-accounting
boundary for Landauer-, Bennett- and Maxwell-demon comparisons after
derivation. It does not assign energy, temperature or a hardware coefficient;
those remain physical measurements.

The semantic families retain symbol, reference and context as separate
records. A symbol can be formally bound to a source and use context without
claiming that information quantity alone is biological function, cognition,
consciousness or lived qualia. Those meanings remain explicit downstream
ownership boundaries.

### 10.5 Symbols, records and source identity

The `SYMREP` family begins by generating a complete alphabet from held
distinctions. A symbol has a canonical identity and observation class; two
symbols are distinguishable only where a retained observation separates them.
Codewords are finite generated sequences with position custody. Parsing is a
relation from a complete word to its candidate decompositions. A prefix or
instantaneous representation is not declared merely because one example
parses: the complete used-word support must rule out every conflicting prefix
or delayed alternative.

Grammar-constrained representations retain production identities and complete
parse trees. Two representations are equivalent only through an explicit
bidirectional reconstruction or isomorphism. Canonicalisation chooses the
unique normal form supplied by the registered equivalence relation rather than
by an undocumented formatting convention. Variable-length codes retain
delimiter, prefix or complete parsing custody; product alphabets retain both
component identities; typed and hierarchical symbols retain their level and
parent relations. Conversion and transduction must preserve the declared
information or record its loss. If several source words produce the same
observed form, the ambiguity family is retained rather than choosing one.

The finite-alphabet successor law appends each generated symbol under the same
complete rule used at the prior depth. The representation completeness claim
then reconciles all fourteen obligations and refuses an omitted symbol,
codeword or parse class. This is what permits a downstream language to cite a
complete representation rather than an informal alphabet.

The `RECORD` family distinguishes an information object from a bare value. A
data item has a source-bound identity. A tuple retains field identities and a
relation retains its complete row support. Metadata is an interpretive record
whose own source and version matter; it cannot be treated as neutral decoration.
A schema names allowed fields and types, while provenance composes the chain of
sources and transformations by which a record arose.

Integrity is evidence that the current artefact agrees with its bound identity,
not proof that the recorded scientific proposition is true. Version and
revision are distinct: a new artefact receives a new identity, while the
earlier one remains recoverable. Missing, absent and unknown are three
different states. Absence is an observed empty class at the declared boundary;
missing means the required record was not supplied or transported; unknown
means the evidence does not yet distinguish the alternatives. Duplicates and
aliases require an identity relation rather than naive string equality. Record
linkage preserves both matches and unresolved candidate links. Dataset
completeness requires an omission ledger, and reproducibility requires custody
of sources, transformations, environment and result.

Fourteen `SOURCE` claims then type the structures from which records arise.
Source support may be a sequence, transition process, spatial adjacency or
network path. Refinement separates distinctions; coarsening closes them.
Stationary and non-stationary descriptions retain the exact support and time
boundary under which their correspondence is asserted. Memoryless structure
requires conditional equivalence after the current source state; finite memory
retains the exact predecessor window. Joint sources preserve pair-cell support,
and dependence is witnessed by departure from complete product structure.
Every successor extension keeps the earlier source rows, and the completeness
claim exposes the boundary beyond which no source behaviour is inferred.

### 10.6 Measurement information and signal custody

The information-measure family deliberately separates several quantities that
are often collapsed into one scalar. Distinction count enumerates separable
source alternatives. Combinatorial quantity counts complete selections or
arrangements. Operational discrimination cost counts the registered actions
needed to resolve a class. Description length counts symbols only inside a
fixed grammar. Algorithmic-description language is a correspondence boundary
because an unrestricted universal-description minimum cannot be asserted from
one finite enumerator.

Partition refinement supplies an information order: a finer observation
retains every distinction of a coarser one and possibly more. Independent
product support permits exact additive composition, whereas shared support
forces subadditive custody so a distinction is not counted twice. Coarsening
cannot increase retained distinctions. Balance ledgers state which
distinctions entered, left, remained or were closed. Relative information and
divergence compare exact observation partitions and preserve cells where one
record cannot distinguish what another can.

Information geometry is built from those exact relations rather than an
imported continuous metric. Multiscale decomposition retains the refinement
tree. Unit conversion keeps the base support, scale relation and conventional
display unit separate. The completeness certificate reconciles all measure
types without declaring them interchangeable.

A signal is an ordered observation record. Its amplitude label and its support
are different objects: a position may be present even when the retained label
corresponds conventionally to a baseline. Sampling is a held selection of
source positions. Sufficiency requires that every admissible source in the
registered grammar reconstruct uniquely from the retained samples. Aliasing is
therefore an exact non-singleton predecessor class, not merely a visual
artefact. Quantisation is an observation partition, and quantisation error is
the retained source-to-class distinction with its held orientation.

Reconstruction is authorised only by the complete retained sample and source
grammar. Interpolation remains a correspondence outside points whose values
were observed directly. Transform representations retain an exact inverse on
the declared finite support. Time–frequency and multidimensional descriptions
keep both coordinate systems and the transform identity. Signal provenance
links acquisition, sampling, transformation and display. The final signal
certificate states which source classes the sampling law closes and which it
does not.

These laws prepare measurement custody without pretending to own a physical
instrument. The information branch can say what a complete record of samples,
positions, transformations and uncertainty must contain. Physics or another
domain branch must supply the apparatus, calibration, units, environmental
conditions and natural source.

### 10.7 Compression, channels, noise and codes

The fourteen compression extensions distinguish a short string from a lawful
compressed record. Lossless reconstruction retains an injective or uniquely
decodable source-to-description relation. Prefix trees, dictionaries and run
records carry their own overhead and parsing rules. Transform compression is a
correspondence through an invertible representation before any coefficient is
discarded. A source model can improve description length only at the declared
source boundary; its success cannot establish that the model is universally
correct.

Redundancy is retained excess distinction structure relative to a complete
reconstructing grammar. Minimum description is therefore grammar-relative. A
lossy compressor declares the coarsening that merges sources and preserves the
complete predecessor class of each output. Distortion is an exact registered
relation, not necessarily a conventional norm. Rate–distortion language is a
post-derivation correspondence between description support and closed
distinctions. Successive refinement retains each layer and reconstruction
stage. Side information is an additional source record whose custody must be
declared; it cannot silently reduce the reported description.

The eighteen channel claims treat a channel as a complete input–output
relation. Deterministic transport has singleton output for each input, whereas
an unresolved or externally varying channel retains all registered outputs.
Observation-equivalent channels are equivalent only for the named observer and
support. Zero-error single-user capacity is the greatest cardinality of a held
input family with pairwise-disjoint output supports; every tied greatest family
is retained. Resource-bounded capacity adds a declared use, cost or process
limit.

Cascades compose relations and expose the distinctions lost at each stage.
Parallel channels retain product support and shared-source dependencies.
Feedback is a named reverse record, not an unexplained improvement. Multiple
access, broadcast, relay, interference and bidirectional structures retain all
senders, receivers, paths and local observations. Network capacity is bounded
by exact cut support. Finite-use succession composes the complete channel at
each generated use without asserting an infinite-use limit. Channel simulation
must reconstruct the declared input–output relation and resource ledger.

Noise is then typed by what it changes. A transformation that closes an
unrecorded source distinction, a source–output mismatch, an erasure, a
substitution, a burst, a correlated pattern and an adversarial mask have
different records. Detection asks whether the received form lies outside valid
support. Localisation asks which positions are forced to differ. Estimation may
return a class of possible error actions. A noise budget is an exact positive
resource boundary, not a fitted probability. Composition transports every
registered mask through the channel and keeps the resulting predecessor
classes.

The eighteen coding claims then apply those complete error families.
Injective representation is necessary but not sufficient for correction.
Minimum separation is the least exact changed-position count among distinct
valid words. A decoder corrects a registered received form exactly when one
valid predecessor remains inside the allowed error support. Parity and syndrome
records are observation partitions over error classes. Repetition and majority
are generated structures; erasure, substitution and burst correction each
retain their own complete mask families.

Convolutional, tree, product, concatenated, sparse-check and network codes are
correspondences with explicit encoder, state and decoder records. List decoding
returns every surviving predecessor instead of falsely reporting uniqueness.
Adversarial correction exhausts every mask inside the declared budget and
retains the first exceeding-budget control. Code composition records both
resource and reconstruction effects. No sampled favourable mask, likelihood
decoder or hidden error rate is permitted to stand in for complete registered
support.

### 10.8 Relational information, coarse-graining, retrieval and inference

Relational information begins with joint support and exact conditional
restriction. Mutual information is the shared distinction ledger between two
observations, while a chain rule partitions retained information without
double-counting the shared source. Data processing is monotonic because a
later deterministic observation cannot reopen a distinction already closed by
an earlier non-singleton fibre unless an additional source record enters.
Conditional independence is product structure inside every retained condition
class.

Common, directed and interaction information retain their declared support,
order and multi-source incidence. Multi-information and total dependence
compare complete joint support with its marginal product. Shared, unique and
synergistic decompositions remain boundary correspondences where the
registered lattice supports them; the branch does not pretend one scalar
decomposition is forced for every possible semantics. Causal information flow
requires an admitted causal path from the computation or domain owner, rather
than inferring causation from association alone. Representation change must
preserve the source distinction ledger.

Coarse-graining asks what summary is sufficient for a declared observation. A
sufficient record preserves every distinction needed for the target; a minimal
sufficient record removes every unnecessary distinction without losing one
that matters. A bottleneck is a lawful coarsening with an explicit retained and
closed ledger. Feature selection, state lumping and multi-stage aggregation
retain the source classes and composition order. Reversibility holds only when
the summary plus retained side record reconstructs each predecessor uniquely.

Retrieval makes location and relevance source-bound. An index maps a query
term or form to registered source locations. Forward and inverted indices are
correspondences only if they reconstruct the same memberships. A query is a
declared observation relation; ranking is an exact finite order that retains
ties and incomparability. Relevance is a registered relation, not an authority
label. Precision and recall are exact parts of complete returned and relevant
support. Taxonomies and citation graphs retain version, edge provenance and
scope. An index may become stale after a source update; the old result keeps
its chronology rather than silently inheriting the new corpus.

Inference is support restriction. Detection chooses an observation class;
estimation selects a representative only with a declared selection rule.
Filtering updates a record sequentially from earlier to later observations,
whereas smoothing uses a complete later record to reconstruct an earlier
state. The smoothing result does not rewrite the historical forward-only
knowledge available at the earlier time. Prediction registers transition
support before the future observation. Evidence accumulation retains every
source and update. Likelihood is a correspondence over exact support rather
than a free causal propensity.

Structural thresholds arise from order and loss conditions, not a tuned scalar.
False-positive and false-negative rows remain separate. Identifiability fails
where two source objects have the same complete observation class. Sequential
stopping requires an explicit rule and trace. Multi-sensor fusion keeps each
sensor's source, timing, overlap and failure rows. The terminal inference
certificate reconciles these distinctions without turning an estimator output
into a proof of the source model.

### 10.9 Privacy, thermodynamics, quantum correspondence and semantics

The privacy family defines leakage as source distinctions that an adversary's
observation retains. Perfect secrecy is the structural condition that every
secret remains in the same adversary observation class at the declared
support. Side information refines that class and must be included in the
adversary boundary. Anonymity is a non-singleton identity class; least
disclosure is the coarsest observation still sufficient for the authorised
task. Composed releases may jointly distinguish what neither distinguishes
alone, so privacy losses require a combined ledger.

Repeated queries can reconstruct a source even where each reply looks coarse.
The query-reconstruction claim retains that complete attack support. A
privacy–utility trade is an exact pair of retained task and adversary
distinctions; no universal scalar preference is imposed. Practical
cryptographic hardness is handed to Classical Computation because it requires
a process and resource-bounded adversary, not merely an information partition.

The thermodynamic family begins by recognising that a physical information
record must have an owning physical carrier, while refusing to derive energy
from information structure alone. An irreversible merge lacks a predecessor
label; reversible retention keeps it. Erasure, measurement, memory reset and a
Maxwell-demon cycle therefore require complete records of what distinctions
are closed, retained and exported. Landauer and Bennett enter as explicit
correspondence boundaries. The non-equivalence claim is essential: an
information count is not an energy quantity, and no dimensional coefficient
is admitted without a Physics law and measurement.

Sixteen correspondence claims then type the route to Quantum Computation.
Classical information is a held source word; deterministic-support probability
is an unresolved observation partition; a probabilistic mixture is a
description of hidden support. Quantum correspondence begins with complete
Fold-word support and one distinction unit. Product support, joint correlation,
measurement records, classical-to-quantum encoding and quantum-to-classical
observation can be stated at the information boundary. Phase, interference,
entangling dynamics, gates, channels and quantum coding remain owned by the
quantum branch. The no-cloning information boundary identifies the source and
copy distinctions that any later operational proof must preserve, but does not
pre-empt that proof.

Finally, the semantic family separates symbol count from content. Reference
binds a symbol to an interpreted source under a context. Context can change
which distinctions are operationally available. Genetic representation,
neural and cognitive information, conscious report, social communication and
pragmatic action are typed handoffs. Meaning-preserving translation requires a
declared bidirectional relation at the intended content boundary. It cannot be
established by matching symbol counts alone.

### 10.10 Validation, chronology and one-owner handoffs

The twelve validation laws do not introduce a new theory of information. They
replay and partition the 244 scientific receipts, their 976 controls,
independent reconstructions, observation rows and source identities. A
favourable execution stays favourable; an ambiguity stays an ambiguity; an
absent or unavailable source retains that classification. The six handoffs
then assign downstream derivation, measurement, formal-to-empirical chronology,
conventional correspondence and open extension.

`SFT-INFO-HAND-MEASUREMENT-BOUNDARY-002` keeps a formal derivation, externally
held observation and exact comparison as three different records.
`SFT-INFO-HAND-FORMAL-EMPIRICAL-003` requires derivation before sealed
prediction and target release before comparison while retaining every
favourable, adverse, absent, unresolved and boundary row.
`SFT-INFO-HAND-CROSS-BRANCH-COMPLETENESS-006` reconciles all six handoffs with
the 256 pre-handoff receipts and assigns one owner to every downstream
interface.

### 10.11 Evidence status, limitations and handoff

The branch preserves favourable, adverse, ambiguous and exceeding-boundary
images inside its complete transformation ledgers. Ambiguity is not reported
as failed transport or null evidence: it is the exact non-singleton predecessor
class. No current authoritative record supplies a basis for turning every
formal execution into a natural measurement, and the branch does not do so.
The current V1/V2 reconciliation retains forward-only records even where a
later smoothing law can reconstruct an earlier state; future evidence is not
inserted into the earlier chronology.

Information Science is complete to its frozen 29 July 2026 census, not to every
future information problem. It does not own a physical channel's measured
capacity, a living system's function, a person's experience or the operational
phase and gates of a quantum machine.

The next unresolved requirement is execution. Information laws specify states,
records, transformations, ambiguity and reconstruction, but a computational
science must derive languages, machines, resources, algorithms and limits.
Information Science therefore hands complete symbol support, encoding,
conservation, channel and evidence custody to Classical Computation.

---

## 11. Classical Computation: the Fold machine

### 11.1 Why computation follows information

A source-to-output relation does not yet specify an executable process. A
machine requires generated configurations, enabled transitions, a language,
composition, terminal and recurrent classes, resources and a complete trace.
Classical Computation owns those objects and their formal limits. It does not
own quantum operations, physical hardware, engineering reliability or an
application's empirical performance.

The current branch contains 369 claims across twelve families, 94,464
candidates, 369 unique survivors, 1,476 passed controls and 369 independent
reconstructions. The 117 foundational and prior-corpus claims are independently
replicated. The remaining 252 complete-field extensions carry post-registry
execution observations and are classified as empirically tested and
independently replicated within their declared computational boundaries.

### 11.2 Native machine constitution and family census

The native machine begins with exact generated configurations, held Fold
labels and a source-bound transition relation. Blank support is empty One. A
word is a complete sequence of held labels at canonical generated positions.
Reading is an observation relation; writing is held-label substitution;
movement is a counted transition. Each configuration retains its word, held
position, process state and proof trace. Every step records its source,
operation, successor, resources and retained or closed distinctions.

| Family | Claims | Candidates | Scientific scope |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| `BASE` | 117 | 29,952 | Foundational Fold machine and prior-corpus reconstruction |
| `FORMX` | 22 | 5,632 | Formal computation |
| `CBLX` | 21 | 5,376 | Computability and its limits |
| `CPLXX` | 33 | 8,448 | Computational complexity |
| `ALGX` | 31 | 7,936 | Algorithms and mathematical data structures |
| `SEMX` | 25 | 6,400 | Semantics and programming theory |
| `DISTX` | 26 | 6,656 | Concurrent and distributed computation |
| `SECX` | 25 | 6,400 | Cryptography and computational security |
| `LEARNX` | 26 | 6,656 | Learning and intelligence theory |
| `SCIX` | 25 | 6,400 | Scientific computation |
| `VALID` | 12 | 3,072 | Dated validation and Grand Lock |
| `HAND` | 6 | 1,536 | One-owner handoffs and open extension |
| **Total** | **369** | **94,464** | **Current full-field census** |

### 11.3 From state to universal machine

`SFT-COMP-FORM-STATE-TRANSITION-001` combines Foundation form enforcement,
mathematical discrete and graph structure, logic and composition, and
information encoding and conservation. Its survivor retains a complete state
carrier, held action labels, source-bound transition rows, initial and terminal
classes and complete execution traces. It does not yet presume a language or
machine model.

The next claims derive, in order, a generated word grammar, finite automata,
rewriting, structural recursion, binding and capture-safe substitution,
abstract machines, circuit syntax, operational processes, serial and parallel
composition, bidirectional model translations and a universal interpreter.
Universality means exact interpretation of every description generated by the
frozen grammar, with preserved terminal result and an explicit overhead
ledger. It is not a completed totality of every possible language object.
Turing, Church and circuit systems enter only after sealing as comparison
encodings.

### 11.4 Computability, halting and incompleteness

Recognition retains acceptance, rejection and unresolved continuation as
different trace classes. For any supplied complete finite generated carrier,
exhaustive state execution can classify a process as terminal or recurrent.
That bounded classification does not imply that every bounded process halts.

The unrestricted internal halting boundary uses exact self-description and a
held complement action. If a total internal decider predicts the behaviour of
every generated universal-machine description, a process can be constructed
that rejects when the decider predicts acceptance and accepts when it predicts
rejection on its own description. Neither held verdict is a fixed point. The
contradiction closes the total internal decision claim without treating
non-termination as numerical zero.

The same missing distinction appears in internal self-verification. An
effective proof enumerator cannot retain consistency and supply every demanded
self-referential verdict. Relative computation remains possible only through a
named query alphabet and a declared oracle relation whose identity and answers
are preserved. A hypercomputation proposal must expose an executable law,
finite evidence boundary, self-reference test, resources, oracle custody and
falsification condition. A finite demonstration cannot silently become an
unrestricted oracle.

### 11.5 Resources, algorithms and semantics

Input size is the complete generated extent of a canonical representation.
Time is a transition trace; space is live retained configuration support;
circuit size and depth are generated gate and causal-layer ledgers.
Randomised computation does not import an uncaused schedule. It retains the
complete registered schedule support and executes one deterministic trace per
held schedule. Exact success parts summarise the resulting output partition.
If a physical source supplies schedules, the source distribution is a separate
measurement.

Reversibility cost is information custody. A non-singleton transition fibre
merges predecessors; reverse execution then requires retained predecessor
labels. Lower and upper bounds must name the problem family, representation,
machine, resource and witnesses. Average, worst, approximate and parameterised
claims similarly retain complete instance support and the exact aggregation or
preference relation rather than a hidden distribution.

The algorithm families derive search, order, arithmetic, strings, trees,
graphs, dynamic programming, optimisation, parallel, distributed, online,
streaming, numerical, symbolic and approximate computation from those resource
objects. Program semantics then retains syntax, binding, evaluation, typed
interfaces, equivalence, termination, specification, correctness,
transformation, compilation and verification. Correctness is always relative
to the exact registered precondition, program, input support and specification;
a proof of the wrong specification is not practical correctness.

### 11.6 Distribution, security, learning and scientific computation

Distributed computation adds causal partial order, synchronisation,
communication, replication, fault models, local knowledge and network
interfaces. Agreement impossibility is derived from two predecessor executions
that produce identical local observation but require different decisions. The
impossibility remains relative to the registered asynchronous and fault
grammar; it cannot be exported to a different model without a new claim.

Security begins with complete message, key, observation, adversary and
resource support. One-wayness is resource-relative forward execution versus
inverse search; unbounded finite inversion is not declared impossible.
Information-theoretic security means the relevant source distinctions remain
closed over complete observation support. Computational security means the
registered resource-bounded adversary grammar fails to reopen them. Hashing,
commitment, signatures, proofs of knowledge, zero knowledge and multiparty
computation therefore retain both their exact adversary and their declared
boundary.

Learning claims retain hypothesis support, development rows, sealed predictors,
withheld target support and failure conditions. Generalisation is empirical
unless a structural successor proof exhausts the target family. Two hypotheses
that agree on every observed row but require different unobserved decisions
establish an exact identifiability limit. No benchmark score, pretrained model
or fitted opacity replaces the premise-to-result trace.

Scientific computation separates exact proof values from bounded
approximations, retains orientation-labelled error, propagates exact error
ledgers through dependency graphs, and requires explicit refinement and
convergence certificates. A simulation derives consequences of its registered
model relation. It cannot establish the corresponding natural law without a
separate sealed measurement claim owned by the relevant natural-science
branch.

### 11.7 Algorithms and data structures as trace-preserving processes

The thirty-one `ALGX` laws reconstruct the common algorithmic families without
letting a library routine or asymptotic slogan become the premise. Searching
begins with a complete input support, a target relation and an ordered sequence
of inspected positions. Ordering retains the comparison witness for every
placed pair and preserves ties. Arithmetic algorithms operate on the admitted
exact positive carrier. String and sequence algorithms preserve position,
symbol and source-word identity. Tree and graph algorithms consume the
mathematical incidence records and emit paths, traversals, components or cuts
with complete predecessor traces.

Algebraic and geometric algorithms retain their exact carrier and predicate
boundaries. Dynamic programming is derived as a dependency-ordered table of
subproblem identities whose entries reconstruct the terminal result; a cached
value without its recurrence and input relation is insufficient. Optimisation
keeps feasibility, objective order and every tied or undominated output.
Randomised algorithms are deterministic executions over complete held schedule
support, and their success part is computed only after every registered
schedule has a result. Parallel algorithms retain a task-dependency graph,
work, causal span and synchronisation records.

Distributed algorithms add message and local-state custody. Online and
streaming algorithms distinguish what was available at each decision time from
the later complete input; a retrospective optimum cannot be inserted into the
earlier trace. Numerical algorithms carry exact reference values or rational
enclosures, precision provenance and stopping certificates. Symbolic
algorithms retain parse, rewrite and identity traces. Approximation algorithms
retain the exact distinction between their output and the optimum at the
declared resource boundary. The family extensions then cover selection,
hash-table, heap, union–find, traversal, shortest-path, flow, matching,
constraint, scheduling, transform and composition structures through the same
input–operation–output custody rather than as opaque callable names.

The consequence is not that one algorithm is universally best. Resource
claims remain representation- and model-relative. An apparent speedup that
changes the input encoding, hides preprocessing or discards failed instances
does not satisfy the comparison grammar. A lawful comparison preserves both
implementations, the same registered instance support, each complete resource
trace and the exact result relation.

### 11.8 Programming meaning, transformation and proof

The twenty-five `SEMX` claims derive a programming language as more than a word
grammar. Syntax retains production and position identity. Binding and
substitution distinguish free from held-bound occurrences and prevent capture.
Evaluation is a source-bound transition trace. Operational semantics records
the transitions a process executes; denotational correspondence records the
mathematical relation assigned to the same complete input support. Agreement is
proved only at their declared interface.

Types are generated carriers and admissible-operation relations, not authority
labels. A type error is a failed interface relation. Program equivalence may be
trace equality, result equality or observational equivalence, but the chosen
boundary must be registered. Termination is a terminal-trace property;
recurrent and unresolved continuations remain distinct. Correctness combines a
precondition support, program execution, postcondition relation and an explicit
total- or partial-termination declaration.

Specification therefore owns the claim the program is meant to satisfy.
Transformation must map source to target programs while preserving the
registered semantics. Compilation retains parse, intermediate representations,
lowering steps, target process and a semantics-preservation certificate.
Optimisation cannot delete an observable distinction outside the declared
equivalence. Verification retains the proposition, proof system, program,
input support and every failed proof condition. A verifier can establish a
formal property of the registered model; it cannot certify an unstated
physical environment or safety requirement.

The later semantic extensions cover modular interfaces, effects, state,
exceptions, nondeterministic branch support, concurrency, refinement,
abstraction, executable specification, proof-carrying code and compiler
bootstrap custody. In each case, the question is which distinctions survive
translation and which records are required to reconstruct meaning. A terminal
binary identity is evidence only when its complete source and compilation
route remain bound.

### 11.9 Concurrency, causality and distributed knowledge

The twenty-six `DISTX` laws begin from interleaved and partially ordered
transitions. Concurrent processes retain component identity and every allowed
schedule. Computational causality is a dependency path from source state and
message to later state; temporal order alone is not enough. Synchronisation is
a registered relation that closes a set of local transitions into one shared
boundary event. Communication keeps sender, receiver, payload, send and receive
positions, and transport outcomes.

A distributed partial order records events that are causally comparable and
leaves independent events unordered. Consensus requires one decision relation,
agreement among admissible non-faulty participants, validity against the
registered input support and the declared termination boundary. The agreement
impossibility law identifies two source executions that are indistinguishable
to a local process yet require different decisions. Because the local trace is
the same, a deterministic process must decide the same way and fail one
requirement. The result belongs to that exact asynchronous and fault grammar;
changing the failure detector or synchrony model creates a new problem.

Replication records source version, copies, update order and observation.
Consistency is an explicit relation among those histories, not a generic
quality. Fault models enumerate allowed omissions, corruptions, timing changes
or adversarial actions. Distributed knowledge is the class of global states
consistent with a process's local observation. Locality names the interfaces a
process may access. Network computation then composes node processes, paths,
messages and failures without a hidden coordinator.

The extension families add mutual exclusion, clocks, snapshots, leader and
membership records, broadcast, commit, replicated state, eventual and stronger
consistency correspondences, fault tolerance, recovery, routing, load and
resource distribution. Every result retains the exact schedule and fault
support on which it was tested. A successful execution under one schedule is
not consensus over all registered schedules; a simulation without the declared
failure rows is not fault-tolerance evidence.

### 11.10 Security without an imported hardness oracle

The twenty-five `SECX` laws use Information Science's observation classes and
Computation's adversary resources. One-wayness compares a total forward map
with complete inverse search under a declared adversary process and resource
bound. It does not declare mathematical inversion impossible. Secrecy asks
which message distinctions the adversary observation retains. Integrity asks
whether an unauthorised transformation produces an accepted record.
Authentication binds identity, challenge, response and source custody.

Hashing retains the input grammar, output relation, collision classes and
resource boundary. A commitment must retain hiding and binding as separate
observation and transformation properties. A signature binds message,
signatory key relation, verification process and forgery grammar. Proof of
knowledge distinguishes possessing a reconstructible witness from merely
producing an accepted transcript. Zero knowledge preserves the verifier's
observation class and simulator correspondence at the declared protocol
boundary. Multiparty computation keeps each input owner, allowed disclosure,
joint process and output.

Adversarial computation is never an unnamed hostile possibility. The
adversary's input, observations, allowed operations, schedule and resources are
part of the candidate grammar. Information-theoretic security means complete
support closes the protected distinction for every allowed adversary action.
Computational security means the registered resource-bounded process family
does not reopen it. These statuses cannot be exchanged. A reduction preserves
the exact problem and resource interfaces; an authority claim that a primitive
is “believed hard” cannot replace the registered witness.

Post-quantum security is a handoff boundary. Classical Computation can state
the current adversary and the formal relation a quantum consumer must preserve,
but it cannot assume a quantum speedup or device. Quantum Computation must
derive the relevant process and resource law independently. Practical security
then remains an Engineering and application measurement problem involving
implementation, side channels, key custody, performance and threat evolution.

### 11.11 Learning, generalisation and intelligence boundaries

The twenty-six `LEARNX` claims treat learning as a registered computation over
hypothesis, evidence and target support. Inference restricts hypotheses by
their compatibility with observed rows. Classification and prediction retain
the label relation, development support, sealed model and target chronology.
Representation learning may transform the input only with a reconstruction or
declared-loss ledger. It cannot make the original source and exclusions
disappear.

Generalisation requires more than development accuracy. The hypothesis class,
development rows, withheld support, structural invariance claim, sealed
predictor, complete target rows and failure condition are all retained. If a
successor proof exhausts every generated target, the result may be formal at
that support. Otherwise generalisation is an empirical result whose status
depends on the seal and target custody. Sample complexity counts exact examples
under a fixed hypothesis and observation grammar; it is not a universal number
detached from representation.

Learning optimisation retains objective, alternatives, ties and resource
trace. Induction is a transparent rule from evidence to hypotheses and cannot
silently import a prior. Search and planning retain state, action, transition,
goal and explored support. Reinforcement computation keeps reward as a
registered observation or preference relation, not a primitive purpose.
Multi-agent learning includes each observer, communication and joint-action
support. Adaptation preserves the version history of data, model and
environment.

The limits law constructs observationally equivalent hypotheses with different
unobserved requirements. Any learner given the same evidence must make the
same decision for both and therefore cannot be guaranteed correct on both.
Interpretability and verification ask which internal relation or trace can be
reconstructed and checked. A plausible verbal explanation is not automatically
the process that produced the output. The classical-learning model collects
these objects without asserting consciousness, agency, biological cognition or
deployment fitness; those belong to downstream owners.

### 11.12 Scientific calculation, simulation and inverse problems

The twenty-five `SCIX` laws provide the formal machinery later branches use to
make computational predictions. Exact and approximate quantities remain
separate. Numerical stability records how input distinctions propagate through
the process. Error propagation follows the dependency graph, retaining local
error classes, held orientation, combination rules and the final enclosure.
Cancellation may close a distinction only when the exact relation and
provenance are retained.

Discretisation maps a registered model relation to generated cells, retains
coverage and boundary conditions, and provides a successor refinement.
Convergence is a certificate that refinement preserves the model interface and
forces the declared error class to descend. Symbolic computation preserves
model syntax and exact rewrite identity. Simulation begins from an explicit
state carrier, update law, initial support, schedule, observation map and
terminal comparison boundary.

Computational dynamics retains every orbit, invariant, recurrence and
perturbation class. An inverse problem begins with a forward relation and an
observation; reconstruction returns the complete predecessor fibre and labels
the source identifiable only when that fibre is singleton. Computational
statistics preserves sample support, statistic map, observation fibres and
uncertainty. High-dimensional computation retains coordinate product or
factorisation and records interactions lost by an approximation. Many-body
computation keeps body identities, local states, interaction graph, joint
support and update trace.

Mathematical modelling closes the family by binding a phenomenon disclosure,
formal grammar, parameter status, derivation or constitutional provenance,
sealed predictions, external measurements, falsification and revision records.
This law does not make every model true. It specifies the evidence objects
required to test one. A model with a complete simulation but no source-bound
measurement remains a formal model. A model fitted on the target cannot later
describe its development result as a blind prediction.

### 11.13 Validation and branch interfaces

The twelve validation claims partition the 351 pre-validation receipts by
family, evidence type, closure boundary and ownership. They preserve theorem,
finite-census and positive-finite distinctions, as well as adverse and
unresolved rows. The six handoff claims then enforce one owner for each
downstream result.

`SFT-COMP-HAND-MEASUREMENT-BOUNDARY-002` states that Classical Computation owns
form, trace and resource laws while measured consequences remain with Physics,
Chemistry, Biology, Medicine, Earth science, Astronomy or another empirical
owner. `SFT-COMP-HAND-FORMAL-EMPIRICAL-003` requires a value-free target
registry, sealed prediction, independently custodied observation and complete
comparison before a formal result gains empirical status. `SFT-COMP-HAND-CLASSICAL-QUANTUM-004`
passes state, trace and resource interfaces to Quantum Computation while
leaving quantum states, gates, channels and fault tolerance quantum-owned.

The branch is therefore internally complete without becoming a universal
owner. Its applications—including Chess Fold, Go Fold, Protein Fold and Unison
Fold AI—may implement and test admitted computation, but their scores and
domain observations cannot select these laws. A successful application is a
new evidence object; a failed application is retained at its own registered
boundary.

### 11.14 Three native exact results

The branch includes three strong native results whose scope is essential to
their meaning.

For `SFT-COMP-CBL-NATIVE-BUSY-BEAVER-002`, every supplied positive finite
description depth `k` in the admitted closing-process grammar halts within `k`
lawful depth-lowering transitions, and a depth-`k` source attains `k`.
Recurrent period-`b` processes carry exact non-halting certificates and are
excluded from the closing maximum. Therefore

\[
BB_F(k)=k
\]

for every supplied positive finite `k` in that grammar.

`SFT-COMP-CPLX-FOLD-P-NP-EQUALITY-002` establishes that exact deterministic
evaluation emits the complete lawful trace already accepted by the proof
system, while sound verification forces the same unique evaluation. Both
resources equal description depth, so

\[
P_F=NP_F
\]

inside the admitted native Fold grammar. The claim explicitly does not assert
conventional `P=NP`.

`SFT-COMP-CPLX-ARBITRARY-CIRCUIT-LOWER-BOUND-002` ranges over every circuit
assembled from admitted Fold edges. Closing a depth-`k` path requires at least
`k` gates; complete source support requires width `b^k`; and complete layered
coverage requires

\[
\sum_{r=One}^{k} b^r
\]

edges. The registered circuit attains all three bounds. These are tight bounds
for admitted Fold-edge circuits, not an unqualified lower bound over every
conventional circuit model.

### 11.15 Preserved corrections and current boundary

The V1/V2 ownership audit read all 763 prior entries, decomposed seventy
computation-relevant source rows into 134 atomic obligations and closed all
134. Four reconciliations remain explicit:

1. unboundedness alone is not a proof of undecidability; the admitted proof
   uses self-description and the held-complement diagonal contradiction;
2. a bounded deterministic process is guaranteed to halt **or recur**, not to
   halt;
3. earlier logarithmic or exponential efficiency prose is replaced by exact
   Fold description depth, support, transition, space and retained-information
   resources; and
4. native `P_F=NP_F` is not a verdict on conventional P versus NP.

The validation layer replays all 351 pre-validation receipts and preserves
their adverse, boundary and ownership rows. There is no open frozen obligation,
but there are permanent scope boundaries: physical device behaviour,
engineering quality and application results require their own owners and
evidence. The six handoffs therefore assign quantum operations, physical
measurement, software and hardware engineering and applications exactly once.

Classical Computation hands exact configurations, languages, processes,
resources, reversible-cost records and proof-preserving interfaces to
Reversible and Quantum Computation. It does not pre-derive quantum state,
phase, interference, entanglement, measurement, gates or fault tolerance.

---

## 12. Reversible and Quantum Computation: one extended Fold machine

### 12.1 Why the quantum branch follows computation

Classical reversibility establishes that a merged predecessor distinction must
be retained if inverse execution is required. It does not yet derive complete
branch support, phase-sensitive merging, non-factorable joint support or an
observation relation over such a carrier. The quantum branch owns those
operations. Physical preparation, energy, timing, fidelity, error rates,
apparatus and outcome distributions remain downstream measurements.

The current branch contains 288 claims across thirteen families, 73,728
candidates, 288 unique survivors, 1,152 passed controls and 288 independent
reconstructions. Its twenty-two foundational claims are independently
replicated. The 266 full-field extensions carry registered post-registry
execution observations and are classified as empirically tested and
independently replicated within their formal computational boundaries.

### 12.2 Fold-native quantum constitution and census

One Fold distinction supplies two held fibre labels and therefore the native
information unit. Complete held-label words across generated positions supply
branch support. A branch carries an exact held phase label from a generated
finite cycle. Joint states are complete pair-cell words retaining both
component identities. A transformation is a total branch-word bijection plus a
period-held phase action. These objects require no Hilbert-space axiom, complex
amplitude, irrational normalisation, imaginary proof value or stochastic
collapse premise.

| Family | Claims | Candidates | Scientific scope |

| `BASE` | 22 | 5,632 | Reversible and quantum computational foundation |
| `REVX` | 18 | 4,608 | Complete reversible computation |
| `QSTATEX` | 28 | 7,168 | Information units, states, support and composition |

| **Total** | **288** | **73,728** | **Current full-field census** |

### 12.3 The twenty-two foundational quantum claims

Every foundational claim has 256 candidates, one survivor and four passed
controls. Together they form a single ordered argument.

| Claim | Principal result and boundary |

| `SFT-QUANTUM-REVERSIBLE-MODEL-001` | Canonical configurations, bijective transitions, held labels, exact inverse traces, universal reversible interpretation and complete resources; no thermodynamic energy is assigned. |
| `SFT-QUANTUM-INFORMATION-UNIT-001` | One Fold distinction, two held labels, complete word support, observation classes and a phase-label carrier; the unit is structural rather than an imported qubit. |
| `SFT-QUANTUM-STATE-COMPOSITION-001` | Complete branch support, pair-cell joint words, component identities, phase composition, marginals and product/correlated classes. |
| `SFT-QUANTUM-SUPERPOSITION-001` | Complete `b^k` word support with one phase-bearing record per branch and no selected branch before observation. |
| `SFT-QUANTUM-PHASE-INTERFERENCE-001` | A reversible period-`b` phase action and total branch-to-image relation with complete predecessor merge and phase provenance. |
| `SFT-QUANTUM-ENTANGLEMENT-001` | Joint support is factorable exactly when it equals the product of its marginals; a strict correlated subset is non-factorable. |
| `SFT-QUANTUM-MEASUREMENT-001` | A total observation relation retains the selected class, closed branch distinctions, full pre-observation record and reconstruction boundary. |
