### 12.4 Superposition, interference, entanglement and measurement

Superposition-equivalent support means that every generated branch is retained
before observation. It is neither a hidden selected branch nor an imported
stochastic mixture. Each branch carries a held phase in a finite generated
cycle. Interference is then a branch-to-image relation whose predecessor fibre
keeps every merging branch and phase identity. No branch disappears from the
audit record merely because several branches share an image.

Two systems compose through complete pair-cell joint support. Their joint is
factorable when it is exactly the Cartesian product of its marginal supports.
A strict correlated joint subset is non-factorable and supplies the derived
entangling class. This definition is structural and operational; it is not a
metaphysical postulate added to the carrier.

Measurement is a total observation relation over complete support. It retains
one observation class as the post-observation state while the measurement
record preserves every pre-observation branch, phase and image identity. Thus
support reduction is exact at the selected-class boundary and reconstructible
at the complete-record boundary. Outcome frequencies are not inferred from the
formal relation alone. They require source-bound physical evidence.

Classical computation is recovered as the single-held-branch,
phase-insensitive reversible submodel. Quantum execution extends that carrier
with complete simultaneous branch support, phase-sensitive merging and
non-factorable joint support. The correspondence is exact where the interfaces
agree, but it does not identify the two modes where phase changes the trace.

### 12.5 Gates, algorithms and exact correction

A reversible gate must be bijective on branch words and invertible on held
phase. A circuit retains generated wires, gate identities, causal order,
complete branchwise evaluation, inverse construction, observation boundary and
resource ledger. Universality is confined to descriptions generated by the
frozen gate grammar. The algorithm and complexity families then derive query,
phase, transform, search, counting, simulation, verification, communication,
upper and lower witness laws without presuming a measured speedup.

The exact correction theorem is particularly clear. If at most `t` labels may
change in a repeated word, width `2t+1` leaves at least `t+1` source labels
against at most `t` changed labels. The source therefore retains a strict
majority. Every width through `2t` has a constructive tie or wrong-majority
counterexample. Consequently the first correcting width and exact redundancy
are

\[
w(t)=2t+1,
\qquad
r(t)=2t.
\]

The executable census exhausts every registered mask through one fault at
width 3, two faults at width 5 and three faults at width 7. Successor checks
run through `t=14`, and the fixed-depth circuit tests round-trip all 128
depth-seven source words at that registered boundary. The proof for arbitrary
supplied positive finite `t` is the majority and counterexample construction,
not an extrapolation from those test depths. Advancing from `t` to `t+1` adds
exactly two labels and defeats every proposed fixed positive finite ceiling.

This is an unbounded **finite-order** theorem. It does not construct a
completed infinite-width word, measure a stochastic device error rate or
derive a physical fault-tolerance threshold. Those quantities belong to
Physics and Engineering.

### 12.6 Reversible computation as complete predecessor custody

The eighteen `REVX` laws develop the reversible carrier before the specifically
quantum families. A reversible state transition is a bijection over complete
registered configurations. Its inverse is not inferred from a final value; it
is an exact relation whose trace reconstructs the preceding state and action.
If a forward process merges two predecessors, reversibility requires a held
record that distinguishes them. This is the operational content inherited from
Information Science's conservation and Classical Computation's reversibility
cost.

Reversible words retain every input label and ancilla-like support. A lawful
reversible extension may add a clean held carrier, compute into it and later
uncompute intermediate distinctions, but it must account for every retained or
closed record. Copying a definite classical held word is possible because the
source label remains available and the output relation preserves both source
and copy provenance. That fact does not pre-empt the quantum no-cloning
boundary, which concerns a different complete support and transformation
grammar.

Reversible gates compose only when output and input interfaces match. Serial
composition applies one bijection after another and reverses in the opposite
order. Parallel composition uses complete product support and retains component
identity. Controlled transformations preserve the control label while applying
the target action on the declared class. Permutations, swaps and reversible
Boolean correspondences enter after this carrier is derived, not as assumed
gate libraries.

Resource accounting retains forward actions, inverse actions, live support,
held history and any record later closed. Reversible simulation maps a
classical process into a bijective process by retaining enough predecessor
information to reconstruct every step. The overhead is part of the theorem.
A terminal output without the history or an uncomputation certificate is not
evidence of reversible execution. Universal reversible interpretation ranges
over every description in the frozen grammar and no further.

The reversible family also supplies the interface to physical thermodynamics
without assigning energy. Information Science has shown which distinctions a
merge closes; the reversible machine shows which record restores them. Whether
retaining, moving or resetting that record consumes a measured energy under a
particular apparatus is a Physics and Engineering question.

### 12.7 State support, phase and joint composition

The twenty-eight `QSTATEX` laws refine the six foundational state results into
a complete state science. One distinction gives two held labels. A depth-`k`
word has one label at each generated position, and complete support contains
all `b^k` generated words. Each support member retains branch identity,
position trace and held phase. Preparation is a registered source-to-support
relation; it does not assume that a physical laboratory has realised the
formal state.

Normalisation is support completeness, not an irrational scalar constraint.
A state is complete when every branch required by its generation grammar is
present exactly once with its source and phase record. A partial support is a
different state with a different registry. Marginalisation groups joint cells
by the retained component and preserves the closed partner distinctions.
Product composition contains every pair of marginal members. Correlated
composition is a declared strict subset with complete joint custody.

Phase is a held position in a finite generated cycle. Phase translation is a
reversible action on that cycle, and relative phase is an exact relation
between held labels. Global-phase-like equivalence may be recorded where a
uniform translation preserves every registered observation, but the source
phase record remains available in the audit. Interference occurs only through
a declared branch-to-image map with complete predecessor fibres. Constructive
and cancelling correspondences are therefore properties of the phase-labelled
merge relation, not arithmetic on hidden complex amplitudes.

Entanglement is tested by factorisation. Compute both marginals from the joint
support, generate their complete product and compare it with the registered
joint. Equality yields the factorable class. A strict correlated joint that
cannot be reconstructed as that product is non-factorable. This definition
retains every joint cell and marginal; a correlation score cannot replace it.
Multipartite extensions retain each component identity and every proposed
partition, so factorability may differ across cuts.

Observation partitions complete branch support into outcome classes. A sharp
observation has singleton classes at the declared label; a coarse observation
retains several branches. Compatible observations are relations that preserve
one another's retained distinctions at the registered interface. Disturbance
is the exact difference between pre- and post-observation support or phase
relations. Repeated observation retains the first record and the new source
state rather than pretending both acts observed the untouched original.

The state family also records mixed-support, purification-like and reduced-state
correspondences as relations among complete hidden, joint and observed
supports. These names enter after the Fold objects exist. No density operator,
amplitude table or stochastic preparation is silently imported. A physical
claim about preparation frequencies needs a sealed device experiment.

### 12.8 Gates, circuits, algorithms and compilation

The twenty-two `GATEX` laws expand the gate foundation into a complete
operational language. A gate's branch map is total and bijective, its phase
action is exact and invertible, and its source identity is retained. Single-
unit, controlled and joint transformations are generated from those relations.
Gate equality is exact operational equivalence over complete registered support,
not agreement on a few test inputs.

A circuit is a directed acyclic dependency structure of wires and gate
locations with a complete branchwise evaluation. Causal depth counts the
longest dependency path; size counts generated gates; live support and
measurement records are separate resources. Reversing a circuit requires
inverse gates in reverse causal order. Circuit composition retains interface,
wire and phase identities. A malformed cycle, unmatched wire or non-bijective
gate fails before an output can acquire authority.

Compilation translates a source description into the admitted gate grammar.
The compiler retains parse, intermediate decompositions, target circuit,
branchwise result equivalence, phase relation and resource overhead.
Optimisation is permitted only under the registered observational equivalence.
A shorter circuit that changes an unmeasured phase may be equivalent for one
decoder and inequivalent for another; the observation boundary must therefore
be explicit.

Universality provides one interpreter for every generated description in the
frozen grammar. It does not claim a completed set of all possible unitary
objects. Approximation, where used, retains exact rational phase or result
enclosures and a successor certificate. Gate synthesis retains the target
relation, generated candidate sequences and the first or least-resource
survivor under the declared order.

The thirty `QALGX` claims organise complete input preparation, reversible
branch generation, phase action, interference, observation decoding and
correctness. Query algorithms retain the oracle as a named external relation;
its answers and resource count remain visible. Transform algorithms operate on
complete finite support. Search and counting preserve every candidate branch
and the decoder by which an answer class is observed. Simulation and
verification algorithms retain the target model or witness rather than being
judged by a terminal label alone.

Historical algorithm names may identify correspondences after sealing, but
they cannot supply an answer table. A claimed quantum advantage requires two
registered machine descriptions, the same problem and input support, exact
resource ledgers and, where hardware is claimed, a sealed experiment. The
formal algorithm family derives quantum process structures and their internal
resources; it does not declare that a current device has achieved practical
speedup.

### 12.9 Quantum resources and complexity

The twenty-six `QCPLXX` claims retain canonical input size, gate count, causal
depth, live branch support, query rows, communication, measurement and error
records. A resource belongs to a representation and model. Complete support
size and the resources needed to represent or traverse it are not silently
interchanged. A compact process description may generate a large support; both
identities remain in the ledger.

Upper bounds require a constructive circuit and complete execution trace.
Lower bounds require an adversarial, indistinguishability or structural witness
inside the declared grammar. A correspondence to a conventional complexity
class is admitted only after the native resources are fixed. Average and worst
cases retain the complete instance family and distribution provenance.
Approximation records exact output error and success support. Parameterised
claims name the parameter carrier and its relation to input size.

Quantum schedule support remains deterministic. A branch or measurement class
may be unresolved to an observer, but no uncaused transition is used as a
complexity resource. If a physical random source supplies an input or schedule,
its distribution belongs to a measurement package. Resource-bounded
probabilistic or quantum success is then an exact part of the complete
registered support, not a floating propensity.

Complexity separations are never granted by vocabulary. A new separation
requires its own generated family, reduction boundary and witnesses. The
foundational complexity claim therefore records lower and upper witness
interfaces without asserting every familiar conventional class relation. The
same caution applies to speedup: fewer native Fold resources in one exact
grammar is a formal result; wall-clock, energy or device advantage is an
empirical engineering result.

### 12.10 Communication, cryptography and no-cloning boundaries

The twenty-four `QCOMMX` laws combine joint support with sender, receiver,
channel and local-operation custody. A quantum channel is a complete
state-to-state relation retaining phase, loss and observation interfaces.
Memory and composition record whether successive uses share source distinctions.
Capacity is relative to the registered channel, code, decoder and resource
support. Physical transmission rate and fidelity remain measured quantities.

Entanglement-assisted communication retains the pre-shared joint support and
its preparation custody. Teleportation-like correspondence records the source
state relation, shared joint support, classical message, receiver operation and
decoded state. Dense-coding-like correspondence records the same resource
ledger in the opposite informational arrangement. Neither relation permits
source-free transmission; the consumed shared support and classical channel
remain explicit.

No-cloning is derived at the complete unknown-support boundary. A copier that
must leave the source intact and create a second exact copy for every admissible
phase-bearing state would need one reversible transformation to map distinct
non-factorable source relations into product copies while preserving their
pairwise distinctions. The complete relation exposes the incompatible
requirements. Copying a known held classical label is outside that unknown
quantum-support condition and remains lawful, so the branch does not conflate
the two results.

Disturbance and information–disturbance relations retain which source
distinctions an observation extracts and which state or phase relations change.
Key-distribution and adversary laws name the channel, basis or observation
choices, public records, retained key classes and attack support. Formal
secrecy is confined to the registered adversary. A practical claim requires
device sources, losses, side channels, authentication and implementation
evidence owned by the appropriate downstream branches.

### 12.11 Coding, correction and fault-tolerant composition

The thirty-two `QCODEX` laws distinguish logical support from its physical or
component representation. An encoder is reversible and retains the mapping
from every logical branch into redundant joint words. An error family is a
complete registered set of actions and locations. A syndrome is an observation
partition over those actions. Recovery is lawful only when the syndrome and
retained records identify a unique correction class or when all surviving
actions are equivalent on the logical support.

Degeneracy is therefore not hidden ambiguity: distinct component errors may
share one syndrome because they induce the same logical relation. Erasure,
substitution, phase-label and joint errors retain different action grammars.
Distance and separation are post-derivation summaries of complete image
supports. Concatenated, product and sparse-check correspondences retain every
level, check and reconstruction map. A decoder that uses a likelihood requires
an external measured error model; the structural decoder itself cannot fit one.

Fault propagation follows each allowed component error through every gate and
wire. A contained or transversal transformation preserves the registered
correctable support at the declared locations. Recovery after a location
retains both the faulted predecessor and corrected logical result. A malignant
set is an explicit action combination that escapes the correctable class. The
fault-tolerance induction composes these records through a finite circuit.

The `2t+1` theorem supplies an exact positive-finite correction family without
supplying a hardware threshold. It proves a strict majority for every mask of
size at most `t` and gives a constructive counterexample for every smaller
width. A physical threshold would additionally need error mechanisms,
correlations, gate and measurement fidelity, timing, architecture and a
registered statistical protocol. Those values cannot be inferred from the
structural width alone.

### 12.12 Simulation, verification and quantum learning

The twenty-four `QSIMX` laws require a simulator to retain target state support,
update law, phase and interference relations, observation map, resource ledger
and exact correspondence boundary. A digital, analogue or mixed simulation is
distinguished by its encoding and update relation. Approximate simulation
retains a rational enclosure or declared observation equivalence. A terminal
match on one row does not establish model equivalence.

Verification retains a claim support, witness, verifier circuit, challenge
schedule, complete branchwise acceptance, soundness and completeness ledgers,
measurement records and tamper controls. Interactive and delegated
correspondences keep prover and verifier observations separate. A verification
claim is only as broad as its prover, witness and resource grammar. It cannot
certify an unknown physical device characteristic that was not part of the
registry.

The twenty-two `QLEARNX` laws extend Classical Learning with reversible
hypothesis processes, phase/interference search and quantum observation. Input
examples may be classical, quantum or joint, but their source and preparation
support remain explicit. Training retains hypotheses, update trace, resources
and all alternatives consistent with the data. Evaluation is sealed before the
target opens. A reported advantage needs a same-task classical comparison and
complete resource accounting.

Quantum representation, classification, regression, clustering, generation,
reinforcement, multi-agent and adaptive correspondences remain formal process
families at their registered support. No pretrained oracle or fitted amplitude
is admitted as a law. Generalisation remains empirical unless target support is
exhausted by a successor proof. Interpretability retains which branch, phase or
measurement relation supports an output; a post-hoc narrative is not equated
with the executed process.

### 12.13 Classical–quantum limits and downstream ownership

The twenty-two `QLIMITX` laws map the exact overlap and separation between the
classical and quantum machines. Classical states embed as single-held-branch,
phase-insensitive reversible states. Classical probabilistic descriptions
correspond to unresolved support classes. Quantum decoding returns an exact
classical record through the observation relation. Bidirectional simulation
retains description and resource overhead rather than declaring the two modes
identical.

Measurement disturbance, no-cloning, communication and correction limits
retain their exact source and adversary grammars. Halting, undecidability and
incompleteness transfer because the quantum interpreter is still a generated
finite description capable of self-reference. An external oracle remains an
external named relation. Complete support does not turn the machine into a
hypercomputer.

Six handoff claims close ownership. Physics receives formal states, processes
and resources but owns preparation, apparatus, units, uncertainty, timing,
energy, fidelity and thresholds. Chemistry and Materials consume admitted
simulators but own composition, structure, observables and domain
falsification. Engineering owns architecture, control, reliability,
performance and safety. Open extension requires a new value-free obligation,
complete enumeration, controls, reconstruction and post-registry evidence.

### 12.14 Quantum limits, preserved halt and open questions

Every admitted quantum circuit remains a generated finite description
executed by an admitted universal process. Self-description and held
complement therefore transfer the classical halting and incompleteness
boundaries. Complete branch support does not supply an undeclared oracle;
quantum computation does not escape the registered computability limit.
No-cloning and measurement-disturbance laws likewise retain the distinction
and observation conditions under which copying or non-disturbance fails.

The validation history preserves a genuine rejected submission. The first
official `SFT-QUANTUM-VALID-REVERSIBLE-001` attempt halted because a sealed
prediction label and an already frozen vector label did not match. Its rejected
receipt remains `sha256:bc2e0eb42e8650c848de9872a8666fc879d84aec5c21201b378721f00fd8a62e`.
The halt is not counted as a success. A separately versioned retry corrected
only the protocol label binding and passed the unchanged route; the scientific
law, target, candidate grammar, observation vector, engine, verifier and
acceptance condition did not change.

The branch has no open row in its frozen 288-claim census, but several domains
remain expressly open or downstream: physical amplitudes and outcome
frequencies, hardware timing and energy, measured gate fidelity and noise,
device thresholds, empirical speedup, chemistry and materials behaviour, and
application performance. A formal simulator derives consequences of its model;
it does not measure nature.

---

## Cumulative dependency spine and handoff to Physics

Across these branches, the same scientific object becomes progressively more
capable without changing owner retroactively.

| Branch | Receives | Derives and owns | Explicitly does not absorb |
|---|---|---|---|
| Methods and Foundation | No earlier SFT law; the operational presentation boundary | Occurrence, One, count, part, Fold, form, exact operations, trace and admission custody | General mathematics, natural measurements and application behaviour |
| Mathematics | Foundation carriers and trace laws | Exact generated-finite mathematical structures, relations, proofs, bounds and certified approximation | Physical meaning, domain measurements and downstream semantics |
| Information Science | Mathematical support, relation, probability and composition | Symbol, record, source, channel, loss, inference, privacy and information custody | Quantum dynamics, biological function, cognition and lived experience |
| Classical Computation | Information carriers and conservation plus mathematical dynamics | Languages, machines, algorithms, resources, semantics, distributed processes, security, learning and simulation | Quantum operations, hardware and domain performance |
| Reversible and Quantum Computation | Classical process and reversibility records | Reversible carrier, branch support, phase, interference, entanglement, measurement semantics, gates, circuits, codes and quantum limits | Physical units, apparatus, fidelity, energy, thresholds and natural outcome distributions |

The handoff is registered as `SFT-QUANTUM-HAND-PHYSICS-MEASUREMENT-002`:
Physics receives sealed state, process and resource identities and owns
preparation, units, uncertainty, apparatus, timing, energy, fidelity and
threshold measurements. This boundary prevents two opposite errors. Quantum
Computation cannot claim that a formal circuit execution measured a device;
Physics cannot use a later measured value to select the earlier formal quantum
law.

Physics therefore begins with a rich but non-physical formal inheritance:
exact positive quantity relations, generated geometry and dynamics,
deterministic-support probability, information and measurement custody,
executable processes, reversible and quantum states, and explicit resource
interfaces. Its next problem is to derive physical carriers, dimensions,
constants, fields, matter and measurement relations without importing the
target values those relations will later face. That is the subject of the next
part.

### Current corrections, adverse records and unresolved boundaries

At this freeze, no registered claim in the five ledgers is omitted from its
dated census. That does not mean the history is uniformly favourable or that
every scientific question is resolved.

- Foundation preserves the corrected distinction between complement and
  half-One phase translation.
- Mathematics preserves the failed Collatz shortcut, the bounded Riemann and
  fluid boundaries, calculator claim 003's reversed enclosure parity, the
  blank desktop widget and phone usability failures, and their separately
  admitted corrections.
- Information Science preserves ambiguity and unavailable distinctions as
  complete predecessor classes rather than forcing a likely decoder.
- Classical Computation preserves the halt-or-recur correction, the exact
  diagonal basis of undecidability, the replacement of vague efficiency prose
  by resource ledgers, and the native-only scope of `P_F=NP_F`.
- Quantum Computation preserves the first halted validation attempt and the
  separately versioned protocol-only retry.
- Every branch preserves favourable, adverse, absent, unavailable, unresolved
  and boundary rows in its audit layer even when the conceptual narrative
  abbreviates them.

The open boundary is extension, not an unregistered hole concealed by the word
complete. “Complete” here means complete to the named frozen census and its
current evidence surface. A new mathematical object, information problem,
machine model, quantum operation, counterexample or stronger empirical test
must receive a new versioned registration and cannot rewrite prior evidence.

## Part II. From physical law to chemical identity and organised matter

## 13. Physics: quantities, constants, fields and measured nature

The preceding branches establish exact mathematics, records, executable
computation and reversible and quantum processes. Physics begins when those
formal objects must be connected to physical quantity, dimension, interaction,
source, response and measurement. This is a genuine change of evidential
boundary. A mathematically unique survivor is not yet an observation of
nature; a measured value is not permitted to become a hidden premise of the
derivation that it later tests. The Physics branch therefore carries two
constitutions at once: an exact derivational constitution for the generated
physical structure, and a source-bound empirical constitution for comparisons
with the world.

The active Physics paper is version 1.3.0. Its current categorical inventory
contains **368 of 368 registered Physics claims**, generated from **257,776
candidates and 257,776 decisions**, with **368 unique survivors**, **1,472 of
1,472 adverse controls passed** and an independent reconstruction for every
claim. All 368 closures are classified as `depth_independent`. The evidence
surface distinguishes **238 sealed empirical-validation claims** from **130
independently replicated formal claims without empirical packages**. Those
categories are not pooled into a generic claim of empirical confirmation.

### 13.1 Scope, ownership and present status

Physics owns physical quantities and dimensions, mechanics, fields, forces,
waves, thermodynamic and vacuum relations, the physical quantum and
relativistic interfaces, constants and scales, matter and interaction
structures, atomic and nuclear physical relations, gravitation and physical
cosmology. It consumes mathematical, informational and computational objects
without taking ownership of their upstream proofs. Chemistry owns the chemical
identity and reaction systems constructed from physical carriers; Materials
Science owns collective structure–property relations in organised matter;
Astronomy owns celestial-system observations; and Engineering owns artefact,
safety and deployment claims. A physical law may be used by all of those
branches while retaining one scientific owner.

| Physics family | Claims | Scientific role |
|---|---:|---|
| Measurement and metrology | 13 | Quantity, unit, comparison, uncertainty and physical evidence custody |
| Mechanics and dynamics | 24 | State, change, momentum, energy and dynamical response |
| Fields, forces, waves and geometry | 41 | Local relation, propagation, orientation, source and spatial response |
| Thermodynamics and vacuum | 31 | Multiplicity, work, temperature, entropy, vacuum floor and global share |
| Physical quantum and relativistic structure | 24 | Quantum weights, measurement boundary, causal order and relativistic relation |
| Constants, scales and precision | 9 | Exact generated constants, dimensional interfaces and precision comparison |
| Matter, interactions and flavour | 62 | Particle families, charges, couplings, masses and interaction structure |
| Atomic and molecular physics | 15 | Bound-state, orbital, transition and molecular physical relations |
| Nuclear and hadronic physics | 17 | Nuclear closure, hadronic structure, decay and binding relations |
| Spacetime and gravitation | 34 | Dimension, inverse-square response, gravitation, metric and hierarchy |
| Continua and collective matter | 18 | Collective modes, continua, radiation and macroscopic response |
| Physical cosmology boundary | 16 | Primordial, matter-share, expansion and vacuum-scale relations |
| Post-seal empirical validation | 64 | Registered measurements, holdouts, corrections and returned physical families |
| **Total** | **368** | **Current registered Physics categorical scope** |

The Physics result also closes **488 of 488 Physics-owned V1/V2 atoms at the
same declared strength**. That reconciliation means that the older obligations
have a current owner and current proof or evidence route; it does not make the
older papers authoritative proof premises. Grand Locks 075 and 076 preserve a
349-claim pre-extension snapshot. Within that historical lock, the formal
certificate covers 347 claims and 534 graph nodes, while the empirical lock
retains 234 pre-lock empirical claims, 147 distinct source IDs, fourteen
unfavourable or scope-boundary claims and six legacy receipt shapes. Nineteen
later claims—including the Unified Constants Object, the Tesla resonance
return, the vacuum/inertia family and the new-sector and Smithion family—are
separately audited. They are not attributed backwards to the earlier lock.

### 13.2 The physical admission boundary

The branch preserves the Foundation distinction between structural absence and
numerical zero. A missing relation may be admitted as a structural absence;
the derivation may not silently import zero as an already available number.
Proof values are generated positive whole or rational values. Direction and
opposition remain held labels until an admitted relation acts on them. The
formal grammar does not take irrational or imaginary values, floating-point
approximations, completed infinity, an ungenerated continuum, fitted
coefficients or free parameters as premises.

Every Physics claim is processed by the same admission engine. Its package
must identify the complete candidate grammar and cardinality, decide every
candidate, retain exactly one survivor, explain minimality and named-shape
uniqueness, pass four registered controls and reconstruct independently. Where
the claim touches observation, the package must additionally isolate the
empirical target, preserve source and custody, retain every row and specify the
falsifier. External numerical values may enter only after the formal result has
sealed. Comparisons use exact rational intervals wherever the source permits
them; displayed decimals are readings of those exact records, not replacement
proof objects.

#### 13.2.1 Prediction capability and target custody

The first three Physics claims do not state a conventional physical law. They
establish the capability boundary required before any later law may be called
a sealed prediction. The prediction form is a finite data-only interpreter. It
accepts registered exact inputs and admitted operations, holds state by
single assignment, emits one declared terminal result and exposes the complete
step trace. It has no ambient capability from which a target could leak. The
portable target-commitment claim then binds a complete named support, a nonce
and a content identity to a distinct custodian. Only a matching experiment and
matching execution envelope may receive the one post-seal release. The hostile
package claim protects the model-admission receipt and candidate census from a
data file that attempts to add an instruction, extra field or alternate route.

These claims explain what target blindness means in the V3 corpus. It is not a
statement that the author had never heard of the phenomenon. It is a machine
relation: the executable process that chooses and evaluates the formal
survivor cannot access the target content, and a distinct custody record
establishes when the content was released. Conversely, where a result was
developed while the target was visible, the result may remain a useful
reconstruction or correspondence but is not relabelled blind. The empirical
status is carried by the receipt, not inferred from confident prose.

#### 13.2.2 Observation, quantity and dimension

Physical observation begins as a retained distinction between a system, a
reference and a record. A quantity is a reference-equivalence carrier: two
physical records are comparable only where the declared equivalence preserves
the kind being measured. Dimension supplies a canonical composition path and
an independence test. A unit is a reversible scale transport within one
dimension; it does not change the physical kind. Reference realisation adds
the operational trace from the definition to the apparatus or procedure that
instantiates it.

This ordering blocks several common conflations. A number without its quantity
kind is not yet a physical value. Two quantities with the same displayed
number need not be comparable. Two measurements with the same nominal unit
need not possess the same realisation, uncertainty or source identity. A lawful
conversion requires a complete dimension path and a reversible factor, while a
calibration requires a chain of comparisons whose links and uncertainties are
retained. Dimensional consistency is therefore derived as a relation among
typed carriers rather than imposed as a visual check on symbols.

The measured-value record is kept separate from the proof value. Its fields
identify the source, method, condition, value, uncertainty or interval,
version and observation boundary. Measurement uncertainty is represented by
the complete retained set of admissible alternatives under the source's
method; it is not a licence to change the sealed prediction. Calibration
closure composes the registered comparison chain. If a link is missing,
incompatible or outside its versioned scope, the result halts rather than
assuming that the source's institutional standing fills the gap.

The exact source-boundary growth discriminator is the bridge from this
metrology to spatial law. It compares positive distance and response ratios
from the same retained source, propagates their complete uncertainty support
and searches the positive successor-power family. Its stopping certificate
must show that no later positive integer can re-enter the admissible interval.
That is why the inverse-square comparison can say that two is the sole positive
integer in the interval, rather than merely reporting that a fitted exponent
is near two.

#### 13.2.3 Exact interval arithmetic and source roles

Finite source decimals are parsed as exact fractions. An uncertainty statement
becomes exact lower and upper endpoints; multiplication and division propagate
those endpoints through the registered monotone relation. A published decimal
followed by an ellipsis is bounded at its next decimal place. This arithmetic
prevents binary floating equality from deciding admission and prevents an
unreported rounding choice from manufacturing overlap.

The principal external custodians have different evidential roles. BIPM and
its metrological records provide definitions, realisations and traceability;
NIST and CODATA provide constants and their stated uncertainties; NIST ASD
provides atomic spectra; PDG supplies versioned particle summaries and
measurement ranges; IAEA and AME records supply nuclear closure and mass
surfaces; IAPWS supplies thermophysical water records; GWOSC supplies
gravitational-wave observations; CERN Open Data supplies collider-bound
records; and NASA LAMBDA supplies cosmological source material. None is cited
as proof by prestige. Each enters only through the rows and discriminators
registered for a particular claim.

This division matters most where familiar physical language invites an
unlicensed inference. A structural correspondence may identify the form of an
inverse-square relation without predicting a dimensional coefficient. A
post-seal value inside a registered measurement interval is an exact numerical
correspondence at that boundary, not automatically a prospective blind
prediction. A successful source comparison validates the registered relation
and protocol; it does not confirm all of Physics or every extension of the
grammar.

### 13.3 From the Fold to spatial and dynamical structure

The minimal Fold supplies a binary distinction, reversible return and ordered
carrier. Physics asks what physical organisation becomes necessary when that
carrier must support location, direction, propagation and restoration. The
generated orbit with multiplier three,

\[
\frac{1}{7}\longrightarrow\frac{2}{7}\longrightarrow
\frac{4}{7}\longrightarrow\frac{1}{7},
\]

has three distinct positions before return. In the Physics grammar this closes
the spatial direction count at three. A boundary has rank two, and the
corresponding spreading relation has inverse-square exponent two. The
empirical exponent comparison is retained separately: the tested form is
\(2+q\), with \(q=(2.7\pm3.1)\times10^{-16}\), and two is the sole positive
integer inside the registered interval. The observation tests the exponent;
it does not serve as the premise from which the exponent was selected.

The same direction structure gives six cubic neighbours: three independent
directions, each with two orientations. Propagating collective response then
separates into one longitudinal and two transverse modes. These results later
become part of the Materials derivation of lattice neighbourhood and acoustic
branches. Their physical ownership remains here; the material branch owns the
way a specimen and its organisation realises them.

Mechanics and dynamics develop ordered state change, conservation and

#### 13.3.1 Events, recurrence and mechanical quantity

The mechanics family begins with an event: a retained physical state and a
generated change at a declared boundary. Duration is not inserted as an
already flowing real line. It is obtained from ordered recurrence, so a clock
is a physical system whose repeated relation can be compared with another
event trace. Location is a relation to retained support; separation compares
two locations; displacement adds a held orientation. Speed compares separation
with recurrence, while velocity retains orientation. Acceleration is a change
of that oriented velocity over a further ordered recurrence.

Inertial persistence follows when a closed motion trace has no registered
transfer that changes it. Momentum is the carrier that composes inertial
weight with oriented motion. Force is a constrained rate of momentum transfer,
not an unexplained primitive push. Work composes force with displacement;
kinetic change records the corresponding change of the motion carrier; energy
is the retained capacity for a permitted transfer. Power orders that transfer
by duration. Angular motion adds cyclic orientation, and angular momentum and
torque carry the associated conserved and transferred distinctions.

Composite motion requires a centre relation without importing numerical-zero
coordinates. The branch constructs the complete weighted part relation among
positive carriers and holds orientation separately. Conservation is then a
bijection across the closed transfer network: every departing distinction has
a named receiving relation, and no unregistered source or sink appears.
Constrained motion adds a restoration relation. Oscillation is the resulting
reversible recurrence around the held constraint, rather than a sinusoid
assumed as a primitive mathematical object.

The order matters. A formula such as work equals force through displacement
would be circular if force, displacement and transfer had not first acquired
distinct carriers. The claim sequence prevents that circularity by admitting
event, duration, separation, velocity, acceleration, inertia, momentum, force,
energy, power and cyclic response in turn. Each later law names the earlier
receipt it consumes. The empirical measured-value successors for momentum,
force and related quantities test those compositions through exact unit and
source adapters; they do not define the formal relation retrospectively.

#### 13.3.2 Phase, spectrum and coupled dynamics

Once a cyclic orientation can be retained, a free carrier can accumulate Fold

The later mechanics family extends this construction to odd-lattice

and coupled ensembles. Expansion and information-rate carriers are related at

normalised exactly; orbital restoration is tested across the generated

apparatus or dataset is used, the relationship is a separately registered

The resulting picture is neither a continuum premise nor a denial that

trace. A continuum description appears later as a coarse-grained
correspondence when the preserving coordinates and error boundary are named.
This is why a numerical integration, a measured trajectory and an exact Fold
orbit retain three different evidence statuses even when their graphs look
similar.

#### 13.3.3 Sources, fields and interaction

A field is introduced only after source and response have distinct physical

Gauge-equivalent descriptions are admitted when distinct descriptions retain

The branch then extends the exact carrier to charge, Coulomb potential and

and planar and three-space Maxwell curvature. Prime-sector structure gives the

The empirical interaction-class claim asks whether the registered source

#### 13.3.4 Waves, propagation and resonance

Period is a completed recurrence and frequency counts recurrence against a

Bounded-cavity recurrence makes the allowed modes discrete. The exact

The quarter-wave family retains the odd harmonics. A common recurrence can

Exact resonant transfer closes only with a complete energy ledger: input,

That ledger is the reason the Tesla return can be stated persuasively without

The finite unattainability boundary replaces an appeal to an actually

expansion family gives \(13/12\) and its refinement \(3305/3048\). The
registered Planck and SH0ES comparisons pass at their declared interfaces.
The scalar–tensor construction gives \(31/32\) and \(1/32\). The paper does
not convert those fractions into an equality with an imported number of
e-folds; that stronger statement is not admitted.

Gravitation inherits the three-dimensional carrier, rank-two boundary and
inverse-square response, then adds physical source, hierarchy and measurement.
The proton–Planck result is a squared hierarchy and retains that exponent in
its statement. Physical cosmology is the outer ownership boundary of this
paper: it supplies exact physical relations that Astronomy may use, while
observations of particular celestial systems and a complete cosmic history
remain with Astronomy and Cosmology.

#### 13.7.1 Relational spacetime and causal structure

Spacetime begins from a relational event rather than a container existing
before every physical relation. An invariant event interval compares two
events while preserving causal kind. The accessibility cone separates events
that can participate in a lawful propagation chain from those outside the
declared causal support. Inertial frames are equivalent descriptions of a
complete relation, and the limiting propagation speed is invariant under the
admitted transformation.

Relative clock rate and relative spatial extent are derived as paired
consequences of the same frame relation. The exact positive velocity
composition law preserves the One-speed boundary without a negative proof
quantity. Fizeau evidence is used as a post-seal discriminator for that
composition. The experiment tests the physical adapter; it does not provide
the algebraic form as a premise.

Inertial–gravitational equivalence introduces the common response boundary.
Source-linked relational curvature records how the physical relation changes
with source, and free propagation follows that curved relation. Field-source
closure preserves the complete source ledger. A weak potential and Gauss
closure connect the local relation to boundary flux. Static Fold-covariant
clock and redshift claims test the same geometry from timing, while the
gravitational-redshift and acceleration equivalence keeps those observational
routes distinct.

The lattice-curvature family constructs exact finite curvature. Gravity
self-sources through its field-energy ledger, producing a finite contraction
towards the registered fixed point rather than an unbounded imported
iteration. The four-coordinate symmetric curvature-source relation preserves
conservation. The static spherical exterior family closes coefficient, flux
and clock at one boundary; the quadrupole third-difference family closes the
radiated-power structure at another. Their conventional correspondences and
dimensional calibrations occur after the formal seal.

#### 13.7.2 Gravitational radiation, horizons and nonstandard geometries

Gravitational-wave propagation retains one limiting speed and two graviton

chirp, merger and damped ringdown. Complete GW151226 and GW190521 records test
that three-stage ordering while retaining the short-signal and

A causal horizon is the boundary beyond which the declared observer lacks an

Horizon information closure requires the complete record to remain in the

it. Compact-object and horizon-thermodynamic claims then state their exact

Wormhole, warp-support and closed-timelike-recurrence claims are admissibility

constitution halts. The post-seal nonstandard-spacetime record preserves the
absence of a qualifying realisation where appropriate. Compatibility of a

The quantum-gravity claim composes one finite Fold quantum carrier with one
finite gravitational carrier. It does not assert that an imported continuum

and open at further empirical boundaries.

#### 13.7.3 Continua, fluids, plasmas and collective order

Finite coarse graining provides the continuum correspondence used for

Plasma collective response adds charge-carrier organisation, oscillation and

The criticality, universality and turbulence family distinguishes an exact

blackbody, acoustic, laser, plasma, Alfvén, critical-exponent, universality and
turbulence rows. These sources test a complete collective-response surface;
they do not collapse distinct media into a single universal measured value.

#### 13.7.4 The physical cosmology handoff

Propagation redshift, relational expansion, background radiation, lensing,

The current Physics paper includes returned stellar hydrostatic, nuclear-chain,

### 13.10 Handoff to Chemistry

Physics hands Chemistry a physical carrier with exact direction and boundary,
charge and coupling structures, atomic and nuclear closures, orbital and
transition relations, energy and scale interfaces, and measurement custody.
What remains unresolved is chemical rather than merely physical: which
physically possible carriers count as the same chemical species; how
composition and bond establish a molecule; how reaction changes one chemical
identity into another; how solvent, temperature and environment condition a
chemical outcome; and how those claims can be tested without treating a
database value as an axiom. Chemistry is required because physical possibility
does not by itself supply chemical identity.

---

## 14. Chemistry: identity, composition, reaction and molecular evidence

Chemistry begins from the physical elements, interactions, orbit structures
and measurement interfaces already admitted. Its new carrier is the chemical
individual: an identity stable enough to be composed, bonded, transformed,
separated and measured, yet sensitive to charge, stereochemistry, phase,
environment and reaction route. The branch must therefore distinguish a
formal chemical constitution from the many source- and condition-bound values
through which real substances are observed.

The active Chemistry paper is version 1.3.0. Its frozen discipline census has
**272 of 272 registered obligations**. Nine later lineage and Smithium returns
bring the live paper to **281 claims**. Their packages enumerate **71,936
candidates**, retain **281 unique survivors**, pass **1,124 of 1,124 controls**
and reconstruct independently in all 281 cases. Closure is
`depth_independent` for 276 claims and `finite_complete` for five. The evidence
surface contains **273 post-seal empirical packages** and **eight formal-only
claims**; those eight are not presented as measured results.

### 14.1 Scope, ownership and family census

Chemistry owns chemical identity, elemental and isotopic chemical roles,
composition, bond, acid–base and redox relations, reaction, catalysis,
stereochemistry, molecular property, chemical phase and transport, kinetics,
organic and inorganic organisation, electrochemistry, radiochemistry,
analysis, computational chemical representations and polymer chemistry.
Physics remains the owner of the upstream particle, atomic and nuclear laws.
Materials owns bulk specimen response, microstructure and phase–property
relations. Biology owns living function and biochemical organisation;
Medicine owns clinical outcome; Earth Sciences owns environmental history;
and Astronomy owns cosmic context.

The field census is not a list assembled after the results were known. It is a
registered surface divided into foundation, complete-field, validation and
handoff families.

### 14.2 From physical element to chemical identity

The physical nucleus and electron structure do not yet define every chemical
question. Chemistry first establishes what must be held fixed for two records
to name the same element, isotope, ion, molecule or stereochemical state. It
then derives composition as a counted relation between retained identities.
Stoichiometry is not imported as a convention: it is the exact preservation
ledger required when a chemical transformation changes relations without
silently deleting the carriers that enter or leave the declared boundary.

Bonding follows because a composition alone does not determine a chemical
individual. The bond family adds a retained relation, order, orientation and
allowed change. Molecular geometry and stereochemistry become necessary when
the same constituent inventory supports non-identical arrangements. Acid/base
and redox families then track distinct transfer relations and their custody;
they do not collapse proton transfer, electron transfer, oxidation state and
measurement into a single word. Reaction families specify the complete
reactant and product identities, stoichiometric balance and route. Catalysis
changes the available route while retaining the catalyst's declared identity;
it is not treated as unexplained acceleration.

This sequence is the Chemistry narrative spine: elemental identity permits
composition; composition plus relation permits bond; bond plus orientation
permits molecular and stereochemical identity; a typed change of those
relations permits reaction; and a comparison between routes permits catalysis
and kinetics. Each later family inherits the prior custody rather than
redefining its inputs.

#### 14.2.1 Chemical measurement and retained identity

A chemical entity is the least carrier whose relevant constituents and
relations remain distinguishable through the declared observation. A species
is an observation-equivalence class of such entities; a substance adds a
composition identity at the sample boundary. Amount of substance is a counted
reference carrier. A chemical formula is the reversible exact encoding of the
registered composition, and nomenclature is admissible only where the name can
be traced back to that identity without loss.

This sequence separates an object from the language used to report it. Two
names may be equivalent if they decode to the same complete chemical identity;
the same short name may be inadequate when isotope, charge, stereochemistry or
phase changes the tested claim. Chemical measurement uncertainty retains every
alternative allowed by the method, and reference traceability records sample,
standard, calibration and source. The observation does not alter which entity
the formal grammar selected.

A sample is not assumed homogeneous merely because it carries one label.
Analyte and matrix are separate carriers. The method must state what portion
was sampled, which species or property it can discriminate, its calibration,
interferences and uncertainty. A result outside the method's discrimination
boundary is unresolved or out of bound, not evidence that the chemical
distinction is absent.

#### 14.2.2 Elements, isotopes and the periodic carrier

Element identity is retained nuclear charge at the chemical boundary. Atomic
number orders those identities. Isotopes preserve the element while changing
the nuclear composition; an atomic-weight record is consequently a
source-bound relation to isotopic composition, not an immutable exact value of
the element in every specimen. Periodic order preserves nuclear identity and
records the recurrence of outer chemical organisation. Group and period are
compositional coordinates of that recurrence.

Valence availability supplies the boundary at which an atomic carrier can
enter a chemical combination. Ion formation changes the held charge state
while preserving elemental identity. The known-periodic-table observation
claim stops at the observed elements through 118. The generated g-block,
Smithium and endpoint returns are later standing predictions, deliberately
kept outside the known-table observation statement.

The distinction between an exact ordering and a measured atomic weight is
important. The ordering is formal once the element carrier is admitted. The
weight depends on isotope distribution, source and reporting convention. A
new isotope measurement can lawfully update the observed record without
rewriting the element identity or periodic grammar.

#### 14.2.3 Composition, mixtures and solutions

Exact composition counts retained constituents. Conservation through reaction
requires the elemental identities entering the boundary to have a complete
route into the products or declared outgoing support. Stoichiometric
coefficients are positive counts of those routes. A limiting component is the
first required constituent whose available count cannot support another
complete product event. Yield is the retained product share relative to the
registered limiting and recovery boundaries.

Mixtures preserve multiple component identities on common support. A solution
adds a solute–solvent role distinction; it does not erase the constituents
into a featureless whole. Composition may be reported through several lawful
coordinates, but a conversion is valid only where all required amounts,
volumes, masses, temperatures and reference states are available. A measured
concentration is condition-bound. The formal conservation ledger cannot infer
an unmeasured loss, side product or evaporation merely to make a preferred
yield balance.

This composition grammar becomes a recurring control throughout Chemistry.
Electrolysis must conserve charge and elemental identity. Polymerisation must
retain repeat-unit and end-group routes. Radiochemical separation must retain
the nuclide and its chemical state. Smithium entrance partitions must sum to
the registered \(Z\), \(N\) and \(A\) coordinates. In every case, a missing
route halts rather than being represented by an imported numerical zero.

#### 14.2.4 Bond, molecule and supramolecular relation

A chemical bond is a retained interaction closure between identified
constituents. Covalent bonding shares support; ionic bonding transfers a held
label; metallic bonding distributes collective support. Bond order counts the
joining multiplicity. Bond length and energy are empirical or reconstructed
coordinates attached to a particular state and method; they do not define the
existence of the bond by themselves.

A molecule is a complete connected bonded carrier. Geometry adds adjacency
and orientation. Isomers share composition while differing in one or more
retained relations. Intermolecular interaction adds residual joining between
otherwise complete chemical individuals. Supramolecular organisation requires
reversible recognition, and a molecular network is a connected whole whose
relations extend beyond a single molecular carrier.

The branch therefore avoids two reductions. A molecular formula is not a
complete structure where adjacency or orientation remains unresolved, and an
observed bulk association is not automatically a covalent molecule. The claim
must preserve the carrier and relation appropriate to its boundary. This is
particularly important at the Chemistry-to-Materials handoff, where a network
or interface may produce a bulk response without creating a new molecular
identity.

#### 14.2.5 Acid/base, redox and electrochemical separation

The conjugate acid–base partition retains the paired states before and after a
transfer. Proton-transfer and electron-pair donor–acceptor relations are
separate because their carriers and evidence differ. Amphoteric behaviour is a
dual-role relation under distinct partners or conditions. A buffer retains a
conjugate pair whose composition supports a bounded response; it is not a
claim of invariant pH under arbitrary addition.

Electronegativity is an ordering of held affinity. Bond polarity records an
unequal relation, while oxidation state is an accounting coordinate. Coupled
oxidation and reduction preserves the complete electron-transfer ledger.
Separating the two sides of that transfer produces the electrochemical cell.
Potential, current and work are downstream measured or derived relations; they
do not replace the chemical identity of the half-reactions.

These distinctions prevent oxidation state from being mistaken for a directly
localised physical charge in every bonding description. They also prevent a
cell voltage from proving one preferred microscopic mechanism. A mechanism
requires its own complete intermediate and elementary-step trace.

#### 14.2.6 Reaction, mechanism, equilibrium and catalysis

A reaction is a source-bound transformation from complete reactant identity to
complete product identity. A mechanism is an ordered set of elementary steps.
An intermediate is retained where it is produced and later consumed on that
path. The activation barrier is the boundary between the adjacent states;
reaction rate counts completed transitions over an ordered recurrence, and
kinetic order records the observed dependency multiplicity at the stated
condition.

Equilibrium is balanced reversible support. Thermochemical accounting retains
the energy transfer, while reaction direction requires the complete energy and
distinction ledger. A chemical phase is a state identity; solubility is an
equilibrium across solution and retained phase support. Photochemical reaction
adds an excitation channel and therefore requires wavelength or state custody
rather than treating illumination as an untyped cause.

A catalyst retains its identity through a completed reaction cycle. Its
scientific role is to supply an alternative path. Selectivity compares the
generated product routes, and turnover counts completed catalyst cycles under
the declared conditions. A reaction network composes multiple paths;
autocatalysis closes when a product increases the availability of its own
generating route. Adsorption, colloidal dispersion and chemical transfer at an
interface extend the reaction carrier to a boundary shared with Materials.

Catalytic evidence is therefore conditional. A higher measured rate can
support the alternative-path relation when reactants, products, catalyst,
temperature, transport and method are retained. It does not show that the
catalyst changed equilibrium, that all competing paths vanished or that its
identity was conserved unless those coordinates were measured.

#### 14.2.7 Stereochemical, polymer and analytical foundations

Chirality is a held orientation not superposable under the admissible
transformations. Enantiomers form the corresponding pair; diastereomers retain
a different relation. Functional-group recurrence provides a local chemical
role that can be traced across a reaction family. Organic reaction families
compose those roles with the complete entering, leaving and rearranged
carriers.

A polymer chain repeats a monomer-derived identity while retaining its
connections and ends. A population requires the chain-length distribution; a
single nominal molecular size cannot stand for every chain. The earliest
Chemistry-to-Biology molecular handoff supplies identity and reaction
capability but does not yet supply biological function.

Analytical foundations distinguish sample, analyte and matrix; establish
calibration; record selectivity and interference; and derive complete
identification surfaces for mass, infrared, electronic and
rotational–vibrational spectra. A complete analytical result includes the
falsification record. One peak without source, calibration and competing
identity support is not sufficient merely because it appears at an expected
position.
