#### 14.3.1 Synthesis grammar versus feasible route

Every one of the 22,875 entrance partitions is a labelled positive two-body
composition whose coordinates sum to \(Z=126\), \(N=184\) and \(A=310\).
That census proves completeness of the conservation grammar. It does not
promote any partition into an experimentally feasible reaction. Feasibility
would additionally require projectile and target availability, beam energy,
barrier and compound-state relations, a positive cross-section, survival
against competing channels and a measurable yield.

The distinction matters because a conserved route can be physically possible
within one grammar and experimentally negligible or unavailable. The formal
census identifies the complete search surface. Future measurements can then
rank, exclude or observe rows without changing which partitions conserve the
registered coordinates.

#### 14.3.2 Decay, lifetime and identification

The gamma, alpha, beta and fission ledgers enumerate permitted channel
coordinates while preserving daughter identity and conservation. A channel's
presence in that grammar is not a measured branching fraction. Numerical
lifetime requires a positive width and unit at the declared source boundary.
Because that input is absent, the lifetime package halts explicitly. The halt
prevents an arbitrary lifetime estimate from entering the standing prediction.

The ion and oxidation ladder, g-to-f and g-to-h transition classes and
twenty-one chemical-state distinctions provide independent identity axes. The
five-record protocol requires those axes to be composed under common custody.
One unexplained event, one mass coordinate or one spectral feature is not by
itself the complete Smithium identification.

The prediction is therefore designed to become more discriminating when data
arrive. A measured cross-section, decay line or chemical separation can fill
one present unresolved coordinate. A contradictory observation can be
adverse. Neither outcome is permitted to rewrite the earlier seal or pretend
that the observation was known before the prediction.

### 14.4 Molecular structure and the cross-property vector

Chemical identity becomes experimentally useful only if the same retained
molecule can be traced across different property records. The molecular
electronic and structure families establish state, orbital, geometry,
conformation, symmetry and correlation carriers. The cross-property vector
then tests whether records from distinct sources preserve a common molecular
identity rather than comparing unrelated rows.

`SFT-CHEM-CROSS-PROPERTY-MOLECULAR-VECTOR-014` covers thirteen property
families, 9,025 source rows and 1,104 molecular carriers. Of those carriers,
676 occur on overlapping property surfaces, accounting for 6,676 overlap
rows. Its receipt is
`sha256:c3953d770e1837fc70536ba386e631a93a812b8e1008fe71d1635af1386999b8`.
The count is an exact custody result for the registered source surface. It is
not a claim that a molecule possesses a context-free scalar summary or that
every property has been measured under the same conditions.

The property packages preserve state, method, temperature, pressure,
preparation, source version and uncertainty where available. Molecular-state
and correlation records retain adverse and unavailable rows. The NIST SRD 101
torsion surface, for example, contains forty-one species, forty-four torsion
targets and 782 path states, with one unresolved row preserved. An unresolved
row is not removed from the denominator to improve the apparent outcome.

#### 14.4.1 Molecular electronic and joint-state constitution

The molecular electronic carrier preserves nuclear identity, electron count,
held spin and the relation joining the parts. Nuclear–electronic, vibronic and
rovibronic states are exact compositions of those typed carriers. Fermionic
occupation retains exclusion; ground and excited states are finitely ordered;
degeneracy is an equivalence under the stated symmetry rather than a deletion
of the distinct supporting records.

Complementary electron-pair exchange and molecular exclusion determine which
joint states may close. Nonfactorisable molecular support records correlation
between parts, while dissociation names the boundary at which the complete
molecular carrier becomes separate products. Multicentre and delocalised
support remain connected relations; they are not forced into a pairwise bond
description when that description loses the registered distinction.

A molecular-state transformation specifies the initial carrier, terminal
carrier and permitted transition. Observation classes and selection rules
state which distinction a method can resolve. Configuration order, basin,
barrier and path distinguish a stable state from the route between states.
Isotopologue laws retain nuclear change while preserving chemical identity and
the scale separation relevant to the comparison.

The chemical measurement-reduction law connects the exact joint state to the
record available to a real instrument. The operational classical–quantum
correspondence names the boundary at which a chemical description may use a
classical state ledger without erasing the underlying quantum support. Neither
law asserts that an instrument observes the complete formal state directly.

#### 14.4.2 Geometry and property relations

The H2/D2 bond-length and dissociation-path claims test isotope-preserving
relations on named molecular carriers. Symmetry can force a bond angle, while
a dihedral or torsional state requires a held axis and orientation. Dipole
organisation preserves charge separation and uses a squared-magnitude relation
only after the vector carrier is defined. Polarisability is a response to a
specified perturbation, and its isotropic composition is distinct from a
direction-resolved measurement.

Ionisation energy and electron affinity are state-order and magnitude
relations with terminal identities. Vibrational recurrence and rotational
constants preserve their axes and mass distribution. Intermolecular binding is
ordered by the retained states; magnetic response and formation energy each
have their own source and condition boundary. The cross-property vector does
not claim that one coefficient predicts all thirteen families. Its
zero-parameter content is the common source-bound molecular carrier and the
complete law by which the distinct property records are composed.

This is why the overlap counts matter scientifically. A table with 9,025 rows
could still be a collection of unrelated measurements. The 1,104-carrier
identity map and 676-carrier overlap surface test whether the same molecule,
state and provenance can be followed across properties. A separate fitted
coefficient for each family is a registered falsifier of the claimed common
carrier, not an accepted escape route.

### 14.5 Thermodynamics, phase, mixtures and transport

Chemistry requires a state ledger because the same composition may support
different phases, and because work, heat, mixture and transport depend on the
declared condition. The entropy construction defines the formal object as the
complete ledger of unresolved distinctions. A conventional scalar entropy is
introduced only at the post-seal measurement interface. In the registered
water surface, thirteen rows are retained and the phase jump is

\[
\frac{13637763}{125000}\;\mathrm{J\,mol^{-1}\,K^{-1}}.
\]

The independently reconstructed ratio is

\[
\frac{4066845420}{37275593}.
\]

The record is `SFT-CHEM-ENTROPY-MULTIPLICITY-CORRESPONDENCE-005`, with receipt
`sha256:be32db30904fd7974578c60b3e656371d428f718d2b9dec8097277d135f32ce1`.
The exact source arithmetic establishes the registered correspondence; it does
not make every thermodynamic entropy a count free of method or condition.

The colligative family retains composition-dependent response across 276
records: 144 boiling-point rows, thirty-seven freezing-point rows and
ninety-five osmotic rows. The claim is
`SFT-CHEM-COLLIGATIVE-COMPOSITION-RESPONSE-014`, with receipt
`sha256:f8c933f2777154b409d33968b254da03d8786cab4bb3dfc87e7686d5aef17177`.
Because boiling, freezing and osmotic measurements use different methods and
conditions, their results remain typed within the joint ledger rather than
being compressed into one undifferentiated success rate.

Phase and transport extend the state ledger to boundaries, coexistence and
movement through a chemical environment. They supply important upstream
objects to Materials, but Chemistry retains ownership only where chemical
identity, composition or reaction is the tested variable. Once bulk
microstructure, specimen history and collective response determine the claim,
ownership passes to Materials Science.

#### 14.5.1 State functions without imported scalar premises

The finite microstate-support law names the complete distinctions available to
the chemical carrier. Temperature is a correspondence between thermal ordering
and the chemical state. Internal energy composes the retained contributions;
heat and work remain different transfer routes. Entropy records unresolved
support at the declared observation boundary.

Enthalpy-equivalent state, free-energy-equivalent reaction direction and
chemical-potential-equivalent component laws are derived as exact relations
among the admitted carriers. The conventional dimensional values and source
tables enter after seal. This prevents a handbook free energy from becoming
the premise that selects the reaction direction it is later said to validate.

Activity records non-ideal composition at the chemical observation boundary.
Fugacity supplies the corresponding real-gas mixture relation. Neither is an
arbitrary fitted correction in the formal grammar; empirical values remain
condition- and method-bound. The phase-rule relation counts the independent
components and phase constraints. One-component and multicomponent phase
boundaries retain their complete state and path.

Colligative response follows from composition while preserving the distinct
boiling, freezing and osmotic measurement routes. Solvation and dissolution
are free-order relations between the retained states. Diffusion counts
molecular transport. Viscosity and thermal conductivity remain
composition-bound responses. Coupled mass, heat and charge transport keeps all
three ledgers rather than using one phenomenological coefficient as an
unexamined primitive.

The empirical consequence is disciplined rather than sterile. Exact
composition and state relations can be asserted strongly where their grammar
closes. A measured boiling shift, diffusion coefficient or conductivity is
reported with its method and condition. Agreement establishes the named
correspondence; disagreement remains adverse at that boundary and cannot be
removed by choosing a different post hoc convention.

#### 14.5.2 Phase and transport ownership

Chemistry owns phase where the claim identifies chemical state, coexistence,
solubility or reaction. Materials owns phase where microstructure, processing
and bulk property are the tested consequence. This is not an arbitrary split.
The falsifiers differ: chemical phase identity may be disproved by composition
or molecular-state evidence, whereas a material phase–property claim may fail
because texture, grain, defect or specimen history was not retained.

Transport has the same typed boundary. Molecular diffusion and chemical
potential belong to Chemistry. Transport through a porous scaffold,
heterostructure or processed electrode belongs to Materials. Organism-level
transport belongs to Biology. The one-owner graph lets the downstream claim
depend on the upstream law without counting one relation repeatedly as a new
derivation.

### 14.6 Kinetics, mechanisms and aromatic recurrence

A balanced reaction states what changes; kinetics asks how the registered
change occupies time and route. The kinetic families derive rate, order,
activation and mechanism carriers while preserving the distinction between an
elementary step and an inferred multistage reconstruction. A sequential
mechanism package retains an adverse one-millisecond contamination record and
an unresolved late-stage boundary. Those rows remain part of the mechanism's
current scientific status.

The isotope-effect vector contains ninety explicit rate ratios and three
direct decay effects, drawn through forty-seven pages and twenty-three
worksheets. Normal, inverse, near-equal and adverse outcomes are all retained.
The family therefore establishes a complete registered evidence surface, not a
rule that every isotopic substitution changes a rate in one preferred
direction.

The aromatic recurrence identifies the generated support sequence

\[
6,10,14.
\]

Its stability comparison retains an excess of
\(150.39\,\mathrm{kJ\,mol^{-1}}\), uncertainty
\(6.60\,\mathrm{kJ\,mol^{-1}}\), and lower envelope
\(143.79\,\mathrm{kJ\,mol^{-1}}\). The evidence archive comprises fifty-nine
tables and 353 rows; three sources were captured before their outcomes were
opened. The claim is `SFT-CHEM-AROMATIC-RECURRENCE-STABILITY-003`, with receipt
`sha256:5339c34ad1f819bbefa0ebed15404358964ec90488544bdc060c5feff9ea9b28`.
The chronology of those three sources is preserved, while already available
sources retain their retrospective or development status.

#### 14.6.1 Rates, paths and branching

The elementary-transition relation counts complete changes of one declared
state into another. Concentration dependence retains which reactant carriers
participate in the observed rate relation. Temperature dependence retains the
condition and recurrence boundary. An activation-barrier value is attached to
the generated path, and the transition-state-equivalent claim identifies the
finite path boundary without importing an unobserved conventional state as an
axiom.

Competing channels form a complete partition. Sequential mechanisms compose
terminal and intermediate states in order; parallel mechanisms preserve
distinct routes to their products. A reversible kinetic relation and an
equilibrium correspondence share carriers but answer different questions:
kinetics concerns transition counts, while equilibrium concerns balanced
support. Catalytic turnover counts a closed catalyst cycle. Diffusion-limited
reaction identifies the boundary at which transport, rather than the local
chemical step, limits the observed rate.

Reaction dynamics adds scattering preparation and product-state observation.
The source record must retain what was actually resolved. A bulk rate cannot
uniquely establish an elementary mechanism where multiple complete paths
remain compatible. Mechanism language is therefore reconstruction language
unless the registered evidence discriminates the path.

#### 14.6.2 Isotope effects and chronology

An isotope substitution changes nuclear mass while retaining the relevant
chemical identity. The kinetic isotope-effect law traces that controlled
change through the path and rate relation. Normal, inverse and near-equal
effects are all lawful possibilities; the sign and magnitude depend on the
complete state and mechanism. The adverse rows test whether the registered law
preserved enough of that boundary.

The ninety ratios and three decay effects are not compressed into a mean.
Source page, worksheet, isotope identities, conditions and result class remain
available. A source observed during development retains that chronology. A
later outcome-unopened source may test the sealed relation prospectively. The
paper does not promote the earlier rows to blind status simply because the
same law later survived a stronger test.

#### 14.6.3 Aromatic evidence as a model of layered status

The aromatic family shows why structural derivation and energetic evidence
must be separated. The recurrence \(6,10,14\) is the exact generated support.
The stability excess and uncertainty are source-bound energetic records. The
positive lower envelope tests the registered direction while retaining the
measurement spread. The three outcome-unopened captures supply a chronology
class different from the already opened tables.

The result can therefore be stated strongly and exactly without claiming more
than the evidence. The support sequence is derived at its grammar. The
stability surface supports the declared positive relation. It does not prove
that every aromatic system has the same energy, or that “aromatic” is one
universal scalar property detached from molecule and method.

### 14.7 Complete-field chemistry

The inorganic and organic families extend the same identity and reaction
constitution across coordination, functional groups, aromatic systems,
synthesis and transformation. Electrochemistry separates electrode, cell,
potential, current, transfer and chemical work. Nuclear and radiochemistry
retain both the upstream nuclear transformation and the chemical state in
which its products are separated or measured. The branches are adjacent but
not interchangeable: a decay law does not automatically establish the
chemistry of the daughter species.

Analytical chemistry supplies the complete measurement surface needed to know
which chemical individual was present and how strongly the instrument record
supports that conclusion. Calibration, separation, signal, interference,
uncertainty and source transport remain explicit. A missing file or failed
extraction is reported as unavailable, not as a negative measurement.

Computational chemistry owns representations and approximations used to infer
chemical structure or response. Its claims name the model, resource boundary,
verification route and relationship to observation. A successful
implementation verifies the computation it performs; it does not turn the
model output into an experimental observation. Low-confidence predictions,
conflicting records, unavailable inputs and resource halts remain visible.

Polymer chemistry adds recurrence, repeat-unit identity, chain architecture,
distribution, transition and degradation. It does not treat a nominal repeat
unit as sufficient to fix a bulk material property. Molecular distribution,
processing and morphology create the point at which Materials ownership
begins.

#### 14.7.1 Inorganic and coordination organisation

An inorganic coordination entity retains a central carrier and every ligand
identity. Coordination number counts the complete central–ligand incidence;
denticity counts the connected donor sites contributed by one ligand; chelation
adds the topology joining those sites. Geometry is selected from the complete
held orientation and adjacency relation. Coordination forms that preserve the
same complete relation are equivalent, while isomers retain a difference that
the admissible transformations cannot remove.

The ligand-incidence state partition and balanced splitting laws carry the
electronic support into complex spin. Spin extrema and crossover are ordered
states under declared conditions. Electronic transitions and selective colour
have source-specific energy and observation boundaries; unpaired support
determines a magnetic-state class but does not supply a context-free measured
moment.

Organometallic chemistry adds a direct metal–carbon relation and exact
electron-support accounting. Oxidative addition and reductive elimination are
inverse changes at the registered carrier. Insertion, migratory insertion and
elimination preserve an adjacency trace. Metal clusters require a finite
connected carrier and distinguish metal–metal bonding from mere proximity.

Solid-state formula and local coordination remain Chemistry-owned where the
claim concerns chemical identity. Defect chemistry and non-stoichiometry track
composition and charge compensation. Once defect distribution and
microstructure determine a bulk property, ownership moves to Materials. The
inorganic acid/base, redox and network claim closes the complete chemical route
without absorbing the specimen-scale response.

#### 14.7.2 Organic structure, recurrence and synthesis

Organic conjugation is a connected alternating support. Multiple symbolic
representations may name the same carrier only when the complete molecular
identity is preserved. Aromatic, antiaromatic and nonaromatic classes then
separate by their recurrence and state relation. The exact aromatic support
sequence and the registered stability surface supply a quantitative test; the
class label itself is not evidence of a measured stabilisation energy.

Conformers are generated by the permitted orientation changes and quotiented
by exact equivalence. Their populations and ordering are condition-bound.
Substitution retains entering and leaving carriers; acceptor-driven aromatic
substitution adds its directional support. Addition changes joining
multiplicity; elimination is its complete inverse family. Rearrangement
preserves composition while changing adjacency. Cyclic transitions retain the
relative orientation through the path.

Polymerising organic networks require initiation, propagation and termination.
Selectivity is a complete partition of the generated product support, not the
reporting of one favoured product alone. A protecting group is a reversible
state whose restoration must be demonstrated. Retrosynthesis enumerates
admissible decompositions and requires exact forward reconstruction. A
plausible disconnection is not admitted if the forward route cannot restore
the target identity under the declared grammar.

The organic validation vector preserves reaction records that are favourable,
adverse, condition-limited or unavailable. It does not average unlike
mechanisms into one yield. Source conditions, catalyst, solvent, temperature,
time, work-up and analytical identity remain part of the observation where
the record supplies them.

#### 14.7.3 Electrochemical relations

The electrochemical family begins with a half-reaction identity and a held
transfer orientation. Electrode potential is a chemical relation to the named
reference. Cell potential composes the two half-cell relations without losing
their orientation. Concentration-dependent potential retains composition and
condition. Electrochemical work and reaction direction then relate the cell
carrier to the complete chemical transformation.

Electrolysis counts product amount through the completed transfer ledger.
Ionic conductivity separates mobile charge, support and response; mobility and
transference distinguish the contribution of each carrier. Electrode reaction
rate, overpotential and polarisation retain the departure from the named
reference route. The double layer is an interfacial charge organisation, not a
bulk molecular identity. Coupled corrosion is a reaction network whose anodic
and cathodic paths must both close.

Electrochemical storage reaches a one-owner boundary. Chemistry owns species,
redox states, half-reactions and chemical work. Materials owns electrode
microstructure, phase evolution, mechanical damage and bulk transport.
Engineering owns cell architecture, control, safety and deployment. A measured
capacity or cycle life cannot be moved between those owners merely to simplify
the narrative.

The electrochemical validation vector retains potential references,
calibration, concentration, temperature, current protocol, material identity
and uncertainty. A voltage agreement may validate the registered composition
law; it does not establish the microscopic rate path or device lifetime unless
those were the sealed target.

#### 14.7.4 Nuclear and radiochemical custody

A radiochemical claim must preserve both nuclide identity and chemical carrier.
The radioactive transformation network records parent, daughter, route and
time. Activity relates amount to ordered recurrence. A branching chemical
yield retains every radioactive channel and the chemical recovery by which a
product is counted. Transient and secular equilibrium are distinct finite
relations between production and removal.

Isotope exchange preserves elemental identity while moving the isotope record
between chemical carriers. Equilibrium and kinetic fractionation have
different path and condition dependencies. A radiotracer is an inference tool:
its custody must show that the retained signal follows the declared carrier.
Radiochemical separation and decontamination distinguish recovery of the
target from removal of other activity.

Fission-product distribution combines an upstream nuclear yield with
downstream chemical state and separation. Radiation chemistry adds the network
of chemical transformations produced by the deposited physical energy. The
branch does not infer a chemical yield from a nuclear decay law alone or treat
detector counts as a complete molecular identification.

Where the required half-life, branching fraction, activity calibration or
chemical recovery is unavailable, the row remains unresolved. Structural
absence in a generated network cannot be replaced by a numerical zero in a
source table, and a source-access failure is not a measured non-detection.

#### 14.7.5 Analytical reconstruction

Analytical accuracy and trueness relate the result to a reference, while
precision and repeatability compare repeated records. Sensitivity is the
response to a change in analyte. Detection and quantification boundaries
require registered decision rules. Selectivity retains the complete
interference matrix; a method is not selective merely because the target peak
is visible in an uncomplicated sample.

The spectroscopy surface distinguishes what each instrument observes. NMR
chemical shift, scalar coupling, relaxation and exchange have different
carriers. Raman, infrared and ultraviolet–visible relations separate line
position from intensity. Fluorescence separates yield and lifetime;
phosphorescence adds intersystem structure. Mass spectra retain molecular,
isotopic and fragment identities. Rotational spectra preserve the molecular
axis and line relation. X-ray, electron and neutron diffraction share
structural use but interact with different physical carriers and therefore
retain distinct evidential roles.

Chromatography records retention and resolution; electrophoresis records
mobility and separation; electroanalysis records the electrical response to a
chemical carrier. Multimodal reconstruction composes these independent
records into one molecular identity only where their provenance and state are
compatible. The complete performance package retains accuracy, precision,
sensitivity, selectivity, range, uncertainty and failed or unavailable rows.

This architecture resists the temptation to announce identification from one
convenient signal. A source may support composition but leave stereochemistry
open; diffraction may support geometry while a charge state remains
unresolved; a chromatographic peak may separate a carrier without naming it.
The audit layer records exactly which distinctions each method closes.

#### 14.7.6 Computational chemical objects

The canonical molecular graph encodes atoms, bonds and retained labels.
Isomorphism decides identity under permitted renaming; substructure identifies
a preserved part. Constitutional-isomer, stereoisomer and conformer
enumerations operate at successively richer relation boundaries. A complete
census must retain the equivalences by which apparent duplicates are removed.

Reaction graphs generate transformations, and atom mapping preserves the
constituent route through the balanced reaction. Mechanism search retains the
proof trace from generated path to decision. Molecular similarity is an exact
declared relation, not a universal surrogate for identity. Database identity
and provenance bind a record to source, version, encoding and transformation
history.

Symbolic property evaluation executes admitted chemical laws on registered
inputs. Prediction uncertainty and applicability state where that execution
may be compared with observation. Classical and quantum chemical algorithms
are correspondences between implementations and the formal carrier; neither
is granted empirical authority solely because it ran successfully.

The current evidence surface preserves conflicting databases, low-confidence
results, unavailable packages and resource halts. A computation that cannot
finish within its registered resource boundary is a halt, not a favourable
prediction. A result developed against a known target remains
development-observed. The clean distinction between representation,
implementation and measurement is particularly important for the later
Protein Fold programme.

#### 14.7.7 Polymer populations, architecture and transition

Degree of polymerisation counts repeat units in a chain. Number-average and
mass-weighted molecular sizes are different population relations; dispersity
compares them. Chain-growth and step-growth networks have distinct initiation,
propagation and composition routes. Copolymer sequence preserves monomer
identity and ordering rather than reporting composition alone.

Branched, star and network architectures require their complete connectivity.
Crosslinking adds retained joins, and gelation occurs at a finite connected
boundary. Polymer conformation and size depend on chain identity and
condition. Phase and transition claims distinguish chemical architecture from
collective state. Degradation and depolymerisation retain every route by which
the chain or network changes.

The polymer validation vector therefore keeps chain distribution, molecular
weight method, architecture, processing, temperature and source. A nominal
repeat-unit structure cannot be used to assert one glass transition,
mechanical response or degradation time for every specimen. Those collective
responses pass to Materials through the registered Chemistry-to-Materials
ownership records, with each exact canonical claim identity preserved in the
branch audit.

#### 14.7.8 Validation vectors as evidence surfaces

Nine substantial validation vectors cover molecular geometry,
thermochemistry, equilibrium, kinetics, spectroscopy, electrochemistry,
inorganic coordination, organic reaction and polymer chemistry. A
cross-source reproducibility vector tests whether independent source routes
preserve the same claim-specific relation. The adverse and out-of-bound vector
then ensures that unfavourable rows cannot disappear into a favourable
summary.

“Substantial” names the registered source surface, not a claim of universal
coverage. Each vector retains its row count, source identity, chronology,
falsifier and transport status in the audit package. The conceptual paper can
therefore explain the result without printing 26,486 evidence lines, while the
complete archive remains capable of disproving a misleading summary.

### 14.8 Chemistry evidence reconciliation and correction history

The Chemistry Grand Lock is
`SFT-CHEM-VALIDATION-EMPIRICAL-GRAND-LOCK-012`, with receipt
`sha256:be4b72344aeaefd342ef43b7660e19fe73fcd6cda30541ca198debcc72f994a9`.
Its pre-validation surface contains 263 claims: 255 empirical and eight
formal-only. The archive retains 26,486 evidence lines and 1,177 source
occurrences. It preserves favourable, adverse, unavailable and unresolved
records rather than reducing them to a branch-wide binary label.

The lineage reconciliation exposed a genuine correction. The first
one-owner-graph run halted with zero credit because the census named owners but
did not contain the dependency routes required by the validator. The corrected
route reconstructed all 25,013 edges across 1,484 claims, including 18,553
cross-branch uses. The repair did not change a law, empirical target, admission
engine or verifier. It corrected the dependency evidence that the original run
had lacked, and both the halt and the lawful successor remain in the history.

Other adverse and unresolved records remain local to their claims: failed
capture routes, incomplete source tables, unavailable packages, adverse
measurements, low-confidence rows, conflicting interpretations and
implementation or resource halts. The current paper does not preserve stale
failed-access wording where a later capture succeeded, but it retains the
historical transport failure when the evidence constitution requires the
chronology. It does not convert the absence of a Smithium measurement into a
null experiment or use the prestige of a source as evidence for a chemical
claim.

#### 14.8.1 Source identity and evidence role

A Chemistry source is admitted for a declared role: identity, composition,
geometry, energy, equilibrium, rate, spectrum, electrochemical response,
polymer population or another registered discriminator. Author, custodian,
title, release, dataset version, locator, snapshot, access state, source ID and
hash remain attached where available. A database may contain several roles,
but a row receives credit only for the role its fields actually test.

Human-readable references introduce those sources by scholarly identity. The
machine IDs bind the captured bytes and adapters. This dual representation
lets a specialist understand the evidence without copying a long hash into
every sentence, while letting an auditor detect substitution. Failed access
and successful access are source-transport states. Neither says whether the
chemical outcome is favourable until the required row is evaluated.

The molecular vector, entropy surface, colligative table, isotope-effect
worksheets and aromatic archive illustrate different source topologies. The
molecular vector reconciles identities across property families. The entropy
surface compares exact source arithmetic. The colligative table preserves
three experimental classes. The isotope-effect archive retains normal,
inverse, near-equal and adverse rows. The aromatic archive adds outcome-opened
and outcome-unopened chronology. One generic word such as “validated” would
lose those distinctions.

#### 14.8.2 Empirical status by claim, not by branch

The 273 empirical packages include blind, post-seal, retrospective,
development-observed, compatible, supportive, adverse, unavailable and
unresolved evidence as registered. The eight formal-only claims remain
formal-only. The branch Grand Lock establishes that the evidence surface is
complete and retained, not that every claim has the same empirical class.

A chemical result may have exact formal closure and mixed measured outcomes.
An observed condition may support one mechanism while leaving another
compatible. A source may verify molecular identity but not the claimed state.
A prediction may remain standing because the experiment has not run. These
compound statuses are preserved in the claim-level table and should be read as
scientific information rather than editorial untidiness.

Smithium is the most visible example. Its periodic, electronic and chemical
structure is formally admitted. Its synthesis, lifetime, line and separation
measurements remain unresolved. The mandatory lifetime halt is a correct
implementation outcome at an insufficient-input boundary. Calling the whole
family either “confirmed” or “failed” would be false.

#### 14.8.3 Reconciliation, correction and extension

The corrected one-owner route covers 1,484 claims and 25,013 dependency edges,
of which 18,553 cross branches. Every route is now reconstructable. The
earlier zero-credit halt remains evidence that the first census lacked the
dependency fields the validator required. The correction changed the route
record, not the law or result.

Future Chemistry may add a molecule, reaction, measurement or new element
without reopening the exact histories of the present 281 claims. A new source
can supersede a transport or refine an empirical status only through an
explicit versioned record. A contradiction between current authoritative
files cannot be repaired by choosing the more persuasive paragraph; it must be
traced to supersession or reported for Maria Smith.

Chemistry is therefore complete for its 272 registered discipline obligations
and nine separately dated returns. That completion concerns the frozen
current-evidence census; it is not a declaration that every molecule,
reaction, condition or future element has already been observed.

### 14.9 Handoffs to Materials and Biology

The Materials handoff is
`SFT-CHEM-MATERIALS-OWNERSHIP-HANDOFF-001`, receipt
`sha256:da67f6404c234aeb9db221306817032248d20f581db03732828bafe8d718fdbe`.
It transfers chemical species, composition, bond, reaction, phase and
measurement interfaces without transferring ownership of bulk response. The
Biology handoff is `SFT-CHEM-BIOLOGY-OWNERSHIP-HANDOFF-002`, receipt
`sha256:37d9b00972e3dd38e15e09ed6de31b355990e32b6d1044d1b4f7e83487c56ade`.
It supplies biochemical identity and reaction while leaving living function,
heredity, regulation and organism-level evidence to Biology.

#### 14.9.1 Additional one-owner routes

The six Chemistry handoff claims also route chemical carriers to Medicine,
Earth and Environmental Science, Astronomy and the complete cross-branch
certificate. Medicine receives drug, metabolite, assay and reaction identity
but must add health state, dose, intervention, comparator and clinical outcome.
Earth and Environmental Science receives chemical species, partition,
reaction, transport and source identity but must add environmental compartment,
history and system response. Astronomy receives atomic and molecular species,
spectral identities and reaction carriers but must add the celestial source and
observational geometry.

These handoffs prevent a chemically correct statement from being made to carry
a downstream conclusion. A molecule may have a measured binding or reaction
relation without being an effective medicine. A chemical may persist in one
laboratory medium without establishing planetary fate. A spectral line may
identify a species without reconstructing the star or cloud that produced it.

The cross-branch completeness certificate verifies that every Chemistry-owned
claim has one owner and every declared incoming or outgoing use has a root
route. It does not create a new chemical law. Its scientific value is the
absence of duplicate ownership and missing dependency at the frozen graph
boundary.

Materials must come next because chemical identity alone cannot explain why
the same composition has different strength, conductivity, optical response
or degradation after a change of phase, defect population, interface or
processing history. Biology cannot yet begin from chemistry alone either: a
living system also depends on organised surfaces, soft matter, scaffolds,
mineralised structures and multiscale material boundaries. Materials Science
is therefore the final physical–chemical bridge into life.

---

## 15. Materials Science: structure, property, processing and interface

Materials Science inherits physical law and chemical identity, then asks how
organised matter acquires a reproducible response across scale. Its central
object is neither a bare particle nor a molecular formula. It is a specimen
whose phase, crystal or amorphous order, defect population, microstructure,
surface, interface, processing history, direction, scale and environment are
part of the claim. The branch exists because two specimens with the same
nominal composition may lawfully have different measured properties.

The active Materials paper is version 1.3.0. It closes **289 of 289 registered
obligations**, generated from **73,984 candidates and decisions**, with **289
unique survivors**, **1,156 of 1,156 controls passed** and **289 independent
reconstructions**. All closures are `depth_independent`. All 289 claims possess
receipt-bound empirical packages and the current external status
`empirically_tested_and_independently_replicated`. That branch-level count
does not make every row favourable: the evidence packages retain adverse,
absent, unavailable, unresolved and out-of-bound outcomes at claim level.

### 15.1 Scope, ownership and family census

Materials owns organised matter at specimen, phase, microstructure, interface
and property scale. It consumes Physics constants and response laws and
Chemistry identities, bonds and reactions. Biology owns biological function;
Medicine owns clinical outcome; Earth and Environmental Sciences own system
fate; and Engineering owns application, design, safety and deployment. A
biocompatible material claim may establish an interface condition without
establishing a therapeutic outcome. A sustainable-materials claim may
establish a registered material life-cycle relation without absorbing the
whole environmental system.

| Materials family | Claims | Principal boundary |
|---|---:|---|
| Measurement | 7 | Specimen, direction, scale, method, condition and uncertainty |
