The original pre-source foundation contained eighty-four obligations and
21,504 candidates, sealed under
`sha256:da97a6cb6a001964a069b45a5a3698e7ea90f334a08d69c62bd09c46d8112035`;
the returned family added eight obligations and 2,048 candidates under
`sha256:0e8d7f14a7389b7ec44a37205ce2c9074db65f7b6ed5a466b97f2a01418ef331`.
The frozen current census has identity
`sha256:cfcafa0a3021dfdea20bbf026e48acfa0281ec14207e632c766bbcb846406e3b`.

#### 15.1.1 Material identity, specimen and sampling

A material system is the least complete constituent-and-relation carrier whose
composition, bonding organisation and declared scale remain distinguishable.
A specimen is a bounded realisation of that material system. Sampling is the
registered relation by which the specimen record is taken to represent a
larger batch, component or region. The distinction is indispensable: a law
about an ideal carrier, a measurement on one specimen and an inference about a
production population have different scopes.

Composition records the chemical constituents; phase identity records their
collective organisation; microstructure records the mesoscale arrangement;
and a material property is a response relation between the fully identified
state and a named perturbation. Materials uncertainty and metrological
traceability retain sample selection, preparation, instrument, calibration,
direction, scale, condition and uncertainty. If any of those coordinates is
required by the falsifier, it cannot be omitted from the evidence row.

This constitution explains why the branch does not derive one universal
modulus, conductivity or lifetime for a chemical formula. Those magnitudes are
relations of specimens. Exact derivation can still force which coordinates a
valid record must retain, which structural classes exist and how a response
must compose. The measured magnitude then tests the relation at the declared
boundary.

#### 15.1.2 Pre-source chronology and the single engine

The original eighty-four obligations and their 21,504 candidates were frozen
before NIST or BIPM source identities were selected. The later V1/V2 audit
identified eight nonduplicative Materials-owned omissions; their 2,048
candidates and complete prediction set were likewise frozen before the new
sources were selected. Those two seals establish chronology for the original
foundation. The complete-field expansion remains separately dated in the
current inventory.

Every Materials claim uses the unchanged admission engine. Registration checks
the single root, zero axioms, zero free parameters, existing dependency
receipts, source identity where applicable and exact candidate identity. The
engine then checks one decision per candidate, one survivor, minimality,
named-shape uniqueness, four adverse controls and an implementation-distinct
reconstruction. Empirical packages additionally require a verified derivation
seal, capability isolation, target absence before seal, matching custody
release, all-row retention, a falsifier and a tampered unfavourable control.

A failed field is not repaired by the manuscript. The engine preserves a
rejection or halt outside the admitted census. An editorial description may
make the relation easier to understand but cannot change the carrier,
candidate count, source chronology or receipt. This is particularly important
where the familiar material-class name tempts a writer to add a conventional
constitutive model that the exact grammar did not admit.

#### 15.1.3 Source surface and discriminators

Forty-two registered byte-frozen NIST and BIPM/JCGM records form the independent
external boundary of the original and successor seals. Each claim requires at
least two claim-specific discriminators. The predictor lacks filesystem,
network, clock, environment, subprocess, dynamic-import and foreign-function
capability. A distinct custodian verifies the snapshot hash and required
features before releasing the observation to the matching sealed predictor.

This protocol assigns sources narrow roles. A crystallography reference may
test the seven-system and fourteen-Bravais classifications. A metrology record
may test the required uncertainty or calibration structure. A specimen
datasheet can serve only if its provenance, method and conditions meet the
registered discriminator. It cannot prove a universal material law merely
because it comes from a respected producer or standard body.

Later complete-field families introduce additional source records. Their
transport state remains attached: direct capture, proxy capture, unavailable,
failed, unresolved or out of bound. A proxy is not renamed a direct capture.
A document that cannot be fetched is not a null measurement. A row that lacks
the property needed by the falsifier cannot receive favourable credit.

### 15.2 Exact crystal structure

The crystal derivation begins from the physical three-direction carrier. Each
direction has two orientations, giving exactly six nearest cubic neighbours:

\[
3\times2=6.
\]

The admissible periodic rotation orders are

\[
1,2,3,4,6.
\]

Five is the least excluded periodic order and therefore marks the first
quasicrystalline boundary rather than a failed crystal system. Length
equivalence supplies three classes; angle equivalence supplies four. Filtering
their twelve nominal combinations through the admitted metric relations leaves
seven crystal systems. Combining those systems with the five centring classes
and eliminating duplications or incompatible cases leaves fourteen Bravais
lattices.

These are exact structural counts, not fits to a crystallographic table. Their
principal claim records are:

- `SFT-MAT-CRYST-SYSTEMS-001`, receipt
  `sha256:8eea622f2a962a3e3b995693f3ba38acf8c0b79242bd30c1804eff03952d7637`;
- `SFT-MAT-CRYST-BRAVAIS-001`, receipt
  `sha256:c04968088f5382d095822785a3b505081f9a9027b7a8d206438deafdb6f93a52`;
- `SFT-MAT-CRYST-QUASICRYSTAL-001`, receipt
  `sha256:c397a36d1bb97f5bbab246e07e290b327c773ea18ec5c2b074d22794d7d1d094`;
  and
- `SFT-MAT-CRYST-PHONON-001`, receipt
  `sha256:574d1650178ebf71cddebdd5128c2d2e9837d02ab67443c66d787cfc246e5b6c`.

The acoustic result inherits the physical direction decomposition and closes
at three branches: one longitudinal and two transverse. A real dispersion
curve, velocity or attenuation coefficient remains material-, direction-,
mode-, temperature- and method-bound. The exact branch count does not replace
those measurements.

The crystallographic evidence layer retains both direct and proxy captures.
Failed captures remain explicit `MATERIALS_CRYS...HALT` records. A proxy may
support the declared structural boundary; it is not silently represented as
the direct source that failed to transport.

#### 15.2.1 Lattice, cell and translational order

A lattice is a generated adjacency organisation over the admitted spatial
carrier. The primitive unit cell is the least complete repeating carrier from
which the translational order reconstructs. Its identity is not a chosen
drawing convention: two cells are equivalent only where the permitted basis
change preserves the complete lattice relation. A crystal adds the material
basis to that periodic support.

Simple-cubic coordination follows directly from three independent directions
and two held orientations. Reciprocal support is then the exact relation used
to describe periodic diffraction. Diffraction itself remains a physical
measurement: wavelength, scattering carrier, specimen orientation, instrument
and intensity record enter after the structural support has been defined.
Quasicrystalline order preserves long-range organisation without the admitted
periodic translation. The least excluded order five marks that boundary.

The quasicrystal substitution family adds exact inflation under the registered
finite rule. It does not assert that every observed fivefold motif is a
quasicrystal. The evidence must distinguish local motif, periodic twin,
aperiodic long-range order and the actual diffraction or real-space record.

#### 15.2.2 Why the rotation orders stop at six

The rank-two integral rotation boundary admits only orders whose relevant
Euler-totient degree is no greater than two. The factor certificate is
depth-independent. If an odd prime \(p\) divides the order, \(p-1\) divides
the degree, leaving only the odd prime three. The remaining powers satisfy the
registered bounds \(a\leq2\), \(b\leq1\), with the simultaneous terminal case
excluded. The survivors are one, two, three, four and six.

This proof explains why five is excluded from periodic crystallography without
making fivefold organisation impossible. Five becomes the first aperiodic or
quasicrystalline boundary. The classification is therefore more informative
than a memorised list: it connects periodic admissibility and quasicrystalline
extension through one exact stopping certificate.

#### 15.2.3 Seven systems and fourteen Bravais classes

The rank-three metric grammar crosses three length-equivalence classes with
four angle-equivalence classes, producing twelve nominal forms. Rotation and
metric compatibility retain seven quotient classes. Those are the crystal
systems. The centring grammar then crosses the seven systems with five named
centring forms, giving thirty-five candidates. Basis equivalence and symmetry
compatibility reduce that complete product to fourteen distinct Bravais
classes.

The number fourteen is not obtained by consulting the observed table and then
designing a grammar of the same size. Candidate identities and eliminations
are retained in the claim packages. The post-seal crystallography record tests
the seven and fourteen classifications against its registered source. A
different conventional nomenclature may group labels differently, but it must
preserve the exact metric and centring relation before it can count as the same
classification.

#### 15.2.4 Diffraction, texture and non-ideal order

The complete-field crystallography family extends the ideal classifications to
measurement. Diffraction amplitude composes scattering contributions with
their retained phase, while intensity is the corresponding measured ledger.
A finite structure factor keeps every constituent and position. Polycrystal
texture is an orientation distribution, not a single-crystal direction copied
onto a bulk specimen.

Short-range order and diffuse scattering retain correlations that do not close
as a perfect lattice. Stacking faults alter the layer sequence and therefore
the diffraction relation. Twinning preserves the relation between domains.
Incommensurate and modulated structures require an additional organisation
whose period does not collapse into the base cell. Total-scattering
pair-distribution reconstruction infers real-space pair organisation from the
complete registered scattering record.

These claims preserve reconstruction status. A pair-distribution function is
an inference from source-bound scattering, not a direct photograph of every
atom. Texture, fault and domain models remain tied to their method and
resolution. The validation vector retains alternative fits, absent features,
failed transports and out-of-bound source rows.

#### 15.2.5 Acoustic, optical and thermal mode boundaries

One held propagation orientation in rank three separates displacement into one
parallel and two independent transverse directions. The acoustic branch count
is therefore three. Optical lattice modes and thermal mode limits require the
material basis, interaction and occupation boundary in addition to that
geometric count. A real dispersion surface can contain many branches when the
cell contains more than one constituent; the exact three refers to the
long-wavelength acoustic classes, not the total spectrum of every crystal.

Velocity, attenuation, frequency and occupation remain measured or
condition-bound. The structural mode count constrains those records but does
not supply their dimensional values. This precision is what allows the result
to hand physical wave structure into Materials without overstating the
measured response.

### 15.3 Defects, microstructure and phase

An ideal lattice cannot account for the response of a manufactured or natural
specimen. The next required carrier is departure from ideal order. Point,
line, surface and volume defects are distinguished by the part of the
organisation they alter. Their density, orientation and interaction then form
microstructure: grains, boundaries, inclusions, pores, dislocations and
interfaces whose collective organisation changes the measured response.

The multiscale family specifies how a record at one scale may be related to
another without pretending that every microscopic detail survives coarse
description. The preserving coordinates—specimen, scale, direction,
preparation, condition and method—remain attached to the result. A
microstructure source-capture halt and an independent extreme-label halt are
retained in the current evidence surface; neither is converted into an absent
phenomenon.

Phase science follows because organisation may change discontinuously or
coexist under controlled conditions. The branch distinguishes composition
from phase, equilibrium boundary from path, and phase identity from the
measured property used to detect it. Processing can therefore change a
specimen without changing its nominal chemical formula, and two specimens can
share a phase label while differing in grain size, defect structure or
texture. Those distinctions are what allow a later property claim to be
falsifiable rather than anecdotal.

#### 15.3.1 Point defects and constituent balance

A point defect is a local departure from the registered site identity. A
vacancy is structural absence at that site; it is not numerical zero inserted
into the proof. Interstitial and substitutional defects introduce a constituent
on a different retained relation. Defect populations are counted through site
fractions, with composition and charge balance preserved where required.

Migration is an ordered path between sites. Its empirical rate depends on
temperature, material, path and method, while the formal relation requires the
complete starting, intermediate and terminal states. Doping in a semiconductor
is a controlled substitution that changes carrier availability; it remains a
chemical and structural operation before its electronic response is measured.

Defect chemistry is shared with Chemistry through one-owner dependency.
Chemistry owns the constituent and charge-compensation identity. Materials
owns the spatial population, microstructural interaction and resulting bulk
property. One source row may be used by both branches only through that typed
dependency, not counted as two independent observations of the same claim.

#### 15.3.2 Dislocations, grains and interfaces

A dislocation is a line defect whose retained relation supports slip.
Dislocation reactions, climb and cross-slip change the line organisation under
declared carriers and conditions. Grain identity adds a region of common
orientation; a grain boundary is the interface between such regions. Curvature
can drive grain growth through a complete transfer ledger.

Segregation records constituent preference at an interface or boundary.
Precipitation distinguishes the new phase, its composition and its coherent or
incoherent relation to the matrix. Coarsening transfers material among
inclusions while conserving the retained carrier. Interface migration has its
own mobility record and driving relation.

Microstructure-to-bulk correspondence is therefore a multiscale
reconstruction. It must state which grain, defect, inclusion or interface
statistics survive the scale change and which response is inferred. A
micrograph alone is not the bulk property; a bulk property alone does not
uniquely reconstruct the microstructure.

#### 15.3.3 Phase fractions and coexistence

A complete multiphase ledger retains every phase identity and fraction. Tie
lines and lever partitions are exact composition relations at their registered
boundary. Component potential is handed from Chemistry into the phase
coexistence relation. The measured phase diagram remains source-, method- and
condition-bound.

Metastability is a retained local state whose path to another state is blocked
at the declared timescale or barrier. Spinodal-equivalent instability is a
different organisation: the state has no retained local barrier under the
registered perturbation. Displacive and martensitic transformations preserve a
coordinated relation; reconstructive transformations require bond or adjacency
reorganisation. Order–disorder transitions change retained arrangement without
necessarily changing composition.

Glass transition is a kinetic-arrest boundary, not a universal equilibrium
phase point. Its measured value depends on recurrence scale and history.
Transformation kinetics retain the complete time–temperature path. Thus the
same nominal composition may lawfully display different phase fractions and
properties after different cooling, deformation or annealing histories.

#### 15.3.4 From electronic structure to junctions

Electronic bands and structural gaps arise from the collective support of the
material carrier. Conductor, semiconductor and insulator classes are response
distinctions at the declared conditions. Electron and hole are complementary
carrier descriptions. Exclusion bounds occupation. Controlled substitution
changes carrier availability, producing p-type and n-type organisations.

Joining those organisations creates a p–n junction and its depletion region.
The rectification claim closes the direction-dependent response at the exact
registered boundary. Semiconductor transport then adds mobility,
concentration, scattering and condition. Interband optical transition
connects electronic support to a photon observation.

No conventional band diagram is treated as a premise merely because it is
standard. The exact carrier and its admissible relations must be rooted in the
upstream Physics and Chemistry receipts. Measured band gaps, mobilities and
junction curves enter through their source packages. A match to a rectifying
curve does not by itself identify every defect or interface state that produced
it.

#### 15.3.5 Coherent and topological states

A superconducting state is a coherent paired-carrier organisation. Its
dissipation-closed transport, flux exclusion, flux quantisation and weak-link
transfer are separately registered. A superfluid likewise has
dissipation-closed transport and quantised circulation. Topological materials
retain an invariant under the admitted transformations and a protected
bulk–boundary transport relation.

The empirical surface includes the superconducting isotope response, integer
and primary fractional Hall classes and exact boundary-mode count. The isotope
record is a measured response with material and condition custody, not a proof
of every microscopic mechanism. Hall classes test the registered topological
relation. A protected mode remains protected only within the stated disorder,
gap and boundary conditions.

Later complete-field claims add critical-field organisation, vortex matter and
pinning, coherence length, superfluid excitation and critical flow. Each is a
property of an identified specimen and state. The exact state classification
does not supply one universal transition temperature or critical field.

### 15.4 Mechanical, thermal, electronic, magnetic and optical response

The property families all use the same general relation: a fully identified
specimen and state are subjected to a named perturbation, and a method-bound
response is recorded. The shared grammar does not imply that all properties
are the same quantity.

Mechanical claims distinguish elastic return, plastic retention, strength,
fatigue, creep, fracture and toughness. Load mode, direction, geometry,
strain rate, temperature and preparation are part of the evidence. A modulus
is not a universal exact number generated from composition alone.

Thermal claims distinguish stored response, transport, expansion and phase
change. Electronic and ionic claims distinguish carriers, transport paths,
band or gap relations, junctions and environmental dependence. Magnetic and
superconducting claims retain order, field history, hysteresis, transition and
measurement protocol. Optical claims retain wavelength, direction,
polarisation, absorption, transmission and emission. Exact structural
relations may constrain these families, but conductivity, strength,
transition temperature, index and attenuation remain values of identified
specimens under identified methods and conditions.

This exact-versus-measured distinction is one of the branch's central results.
It prevents two opposite errors: fitting a structural law to a preferred
specimen value, and dismissing a genuine exact relation merely because real
specimens exhibit conditional variation. Formal structure and empirical
response meet through an explicit interface; neither replaces the other.

#### 15.4.1 Mechanical response and deformation history

Stress records transferred load over the declared support; strain records the
resulting change of shape or extent. Their tensor-resolved relation preserves
direction and load mode. Elastic recovery closes when removal of the
perturbation restores the retained state. Plastic deformation preserves a
changed state. Elastic modulus is a response ratio on the registered interval,
not a universal scalar of a chemical formula.

Transverse-to-longitudinal strain adds a directional comparison. Viscoelastic
response retains memory and recovery over time; viscoplastic flow retains both
rate and non-restoring change. A yield surface is a boundary in the complete
loading-path support. Work hardening preserves deformation history rather than
assigning the same state to a previously deformed and undeformed specimen.

Strength and hardness are distinct boundaries. Fracture energy and toughness
retain crack creation and propagation. Stable and unstable crack growth are
separate regimes. Fatigue distinguishes initiation from propagation under
cyclic loading; creep distinguishes mechanism and rupture-time custody under a
sustained condition. Impact and high-rate response require their recurrence
scale. A result measured slowly cannot be copied into the shock boundary.

Friction relates two contacting surfaces and their state. Lubrication adds an
intervening carrier; a tribofilm is a generated and retained interface.
Rheological response distinguishes flow and relaxation classes. Every
mechanical measurement therefore names geometry, orientation, rate,
temperature, preparation and method where those fields affect the falsifier.

#### 15.4.2 Thermal storage and transport

Heat capacity is excitation storage per temperature relation at the declared
state. Thermal conductivity records energy transport through the specimen,
while diffusivity composes conductivity with stored response and density.
Thermal expansion is an oriented change of extent. None is inferred solely
from the existence of lattice modes.

Interfacial thermal-boundary resistance belongs to the interface, not either
bulk constituent alone. Phonon scattering retains the complete mean-path
ledger. Radiative transport in a material retains absorption, emission and
geometry. Thermoelectric response is a coupled electrical and thermal
relation, with its performance boundary explicitly named rather than
presented as a context-free efficiency.

Phase-change storage distinguishes sensible storage from the retained phase
transition. Thermal shock combines gradient, constraint and mechanical
response; thermal fatigue adds recurrence and accumulated damage. The
validation vector keeps specimen, temperature range, direction, method and
uncertainty. A favourable conductivity row does not validate a different
microstructure or temperature regime.

#### 15.4.3 Electronic and ionic transport

Electrical conductivity and resistivity are reciprocal response relations
only at the compatible state and method boundary. Carrier mobility and
concentration are separated so that one measured conductivity does not
uniquely infer both. The Hall response provides a carrier-sign and density
ledger under its declared model and field. Dielectric permittivity and loss
retain frequency, direction and condition.

Ionic conductivity records mobile chemical carriers through organised matter;
transference partitions their contributions. Mixed ionic–electronic transport
retains both carrier classes. Tunnelling is a finite barrier relation.
Band alignment and interface offset belong to a pair of materials and their
interface; heterostructure confinement adds the resulting bounded carrier.
Defect and trap states preserve their structural source, while screening and
depletion retain the response length.

Electrochemical insertion changes the material state as a chemical carrier
enters or leaves. Chemistry owns the species and reaction; Materials owns the
phase, strain, defect and transport response. This distinction prevents a
successful half-cell reaction from being advertised as a complete electrode
material lifetime.

#### 15.4.4 Magnetic, superconducting and superfluid response

Ferromagnetic, antiferromagnetic and ferrimagnetic order differ by how local
magnetic carriers compose. Paramagnetic and diamagnetic responses are
perturbation-bound classes. Spin-glass freezing retains disorder and history.
Domains and walls add mesoscale organisation, and the hysteresis loop is a
complete path ledger rather than one magnetisation number.

Magnetocrystalline anisotropy attaches the response to crystal orientation.
Magnetoresistance retains electrical and field coordinates. Spin transport
and relaxation preserve preparation, path and decay of the held spin record.
Critical fields, vortex matter, pinning and coherence length describe distinct
boundaries of a superconducting specimen. Superfluid excitation and critical
flow likewise retain state and apparatus.

The branch does not infer a universal mechanism from a transition alone. A
measured critical temperature is condition- and specimen-bound. Flux
quantisation and topological class may be exact structural relations while the
observed plateau, field or transition remains empirical. The validation vector
keeps those evidence classes separate.

#### 15.4.5 Optical and photonic response

Optical absorption and extinction retain wavelength, path and material state.
Reflection and transmission close an energy ledger at the interface.
Luminescence separates generated excitation, emitted record and quantum yield;
the yield is source- and method-bound. Elastic and inelastic scattering retain
whether the material state changed.

Birefringence follows from optical anisotropy. Nonlinear mixing requires the
complete participating frequencies, orientations and material response.
Waveguides retain confinement and loss. A photonic band gap is an organised
propagation boundary; a defect mode is a permitted state inside that boundary.
Plasmonic response is collective. Exciton generation, transport and
recombination retain separate parts of the state trajectory.

An optical classification does not automatically establish device utility. A
measured emission line, propagation loss or yield must be tied to specimen,
preparation, direction, wavelength, excitation and detector. Engineering later
owns whether those relations satisfy an application requirement.

#### 15.4.6 Evidence across property families

The mechanical and tribological, thermal and transport, electronic/ionic and
dielectric, magnetic/superconducting/topological, and optical/photonic
validation vectors each preserve their own source rows. Cross-source
reproducibility asks whether independent records retain the same
structure–property relation. It does not require every specimen to have the
same magnitude.

An adverse row may show that a proposed universalisation fails while the
narrower condition-bound law remains. An absent feature is different from an
unavailable source. An out-of-bound measurement may be accurate but unable to
test the registered claim. The complete adverse vector retains all of these
states, which is why the branch-level empirical status cannot be read as
“every reported measurement agreed”.

### 15.5 Material classes, soft matter and nanoscale organisation

Material classes group recurring structure–property organisations without
making the class name the cause. Metals, ceramics, polymers, composites and
other classes are defined through their retained chemical, structural and
response relations. A polymer's Chemistry owner defines repeat units and
chemical architecture; Materials owns morphology, phase, orientation,
processing and bulk response. A composite requires constituent identities,
interface and spatial organisation, not merely an averaged composition.

Soft matter adds fluctuation, relaxation, viscoelasticity, colloidal relation,
gelation and self-organisation at condition-sensitive boundaries. The branch
retains the timescale and protocol under which an object appears solid-like or
fluid-like. Nanomaterials add size, confinement and surface-to-volume
organisation. “Nano” is not used as a universal property claim; the evidence
must name the dimension and the response that changes at that scale.

Surface and interface science is required wherever neighbouring materials do
not reduce to either bulk constituent. Adhesion, wetting, boundary energy and
interfacial transport depend on both sides of the boundary and on its
preparation. This interface carrier is a direct prerequisite for biomaterials
and for the Biology handoff.

#### 15.5.1 Metals, ceramics, polymers and composites

A metallic material retains collective electronic support together with its
crystal, defect and microstructural organisation. A ceramic material retains
its ionic or covalent chemical carrier and the corresponding structural
relations. A polymeric material retains the chemical chain architecture and
adds morphology, phase, orientation and bulk response. A composite contains
distinct constituents and the interfaces through which load, heat, charge or
damage transfers.

These classes are generated through their retained relations, not through a
list of familiar examples. Glass and amorphous organisation lack long-range
periodic order while preserving local structure. Porous materials add a void
network whose size, fraction, shape and connectivity affect the response. The
molecular-to-bulk correspondence states which chemical and physical
coordinates survive the scale change. Anisotropy retains direction. Size and
surface effects state when a boundary contribution is no longer negligible.

The complete-field class family then separates solid solutions and alloy
phases, ordered intermetallic compounds, compositionally complex alloys,
refractory and ultra-high-temperature classes, cementitious and concrete
composites, fibre- and particle-reinforced composites, metallic glasses,
structural and functional ceramics, thermoplastics, thermosets and elastomers,
graded materials and architected cellular materials.

Each class supplies a grammar for what must be retained. “Compositionally
complex” does not mean composition is irrelevant; “metallic glass” does not
mean crystal defects can be copied unaltered into the description;
“functionally graded” requires a spatially retained variation; and an
architected material requires geometry in addition to constituent identity.
Measured properties remain attached to the actual specimen and processing
route.

The water bulk-response ledger provides an explicit molecular-to-bulk case.
Hydrogen-bonded molecular identity is inherited from Chemistry, while the
collective response belongs to Materials. The source rows test the declared
bulk relation; they do not turn one water condition into a universal model of
all hydrogen-bonded materials.

#### 15.5.2 Soft matter and timescale

Colloidal stability retains particle identity, medium, interaction and
aggregation route. Gelation creates a percolated soft network. A foam retains
cells, films and drainage; an emulsion retains droplets and continuous phase.
Liquid crystals preserve orientational order without requiring full
translational order. Membranes and thin films add a bounded geometry whose
surface and transport cannot be treated as bulk.

Granular matter retains packing and force-chain support. Jamming is a boundary
between rearranging and mechanically retained organisation under the declared
load and recurrence scale. Responsive materials change state under a named
stimulus. Active materials consume a retained energy source and remain
nonequilibrium organisations; “active” is not permission to omit the energy
ledger.

Soft response is inseparable from timescale. The same specimen may be
solid-like on one observation interval and fluid-like on another. A complete
claim therefore retains recurrence, perturbation, recovery and history.
Classifying a material by one photograph or instantaneous modulus would lose
the relation the family is designed to preserve.

#### 15.5.3 Nanoscale confinement and collective state

A nanomaterial claim begins with a measured size or confinement coordinate.
Nanoparticles retain a size and shape distribution, not one nominal diameter.
Nanowires have one-dimensional confinement; two-dimensional materials retain
layer and stacking; quantum dots retain finite confinement in every spatial
direction. Surface-to-volume dominance is a relation between those exact
supports.

Nanoscale phase and melting boundaries remain material- and condition-bound.
Quantum materials are classified by their collective state, not simply their
small size. Moiré and twisted-layer structures add a superlattice relation.
Nanocomposites retain interface density. Aggregation and dispersion custody
must distinguish the primary particle from the state actually measured in the
medium.

The branch therefore avoids using “nano” as a promotional adjective. A source
must name which dimension lies in the registered range, which surface or
confinement relation changes and which observation discriminates it. A small
particle without a demonstrated scale-dependent relation remains a small
specimen, not evidence for every nanoscale law.

#### 15.5.4 Surfaces, interfaces and coatings

A surface free-state and energy relation arises because the boundary lacks the
same neighbourhood as the bulk. Wetting and contact angle retain the liquid,
solid, vapour, preparation and measurement geometry. Adhesion and work of
separation record the complete interface ledger. A coating adds a layer and
substrate whose individual identities and joining relation remain distinct.

Roughness is scale-bound: the numerical record changes with sampling length,
resolution and filtering. Surface reaction and catalysis are handed to and
from Chemistry through the identity and reaction carrier, while Materials owns
surface organisation and resulting response. Tribofilm formation retains the
generated interfacial layer; interface fracture and delamination retain the
path by which that relation fails.

An interface is not merely the average of its neighbouring bulk phases. It can
carry a distinct composition, structure, energy and transport relation. This
is why interface custody recurs in electronics, composites, corrosion,
biomaterials and processing. The one-owner graph permits those uses while
preventing five different branch sections from claiming to derive the same
primitive interface.

### 15.6 Biomaterials and the boundary of living function

The biomaterials family tests material conditions at a biological interface
without taking ownership of life or health. Biocompatibility is therefore a
typed material–biological relation, not a claim that a material is universally
safe or therapeutically effective. Its current record is
`SFT-MAT-BIO-BIOCOMPATIBILITY-INTERFACE-001`, with receipt
`sha256:a10aa7bcb625a83b2d3ebf9dd8e4ff28df0ae2d5d74ab13268cb4ef711b8bd2f`.

Scaffold organisation separates porosity from connectivity and both from a
later biological outcome. The registered scaffold claim is
`SFT-MAT-BIO-SCAFFOLD-POROSITY-CONNECTIVITY-003`, receipt
`sha256:1fe3a1e81c362902e3c4071d0e9dde9d04e9361616fa13f22c91314c8744f559`.
Mineralised organisation similarly identifies the material architecture
without claiming the developmental process that builds it. Its record is
`SFT-MAT-BIO-MINERALIZED-ORGANIZATION-007`, receipt
`sha256:bd00986cced114e52724965d3b2775686ee13a2353b6a88760f34404a1b4bd07`.

Those distinctions make the downstream handoff scientifically clean. Biology
may consume an interface, scaffold or mineralised structure, but it must add
function, regulation, reproduction, development and organismal evidence. A
medical claim must add clinical population, intervention, comparator and
outcome. Neither can be inferred from a favourable materials test alone.

#### 15.6.1 Bioresorption, adhesion and controlled transport

Bioresorption is a material degradation ledger in a biological environment.
It requires the initial material, degradation route, products, transport and
remaining structure. The biological response to those products is a Biology
or Medicine question. A materials record may establish that mass or structure
changes under the declared medium; it cannot by itself establish safety.

Cell–material adhesion is a handoff relation. Materials owns surface chemistry,
roughness, stiffness and the interfacial state. Biology owns cell identity,
attachment machinery and function. Mechanical matching likewise compares a
material response with a biological tissue boundary without declaring that the
match produces healing.

Controlled release is material transport across a designed boundary. The
carrier identity and chemical stability come from Chemistry; pore, phase,
degradation and diffusion organisation belong to Materials; biological or
therapeutic action belongs downstream. A release curve is therefore not a
clinical outcome.

Biologically derived and biofabricated material identity retains provenance
and processing. The fact that a constituent came from a living source does not
make its current material property biologically owned. Conversely, a scaffold
manufactured without living input may later participate in a biological system
without acquiring function at the Materials proof boundary.

#### 15.6.2 What the Biology handoff preserves

The handoff preserves porosity, connectivity, interface, transport,
mechanical response, mineralised organisation and source custody. It also
preserves the limitations of every material row: specimen, preparation,
condition, method, uncertainty and adverse state. Biology receives those
records as inputs and cannot rewrite them as biological axioms or omit an
unfavourable interface merely because a later cellular result is positive.

The scientific opportunity lies precisely in this clean boundary. A living
system can be studied as a new organisation acting through exact chemical and
material carriers, while its function and history remain genuinely biological.
Reduction to chemistry would lose that organisation; separation from
chemistry would lose the dependency route.

### 15.7 Processing, degradation, computation and extreme conditions

Processing supplies the path by which chemical constituents and physical
conditions become a specimen. The complete relation is process → structure →
property, with intermediate states retained where the evidence permits. A
heat treatment, deposition, deformation or synthesis route can change phase,
grain, defect, texture or interface and thereby change response. Reproducible
materials science therefore requires the path, not only the final label.

Degradation is the time-dependent inverse problem. Corrosion, wear, chemical
attack, fatigue and environmental damage alter the retained organisation.
Lifetime is a conditional reconstruction or measurement, not an intrinsic
number detached from load, environment and method. Missing exposure data or a
failed source capture remains unresolved or unavailable, not a favourable
durability result.

Computational materials claims preserve representation, model, approximation,
scale, resource and validation. Simulation may reconstruct a mechanism or
predict a registered response; it becomes empirical evidence only through the
declared comparison. Test success establishes implementation identity at the
tested boundary, not physical confirmation. Resource halts and low-confidence
outputs remain in the audit surface.

Extreme-condition claims retain the high- or low-temperature, pressure, field
or rate boundary at which a response was measured. An independently generated
label that cannot be resolved from the current source remains a halt rather
than being harmonised by prose. Sustainable-material claims similarly state
their bounded resource, recovery, durability or life-cycle relation. They do
not absorb Earth-system consequences or Engineering deployment decisions.

#### 15.7.1 Degradation as a complete change ledger

Oxidation-scale growth composes chemical reaction with transport through the
new layer. Corrosion rate retains the coupled electrochemical path, specimen
area, exposure and time. Passivation is the formation of a protective state;
film breakdown is a distinct boundary. Stress-corrosion cracking composes a
mechanical crack path with the corrosive environment rather than attributing
failure to either in isolation.

Hydrogen uptake and embrittlement retain ingress, distribution,
microstructural interaction and mechanical consequence. Abrasive, adhesive and
erosive wear are different transfer relations. Radiation damage accumulates
defects and may also permit recovery. Physical ageing records drift in a
retained state without requiring a change of chemical identity. Environmental
attack and weathering preserve the complete exposure condition.

Service life and failure time are evidence boundaries. A predicted lifetime
is a reconstruction from a named load and environment; an observed failure is
a source-bound event; a censored specimen that has not failed by the end of a
test is neither an infinite lifetime nor a null degradation result. Those
statuses must remain visible in the complete table.

The degradation family therefore carries favourable durability results and
early failures under one constitution. A later coating or processing change
may lawfully create a new specimen and new status, but it does not delete the
adverse history of the earlier state.

#### 15.7.2 Processing as the construction of a specimen

Casting retains the mould-filling and solidification history. Thermomechanical
forming adds deformation and texture. Machining creates a surface and possible
damage state. Additive manufacture retains every layer, melt-pool relation and
build history required by the claim. Thin-film deposition records incoming
material and growth; epitaxy adds lattice matching and orientation.

Welding, brazing and joining create an interface whose thermal, chemical and
mechanical histories differ from either parent material. Polymer processing
retains flow and orientation. Powder processing retains particle identity,
packing and compaction. Heat treatment, sintering and densification already
introduced in the foundation are composed into the complete process–structure
route.

A process window is a source-bound region of conditions that produces the
registered state with the stated reproducibility. Provenance includes machine,
feedstock, geometry, atmosphere, calibration and post-processing where the
falsifier requires them. A nominal process name cannot substitute for those
coordinates.

Processing evidence is not merely procedural detail. If two specimens have
different histories, treating them as one observation can conceal the actual
structure–property relation. Conversely, a reproducible path can explain why
different laboratories obtain compatible response without asserting that
every uncontrolled specimen must agree.

#### 15.7.3 Computational representation and forward simulation

An exact material-structure representation identifies constituents, sites,
relations, phase, boundary and scale. A structure–property computation names
the law by which that input produces a response. A finite numerical simulation
adds discretisation, update rule, termination and error. Multiscale composition
specifies how outputs and uncertainties cross scales.

Numerical stability and error propagation remain implementation evidence.
Passing a convergence test verifies the behaviour of the registered
discretisation; it does not prove that the underlying physical model describes
the specimen. The inverse materials problem reverses the relation from target
response to candidate structure and must retain non-uniqueness. Machine-learning
inference adds training provenance, representation, applicability and
uncertainty. A pretrained model is not an admissible proof premise.

Materials databases require identity and provenance. Two rows can be compared
only where material, phase, processing, specimen, direction, method and
condition are compatible. Phase-field, molecular-dynamics and
electronic-structure correspondences each operate on a different carrier and
scale. Their outputs cannot be merged as if they were three measurements of
one quantity.

Simulation-to-experiment validation is separately registered. The sealed
simulation, input state, comparison metric and complete observation rows must
be retained. A model may be useful and compatible without being uniquely
confirmed. A failed run, missing potential, unavailable input or resource halt
remains in the archive.

#### 15.7.4 Extreme conditions and path custody

High pressure, high temperature and cryogenic response each define a material
state under a named boundary. High electric and magnetic fields add direction,
magnitude and exposure. High strain rate and shock add a short recurrence
scale and propagation path. Extreme radiation adds source, dose, spectrum and
recovery. Combined extremes require the complete order in which conditions
were applied.

Path custody matters because material state can be hysteretic or metastable.
A specimen compressed and then heated may not equal one heated and then
compressed. A cryogenic value cannot be extended to room temperature by
silence. A shock response cannot be inferred from a quasi-static curve without
the registered correspondence.

The independent extreme-label halt records a source whose current information
does not resolve the required generated class. The correct scientific action
is to preserve the halt. Choosing the closest familiar label would manufacture
agreement and destroy the falsifier.

#### 15.7.5 Sustainability and bounded system claims

Embodied material and energy ledgers retain the inputs required to produce the
declared specimen. Critical-material availability is a source- and
time-dependent boundary. Reuse and remanufacture preserve enough identity and
function for a further cycle; recycling separates and recovers constituents;
yield retains every residual route.

A circular material flow is a network ledger, not the claim that no material
leaves the system. Durability and life extension compare complete service
histories. Material toxicity is handed to Medicine and Environmental Science
for biological and system outcomes. Substitution preserves a declared
function while changing material identity. End-of-life fate retains recovered,
dispersed, stored and unresolved residues.

These claims are deliberately bounded. Materials Science can derive and test
the material flow, durability or recovery relation. Earth and Environmental
Sciences own the wider ecosystem and planetary consequence. Engineering owns
whether a design meets safety and function. Economics and Social Systems own
institutions, incentives and access. A favourable recycling yield cannot be
promoted into a complete sustainability judgement without those downstream
relations.

#### 15.7.6 Processing, class and sustainability evidence

The material-class and processing validation vector tests whether the retained
identity and path distinguish the registered responses. The cross-source
vector then asks whether compatible independent records reconstruct the same
relation. Reproducibility is not numerical sameness stripped of uncertainty;
it is agreement under the declared specimen and method coordinates.

Adverse, absent and out-of-bound outcomes remain scientifically informative.
An adverse process result may falsify a claimed path. An absent phase may be a
completed negative observation. An unavailable process log is a provenance
failure. An out-of-bound source may report a different material or condition.
The Grand Lock retains all four instead of treating them as interchangeable
failures or silently excluding them.

### 15.8 Materials evidence reconciliation

The pre-validation surface contains 271 claims, 1,040 evidence lines and 334
source occurrences. The complete one-owner graph contains 1,681 predecessor
claims and 28,437 edges, including 19,852 cross-branch uses. Every current
route traces to the root under reconciliation identity
`sha256:4d2a8f47c39d945fbe31a8de2c78d2dc66462db7c4773f1a785b8b139d2c148b`.
Forty-two byte-frozen NIST and BIPM records support the registered base source
surface; later complete-field families retain their additional source records
and transport states.

The Materials Grand Lock is
`SFT-MAT-VALIDATION-EMPIRICAL-GRAND-LOCK-012`, with receipt
`sha256:dbb75828d061c43824bfad454e100d0502d76b5ebc3ee74e9dd03cbcd8967268`.
It retains favourable, adverse, absent, unavailable, unresolved and
out-of-bound rows across all 271 pre-validation claims. Failed direct and
proxy crystallographic captures, the microstructure capture halt, unavailable
source rows and the extreme-label halt remain visible. A later successful
transport can update the present access state while preserving the historical
failure required by the evidence chronology.

Historical step 291 contained astronomy content. The current ownership audit
routes it to Astronomy rather than enlarging Materials by inheritance. That
correction preserves the scientific content while refusing duplicate or
convenient ownership. The same boundary prevents the materials paper from
claiming biological function, clinical outcome, environmental fate or
engineering deployment.

#### 15.8.1 Validation-vector architecture

The twelve validation claims are not duplicates of the 271 scientific claims.
They assemble typed evidence surfaces. Crystallography and diffraction,
defects and microstructure, phase and transformation, mechanical and
tribological response, thermal and transport response, electronic/ionic and
dielectric response, magnetic/superconducting/topological response,
optical/photonic response, and material-class and processing response each have
their own vector. Cross-source reproducibility composes compatible independent
records. The complete adverse vector preserves everything that cannot receive
favourable credit. The Grand Lock proves that every pre-validation claim is
represented.

This architecture answers a difficult editorial problem. Printing the same
generic source-custody language 271 times would make the scientific argument
unreadable. Omitting the rows would make it unauditable. The vectors permit a
compact conceptual account while the machine packages retain every source
identity, discriminator, value, status and receipt.

The vector status is not a vote. One favourable source does not overrule a
discriminating adverse source, and ten compatible rows do not automatically
confirm a non-unique relation. Each row retains its evidence class. The claim
status follows the registered protocol and falsifier.

#### 15.8.2 Source transport and historical state

Source transport is itself versioned. A direct byte-frozen capture supplies
content identity. A proxy can support only the features it actually preserves.
A failed access records that the route failed at the recorded time; it does not
establish that the source or phenomenon is absent. An unavailable row lacks the
required record. An unresolved row contains evidence that the current
classifier cannot lawfully decide.

If transport later succeeds, the current access field may change. The
historical failure remains where chronology requires it. If a newer dataset
supersedes an older source, the manifest must state the supersession and retain
the old identity. A numerical value is not silently replaced merely because a
later handbook reports a cleaner number.

The crystallographic capture halts, microstructure halt and extreme-label halt
are therefore part of the publication, not embarrassing debris. They show
where evidence is incomplete and specify what a future source must supply.
That transparency makes a later lawful resolution stronger because the test
was visible in advance.

#### 15.8.3 One-owner reconstruction

The Materials dependency graph traverses 1,681 predecessor claims and 28,437
edges. Nineteen thousand eight hundred and fifty-two edges cross branch
boundaries. A cross-branch use says that the Materials result requires an
upstream admitted object; it does not transfer the object's ownership.

The graph is independently reconstructed from materialised registrations.
Every one of the 289 current claims reaches
`SFT-ROOT-THERE-IS-NO-NOTHING`. A branch label alone is insufficient: the
route must name the actual dependency receipt. This is why the correction to
historical step 291 matters. Its planetary and Tully–Fisher content belongs to
Astronomy and Cosmology. Routing it there preserves the question and evidence
while preventing Materials from claiming closure by absorbing another
branch's work.

The handoff claims to Engineering, Biology, Medicine, Earth and Environmental
Science and Astronomy make the outgoing boundary equally explicit. They
supply material carriers and evidence coordinates. They do not derive an
application, organism, clinical result, planetary fate or astronomical
system.

#### 15.8.4 Limitations retained inside completion

All 289 claim packages and empirical packages close at the frozen boundary,
but new materials and conditions remain possible. A newly synthesised phase,
an improved microscopy method or a resolved transport can extend the branch.
Such an extension must receive a new registration and version; it cannot be
inserted retroactively into a sealed claim.

The branch also retains conventional-model boundaries. Exact classification
does not mean every constitutive coefficient is derivable without specimen
input. A simulation can be verified without being uniquely validated. A
condition-bound measured law may be robust without being universal. A
favourable biomaterial interface does not establish health benefit. These are
not rhetorical disclaimers appended to weaken the paper; they are coordinates
of the scientific claims themselves.

Current full-field completion therefore means that all 289 registered
Materials obligations close with their declared candidates, controls,
dependencies, evidence packages and reconstructions. It does not mean that
every possible material, preparation, direction, scale or condition has been
measured. Exact crystal counts and mode counts remain exact; measured moduli,
conductivities, strengths, transition temperatures and lifetimes remain
specimen- and protocol-bound.

### 15.9 Handoff to Biology

The formal Biology handoff is
`SFT-MAT-BIOLOGY-OWNERSHIP-HANDOFF-002`, with receipt
`sha256:f24161d22e77e070e6bd4b052173fe4072996c01e3a29c7755c1dfa8d3252293`.
Together with Chemistry's biochemical handoff, it supplies Biology with
physical quantities, chemical identities and reactions, phase and transport,
soft and interfacial organisation, scaffold and mineralised structures,
measurement custody and a complete one-owner dependency route.

#### 15.9.1 Engineering, Medicine, Earth and Astronomy routes

The Engineering handoff supplies material identity, property, uncertainty,
processing history, degradation and failure boundaries. Engineering must add a
specified function, load case, architecture, control, safety margin,
reliability and deployment decision. A material with a high measured strength
does not by itself establish that a component made from it is safe.

The Medicine handoff supplies a material–biological interface, degradation or
release carrier and every adverse material row. Medicine must add patient or
population, indication, intervention, comparator, dose, follow-up and outcome.
Biocompatibility at one assay boundary is not clinical safety. Bioresorption is
not therapeutic efficacy. The handoff preserves those distinctions so that a
later medical claim cannot cite the Materials receipt at a stronger level than
it earned.

Earth and Environmental Science receives material flow, degradation, toxicity
handoff, durability, recycling and end-of-life carriers. The downstream branch
adds environmental compartment, transport, ecological interaction and system
history. A bounded recycling yield or weathering test cannot establish global
environmental benefit without those relations.

Astronomy receives material-state, phase, optical, thermal, high-pressure and
extreme-condition carriers where they are needed to interpret celestial
matter. It must add the astronomical source, distance, observation and system
model. Historical step 291 is routed there precisely because planetary and
galactic evidence cannot be owned by Materials simply because matter is
present.

#### 15.9.2 Handoff evidence does not duplicate evidence

A downstream claim may cite an upstream source through its receipt, but that
use is not an independent replication of the upstream observation. Independent
replication requires a distinct implementation or source route under the
registered protocol. The one-owner graph counts dependency edges, not extra
confirmations.

Corrections also travel forward. If an upstream source is superseded or a
status becomes adverse, dependent claims must be audited for impact. They do
not automatically fail if their relation did not use the changed coordinate,
and they do not automatically survive if it did. The explicit dependency route
makes that impact analysis possible.

What remains unresolved at this boundary is the defining problem of life.
Neither a molecule nor a material specimen explains why a bounded organisation
maintains itself, uses inherited information, regulates competing processes,
develops, reproduces or evolves. Biology must introduce those relations and
their evidence without taking physical constants, chemical reactions or
material interfaces back as new biological axioms. The next part therefore
begins with the living carrier and the distinction between organised matter
and organisation that acts to preserve and reproduce its own conditions.

---

## Part II synthesis: the physical–chemical dependency bridge

Physics, Chemistry and Materials Science contribute **938 live claims**,
**403,696 registered candidates**, **938 unique survivors** and **3,752 passed
controls** to the current V3 corpus. The aggregate is useful only because its
members remain typed. Physics contributes 368 current categorical claims;
Chemistry contributes 272 frozen obligations plus nine returned claims; and
Materials contributes 289 frozen obligations. Their evidence classifications
are not made uniform for presentation: Physics separates 238 empirical
validations from 130 formal or independently replicated claims, Chemistry
separates 273 empirical packages from eight formal-only claims, and Materials
retains 289 receipt-bound empirical packages with claim-level adverse and
unresolved rows.

### Authoritative freeze for this part

This chapter was reconstructed from the active papers and current repository
records, not from remembered summaries or superseded drafts. The active
manuscript identities checked at the editorial freeze are:

| Authority | Repository path | SHA-256 |
|---|---|---|

The active-paper hashes identify the scientific prose assessed here. Branch
manifests and claim receipts bind the underlying release and machine packages.
The roadmap hashes identify current dependency and completion guidance. The
global ledger supplies the theory-wide owner and root routes. If any of these
files changes before release, the chapter must be audited again rather than
assuming that the change was editorial.

The aggregate arithmetic is independently transparent:

\[
368+281+289=938\quad\text{live claims},
\]

\[
257{,}776+71{,}936+73{,}984=403{,}696\quad\text{candidates},
\]

\[
1{,}472+1{,}124+1{,}156=3{,}752\quad\text{passed controls}.
\]

The sums do not erase branch status. Chemistry's 281 is 272 frozen discipline
obligations plus nine returned claims. The empirical package counts remain
238/130 by type in Physics, 273/eight by type in Chemistry and 289
receipt-bound packages in Materials.

### How this part can change

A lawful correction begins with a discrepancy against one of the current
authorities. If the active paper, inventory and machine record disagree, the
records are traced to their source and explicit supersession. A clerical
identity can be corrected when the authoritative direction is unambiguous. A
scientific disagreement—different count, value, chronology, evidence class or
owner—must remain visible for Maria Smith unless the repository itself
authorises the resolution.

A lawful extension registers a new question, candidate grammar, dependency
route, controls, evidence boundary and receipt. It may add a new physical
measurement, chemical species, material class or downstream use. It may not
change a failed historical row into success, call a new source an old blind
target, or insert a measured value into the earlier proof grammar.

Falsification is likewise local and exact. A disjoint CODATA interval would be
adverse to the registered fine-structure empirical claim. A qualifying
Smithium experiment that fails the sealed identification protocol would be
adverse to that protocol; no experiment leaves the status unresolved. A
crystallographic classification outside the complete registered grammar would
challenge the relevant Materials claim. The wider corpus would then require a
dependency impact audit rather than a rhetorical declaration that everything
either survives or collapses.

The dependency relation among the branches is now explicit:

1. Physics turns exact formal carriers into quantities, dimensions, fields,
   interactions, constants and physical measurement interfaces.
2. Chemistry uses those carriers to derive stable chemical identity,
   composition, bond, reaction, periodic extension and source-bound molecular
   evidence.
3. Materials Science organises physical and chemical carriers across phase,
   defect, microstructure, surface, process and scale to derive
   structure–property relations.
4. Biology receives the resulting biochemical and material interfaces but
   must derive living organisation in its own right.

### Three worked dependency routes

The fine-structure route shows how an upstream exact carrier becomes a
measured physical constant and then a downstream constraint. Foundation
supplies binary distinction and reversible return; Mathematics supplies exact
finite ratios and order; Information Science supplies immutable record and
custody; Computation supplies enumeration and checking; Reversible and Quantum
Computation supplies the reversible process boundary; Physics supplies the
three-generator, least covers, ladder and exact \(\alpha\). Chemistry then uses
the resulting atomic boundary without re-deriving \(\alpha\), and Materials
uses the chemical and physical carriers in its electronic and optical response
claims. One law is depended upon many times but owned once.

The Smithium route begins with the Physics nuclear closure
\((126,184)\), orbital capacity and fine-structure endpoint. Chemistry adds
elemental identity, electronic configuration, entrance-partition grammar,
decay coordinates, ion and oxidation relations, transition classes, chemical
states and identification protocol. The empirical status remains a standing
prediction because the required synthesis and identification observations are
not present. Materials could later study a Smithium phase or specimen only
after Chemistry supplies the observed chemical carrier; it cannot manufacture
that observation from a formal materials model.

The biomaterial route begins with physical quantity, field, transport and
mechanical response; adds chemical species, bond, reaction, solvent and polymer
identity; then adds material phase, pore connectivity, surface, interface,
degradation and scaffold organisation. Biology receives those carriers and
must add cell, function, regulation, development and living-system evidence.
Medicine must further add clinical population, intervention, comparator and
outcome. A favourable early interface result cannot leap over those dependency
boundaries.

### One object, different scientific coordinates

| Object encountered downstream | Physics coordinate | Chemistry coordinate | Materials coordinate | Boundary handed onward |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Atom or ion | Physical bound state, shell, transition and charge | Element, isotope, ion and chemical role | Constituent in phase, lattice, defect or interface | Biological or device carrier only after further organisation |
| Energy transfer | Work, heat, radiation and conserved physical ledger | Reaction, activation, thermochemical and electrochemical route | Stored, transported, dissipated or damage-producing specimen response | Metabolic, environmental or engineered use remains downstream |
| Phase | Collective physical state boundary | Chemical state and coexistence identity | Specimen phase, fraction, microstructure and path | Biological compartment or Earth-system phase needs its owner |
| Spectrum | Physical transition and detector record | Molecular/chemical identity and selection rule | Band, mode, defect, optical or structural response | Instrument or application claim remains Engineering-owned |
| Interface | Physical boundary and transferred response | Chemical adsorption, reaction and constituent transfer | Surface, adhesion, segregation, coating and interfacial property | Cell response, clinical safety and system fate remain downstream |
| Prediction | Sealed physical consequence and measurement adapter | Sealed identity, reaction or property consequence | Sealed specimen relation under complete coordinates | Later branches must preserve the original chronology |

The table makes reduction precise. A later coordinate does not contradict the
earlier carrier; it adds a relation that the earlier branch did not own. The
material phase depends on physical and chemical phase but includes specimen
history and microstructure. A biological membrane depends on a material
interface but adds function and maintenance. The dependency spine is therefore
neither a collection of independent essays nor a claim that one vocabulary can
replace every scale.

### Exact structure and empirical magnitude

Across the three branches, exactness has a consistent meaning. The
fine-structure fraction, cover depths, nuclear closures, periodic capacities,
entrance-partition count, rotation orders, crystal systems, Bravais classes and
acoustic branch count are exact at their registered grammars. Their exactness
does not depend on a displayed decimal or sample mean.

Measured values have a different completeness. A CODATA interval is complete
for the named release and uncertainty statement. A molecular property is
complete only with species, state, method and condition. A material modulus is
complete only with specimen, direction, preparation, scale, method and
condition. Exact rational interval arithmetic can preserve those records
without promoting them into universal proof values.

This separation also supports persuasion. The paper can state that the exact
\(\alpha^{-1}\) lies inside the registered CODATA interval, that the aromatic
support sequence is generated and its energy surface is positive at the
registered boundary, and that seven crystal systems and fourteen Bravais
classes survive complete enumeration. It need not weaken those results with
generic doubt. It must, however, preserve the different chronology and
evidence class that makes each statement true.

### Adverse and unresolved evidence through the spine

Adverse evidence is inherited as a constraint, not discarded at a handoff.
Physics carries the electroweak all-input tension, historical proton-radius
mismatch, unavailable driven vacuum/inertia test, resonance losses and standing
unobserved sectors. Chemistry carries failed source routes, unavailable and
adverse molecular rows, the mechanism contamination record, unresolved
Smithium measurements and the corrected dependency graph. Materials carries
direct/proxy capture halts, unresolved source rows, a microstructure halt, an
extreme-label halt and all claim-level adverse outcomes beneath the Grand
Lock.

Later evidence may lawfully change current status. A new driven apparatus
could test the vacuum/inertia family. A qualifying synthesis and five-record
identity could change Smithium's empirical state. A recovered source could
resolve a Materials transport. In every case, the original status, date and
falsifier remain visible. A successful later result cannot become an earlier
blind prediction unless the chronology already establishes that classification.

### Reproducibility and publication authority

The conceptual chapter presents the dependency argument. The complete branch
papers preserve every claim section and status. Candidate censuses, decisions,
controls, evidence packages, source snapshots, receipts and manifests preserve
the machine archive. A specialist may begin with the result and follow a claim
ID into its audit; an independent reproducer may ignore the narrative and
reconstruct the machine package. Neither access route is subordinate to
credentials.

Publication remains a separate act. Maria Smith alone authorises the paper and
its version. A passing engine receipt cannot publish itself, and publication
does not strengthen a scientific status. The active paper identities and
manifests freeze what was assessed; any later edit that changes a count, hash,
source or status requires a new verified release state.

### The precise boundary of current-evidence full-field completion

Physics, Chemistry and Materials are field-wide complete for their frozen
current-evidence censuses. That statement includes exact candidate enumeration,
one survivor per claim, controls, root routes, source custody and retained
adverse states. It excludes every unregistered material, molecule, condition,
apparatus and future observation. It excludes biological function, clinical
outcome, Earth-system fate and engineering deployment.

The remaining openness is productive rather than evasive. Standing predictions
have declared measurements that can change their status. Exact structures can
be extended by new registered questions. Source transports can be repaired
without rewriting history. Downstream branches can add genuinely new laws
while preserving their upstream carriers. This is the end of the project's
current V3 investigation at these three field-wide boundaries, not the end of
science.

### Why the order cannot be reversed

Chemistry cannot precede Physics because element, isotope, electron state,
energy, charge, spatial orientation, transition and measurement already have
physical meanings. Treating them as chemical axioms would duplicate upstream
ownership and conceal their derivation. Physics cannot complete Chemistry
because a physically possible collection of nuclei and electrons does not by
itself select species identity, bond, stereochemistry, reaction route,
solvation or analytical equivalence.

Materials cannot precede Chemistry because composition, bond, phase identity
and reaction supply the constituents whose organisation is measured. Chemistry
cannot complete Materials because chemical formula does not fix grain,
texture, defect population, interface, processing history or specimen response.
The same chemical carrier can support materially distinct states, and that
difference is not an error to be averaged away.

Biology cannot be inserted before the physical–chemical bridge because living
systems use quantity, energy, molecule, reaction, membrane, scaffold, soft
matter and material transport. Yet Materials cannot complete Biology because
maintenance, heredity, regulation, development, reproduction and evolution
are new relations. The handoff is therefore a dependency, not a reduction.

The empirical order has the same direction. A target is not allowed to select
the formal grammar. A molecular database cannot define the Physics constant
used to structure it. A material measurement cannot be used to revise a
chemical identity silently. A biological outcome cannot retroactively make an
adverse interface test favourable. Later evidence may test, extend or falsify
upstream work; it does not become an earlier premise.

The order also clarifies the role of computation. Enumeration, independent
reconstruction and tests execute the registered relations, but successful
execution does not own the physical, chemical or material result. The
application programmes—including Protein Fold—consume these branches through
explicit dependencies. Their eventual successes or failures will be important
evidence at their registered application boundaries, not selectors of the
foundational laws already sealed.

This dependency discipline is what allows the three branches to form one
continuous theory without becoming one undifferentiated vocabulary. Every
handoff carries enough structure to make the next problem possible and leaves
enough unresolved that the next branch has genuine scientific work to do.

This sequence prevents both reduction by assertion and independence by
forgetfulness. Chemistry is constrained by Physics but is not merely renamed
particle physics. Materials is constrained by Physics and Chemistry but is not
merely a list of compositions. Biology will be constrained by all three but
cannot inherit function or life from them without a registered biological
relation.

For an unfamiliar specialist, the shortest verification route is therefore
claim-specific. Begin with the exact statement and owner; inspect its declared
dependencies and grammar; reproduce the candidate census, decisions, survivor
and controls; then inspect the evidence package only if the statement makes an
empirical claim. Check source identity, target chronology, preserving
coordinates, all-row treatment and falsifier before reading the interpretation.
Finally, follow the outgoing handoff to see whether the next branch uses the
result at the same strength. This order prevents a compelling conclusion from
concealing a weaker receipt and prevents a machine success from being mistaken
for a measurement.

The same route permits efficient criticism. A critic need not reject or accept
the entire theory. They may challenge one dependency, candidate omission,
decision, source adapter, uncertainty interval, chronology field or ownership
edge. A valid defect remains local until the explicit graph shows its
downstream consequences. The public archive is designed to make that impact
trace possible and to preserve the correction if the challenge succeeds.

The current completion claim is correspondingly bounded. These three branches
are complete at their frozen registered current-evidence scopes. Their exact
derivations, machine receipts and evidence ledgers are closed at those scopes;
their adverse and unresolved records remain part of the result; their standing
predictions remain open to measurement; and their ownership handoffs are
explicit. This is completion of the project's present V3 investigation at the
declared boundaries, not the end of Physics, Chemistry, Materials Science or
science itself.

---

## Part III. Life, Mind, Earth, Cosmos, Society, Engineering and the Computational-Proof Programme

### Orientation: from physical organisation to living, observing and constructed worlds

The preceding branches establish the exact mathematical, informational,
computational, physical, chemical and material carriers on which this part
depends. They do not, by themselves, establish life, illness, experience,
planetary history, astronomical observation, collective action or a working
engineered artefact. Each of those domains adds an ownership boundary that
cannot be obtained by changing the vocabulary attached to an upstream law.
A chemical reaction is not thereby a living process; a biological deviation
is not thereby a diagnosis; an information-bearing computation is not thereby
phenomenal presence; a physical flow is not thereby climate attribution; a
correlation between people is not thereby power; and a successful prototype is
not thereby validation of either its intended use or the theory from which its
design began.

This part follows those boundaries in dependency order. Biology adds bounded
self-maintenance, heredity, regulation and evolutionary continuation to the
chemical and material substrate. Medicine takes the resulting living carrier
as an upstream condition but owns patients, disease, prognosis, intervention
and clinical evidence. Consciousness and Cognitive Science require living,
informational and computational organisation while adding interior
observation, perspective, phenomenal differentiation and cognitive evidence.
Earth and Environmental Sciences retain physical, chemical and biological
flows within one source-dated planetary system. Astronomy and Cosmology then
extend the observation chain beyond the immediate observer while separating
universal law from object history, source propagation and catalogue custody.
Social and Collective Sciences add identified agents, typed relations,
institutions, power, history and normative bridges. Engineering Translation
turns admitted knowledge into specified, measured, tested and maintained
artefacts without allowing application performance to select a scientific
law. Cross-Branch Synthesis composes these typed results only after their
owners have admitted them.

The computational-proof programme is reported last because it tests whether
selected formal structures can be carried through exact implementations and
external comparisons. Its programmes do not constitute hidden premises of the
field branches. Their status is deliberately non-uniform. Protein Fold V3 is
an active exact search and scientific-gate programme with a large verified
parent census and a closed high-attraction slice, but its global fold frontier,
whole-chain recurrence and protected AlphaFold parity campaign are not closed.
Chess Fold and Go Fold retain historical V2 results while their clean V3
recreations have not begun in the current workspace. Unison Fold AI retains a
substantial V2 implementation record and a V3 scaffold whose mathematical
closure is `OPEN`, whose empirical validation is `NOT_RUN`, and whose remote
deployment is `NOT_AUTHORIZED`.

### Current numerical boundary of this part

The seven domain branches and the current Cross-Branch Synthesis represented
here contain **555 model-admitted claims**, **142,080 exhaustively registered
candidates**, **555 unique survivors** and **2,220 passed required controls**.
Every one of the 555 current claims is root-traced and recorded as
depth-independent at its declared grammar boundary. Those totals must not be
misread as a declaration that every possible question in the corresponding
disciplines has been answered. They combine published foundation claims with
later, separately registered return families:
