| Branch | Published foundation | Current additions | Current claims | Candidates | Survivors | Controls | Current completion statement |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|
| Biology and Life Sciences | 75 | 7 | 82 | 20,992 | 82 | 328 | Foundation and returned families complete; 82/424 registered full-field obligations complete |
| Medicine and Health Sciences | 72 | 4 | 76 | 19,456 | 76 | 304 | Foundation and returned placebo family complete; full-field census not frozen |
| Consciousness and Cognitive Science | 72 | 5 | 77 | 19,712 | 77 | 308 | Foundation and returned family complete; full-field census not frozen |
| Earth and Environmental Sciences | 74 | 1 | 75 | 19,200 | 75 | 300 | Foundation and returned tipping claim complete; full-field census not frozen |
| Astronomy and Cosmology | 72 | 5 | 77 | 19,712 | 77 | 308 | Foundation and returned family complete; full-field census not frozen |
| Social and Collective Systems | 72 | 4 | 76 | 19,456 | 76 | 304 | Foundation and returned family complete; full-field census not frozen |
| Engineering Translation | 72 | 8 | 80 | 20,480 | 80 | 320 | Foundation and returned protocol family complete; full-field census not frozen |
| Cross-Branch Synthesis | — | 12 | 12 | 3,072 | 12 | 48 | Frozen inherited-return family complete; final global synthesis remains open |

The evidence classes are also non-uniform. In Biology, 76 current claims are
recorded as empirically tested and independently replicated and six as
independently replicated at their stated structural boundaries. Medicine has
73 and three respectively; Consciousness has 73 and four; Earth has 74 and
one; Astronomy has 73 and four; Social has 73 and three; Engineering has 72
and eight; and the twelve synthesis claims are independently replicated formal
compositions. These labels describe the actual claim packages. They do not
license a translation from structural replication into empirical confirmation,
nor from a source correspondence into a new exact numerical prediction.

### The evidence rule governing every chapter

Formal proof language is used only for exact derivational closure at a stated
candidate boundary. *Derivation* names a generated structure or law.
*Implementation* names an executable realisation. *Prediction* is reserved for
a prospectively sealed consequence. *Observation* remains tied to an external
source record, and *measurement* to an instrument, method, unit and uncertainty
or interval. *Reconstruction* denotes an inferred history or latent state.
*Correspondence* denotes a relation established after derivation;
*compatibility* means non-adverse but not discriminating evidence; *support*
means relevant but non-unique evidence. *Confirmation* and *validation* are
used only where the registered evidence protocol warrants them.

This vocabulary matters most where the record is difficult. The first Earth
earthquake catalogue comparison is adverse and remains adverse. The later
homogeneous holdout is compatible and does not erase the first result. The
first SPARC comparison in Astronomy is adverse and remains adverse, even
though a separate uncertainty-aware method record places exact rank four at a
reported systematic boundary. The Protein V2 whole-chain result is adverse,
while several local windows are strong and a theoretical support construction
is stronger still; none of those facts may be substituted for another. Failed
source transports remain failed transports, not observed absences. Historical
corrections remain part of the scientific record even when a later claim
supersedes their current wording.

### The handoff rule

Every branch owns the distinctions it introduces and passes only typed
outputs downstream. Biology may use Chemistry's reaction identities but owns
the boundary between chemistry and life. Medicine may use biological state but
owns the clinical relation between a person, a condition, an intervention and
an outcome. Consciousness may use neural and computational carriers but owns
the distinction between public access and interior presence. Earth may use
physical laws but owns a particular planet's source-dated history. Astronomy
may use those same physical laws but owns distant-source reconstruction and
cosmic chronology. Social science may use cognitive and biological capacities
but owns collective relations and institutions. Engineering may use any
admitted law but owns requirements, design, safety, testing and deployment.

The handoff is therefore neither reduction nor insulation. Upstream laws
constrain what is possible; downstream branches add boundary conditions,
identities, histories, relations and evidence constitutions. A downstream
adverse application result returns a typed question to the responsible owner.
It cannot silently repeal an upstream theorem, and successful application
cannot retroactively prove one.

---


## 16. Biology and Life Sciences: from organised chemistry to retained life

### 16.1 Why Biology is required

Chemistry establishes entities, compositions, reactions, stoichiometric
conservation, molecular distinction and stereochemical relations. Materials
Science establishes organised interfaces, phases, defects and collective
structure–property relations. Yet neither branch supplies the criterion by
which a process network remains the same living organisation while its
material constituents turn over. Biology begins at precisely that unresolved
point.

The least living carrier admitted here is a bounded process network that
retains its identity by regulated material and energy turnover inside a
declared viability window. The classification is joint. Compartment,
maintenance, heritable continuation and regulated resource exchange must be
retained together. Chemistry alone is not relabelled life, and a dissipative or
autocatalytic pattern alone is not assigned every biological property. This
boundary gives the subsequent families their order: a living organisation
must first have a distinguishable compartment; the compartment must support
resource and metabolic relations; those relations must permit retained and
copied organisation; inherited copies make variation and evolutionary
continuation possible; regulated genomes and proteins realise bounded cellular
processes; cells support development, organisms, physiology, populations and
ecosystems; and all of these require an evidence handoff that preserves the
conditions under which a biological statement was observed.

The published Biology foundation contains 75 claims in twelve ordered
families. Seven later claims close the current returned origin, homochirality,
somatic, neural, differentiation, ecosystem and validation obligations. The
current branch therefore contains 82 claims, 20,992 candidates, 82 survivors
and 328 controls. Its foundation and current returned families are complete.
Its registered full-field programme is not: 82 of 424 obligations are closed,
leaving 342 open. That distinction is the present meaning of “Biology
complete” in the repository.

### 16.2 Living identity, compartment and resource closure

Living identity is identity through controlled turnover, not identity of a
fixed material inventory. A cell or organism may exchange molecules, energy
and information while preserving the organised relation by which those
exchanges remain viable. The boundary cannot be implicit. It must identify
what is inside, what is outside, which transfers are permitted, how a change
in compartment state is detected and what loss would end the retained
organisation.

The compartment families therefore preserve membrane or boundary identity,
selective exchange, internal and external states, gradients, transport routes
and failures. A boundary is not merely a line drawn around reactions. It has a
causal role in maintaining distinguishable conditions. Conversely, a sealed
container with no regulated exchange does not satisfy the living relation.
The system must couple a maintained interior to resources and waste pathways
without hiding an external supply.

Metabolism is consequently expressed through complete ledgers. Source,
carrier, transformation, useful retention, export and loss stay visible.
Energy and matter are not interchangeable words, and neither becomes
biological purpose simply because it flows. A metabolic route belongs to the
living organisation only when its inputs and outputs participate in the
bounded maintenance relation. Autocatalysis can be an important organising
mechanism, but it does not independently supply compartment, heredity or
regulated exchange.

The returned origin claim sharpens this point. For a driven complete
**m**-role autocatalytic carrier, ignition occurs when **m − 1** roles are
internally supported and one declared seed is supplied. The exact statement is
conditional on that generated carrier and seed boundary. It identifies the
least supplied transition required for the complete role relation to become
self-supporting; it is not an observation that one such abstract carrier was
the unique historical origin of terrestrial life. The origin-of-life
extension remains responsible for connecting exact ignition structures to
measured prebiotic chemistry, compartment formation, environmental
conditions, chronology and competing mechanisms.

### 16.3 Inheritance, code and evolutionary continuation

Once a living organisation can retain itself, the next unresolved question is
how a structure may be continued through copies that are similar enough to
preserve identity and different enough to permit change. The inheritance
families distinguish template, copy process, copied record, error, repair,
lineage and environment. Replication is not perfect identity, and mutation is
not automatically functional novelty. The record must show what was retained,
what changed and how that change passed into a continuing lineage.

The branch's exact coding construction is among its clearest finite results.
Two held distinctions generate four coding labels. Three ordered positions
then generate

\[
4^3 = 64
\]

complete triplet words. Holding the first two positions produces

\[
4^2 = 16
\]

first-two-position boxes, each containing four possible third-position words.
The exact combinatorial construction precedes its biological naming. The DNA
and codon interpretation is admitted only through the biological carrier and
source boundary; it does not arise by inserting the known genetic code as a
premise. Nor does the construction by itself specify every mapping between
codons, amino acids, regulatory context, translation machinery and observed
organisms. Those mappings remain biological records.

Genes and genomes are treated as organised, regulated carriers rather than as
strings detached from expression. Sequence identity, regulatory state,
copy-number relation, chromosomal context, repair, transcription, translation
and phenotype are kept distinct. A sequence difference is not automatically a
functional difference; an expression association is not automatically a
causal mechanism; and a population frequency is not an individual destiny.
The same custody permits the branch to distinguish a heritable variant from a
somatic event and a selected continuation from drift.

Evolution is derived as heritable variation, differential continuation and a
controlled environmental relation. Selection and drift are not synonyms.
Fitness is condition-bound rather than an intrinsic scalar permanently
attached to a variant. A beneficial variant does not inevitably fix: finite
population history, competing variants, environmental change and stochastic
sampling records remain relevant at the empirical boundary. This correction
supersedes stronger earlier language while preserving the earlier claim in the
historical reconciliation. The V3 result is more specific, not less
evolutionary: it states exactly which relation must be present before
differential continuation can be attributed to selection.

### 16.4 Protein structure, function and the Fold handoff

The protein family begins with a sequence-bearing chemical carrier and asks
how ordered local interactions, geometry, environment and retained alternatives
can constrain a folded ensemble. The admitted biological statement is a
directed, condition-bounded fold with a retained ensemble. A protein is not
reduced to one universal rigid native shape, and a sequence is not treated as
if it carried no environmental or interaction context. Conformation, ensemble,
function, binding, modification and observation method remain distinct.

This correction matters to the computational programme. The conceptual law
does not promise that any finite search implementation will immediately
predict every whole-chain experimental structure. It defines the relations an
implementation must preserve: sequence identity, interaction custody,
geometric compatibility, environmental condition, alternative-state
retention, evaluation boundary and falsifiable comparison. A computational
candidate can therefore fail while the broader biological relation remains
properly posed. Equally, local success cannot be promoted to global prediction
without closing the missing whole-chain relation.

The current Protein Fold V3 programme takes this handoff seriously. It uses an
exact sequence representation and a six-axis joint relation, retains every
registered interaction class and separates the search census from external
structure comparison. Its current results are presented in §24.3. The
essential point at the Biology boundary is that the branch has supplied a
formal protein-folding constitution and a testable implementation route, while
the active programme has not yet closed the global fold frontier or the
registered AlphaFold parity campaign.

### 16.5 Cells, development, organisms and physiology

Cells integrate compartment, metabolism, inheritance, expression and protein
function into regulated state transitions. The cell-process family therefore
keeps state, signal, response, cycle, repair, death and lineage records
separate. Recurrence may be necessary for a continuing cell cycle, but
recurrence alone does not diagnose cancer. The returned cancer-state claim
requires a persistent non-terminal cycling carrier together with loss of a
differentiation transition and escape from the combined controls on division,
death and differentiation. It remains a structural classification; clinical
diagnosis belongs to Medicine and requires patient-, tissue-, assay- and
outcome-specific evidence.

The returned somatic–germline result uses denominator factorisation to
separate a finite somatic transient from an odd-denominator recurrent carrier.
It provides an exact orbit distinction at its generated boundary. It does not
make every measured somatic or germline history a function of denominator
parity. Biological mapping still requires cell lineage, inheritance route,
developmental timing and measurement.

Multicellular development adds positional, lineage and differentiation
relations. A multicellular body is not just a collection of identical cells;
its organisation depends on controlled difference, signalling, spatial
constraint and retained whole-body coordination. Differentiation is therefore
not an optional label added after a cell state is measured. It is an owned
transition in the organisation. The current cancer correction follows because
loss of that transition changes the reachable state relation, while cycling
alone does not.

Organism physiology extends the same rule across coordinated systems. A
measurement is retained with organism, tissue or organ, condition, method,
time and reference boundary. The returned neural threshold claim identifies
Half-One as the least normalised activation support in its exact carrier. It
does not declare a universal measured membrane voltage, firing rate or
behavioural threshold. Dimensional values remain instrument- and
system-specific observations.

### 16.6 Populations, ecosystems and scale

Population and ecological statements require more than an average over
individuals. Population identity, sampling frame, births, deaths, movement,
inheritance, resource relations, interactions and observation window must be
preserved. An ecological network may be represented by nodes and links, but
the representation does not establish a universal topology. The branch's
source reconciliation preserves the empirical result that strongly scale-free
networks are rare rather than treating scale freedom as a default law of life.

The same discipline corrects a claimed universal three-quarter allometric
exponent. Current evidence retains three-quarter, two-thirds, unity and
non-universal cases. The exact SFT fractions remain formal carriers at their
declared boundaries; they are not silently converted into one universal
measured exponent across taxa, traits and methods. Source population, body
size range, regression choice, uncertainty and biological mechanism remain
part of any empirical allometry claim.

The returned ecosystem claim provides an exact bounded period-four orbit on
the **3/5** Fold carrier. It demonstrates a finite recurrent structure and
supplies a candidate carrier for bounded ecosystem dynamics. It does not
forecast every ecosystem, prove stability from cycling alone or replace
measured species, forcing, resource and disturbance relations. One periodic
orbit cannot identify an ecosystem's empirical stability class without those
records.

### 16.7 Homochirality: observed asymmetry and unresolved origin

Biological homochirality is retained as an observation: terrestrial proteins
predominantly use L-amino acids and biological sugars predominantly use the
opposite handed convention. The V3 branch does not use that observation to
claim that the historical origin of the asymmetry has already been measured.
Earlier parity-selection language is corrected accordingly.

The returned homochiral-amplification claim begins with a held Fold parity and
a successor-amplification relation. It generates increasing finite dominance
of one label without deleting the other label from the complete carrier. This
is a structural result about amplification from a retained asymmetry. It does
not establish which prebiotic process supplied the initial bias, the magnitude
of that bias, the environmental route by which it was amplified or the full
history connecting the formal carrier to terrestrial biomolecules. Those are
open empirical obligations.

### 16.8 Biological evidence, corrections and current status

The Biology foundation sealed all 75 foundation predictions and 19,200
candidate forms before source selection. A separate custodian opened the
registered sources after sealing, required claim-specific fragments, retained
adverse and failed rows and compared the source-derived held label with the
registered prediction. The current seven-claim return family was registered
separately and is not backdated into that foundation chronology.

The foundation used 17 usable sources and preserved four failed transports:
one TLS failure, one HTTP 403 response and two challenge-page failures. A
failed transport is a statement about access, not a statement about the
scientific content that could not be obtained. It remains visible unless a
later, separately registered source capture lawfully adds evidence. Thirty
Biology-owned V1/V2 atoms were reconciled; 21 required correction at the V3
boundary. The major corrections are retained here because they prevent the
current branch from inheriting stronger but unsupported language:

| Topic | Current scientific record |
|---|---|
| Beneficial variants | Differential advantage does not imply inevitable fixation; drift, finite history and environment remain |
| Protein state | Condition-bounded ensemble retained; no universal rigid single-shape reduction |
| Homochirality | Biological handedness observed; historical origin unresolved; amplification result is structural |
| Allometry | No universal three-quarter exponent; multiple exponents and non-universal cases preserved |
| Biological networks | Strong scale-free form is not a default; empirical rarity retained |
| Senescence | Denominator parity alone does not identify or predict organismal senescence |
| Cancer | Recurrence alone does not diagnose cancer; differentiation loss and escape from controls are required in the structural claim |
| Ecosystem stability | One bounded orbit is not a universal stability forecast |

The seven-claim validation record compares the current origin, homochirality,
somatic, neural, differentiation and ecosystem structures with seven
registered sources. The source-derived categorical labels match the registered
claims at that comparison boundary. Observed counterparts are retained, while
exact Fold thresholds and orbits remain structural predictions where no direct
dimensional measurement establishes them. The homochirality origin and the
quantitative mapping from abstract carriers to organisms remain unresolved.

### 16.9 Biology's downstream handoff and open frontier

Biology hands Medicine an identified living carrier with compartment,
metabolic, hereditary, developmental, physiological and population relations.
It hands Consciousness a neural and organismic carrier without claiming that
neural activity is already phenomenal presence. It hands Earth Science living
stocks, flows and ecological interactions without claiming that biological
laws determine a planet's contingent history. It hands the Protein Fold
programme a formally constrained structure–function problem without claiming
that implementation closure has already occurred.

The current branch is complete at its published foundation and returned-family
boundary, and open across 342 registered full-field obligations. The open work
includes deeper origin-of-life mapping, quantitative homochiral pathways,
expanded molecular and systems biology, complete protein computational tests,
additional organismal and ecological evidence and the remaining registered
family census. Completion here means completion of a defined current
investigation, never the end of Biology or the end of science.

---


### 16.10 Dependency-resolved family ledger

#### 16.10.1 From a living boundary to a continuing lineage

The first six Biology families solve a sequence of different problems. A
living boundary identifies the organisation; a compartment gives that
organisation an oriented inside and outside; metabolism connects the
compartment to finite resources; inheritance makes continuation possible;
variation makes descendants distinguishable; and evolution specifies how
those differences continue under an environment.

| Family | Unresolved problem entering the family | Admitted output |
|---|---|---|
| Life Boundary | Chemistry and dissipation do not classify a process as living | Bounded self-maintaining organisation, life/non-life boundary, autocatalytic boundary, turnover identity and loss of closure |
| Compartment Identity | A living process needs an oriented, continuing boundary | Compartment, inside/outside orientation, membrane organisation, selective permeability, nesting and lineage provenance |
| Metabolism Resource | A compartment cannot persist without controlled exchange | Metabolic network, uptake, conserved transformation, coupling, export and finite resource closure |
| Inheritance Replication | Turnover alone does not continue organisation across copies | Information-bearing inheritance, template copying, replication, fidelity/error, heritable variation and lineage |
| Variation Evolution | Heritable copies require a rule for differential continuation | Variation, heritability, differential reproduction, selection, fixation/loss and drift distinction |
| Gene Genome Regulation | Inheritance must be carried and regulated in an organism | Four-label nucleic-acid alphabet, 64 triplet words, 16 boxes, gene interval, genome organisation and expression regulation |

The ordering prevents a circular definition of life by reproduction alone.
A crystal can continue a pattern without satisfying the complete living
relation; an autocatalytic chemistry can amplify without heredity; a genetic
sequence can be copied outside a self-maintaining organism. The branch admits
these structures where their own conditions hold and calls the joint system
living only after every required relation is present.

Compartment lineage provenance is particularly important. If a boundary
divides, merges or is rebuilt, the record must show how the descendant
compartment derives from the predecessor. Mere spatial continuity may be
insufficient, while complete material identity is unnecessary because turnover
is expected. The lineage is carried by the organised transition relation.

Finite resource closure also distinguishes biological persistence from a
perpetual-motion description. A culture, cell or organism may be sustained by
an environment, but the supplied resources must be inside the declared system
ledger or explicitly cross its boundary. Exhaustion, toxic by-product and
failed export are possible biological failures and cannot be omitted from a
favourable maintenance trace.

The variation family keeps three events distinct: a new difference arises, it
is heritable, and its descendants continue differentially. Only the complete
relation warrants selection language. Fixation and loss are population
histories, not logical consequences of the word “beneficial”. The drift claim
preserves finite sampling and lineage contingency as an alternative route to a
frequency change.

#### 16.10.2 From regulated code to organism and ecosystem

The second half of the foundation carries regulated inheritance into physical
form, cells and larger living organisations:

| Family | New carrier or relation | Boundary retained |
|---|---|---|
| Protein Structure Function | Sequence, directed fold, ensemble, function, interaction, assembly and homochirality | Sequence is not one rigid shape; function and conformation remain condition-bound |
| Cell Process | Cell identity, transport, signalling, excitation, division and dysregulated continuation | Cycling does not by itself diagnose cancer |
| Multicellular Development | Composition, adhesion, tissue, differentiation, lineage, morphogenesis and regeneration | Whole-body organisation is not an aggregate of interchangeable cells |
| Organism Physiology | Organism identity, process, homeostasis, reproduction, life cycle, senescence and longevity | One orbit or parity cannot determine lifespan |
| Population Ecology | Population, species, community, trophic network, ecosystem, recurrence, topology and allometry | Individual and population levels, topology classes and exponents remain distinct |
| Observation Handoffs | Specimen, sampling, condition, control, assay, uncertainty, causality and branch ownership | A favourable assay row is not a universal biological law |

Protein identity begins with the ordered residue carrier but persists through
condition-bound conformation and modification only under declared identity
rules. Folding is directed because the transition relation constrains which
states can follow; it is ensemble-bearing because accessible alternatives and
dynamics may remain. Function adds a biological relation to another carrier
or process. Interaction and quaternary assembly require partner identity,
orientation, stoichiometry and condition. The V3 family thereby avoids using
“fold”, “shape”, “state” and “function” as interchangeable words.

Cell signalling requires a sender or source state, a transmitted or local
carrier, a receiver relation and a response boundary. A molecular association
without direction, timing or response cannot establish signalling. Excitable
transition adds a held pre-state, threshold relation and post-state. The
returned Half-One result provides the least normalised support at its exact
boundary, while Biology retains ownership of real membranes, ion channels,
networks and measured thresholds.

Multicellular composition introduces relations that no isolated cell owns.
Adhesion supplies a continuing interface; a tissue adds ordered collective
organisation; differentiation changes reachable roles; developmental lineage
preserves the route by which those roles arose; morphogenesis links local
changes to whole form; regeneration requires restoration under a declared
damage boundary. These distinctions make the differentiation-loss cancer
claim possible without reducing cancer to one molecular event.

Homeostasis is regulated bounded return, not perfect constancy. A physiological
variable may vary while remaining inside a viable relation, and similar
observed values may arise from different control histories. Senescence and
longevity require organism, cohort, environment, cause of loss and observation
window. A recurrence in a component is not a lifespan theorem.

The population and ecology family marks a second aggregation boundary. Species
identity requires the stated reproductive, phylogenetic or other empirical
criterion; the word “species” does not fix one universal classification
method. Trophic links require measured resource relations. Ecosystem
organisation retains abiotic conditions and external forcing. Network topology
and allometry are empirical families with explicit counterexamples, not
decorations added to the exact orbit work.

#### 16.10.3 The seven returned Biology claims as a connected programme

The returned claims are not seven isolated analogies. They revisit points at
which the historical corpus had proposed a stronger connection between exact
Fold structure and living evidence:

1. Autocatalytic ignition gives a least seed relation for a complete driven
   role carrier.
2. Homochiral amplification shows how a held parity can grow into finite
   dominance without deleting the other label.
3. Somatic–germline factorisation distinguishes a finite transient from an
   odd-denominator recurrent carrier.
4. Half-One excitation gives a least normalised event support.
5. Differentiation loss joins continuing division to escape from the full
   division/death/differentiation control.
6. The **3/5** period-four carrier supplies an exact bounded ecological orbit.
7. The validation claim freezes the seven-source comparison, including the
   limit between categorical correspondence and unmeasured exact mapping.

The dependency route runs from ignition to retained asymmetry, from lineage
type to excitable transition, from cellular control to ecosystem recurrence,
and finally to external comparison. None of the exact values is allowed to
enter a source row as if it were already measured. This is why the returned
family can be formally complete while some organismal mappings remain
unresolved.

#### 16.10.4 Biological falsification surface

Biology's categorical claims fail if another generated candidate preserves all
registered dimensions, if a dependency route is missing, if the independent
implementation does not reconstruct the survivor or if a registered source
contradicts a required empirical relation. Quantitative mappings have their
own, narrower conditions. The codon count would fail if its two held
distinctions and three ordered positions did not reconstruct four labels and
64 words. The earthquake- or SPARC-style notion of a measured interval does
not apply to that finite combinatorial identity. By contrast, a proposed
universal allometric exponent must survive measured populations and
uncertainties; it cannot be rescued by the exact existence of the fraction in
another grammar.

Protein computational claims fail at their implementation and blind-comparison
boundaries. A local TM-score does not satisfy a whole-chain criterion. A
theoretical support path does not satisfy an achieved sequence-only output.
An unopened target is unresolved, not a null result. These separations are the
operational bridge from Biology to the computational programme.


## 17. Medicine and Health Sciences: from living variation to clinical knowledge

### 17.1 Why Medicine is not reducible to Biology

Biology supplies organisms, variation, dysfunction, repair and population
relations. Medicine begins when those relations must be judged for an
identified person or population under a health, disease, prevention,
intervention and care boundary. A biological difference does not become a
disease merely because it can be measured. A group association does not become
a diagnosis in one person. A mechanism does not become a demonstrated clinical
benefit. A favourable mean does not erase adverse events, non-response,
censoring or loss to follow-up.

The Medicine foundation therefore begins with patient and population identity
and proceeds through health, variation, dysfunction, disease, injury, symptom,
sign, measurement, diagnosis, cause, association, mechanism, confounding,
risk, prognosis, outcome, intervention, comparator, exposure, adverse events,
efficacy, effectiveness, safety, trials, observation, synthesis, screening,
prevention, treatment, rehabilitation, individual inference, consent, ethics,
privacy and uncertainty. Its order is clinical rather than merely biological:
one must first know whose state is being described, then what observation was
made, then which causal or prognostic inference is warranted, and only then
what intervention and decision can be evaluated.

The published foundation contains 72 claims. Four current returned claims add
the expectation/body fibre, the available-state boundary, the separation of
objective physiology from report and their source-bound validation. The
current Medicine branch therefore contains 76 claims, 19,456 candidates, 76
survivors and 304 controls. The foundation and this return family are complete;
a full-field Medicine census is not yet frozen.

### 17.2 Patient, population, health and disease

The least clinical carrier retains the patient rather than replacing that
patient with an aggregate. Identity includes the relevant person, time,
condition, observation and decision context. Population evidence may constrain
what can reasonably be believed about a person, but it does not uniquely
determine that person's outcome or preferred course. Conversely, a single
clinical history does not establish a population effect.

Health is not defined as the numerical absence of disease. Health and disease
are organised, condition-bound states whose components may coexist. A person
may have a diagnosed condition while retaining substantial function; a person
may have no current diagnosis while reporting serious impairment or while an
unmeasured disease process continues. Variation, dysfunction, disease and
injury are kept distinct because each supports different causal, prognostic
and intervention questions.

This prevents molecular language from doing clinical work it has not earned.
A stereochemical distinction can be chemically exact and biologically
important, but stereochemistry alone cannot establish efficacy, toxicity,
appropriate dose or patient benefit. Those require exposure, comparator,
outcome and adverse-event records. The V3 reconciliation corrects earlier
mixed claims that crossed this ownership boundary.

### 17.3 Symptom, sign, measurement and diagnosis

A symptom is a participant-reported state. A sign is an observed clinical
feature. A measurement is an instrument- and method-bound value. A diagnosis
is a classified clinical judgement supported by declared criteria and
alternatives. These carriers may inform one another, but they are not
interchangeable.

The distinction is especially important when a report is the primary access
to pain, mood, expectation or another first-person state. The report remains a
real observation of what was reported; it is not automatically an objective
measure of every proposed physiological mechanism. Equally, a physiological
change does not automatically establish the participant's experienced state.
The returned placebo family preserves both channels.

A complete diagnostic record must identify the patient and presentation,
retain the available signs and measurements, state the decision rule and its
version, preserve relevant alternatives and record missing or unavailable
evidence. Classification success within one dataset does not by itself prove
causation, future outcome or generality. A non-detection is not an observed
absence unless the method, sensitivity and sampling boundary warrant that
statement.

### 17.4 Association, cause, risk and prognosis

Clinical records often begin with association. The Medicine branch refuses to
rename association as cause. Causal attribution must retain the proposed
mechanism, alternative causes, confounding, selection, timing and an
intervention or comparison structure capable of distinguishing the claimed
route. A plausible mechanism may support a hypothesis while remaining
insufficient to establish the magnitude or existence of a clinical effect.

Risk is tied to a target population, an event definition and a time horizon.
Prognosis additionally retains the patient's current state, competing events,
treatment history, censoring and uncertainty. A denominator pattern or Fold
orbit cannot on its own set a person's prognosis. This is another explicit V3
correction: exact mathematical structure may organise a registered prognostic
model, but patient-specific outcome requires clinical evidence.

The same discipline applies to screening. Test performance is not one number.
With condition present or absent and test positive or negative, the complete
finite carrier contains four cells. Sensitivity, specificity, predictive
values and error counts describe different conditionings of that carrier.
Their interpretation also depends on the population and prevalence boundary.
A screening programme must add access, uptake, follow-up, treatment, harms and
missed cases; the test result alone does not establish programme benefit.

### 17.5 Intervention, comparator and the alternative course

An intervention claim must preserve what happened and what it was compared
with. Treatment name, dose, route, exposure, timing, adherence and co-
interventions remain part of the administered course. Comparator identity must
be equally complete. “Usual care”, “placebo” and “no treatment” are not
interchangeable labels unless their realised conditions are shown to be the
same for the claim at issue.

The finite arm-by-outcome relation again exposes all four cells for a binary
comparison: intervention with and without the registered outcome, and
comparator with and without it. Favourable responders cannot be counted while
adverse outcomes, non-response or missing follow-up are discarded. Efficacy,
effectiveness, safety, benefit and harm remain distinct. Efficacy concerns the
effect within the registered trial conditions; effectiveness concerns intended
use in a wider practice setting; safety requires a scoped account of harms and
exposure; benefit and harm additionally depend on affected people, timing and
the decision context.

The branch permits deterministic allocation to supply auditable randomisation
through concealed held labels. The relevant scientific properties are
prospective allocation, concealment before assignment, preserved sequence,
absence of target-based selection and complete custody—not an imported claim
of metaphysical chance. Blinding is separately recorded. Random allocation
does not imply blinded participants, clinicians, outcome assessors or analysts
unless those controls are actually present.

Trials, observational studies and evidence syntheses therefore retain
different inference structures. A randomised trial can estimate its registered
comparison under its conduct and analysis conditions. An observational study
requires explicit confounding and selection control. A synthesis must retain
the eligibility rules, source studies, heterogeneity and missing evidence; it
cannot convert incompatible source questions into one homogeneous result by
averaging them.

### 17.6 Expectation, placebo and the available-state boundary

The returned placebo work derives a complete two-preimage expectation/body
fibre. The exact structural values are **1/4** for the body-labelled route and
**3/4** for the expectation-labelled route; both map to the common **1/2**
image, and together the retained preimages compose to the One:

\[
\frac14 + \frac34 = 1.
\]

This is an exact relation in the generated Fold carrier. It is not a claim
that every placebo response is 75 per cent, every bodily response 25 per cent,
or every clinical outcome a mixture with those measured weights. The fractions
are structural coordinates, not imported clinical effect sizes.

The important clinical consequence is the **available-state boundary**.
Expectation may order or select among routes that the organism can reach under
the declared conditions. It does not manufacture a physiologically absent
state merely because a participant or clinician expects it. The claim thus
supports a strong, testable role for expectation without turning expectation
into an unlimited causal substance.

The empirical constitution separates report from objective physiology. A
participant's account, behavioural response, neural measure, autonomic signal
and clinical endpoint remain separate rows with separate comparators and
controls. The current validation record uses nine PubMed-indexed source
records and retains objective neural, autonomic and other
neurophysiological modulation alongside null, small, context-dependent and
unresolved findings. The observed evidence supports the categorical
distinction and the existence of condition-dependent physiological routes. It
does not measure the exact SFT fractions as universal effect magnitudes.

### 17.7 Ethics, consent and privacy as operational boundaries

Ethics is not an ornament added after the experiment. Consent, capacity,
privacy, data minimisation, equipoise, stopping and access conditions change
what may lawfully and scientifically be observed. A result acquired by
violating its participant boundary is not repaired by statistical accuracy.

Consent requires information, voluntariness, capacity, an identifiable choice
and a record. Capacity is decision- and time-specific rather than a permanent
global label. Privacy requires declared collection, access, use, retention and
disclosure boundaries. Data minimisation forbids collecting an unlimited
record merely because it may later prove convenient. Equipoise and stopping
rules preserve the relationship between uncertainty, accumulating evidence and
participant welfare.

These requirements also govern computational medicine and any future
Consciousness or Unison Fold study involving people. A model output is not a
medical decision by itself. Its training source, target population, failure
modes, calibration, uncertainty, human responsibility and intended-use
validation must remain available to the clinical evidence chain.

### 17.8 Evidence custody, adverse records and current boundary

The Medicine foundation sealed its 72 claims and 18,432 candidates before its
external source comparison. Eleven of thirteen registered authority sources
were captured. Two HHS transports failed and remain recorded as transport
failures. Purpose-matched ICH content was captured separately; it does not
retroactively turn the HHS failures into successes.

All 356 V1 rows and 407 V2 steps were reviewed. The inherited Medicine content
was decomposed into owned atoms, and the current record corrects four recurrent
overreaches:

| Earlier conflation | Current boundary |
|---|---|
| Placebo named without complete comparator or outcome | Placebo claims retain realised comparator, outcome, expectation, blinding and chronology |
| Molecular stereochemistry treated as proof of efficacy or toxicity | Chemical identity is upstream; clinical exposure, outcome and harm evidence are required |
| Denominator parity treated as prognosis | Exact orbit structure does not establish a patient's future clinical event |
| Cellular recurrence treated as cancer diagnosis | Biology supplies a structural carrier; diagnosis requires clinical, tissue and measurement criteria |

Every favourable, adverse, null, missing and unresolved row remains part of
the relevant evidence record. The branch is current-evidence closed for its
foundation and four returned claims, not for all medicine. Open work includes
the unfrozen full-field census, disease- and intervention-specific validations,
individual decision boundaries, longer-term harms, diverse populations,
transportability and additional prospective tests of the expectation/body
carrier.

Medicine hands Consciousness a rigorous distinction between first-person
report and objective measure. It hands Social Science population and
intervention structures that do not erase individuals. It hands Engineering
the intended-use, safety, ethics and validation requirements for health
technologies. It preserves clinical ownership whenever a biological,
computational or engineering result is proposed for patient care.

---


### 17.9 Dependency-resolved family ledger

#### 17.9.1 From patient identity to an interpretable diagnosis

The first Medicine families progressively constrain what a clinical statement
can mean:

| Family | Required distinction | Principal output |
|---|---|---|
| Patient Population Health | Person, episode, population, health state, observation and course | An identified longitudinal clinical carrier that cannot be replaced by an aggregate |
| Variation Dysfunction Disease Injury | Normal variation, dysfunction, disease, injury, comorbidity, severity and stage | A typed condition relation rather than a numerical deviation labelled disease |
| Symptom Sign Measurement Diagnosis | Report, observed sign, instrument value, classification, alternatives and accuracy | A reconstructible diagnostic chain |
| Cause Association Mechanism Confounding | Association, causal route, mechanism, confounder, mediator and modifier | A causal claim with its alternatives and context retained |
| Risk Prognosis Outcome | Risk, incidence/prevalence, prognosis, outcome, censoring and calibration | A time- and population-bound future statement |

Patient identity must survive repeated episodes. The same person can occupy
different health states; different people can produce similar measurements.
A clinical episode therefore links time, presentation, investigation,
intervention and outcome without treating one visit as a complete person.
Longitudinal course preserves change and the gaps between observations.

Normal variation is reference-bound. A value may be uncommon without being
pathological, or common while still harmful. Dysfunction requires impaired
organisation under a declared task or viability boundary. Disease adds a
clinical classification and evidence route. Injury adds an event or damaging
process. Comorbidity preserves multiple interacting conditions rather than
forcing all observations into one diagnosis. Severity and stage retain the
criteria and version by which the classification was made.

Diagnostic accuracy requires the complete condition-by-test carrier, target
condition, reference procedure, population and uncertainty. Differential
diagnosis retains live alternatives and the evidence that favours or rejects
each. The model may identify a best-supported classification without claiming
that all alternatives are ontologically impossible.

Causal mediation and effect modification solve different questions. A
mediator lies on a proposed route; a modifier changes the relation across
conditions or groups. Confounding supplies an alternative common cause.
Failure to distinguish them can turn adjustment into either removal of part of
the effect or preservation of a spurious association.

Prognostic calibration compares predicted and observed event frequencies at a
declared horizon. Discrimination and calibration are not interchangeable. A
model may rank patients while mis-estimating absolute risks, or be calibrated
in one population and fail after transport. Competing events and censoring
remain in the denominator under their proper class.

#### 17.9.2 From an intervention to a responsible decision

| Family | Question entering the family | New obligation |
|---|---|---|
| Intervention Comparator Counterfactual | What alternative course makes an effect interpretable? | Intervention, comparator, counterfactual, allocation, adherence and co-intervention |
| Dose Exposure Response Adverse Event | What was received and what followed? | Dose, actual exposure, response, dose–response, adverse event and toxicity |
| Efficacy Effectiveness Safety Benefit Harm | Which evaluative claim is warranted? | Separate efficacy, effectiveness, safety, net outcome and heterogeneous effect records |
| Trial Observational Synthesis | Which design supports the inference? | Trial, deterministic concealed allocation, blinding, observational design, synthesis and missing reporting |
| Screening Prevention Treatment Rehabilitation | Where in the care pathway does action occur? | Screening, prevention, treatment, rehabilitation, monitoring and withdrawal |
| Individual Population Inference | How can group evidence inform one person? | Transportability, subgroup and multiplicity controls, decision and uncertainty |
| Consent Ethics Privacy Uncertainty | What may be done and recorded? | Consent, capacity, privacy, minimisation, equipoise, stopping and handoff |

Dose is the intended or administered quantity under a route and schedule;
exposure is what reached the relevant carrier. Response may be beneficial,
adverse or unrelated. A dose–response claim needs ordered exposures,
comparators and uncertainty. An adverse event is temporally associated with an
exposure; clinical toxicity is a supported harmful relation. Recording one
does not automatically establish the other, but discarding adverse events
would destroy the safety record.

Absolute and relative effects must remain related to their denominators. A
large relative change can correspond to a small absolute difference, and
neither identifies who will benefit. Treatment-effect heterogeneity can be a
real interaction or a multiplicity artefact; subgroups and hypotheses must be
registered with sufficient control.

The deterministic randomisation claim is consequential for the SFT
constitution. Concealed held labels can allocate participants in a sequence
that is auditable and target-independent. The scientific protection comes from
concealment and prospective custody. If an allocator can see the next label or
choose enrolment after seeing it, the design no longer preserves the intended
comparison.

Monitoring and stopping retain the evidence available at each time. A trial
stopped early for benefit, harm or futility has a different chronology from a
completed fixed sample. A patient who withdraws consent cannot be treated as a
favourable outcome or simply deleted from the historical record. The permitted
retention and analysis follow the consent and protocol boundary.

#### 17.9.3 The expectation/body family as a clinical bridge

The exact fibre is useful because it forces expectation and body-labelled
routes to be held simultaneously under one observed image. In a trial, similar
reported outcomes can arise from different combinations of expectation,
natural history, co-intervention, measurement, physiological response and
target treatment. Blinding and objective measurement help discriminate some
routes; they do not make expectation unreal.

The available-state claim supplies a direct falsification boundary. If an
expectation appears to create a state that the registered biological carrier
cannot reach, either the carrier grammar is incomplete, an external input is
missing, the measurement is misclassified or the claim fails. The theory does
not authorise an appeal to expectation as an explanation immune from testing.

The source comparison's null, small and context-dependent results are thus
scientifically productive. They limit generality. A physiological modulation
in one outcome or context cannot be promoted to a universal clinical effect.
Future prospective tests should freeze expectation manipulation, realised
comparator, blinding, report, objective channel, adverse event and follow-up
before enrolment.

#### 17.9.4 Medical ownership and falsification

Medicine owns whether a result warrants prevention, diagnosis, prognosis or
treatment for people. Biology owns the upstream mechanism; Engineering owns
the device; Social Science owns distribution and institutional context. A
medical claim fails if the patient or population boundary is lost, the
comparator is missing, an alternative course is manufactured after the result,
adverse or missing rows disappear, or the decision exceeds the evidence's
transport boundary.

This ownership is protective, not restrictive. It permits an exact biological
or computational discovery to enter clinical research while preventing the
discovery's precision from being mistaken for clinical validation. It also
permits an adverse clinical result to identify a missing mechanism or use
boundary without being diluted into a generic “mixed evidence” label.


## 18. Consciousness and Cognitive Science: from public information to interior observation

### 18.1 The unresolved boundary

Information Science establishes distinctions, records, codes, channels and
inference. Computation establishes state transitions and executable processes.
Biology supplies a living, regulated neural carrier. None of these facts alone
licenses a relabelling of access, report, neural activity or computation as
phenomenal presence. The Consciousness branch begins by preserving that gap as
a scientific boundary rather than filling it with a synonym.

Its published foundation contains 72 claims across observation, access,
binding, subject, self-observation, memory, self-model, attention, cognition,
substrate independence, qualia differentiation and the red-of-red. Five
returned claims add directional synaesthesia, a nonordinary three-quality
orbit, sleep and dream recurrence, cessation and a 27-source validation. The
current branch contains 77 claims, 19,712 candidates, 77 survivors and 308
controls. Its foundation and returned family are complete at current evidence;
the full-field census remains unfrozen.

### 18.2 External observation and the interior return

An external observation is represented as a many-to-one state image. Different
predecessor states can generate the same public image, report or measurement.
The image therefore does not contain a predecessor label that was not retained
through the observation relation. This is not scepticism about evidence. It is
the exact information boundary that makes evidence custody necessary.

Interior observation is forced when the image re-enters the same organised
process that generated or received it. The process does not merely carry a
public token; it retains a source-located predecessor relation while the common
image is taken up again within the continuing organisation. This produces the
branch's structural answer to the hard-problem boundary: interiority is not an
extra word pasted onto computation, and it is not inferred solely from an
external correlation. It is the self-involving return relation of an organised
carrier.

The complete two-preimage fibre is central. The exact preimages **1/4** and
**3/4** share the image **1/2** and compose to the One. They can carry
observer/observed roles without being collapsed into one predecessor:

\[
\frac14 \longmapsto \frac12,
\qquad
\frac34 \longmapsto \frac12,
\qquad
\frac14 + \frac34 = 1.
\]

An external observer may receive the common image while the participant's
organised process retains a predecessor label. Phenomenal privacy is therefore
mathematically intelligible without making first-person evidence
unscientific. The public record and the retained interior relation are
different evidence carriers.

### 18.3 Access, report and phenomenal presence

Access means that content is available to a specified process. Report means
that a content-bearing output was produced under stated conditions. Behaviour
is an observed action. Neural correlation is an association between a neural
record and another state. Computation is a realised transition system.
Physical measurement is an instrument-bound public record. None of these
carriers becomes phenomenal presence by changing its label.

This separation protects both sides of the inquiry. First-person report is not
dismissed because it is private; it is retained as a report by an identified
participant under an elicitation and memory boundary. Objective measurement is
not dismissed because it lacks direct access to the retained predecessor; it
provides a source-bound record of neural, behavioural or physiological state.
Scientific progress comes from relating the records without erasing their
different access conditions.

The same rule limits inference from silence or fluent output. Failure to report
does not prove absence of experience when access, memory, motor output or
communication may have failed. Fluent language does not prove interiority when
the required self-involving relation and causal realisation have not been
shown. These are evidence boundaries, not declarations about which biological
or artificial systems can ever be conscious.

### 18.4 Binding, unity, subject and perspective

Binding is derived as joint closure with component distinctions retained. A
bound state is not a bag of independent features and is not a featureless
fusion. Each component participates in a complete relation that can be traced
to the same organised process. Unity is complete mutual participation within
that interior process.

The subject is the continuing source-located relation across changing content.
Perspective follows from that location and continuation. It is not an
unchanging substance added to the carrier, and it is not identical to any one
momentary representation. Because the organised relation continues while
contents change, subject identity can be retained without requiring every
material component or every cognitive state to remain fixed.

Introspection is a further return within this structure. It may reveal a
retained content or relation while altering attention, report and memory. It is
therefore neither infallible nor reducible to an unrelated external
measurement. Error, uncertainty and inaccessible content remain possible
inside the same exact observation constitution.

### 18.5 Finite self-model, memory and temporal identity

The self-model construction begins at **1/4**, reaches **1/2** and closes at the
Fold-fixed One in two non-identity acts:

\[
\frac14 \longmapsto \frac12 \longmapsto 1.
\]

The sequence supplies a finite self-referential closure. It avoids an infinite
regress of a model that would require another complete modeller behind it at
every step. The final One is fixed under the Fold, so the closure does not need
an additional observer to certify the whole. The construction is exact at its
formal boundary; mapping each coordinate onto measured neural events remains
an empirical task.

Memory requires retention across change and a route by which a state can be
reidentified. The least period-two label-bearing witness is the orbit

\[
\frac13 \longleftrightarrow \frac23.
\]

The orbit preserves difference and recurrence. It is a structural carrier for
continued memory and temporal identity, not a universal measured oscillation
frequency or a claim that every memory alternates between two neural states.
Content, storage medium, retrieval condition, reconstruction and report remain
separate.

The returned sleep–dream claim uses the same **1/3 ↔ 2/3** period-two carrier
to distinguish a continuing interior return from ordinary waking input. Sleep,
dream and physiological-stage labels are attached only after the structural
closure is fixed. The claim therefore predicts a recurrence form without
importing a sleep-stage taxonomy as a mathematical premise. Measured sleep
architecture, reports, neural signals and individual differences remain
source-bound evidence.

### 18.6 Qualia differentiation and the red-of-red

Qualia are treated as differentiated participant-retained qualities rather
than as public stimulus labels. Physical stimulus, public word, participant
quality and report about that quality are four different records. The branch
can therefore investigate their relation without treating a wavelength as an
experience or a word as the quality it names.

The red-of-red construction uses the exact period-three support

\[
\frac17 \longmapsto \frac27 \longmapsto \frac47
\longmapsto \frac17,
\qquad
\frac17 + \frac27 + \frac47 = 1.
\]

The three labels provide a finite self-reidentifying carrier whose parts close
to the One. The construction represents qualitative differentiation,
recurrence and whole participation. It does not equate **1/7**, **2/7** or
**4/7** with a wavelength, intensity, cortical voltage or universal measured
colour coordinate. Those dimensional mappings require registered evidence.

The returned synaesthesia claim adds a directional cross-link. A stable Fold
lock from one quality carrier to another need not reverse. This preserves the
observed possibility that one class of inducer reliably elicits another
quality while the reverse route is absent or different. The claim is
structural; the individual mappings, development, prevalence and neural
mechanisms remain empirical.

The returned nonordinary-state claim uses a three-quality orbit and preserves
the boundary that an internally generated report contains no external
information beyond the support supplied to the organised process. A vivid or
coherent experience can be scientifically real as an experience without being
evidence that an independently existing external event occurred. This protects
phenomenal report while preventing experience alone from manufacturing an
external observation.

### 18.7 Cessation and the continuation boundary

The cessation claim states that loss of the integrated half-One occupation
releases the unified process while component carriers and records may remain
separately held. This gives a structural distinction between loss of integrated
interiority and immediate destruction of every substrate component.

The result does not imply survival of the same person after the integrated
relation ends. Nor does it establish a medical criterion for death, which
belongs to clinical and legal evidence boundaries. It supplies a formal
carrier by which integration, remaining components and historical records can
be distinguished. Any empirical application must identify the organism,
measurement, reversibility, time and clinical context.

### 18.8 Substrate independence and artificial systems

Substrate independence is not established by copying the superficial input and
output behaviour of one system into another. The current claim requires a
complete bidirectional realisation map preserving state roles, transitions,
relations and causal participation. A one-way behavioural imitation or fluent
language model does not satisfy that criterion by itself.

The boundary remains open to artificial realisation. It does not decree that
only biological tissue can carry consciousness. It specifies what must be
shown before a different substrate can be said to realise the same organised
relation rather than merely emulate selected outputs. The distinction is
directly relevant to Unison Fold AI: architecture, checks and language output
may establish implementation facts, but they do not certify phenomenal
presence. The Consciousness foundation explicitly records that no current
application, including Unison, was used to select or confirm its laws.

### 18.9 External evidence and current closure

The foundation registered 15 source identities, 61 required source features,
58 present features, three absent full-text features and 18 failed transport or
content rows. Those absent and failed rows remain visible. The returned
validation family compares the new claims with 27 registered sources. At the
categorical comparison boundary, the source-derived distinctions match the
registered structures. Heterogeneity, null rows, non-purpose-matched material
and unresolved rows remain part of the record.

Observed evidence includes directional and asymmetric synaesthetic relations,
nonordinary reports with retained source boundaries, recurrent sleep and dream
organisation, and evidence relevant to integration and cessation. It does not
measure the exact Fold fractions as universal physiological magnitudes or
times. The validation is therefore evidence for the categorical carriers and
their distinctions, not a licence to report structural numbers as instrument
readings.

The branch reconciled 46 Consciousness-owned inherited questions, including
the stronger separately registered qualia-resonance and red-of-red
obligations. Corrections remain visible wherever earlier wording treated
access, report, correlation or computation as sufficient for experience. The
current foundation and returned family are complete; the full-field census,
quantitative neural mappings, broader prospective tests and artificial-
realisation programme remain open.

Consciousness hands Social Science identified agents with report, belief,
perspective, memory and action kept distinct. It hands Medicine a strict
boundary between first-person and objective evidence. It hands Engineering a
realisation criterion and a human-participant protocol boundary. It hands the
computational programme a question that cannot be answered by test counts or
fluent output alone.

---


### 18.10 Dependency-resolved family ledger

#### 18.10.1 Observation, access and binding

| Family | Problem | Admitted distinctions |
|---|---|---|
| Observation and Interior Observation | How can an observation belong to the observing process? | Act, external image, interior return, observer/observed roles, equivalence class, atomic moment and measurement boundary |
| Access, Report and Phenomenal Presence | What does public availability establish? | Access, report, presence, reportability, unavailable processing and altered-state evidence |
| Binding and Unity | How can several contents form one experience? | Plural contents, binding, unity, integration, synchrony distinction and fragmentation |
| Subject, Perspective and Interiority | What continues across changing content? | Perspective, interiority, subject carrier, self invariant, evidence perspectives and privacy |

The atomic observational moment is the least complete source–relation–image
unit at the branch boundary. It does not imply that measured experience is
instantaneous or that time has no further structure. Observation equivalence
classes collect states that share a public image while preserving the fact that
their predecessor labels may differ.

Active unavailable processing is evidence that information can influence a
system without becoming reportable content. It prevents “not reported” from
being read as “not processed” and prevents processing from being read as
phenomenal presence. Altered-state evidence adds condition, induction,
physiology, behaviour, report and recovery; the unusualness of a report does
not by itself decide its external reference.

Synchrony is neither necessary nor sufficient by name alone for binding. A
measured temporal correlation requires participating contents, causal route
and joint closure. Fragmentation and divided access demonstrate that one
organism may support partially separated access and report routes without the
observer declaring multiple or unified subjects from one behavioural sign.

First- and third-person evidence remain complementary. A participant has the
retained interior relation; an external observer has the public source path.
Phenomenal privacy does not make reports beyond criticism, and public
measurement does not exhaust what the participant retains.

#### 18.10.2 Self, memory, attention and cognition

| Family | New question | Resulting carrier |
|---|---|---|
| Self-Observation and Introspection | Can a process completely inspect itself? | Self-observation closure, blind spot, reconstructive history, loss, confabulation and deterministic self-opacity |
| Memory, Temporal Continuity and Identity | How is a changing process reidentified? | Memory carrier, persistence, recall, continuity, identity and cessation boundary |
| Finite Self-Model and Self-Simulation | Must self-reference regress indefinitely? | Representation, application, finite depth, simulation boundary and self-verification limit |
| Attention, Selection and Availability | How is one content made available among alternatives? | Focus, availability, capacity and switching |
| Cognition, Inference and Representation | How does the system carry and act on represented relations? | Representation, intentional relation, inference, anticipation, expectation and determined agency |

An introspective blind spot follows because the observation act changes or
selects the very process it observes. Accumulated introspection loss means that
a sequence of self-observations may compress predecessor detail. Confabulation
is not equated with deliberate falsehood; it is a reconstructed self-history
whose evidential route does not preserve the claimed predecessor. Deterministic
self-opacity is therefore possible: a determined process need not contain a
complete usable prediction of its own future state.

Recall is reconstructive rather than a perfect reopening of the past. A memory
record, retrieval cue, current state and report combine to produce the recalled
content. Temporal continuity can persist through change, while identity
continuity requires the source-located organisational route. Cessation names
loss of that continuing relation, not absence of all material or archival
records.

Attention is selection under capacity and switching constraints. Focus does
not establish phenomenal presence by itself, and unattended processing can
remain active. Cognitive representation has an intentional relation to what it
represents, but an internal token can be mistaken, incomplete or detached from
an external referent. Prediction and anticipation are internal forward
relations; expectation can shape selection among reachable states. Determined
agency preserves the system's own reason- and state-mediated action without
requiring ontological randomness.

#### 18.10.3 Realisation, quality and red-of-red

| Family | Boundary | Current result |
|---|---|---|
| Substrate Independence and Realisation | When do two implementations realise the same conscious organisation? | Carrier, structural criterion, bidirectional map, feed-forward limit, equivalence and AI evidence boundary |
| Qualia Differentiation, Resonance and Composition | How are qualities distinct, similar, recurrent and jointly bound? | Qualitative identity, similarity, resonance, recurrence, composition and cross-modal binding |
| The Red-of-Red | How can one quality reidentify itself without becoming its stimulus or word? | Stimulus/experience boundary, qualitative identity, period-three reidentification, recurrence, controls and empirical boundary |

Feed-forward organisation can transform inputs into outputs, but the current
structural criterion requires the relevant return and self-involving causal
relations. Realisation equivalence is bidirectional: each state role and
transition in one carrier must have a preserving correspondent in the other.
If the map preserves outputs but discards internal causal organisation, it is a
simulation or emulation at that boundary, not an established equivalent
realisation.

Qualitative similarity does not erase identity. Two experiences may share a
relation while remaining distinguishable. Resonance and recurrence require a
return path and retained label; composition requires a whole in which parts
remain. Cross-modal binding preserves modality-specific qualities while
admitting one integrated episode.

The red-of-red contrast controls are essential. A physical red stimulus without
the participant quality, the public word without stimulus, a report copied
without experience and another quality under similar task conditions must not
all be counted as the same event. The empirical boundary specifies which
records can support the period-three structural correspondence and which remain
unmeasured.

#### 18.10.4 The returned family and open artificial-realisation test

Directional synaesthesia, nonordinary experience, sleep–dream recurrence and
cessation exercise four different limits of the foundation: cross-modal
direction, internally generated content, state-dependent recurrence and loss
of integration. Their 27-source comparison is deliberately heterogeneous
because the claims do not share one instrument or effect size.

A future artificial-realisation test must do more than ask a system to say it
is conscious. It must freeze the candidate architecture and bidirectional
realisation map, identify causal interventions capable of distinguishing it
from an output-equivalent feed-forward system, preserve negative and
unavailable evidence, and keep report, behaviour, internal state and external
measurement separate. Participant-like rights and welfare questions must be
handled through a declared ethical bridge before potentially harmful testing.

This is why Unison's current `NOT_RUN` empirical state is scientifically
appropriate. There is no lawful Consciousness result to infer from a scaffold,
from inherited V2 certificates or from a language output alone.


## 19. Earth and Environmental Sciences: one world, many coupled ledgers

### 19.1 Why a planetary branch is required

Physics supplies universal quantities, fields, matter and propagation laws.
Chemistry supplies entities, reactions and composition. Materials Science and
Biology add heterogeneous structures and living processes. Earth Science is
still required because a planet is a source-dated, bounded history in which
all of those carriers interact. Universal law does not determine the actual
inventory, layering, forcing history, observation network or future trajectory
of one world without initial conditions and measured boundary records.

The Earth foundation therefore proceeds as a series of connected ledgers:
Earth-system identity and observation; energy, matter and information budgets;
geological material and history; interior and geodynamics; stress, rupture,
seismicity and volcanism; hydrosphere and cryosphere; atmosphere, weather and
Earth–ionosphere recurrence; oceans and coasts; climate, forcing, feedback,
attribution and tipping; biogeochemical and ecological coupling;
environmental transport and quality; and field, remote-sensing, forecast and
hazard handoffs.

The published foundation contains 74 claims. One returned tipping claim brings
the current total to 75 claims, 19,200 candidates, 75 survivors and 300
controls. The foundation and returned claim are complete at current evidence.
A field-wide Earth and Environmental Sciences census has not yet been frozen.

### 19.2 Earth identity, reservoirs and complete budgets

An Earth-system statement first requires a declared system boundary. Reservoir
identity, location, phase, composition, observation time and interface must be
retained before transfers can be calculated. A stock is not a flow. A measured
concentration is not a total inventory. A local sample is not a global mean.
A modelled field is not an instrument reading.

Energy, matter and information budgets must therefore identify sources,
stocks, flows, transformations, sinks, times and residuals. A budget that
omits an external supply or hides an unmeasured sink is not closed because its
displayed columns happen to balance. Likewise, a numerical residual is not
automatically a new physical effect: uncertainty, sampling, calibration,
processing and missing components remain alternative explanations until they
are tested.

The geological ledger adds order and provenance. Layer, material, process,
age, deformation, exposure and sampling history remain linked. A present
outcrop is evidence about a retained geological record, not the past event
itself. Reconstruction therefore remains distinct from observation. The
planetary-interior and geodynamic families make the same distinction between
surface measurement, inferred structure, dynamic model and historical claim.

### 19.3 Water, ice, atmosphere, oceans and living coupling

Hydrosphere and cryosphere claims retain phase, reservoir, transfer route,
residence time, forcing and observation. A glacier, sea-ice field, aquifer,
river or vapour column occupies a different carrier and cannot be substituted
for the others in a planetary ledger. Melting, freezing, accumulation, flow
and loss require source- and time-bound records.

Atmosphere and weather add coupled motion, composition, radiation and
measurement networks. Weather describes state and evolution on its declared
spatiotemporal boundary; climate describes distributions and persistent
relations over another boundary. Neither is made more exact by collapsing one
into the other. The Earth–ionosphere result derives bounded distinct recurrence
modes, but it does not derive a universal dimensional frequency in hertz. A
measured resonance requires Earth geometry, propagation conditions,
instrumentation and uncertainty.

Ocean and coast claims similarly preserve basin, depth, circulation, heat,
salt, chemistry, biology, boundary exchange and observation. A surface record
does not uniquely reconstruct an unobserved deep-ocean history without a
model, and a model projection is not a measured future. Coastal hazard depends
on exposure and vulnerability as well as the physical event.

Biogeochemical and ecological coupling connect living stocks and processes to
planetary reservoirs. Carbon, nutrient and contaminant relations retain
chemical identity and phase. Transport is not transformation; a substance may
move without changing identity, change identity without leaving the system or
become unavailable to one method without being absent from the environment.

### 19.4 Climate, attribution and tipping

A climate forcing is not a feedback, an observed trend is not by itself a
causal attribution, and a projection is not a forecast unless its conditions
and issue time are fixed. The branch retains forcing source, response carrier,
feedback sign and path, uncertainty, counterfactual or comparison and the
observational record used to discriminate alternatives.

The foundation derives a two-basin tipping structure. The returned claim,
`SFT-EARTH-TIPPING-FOLD-LOCK-002`, identifies **1/4** and **3/4** as exact
normalised basin preimages of the common **1/2** boundary. That is a structural
Fold lock. It does not establish that every physical Earth subsystem tips at a
measured value of one-half. Dimensional thresholds depend on the actual
system, forcing path, state variables, rate, hysteresis, spatial scale and
measurement method.

This distinction makes the claim more useful. It supplies a finite grammar for
two retained basins and their common boundary while leaving empirical science
responsible for identifying the correct variables and values. It also
prevents a post hoc choice of units from manufacturing an apparent universal
fraction.

### 19.5 Earthquake magnitude–frequency prediction: adverse and compatible records

The Earth branch contains a prospectively registered quantitative test. Exact
candidate enumeration selected magnitude–frequency exponent **1** before the
catalogue was opened. For one unit increase in magnitude, that exponent gives
the registered exceedance share **1/10**.

The first test used a global 2010–2020 mixed-magnitude catalogue. It recorded

\[
N_{\ge 5}=18{,}469,
\qquad
N_{\ge 6}=1{,}494,
\]
