with the registered interval

\[
0.076998990840681139
\;\text{to}\;
0.084918164588559525.
\]

The interval excludes **1/10**. This result is **adverse** and remains adverse.
It is not reclassified because a later test used a different, more homogeneous
record.

The later preregistered holdout used a 2020–2026 homogeneous `mww` catalogue.
It recorded

\[
N_{\ge 6}=253,
\qquad
N_{\ge 7}=27,
\qquad
\frac{N_{\ge7}}{N_{\ge6}}=\frac{27}{253},
\]

with interval

\[
0.085657503235220589
\;\text{to}\;
0.13089188889528297.
\]

That interval contains **1/10**, so the holdout is **compatible** with the
registered exponent at its declared boundary. Compatibility is not
confirmation, and it does not erase the earlier adverse result. The two rows
together show why catalogue composition, magnitude scale, completeness and
chronology must remain part of the evidence.

### 19.6 Observation, retrieval, proxy, reconstruction and forecast

The Earth evidence constitution distinguishes direct field observation,
instrument retrieval, proxy, reconstruction, model, forecast, non-detection,
censoring, missingness and observed absence. A satellite retrieval is a
processed estimate tied to an algorithm and version. A proxy is an observed
carrier related to another state through a calibrated interpretation. A
reconstruction estimates a past state. A forecast is issued before its target
period under stated initial conditions. These distinctions cannot be collapsed
into the single word “data”.

Hazard is the possible damaging event or process. Risk additionally includes
exposure, vulnerability, probability or scenario and consequence. A high
physical hazard does not imply equal risk for all populations; a low recent
loss record does not prove absence of hazard when exposure or observation is
limited. Engineering owns the designed mitigations, while Social Science owns
collective decisions and unequal exposure.

The foundation registered 21 source identities and 91 required features: 67
were present and 24 absent. The original Smithsonian transport returned HTTP
403 and remains recorded as a failed transport. A separately registered WFS
addendum supplies its own evidence and provenance; it does not rewrite the
historical failure as success.

### 19.7 Earth handoffs and open frontier

Earth hands Astronomy a local observation, instrument and planetary-history
constitution without making Earth history universal. It hands Social Science
hazard, resource and environmental records without deciding policy or ethics.
It hands Engineering measured loads and environmental conditions without
certifying a design. It receives biological and chemical carriers while owning
their planetary coupling.

Current closure covers the 74-law foundation and returned tipping claim. The
field-wide census, additional prospective quantitative predictions, regional
and system-specific threshold mappings, long-horizon source updates and
expanded hazard and environmental validations remain open. The permanent
adverse earthquake row and the distinct compatible holdout are part of that
frontier, not editorial problems to be removed.

---


### 19.8 Dependency-resolved family ledger

#### 19.8.1 The planet as a sequence of constrained ledgers

The Earth branch is organised as twelve ledgers because each later claim
depends on distinctions preserved by the earlier one. The ledger structure
also prevents a climate, hazard or environmental conclusion from floating free
of the source and observation system by which it was obtained.

| Ledger family | Carrier added | Evidence boundary preserved |
|---|---|---|
| 1. Earth-System Identity, Reservoirs, Boundaries and Observation | One identified Earth system, reservoirs, interfaces, state and observation | Absence, non-detection and missingness remain different |
| 2. Planetary Energy, Matter and Information Budgets | Sources, sinks, stocks, flows, storage and residual | No hidden input or unclassified residual is called closure |
| 3. Geological Material, Layer, Provenance and History | Material identity, stratigraphic order, cycles and dated provenance | Present observation remains distinct from past reconstruction |
| 4. Planetary Interior and Geodynamics | Interior compartments, heat sources, transport, crust–mantle and plate relations | Inferred interior remains tied to geophysical observations and models |
| 5. Stress, Rupture, Seismicity and Volcanism | Stress/strain, rupture, propagation, catalogue, magnitude–frequency and eruption | Event, signal, catalogue row and population law remain distinct |
| 6. Hydrosphere and Cryosphere | Water reservoirs, phase handoff, cycle, groundwater, surface networks and ice | Phase, location, transfer route and observation time retained |
| 7. Atmosphere, Weather and Earth–Ionosphere Recurrence | Composition, radiation, moisture, circulation, forecast and bounded recurrence | No dimensional resonance is derived from the structural mode alone |
| 8. Ocean and Coast | Ocean identity, salinity, stratification, circulation, exchange and coastal response | Surface, depth, basin and boundary processes cannot be substituted |
| 9. Climate, Forcing, Feedback, Attribution and Tipping | Climate state, timescale, forcing, feedback, variability, attribution and basin transition | Structural half-boundary is not a universal measured threshold |
| 10. Biogeochemical and Ecological Coupling | Carbon, nitrogen, phosphorus, biosphere exchange and biodiversity boundary | Chemical identity and biological process remain owned upstream |
| 11. Environmental Transport, Transformation and Quality | Pollutant identity, source–path–receptor, persistence, accumulation and quality | Transport, transformation, non-detection and harm remain different |
| 12. Field Evidence, Remote Sensing, Forecasts and Hazard Handoffs | Sampling, retrieval, proxy, assimilation, validation, hazard and risk | Observation, reconstruction, model and forecast retain chronology |

The first ledger makes state conditional on system boundary. An atmospheric
inventory and a whole-Earth inventory answer different questions. A reservoir
is identified by contents, location and interface; the same substance can move
between reservoirs without violating matter conservation. An Earth-system
state is a source-dated composition of retained reservoir and relation states,
not a timeless label.

The budget ledger makes closure falsifiable. Source, sink and storage are roles
within a time window. A component can change role under another boundary. The
residual must remain as a measured or calculated difference with its
uncertainty. Calling the residual “zero” because a model imposes balance would
erase precisely the evidence that could reveal missing flux or measurement
error.

Geological order adds directional history. Stratigraphic succession,
cross-cutting relation, material transformation and provenance can constrain a
past sequence while leaving gaps. A cycle in geological material does not mean
that the same specimen returns unchanged. Provenance carries a path through
sources, transformations and deposits.

Interior science depends on surface and orbital observations, experiments and
models. Internal heat sources and transport are not direct views into the
planet. Plate motion, deformation and boundary type require coordinate,
velocity, uncertainty and time. The branch can therefore derive a complete
geodynamic claim without claiming that one measurement uniquely reconstructs
the entire mantle history.

#### 19.8.2 Event populations and the seismic falsification route

Stress and strain describe state; rupture is a transition; seismic propagation
connects the event to a sensor; an earthquake catalogue classifies records;
magnitude–frequency is a population relation. These stages make the exponent
test interpretable. A mismatch can arise because the proposed population law
is wrong, the catalogue is heterogeneous, magnitude types are mixed, the
completeness threshold is misidentified or the interval rule is unsuitable.
The first registered global test remains adverse because its own boundary was
fixed. The homogeneous holdout asks a related but not identical question and
is compatible.

Volcanic process and eruption record follow the same event–observation split.
Unrest, eruption, deposit, remote signal and catalogue entry are different
carriers. A period without a detected eruption is not proof of absent activity
when monitoring is incomplete.

#### 19.8.3 Coupled water, air, ocean and climate histories

The hydrological cycle is not one circulating token. It composes many
reservoir-specific transitions with residence times and phase changes.
Groundwater may be hidden from direct observation; rivers and lakes form
directed networks; the cryosphere stores and releases water while changing
albedo, sea level and habitat. Each coupling is typed rather than inferred from
the common word “water”.

Atmospheric composition affects radiative transfer, while radiation, moisture
and circulation influence weather state. A forecast must be sealed before its
verification period. Reanalysis or hindcast can reconstruct past conditions
but cannot become a prospective forecast by later wording. The Earth–ionosphere
cavity adds a bounded recurrent carrier whose observed frequencies remain
condition- and dimension-bound.

Ocean stratification separates layers with different density and composition.
Circulation moves properties through a basin; air–sea exchange crosses a
boundary; tides and coasts add forcing and geometry. These relations feed the
climate ledger but remain independently observable. An ocean heat uptake
estimate cannot be inferred solely from a surface air-temperature series.

Climate detection asks whether an observed change exceeds a declared
variability and uncertainty boundary. Attribution asks which forcing or causal
combination best accounts for it. Internal variability remains an alternative
component, not a universal explanation. Tipping adds basin, state variable,
path and hysteresis. The exact Fold lock supplies a structural relation but
must be mapped to measured systems separately.

#### 19.8.4 Environmental consequence and decision handoff

A pollutant remains chemically identified across a source–path–receptor
relation. Transformation may create a new entity with different persistence or
toxicity. Accumulation requires a time and compartment ledger. Environmental
quality is a measured state relative to a declared use, organism, regulation
or ecological condition; it is not intrinsic to one concentration without
context.

Field sampling records location, time, specimen, method and custody. Remote
sensing adds propagation, instrument and retrieval. A proxy adds calibration
to another state; assimilation combines observation with model state;
forecast validation compares a pre-issued result with later observation.
Hazard then passes to Engineering and Social Science with the physical event
kept distinct from exposure, vulnerability, mitigation and choice.

The Earth branch can be falsified at every stage. A budget may fail closure, a
source attribution may fail against alternatives, a forecast may miss its
registered interval, a tipping mapping may lack a stable basin or a source
transport may be unavailable. Keeping those failures typed is what permits
theory revision without rewriting planetary history.


## 20. Astronomy and Cosmology: from one sky to a source-custodied cosmos

### 20.1 The astronomical observation chain

Astronomy begins where a source beyond the immediate observer must be known
through propagation and instrumentation. The least complete relation is

\[
\text{source} \longrightarrow \text{path} \longrightarrow
\text{observer} \longrightarrow \text{instrument} \longrightarrow
\text{record}.
\]

A brightness, coordinate, spectrum or catalogue label detached from that
chain is not an admitted astronomical fact. Propagation can transform the
signal; instruments have calibration and selection boundaries; processing can
change a record; catalogues have releases, row identities and censoring. Each
stage must therefore be retained for reconstruction and falsification.

The published foundation contains 72 claims across observation geometry,
object identity, radiation, motion and time, stellar systems, planetary and
small bodies, interstellar enrichment and formation, galaxies and populations,
compact and high-energy multimessenger astronomy, large-scale expansion,
early-universe background and abundance, dark and horizon boundaries and
prediction handoffs. Five returned claims add solar, atomic, planetary and
lithium structures and a source-bound validation. The current branch contains
77 claims, 19,712 candidates, 77 survivors and 308 controls. The foundation
and returned family are complete; the field-wide census remains unfrozen.

### 20.2 Object identity, populations and cosmic history

An astronomical object is identified through source position, epoch,
uncertainty, association rule and retained alternatives. A match across
catalogues or epochs is an inference, not an identity conferred by a similar
name. Blends, transients, proper motion, resolution and changing source state
can all alter the relation. A population additionally requires a selection
function, detection boundary, censored and missing rows, volume or exposure
and release version.

The branch separates cosmic law from cosmic history. Physics owns universal
propagation, gravity, matter and constant relations admitted upstream.
Astronomy owns source-dated objects, initial conditions, populations and
chronology. An instrument cannot select a universal law, while a universal law
cannot by itself reconstruct which stars, galaxies or structures actually
formed in this cosmos.

Stars and planetary systems are consequently described through source,
composition, structure, energy and evolutionary records. Interstellar matter,
enrichment and formation retain transport and chemical provenance. Compact
objects and multimessenger events retain channel, path, timing, localisation
and association. Large-scale expansion and early-universe evidence retain the
observation-to-model bridge rather than presenting a fitted historical model
as a direct observation.

### 20.3 The rank-four luminosity–rotation prediction and its adverse test

The branch fixed structural rank **4** before opening the galaxy data. Its
first preregistered SPARC comparison used an all-positive-row unweighted fit
and obtained the slope

\[
3.486344605219551\ldots .
\]

That registered comparison is **adverse** to exact rank four and remains
adverse. It is not overwritten by a different analysis.

A separate primary-source method record reports an uncertainty-aware
orthogonal slope of

\[
3.85 \pm 0.09,
\]

with systematic movement from **3.5** to **4.0**. Exact rank four lies at the
reported systematic boundary. This is relevant context and support for the
importance of method choice, but it does not reclassify the first test. The
two records retain different selection and fitting boundaries.

The result demonstrates the value of a chronology-preserving theory. A
prediction is meaningful because it can fail. A later compatible method or
boundary may motivate a sharper preregistration, but cannot make the earlier
measurement disappear. Future work must specify the sample, variables,
uncertainty model and fitting method before the target is inspected.

### 20.4 Returned solar, atomic, planetary and lithium structures

The returned solar claim identifies a unit magnitude–frequency relation as an
inverse binary share at its exact carrier. The atomic claim records a
one-Fold burst completion. The planetary claim generates a depth-seven binary
ladder from **1/128** to the One. The lithium-7 claim maps **3/16** through one
Fold to **3/32**, with doubling restoring the prior share. Each is a formal
structure registered before its source-bound categorical comparison.

The 25-source validation preserves several distinct empirical outcomes.
Tipping and multistability are observed across relevant systems, but measured
thresholds are system-specific. Solar, radio and other transient classes show
heterogeneous, frequently non-unit magnitude–frequency exponents. Binary-like
spacing occurs in some planetary records but is not universal. The lithium
direction is retained, while a factor-of-few mismatch and mechanism
uncertainty remain. These are not minor caveats: they are the current evidence
classification.

The categorical validation passes at the registered comparison boundary, but
some exact quantitative mappings remain unresolved or adverse. The returned
validation does not alter the protected earthquake, Parker, Tully–Fisher or
ringdown records and does not backdate later evidence into an earlier
prediction.

### 20.5 Dark sectors, horizons and generated alternatives

The branch keeps direct detection, dynamical inference, lensing, background
evidence, model-dependent reconstruction and unobserved alternatives separate.
Dark matter and dark energy statements are therefore not reduced to either
“directly seen objects” or “mere absence”. Their evidential roles depend on the
specific observation, model and alternative being tested.

A horizon limits an observation relation; it does not make an unobserved state
equal to nothing. A mathematically generated alternative is not an observed
world. The multiverse boundary is therefore explicit: formal multiplicity may
define alternatives within a model, but observation and empirical status
require a source relation that the generated alternative does not obtain by
definition.

Observation also cannot recover an unheld predecessor. When distinct Fold
preimages share one image, the image alone cannot tell which predecessor
occurred. A retained historical or experimental record may make one route
reconstructible; inference cannot manufacture the missing distinction after
it was discarded.

### 20.6 Sources, corrections and handoff

The foundation registered 15 primary source identities. Thirteen transports
succeeded and two failed transports remain visible. Fifty-three Astronomy-
owned V1/V2 questions were mapped to current claims without importing their
old answers. The returned validation used 25 sources and retained
heterogeneity, mismatches and unresolved mechanisms.

Astronomy hands Cross-Branch Synthesis source-custodied cosmic correspondences
only after Physics and Astronomy have each preserved their ownership. It hands
Engineering instrument and observation requirements, not a certified device.
It hands Social Science no authority to turn cosmic analogy into social law.

Current closure covers the foundation and five returned claims. Open work
includes the field-wide census, sharper preregistered quantitative tests,
additional source captures, object- and population-specific histories, dark-
sector discrimination, system-specific planetary ladders and the unresolved
or adverse mappings preserved above. Completion of this branch boundary is not
completion of observational astronomy or cosmology.

---


### 20.7 Dependency-resolved family ledger

#### 20.7.1 From source geometry to stellar and planetary histories

| Family | New unresolved problem | Admitted surface |
|---|---|---|
| Observation Geometry | How does a remote source become a record? | Source, observer, signal, frame, distance, survey selection and non-detection |
| Object Identity and Classification | Which records belong to one object or population? | Identity, class, population, morphology, composition inference and catalogue |
| Radiation, Motion and Time | What transforms across the observation path? | Flux/luminosity, spectrum, redshift, astrometric/radial motion, variability and transient distinction |
| Stellar Systems | How does a star form, persist and end? | Formation, structure, energy generation, evolution, population and terminal state |
| Planetary and Small Bodies | How are bound systems and their members classified? | System identity, orbit, resonance/tide, planet/satellite, exoplanet observation and small-body population |
| Interstellar Enrichment and Formation | How do matter and composition pass between generations? | Medium, dust, cloud, enrichment, formation environment and stellar feedback |

Astronomical distance is never an unqualified number. Its method, calibration,
reference and uncertainty are part of the record. Angular position requires a
frame and epoch. Survey selection and non-detection require coverage,
sensitivity and processing. These constraints precede object classification
because the visible sample has already been shaped by the observation system.

Morphology is a source- and resolution-bound description. Composition is
inferred through spectra, models and calibration rather than directly handled
at the telescope. A catalogue preserves source rows, release version,
selection, cross-match and changes. Removing a row from a later release does
not delete its historical presence.

Flux is an observed received relation; luminosity is a reconstructed source
property requiring distance and propagation. Redshift can contain several
physical and observational contributions. Variability is change across
measurements; periodicity requires a retained recurrence and alternatives;
a transient requires onset, duration, non-detection and event classification.

Stellar energy generation uses admitted Physics and Chemistry but Astronomy
owns the source history and population. Stellar formation, evolution and
terminal state require composition, mass or structural parameters, environment
and time. An inferred sequence across a population is not automatically a
direct longitudinal observation of one star.

An orbit is a relation among identified bodies under a coordinate and time
boundary. Resonance and tidal state require measured periods or dynamics and
uncertainty. Exoplanet observation keeps transit, radial velocity, imaging,
microlensing or another method separate from the reconstructed planet. Small-
body populations preserve detection and orbit-selection effects.

Interstellar enrichment closes the material history between generations.
Dust, gas and molecular clouds occupy different observational and chemical
carriers. Stellar feedback changes the environment from which later objects
form, but the existence of a feedback channel does not uniquely reconstruct
the formation history of a particular source.

#### 20.7.2 Galaxies, compact objects and large-scale structure

| Family | Principal relation | Critical evidence distinction |
|---|---|---|
| Galaxies and Populations | Identity, structure, dynamics, luminosity–rotation, evolution and environment | Registered scaling test remains distinct from later methods |
| Compact, High-Energy and Multimessenger | Remnant and black-hole candidate classes, transient, channel association and population | Horizon inference is not direct inspection of an interior |
| Large-Scale Expansion | Statistical organisation, cosmic web, expansion, distance ladder, lensing and history | Universal law remains separate from contingent history and fitted reconstruction |
| Early Background and Abundance | Thermal history, microwave background, acoustic structure, abundances, recombination and perturbations | Present signals are evidence for reconstructed earlier states |
| Dark, Horizon and Unobserved | Dark matter, dark energy, horizon, topology, multiverse and fate | Generated or inferred carriers are not relabelled direct observations |
| Inference, Prediction and Handoffs | Law/history distinction, model boundary, standing prediction, anomaly, astrobiology and ownership | Prediction chronology and non-detection remain explicit |

Galaxy identity must survive substructure, mergers, changing morphology and
catalogue release. Dynamics relate source motion and mass inference under a
model. The luminosity–rotation family becomes a quantitative test only after
variables, sample and fit are frozen. This is why the unweighted slope and the
orthogonal uncertainty-aware slope cannot be averaged into a retrospective
success.

Compact-object classification distinguishes observed signals from inferred
source. A black-hole candidate and horizon evidence retain the alternatives
and measurement route. Multimessenger association requires independent
channels, timing, localisation, common-source model and chance-coincidence
control. A coincident alert is not automatically one event.

Large-scale statistical organisation retains survey mask, selection, distance
inference and covariance. The cosmic web is reconstructed from tracers rather
than directly observed in an unmediated volume. A distance ladder is a
composition of calibrations; its uncertainty and shared systematics must
propagate. Gravitational lensing is an observation relation that supports mass
and geometry inference under retained alternatives.

The cosmic microwave background is a measured present radiation field used to
reconstruct an earlier state. Acoustic structure, abundance and recombination
claims retain model and source identities. Early perturbations are inferred
through later records. The branch can therefore make exact history claims only
at the boundary established by those records; it does not turn model
parameters into premise-free observations.

Dark-matter and dark-energy evidence are typed families, not single verdicts.
Dynamical, lensing, background and expansion records may jointly support an
inferred sector while direct detection remains unresolved. Cosmic topology and
ultimate fate are model- and observation-bound. Multiverse admissibility is a
formal boundary: mathematical generation establishes a possible model member,
not an observed universe.

#### 20.7.3 Returned claims and next observations

The solar/radio, atomic, planetary and lithium returns probe whether exact Fold
structures recur across four observational classes. Their value lies partly in
the non-uniform evidence. Heterogeneous exponents prevent a universal unit law;
some binary-like spacing prevents a universal absence claim; lithium retains
the predicted direction while exposing a magnitude and mechanism gap.

A next quantitative campaign must therefore register one source population,
release, event definition, completeness surface, exact statistic, interval and
decision rule for each claim. Reusing a structural label across solar bursts,
planetary spacing and atomic recurrence cannot create one common measurement
protocol. Typed common form is the job of Synthesis; dimensional evidence
remains with Astronomy and Physics.


## 21. Social and Collective Systems: from one relation to society

### 21.1 The least social carrier

Social inquiry begins neither with an anonymous average nor with an
institutional name. Its least admissible carrier retains identified people or
agents, typed relations, context, time, capabilities, selection and available
alternatives. A group is not one indivisible person. An institution is not the
word printed on its building. A population is not the favourable respondents
who remained visible after non-response and attrition.

This branch depends on Biology, Medicine and Consciousness because collective
relations involve living, vulnerable, perceiving and acting participants. It
also depends on Information Science and Computation because messages, records,
networks and decisions must be represented. None of those upstream branches
owns the new relation among agents, the institution that persists across its
members, the distribution of resources and power, the source-custodied
history, or the bridge between measured facts and declared values.

The published foundation contains 72 claims in twelve families. Four returned
claims add preferential-flow inequality, consensus/polarisation, dissipative
social recurrence and a 21-source validation. Together the two current Social
ledgers contain 76 claims, 19,456 candidates, 76 survivors and 304 controls.
The foundation and returned family are complete; a full-field census is not
yet frozen.

### 21.2 Aggregation and the individual–collective boundary

Aggregation is a many-to-one operation. A collective total or mean may be
lawful and useful, but it cannot recover individual predecessors that were not
retained. This closes both directions of a common inference error. A
population average cannot silently become the state of each member, and one
person's record cannot silently become a population distribution.

The observation surface must therefore retain eligibility, recruitment,
participation, non-response, measurement, context and time. Selection is part
of the result. A platform's visible users are not automatically its affected
population; recorded actions are not automatically beliefs; institutional
records are not automatically complete histories of those excluded from the
institution.

The returned preferential-flow claim derives exact complementary shares that
conserve the One. It supplies a finite carrier for cumulative advantage and
unequal flow without losing the remainder. The structural relation does not
declare one universal empirical wealth distribution or inequality exponent.
Observed inequality depends on the resource, access rule, inheritance,
network, policy, time and measurement boundary.

### 21.3 Communication, belief and coordinated action

Social observation is intervention-sensitive. The observer, method, setting,
consent and the participants' knowledge of observation can alter the relation.
This is not a reason to abandon empirical study; it is a reason to record the
intervention made by observation.

Communication requires a complete route. Sending does not prove receipt;
receipt does not prove understanding; understanding does not prove belief;
belief does not prove action; action does not by itself establish intent. Each
transition has its own carrier, loss and alternative explanations. Digital
counts such as impressions, clicks or reposts are therefore evidence only for
their registered event definitions.

Coordination retains participants, shared or competing aims, available
actions, information, timing, rules and outcomes. Conflict retains opposed
relations, resources, constraints and costs. A coordinated outcome need not
indicate consensus, and conflict need not indicate total social breakdown.
Both can occur inside one continuing collective organisation.

### 21.4 Norms, institutions, power and legitimacy

Norm, rule, institution, enforcement, compliance and legitimacy are different
structures. Majority behaviour alone does not establish a norm; a written rule
does not prove enforcement; compliance does not prove agreement; and effective
coercion does not establish legitimate authority. Institutional identity
requires roles, rules, records, succession, resources and boundaries that can
persist while particular office-holders change.

Power is directional and dependency-bound. It requires an actor, a target, a
resource or gate, alternatives, costs, resistance and the capacity to change
the target's available actions. Status, wealth, title or a favourable outcome
can be evidence relevant to power, but none establishes the complete relation
alone. Exclusion similarly requires an eligibility or access boundary and the
path by which an agent's options were removed.

Scientific gatekeeping is included in this empirical surface. Criteria,
credentials, costs, decisions, reviews, appeals and lost contributions may be
observed and criticised. Institutional prestige is never a proof operator.
At the same time, criticism of a gate does not automatically admit the
critic's scientific claim. SFT's anti-institutional position is therefore
operational rather than merely rhetorical: no credential, consensus, journal,
laboratory or public authority can replace the derivation and evidence record;
and no declaration of independence can replace it either.

### 21.5 Consensus, polarisation and recurrent collective dynamics

Consensus is a social event, not a truth operator. A collective decision must
retain eligibility, options, information, aggregation rule, participation,
dissent, implementation and affected parties. Majority outcome is not
unanimity, factual truth or ethical legitimacy.

The returned consensus/polarisation claim makes the information boundary
exact. Distinct retained preimages can share the common **1/2** image. A public
aggregate at the common image therefore cannot distinguish whether the
collective arrived there through agreement, balanced opposition or another
retained composition. Polarised preimages may survive under an apparently
centred result. Individual and subgroup records are required to tell the
difference.

The returned dissipative-social claim uses the period-two orbit

\[
\frac13 \longleftrightarrow \frac23.
\]

The carrier never rests at the balance point. It provides an exact form for a
continuing boom–bust, expansion–contraction or alternating collective process.
It is not a universal business-cycle period, electoral forecast or measured
social constant. Synchronisation, hysteresis, external intervention,
institutional constraint and heterogeneous timing remain empirical.

Network diffusion and cascade claims follow the same rule. A connection does
not prove influence, temporal succession does not prove contagion and a
cluster does not prove common belief. The record must distinguish directed
exposure, common causes, adoption, non-adoption and observation windows.
No universal social tipping constant is admitted.

### 21.6 Knowledge, history, causality and ethics

Knowledge claims preserve source, record, transmission, access, correction and
uncertainty. Reputation can guide attention but cannot substitute for evidence.
Culture and language are transmitted through people, practices and records;
neither becomes an unchanging substance independent of those carriers.

Historical claims require dated, source-custodied paths. A later state is not
its own cause and cannot uniquely reconstruct the route by which it arose.
Archives preserve perspectives and gaps as well as content. Causal social
claims retain assignment or comparison, confounding, spillover, attrition and
target population. Interventions retain delivery, uptake, adaptation, harms
and missing outcomes.

Ethical judgement is neither erased nor disguised as measurement. A normative
claim must identify affected agents, rights, harms, benefits, power and
disagreement. Frequency does not establish permission; popularity does not
establish justice; and a moral commitment does not become a measured causal
effect. Empirical and normative claims can interact only through a declared
bridge.

This is integral to the Ernos Labs open-science mission. The platform separates
credentials from evidence and criticism from admission. Public access,
reproducibility and contestability are institutional commitments, while the
truth status of an individual scientific claim still depends on its derivation,
evidence class and falsification record.

### 21.7 Evidence boundary and open continuation

The foundation registered 14 primary custodians. Ten source transports
succeeded and four failed transports remain failed. The inherited five Social-
owned questions were reconciled without importing their prior answers. The
returned family used 21 sources and preserves evidence for cumulative and
preferential advantage, controversy and dissent, boom–bust behaviour,
hysteresis, variable synchronisation, intervention, reversal and institutional
dependence. Unresolved causal relations remain unresolved.

Social Science hands Engineering identified users, institutions, access and
governance relations without certifying a technical system. It hands
Cross-Branch Synthesis typed collective carriers without allowing physical or
biological metaphors to become social identities. It receives medical and
cognitive evidence while preserving privacy, agency and population boundaries.

Current completion covers the foundation and four returned claims. Open work
includes the field-wide census, broader populations, prospective interventions,
institutional and historical source capture, cross-cultural transport,
quantitative mappings and the explicit normative programme. Society remains
extension-open by constitution.

---


### 21.8 Dependency-resolved family ledger

#### 21.8.1 Agents, records and coordinated institutions

| Family | Owned relations | Conflation prevented |
|---|---|---|
| Agents, Groups and Identity | Agent, relation, group, collective state, organisation and population | Group is not one person; population is not visible sample |
| Observation, Action and Communication | Observation, action, message, shared record, common knowledge and noise | Send, receive, believe and act remain distinct |
| Coordination and Conflict | Coordination, cooperation, competition, conflict, bargaining and collective action | Common outcome does not imply common aim |
| Norm, Institution and Enforcement | Norm, rule, institution, enforcement, compliance/resistance and change | Frequency, rule, enforcement and legitimacy remain separate |
| Trust, Reputation and Knowledge | Trust relation, reputation, testimony, collective knowledge, expertise and false information | Prestige and repetition are not proof |
| Exchange, Allocation and Distribution | Exchange, valuation, allocation, production, distribution/inequality and mobility | Price, value, access and welfare are not interchangeable |

A social agent is identified by the capability and relation relevant to the
claim. The branch can include people, organisations or artificial agents only
when their level and available actions are explicit. Group identity adds a
membership and continuation rule. Organisation identity adds roles, records
and succession. Population identity adds selection and coverage.

Common knowledge is stronger than several agents separately receiving a
message. It requires the recursively shared relation established by the
communication conditions. Noise can enter transmission, interpretation or
record. A shared document is not proof that every eligible agent read or
accepted it.

Bargaining retains alternatives and costs; cooperation retains a jointly
continued relation; competition retains incompatible claims on a resource or
outcome; conflict retains active opposition. Collective action adds the path
from individual capabilities to a joint effect. These relations can overlap
without becoming synonyms.

Norms may be inferred from expectations and sanctions, while rules have an
explicit source and content. Enforcement is an action, compliance an observed
relation, resistance a contrary or evasive action and institutional change a
transition in rules, roles or practices. Legitimacy requires a normative and
social bridge beyond effectiveness.

Trust is directional: one agent accepts vulnerability with respect to another
under a task and evidence boundary. Reputation is a transmitted social record.
Testimony has source, content, chain and credibility. Expertise is relevant
evidence about capability but never an operator that makes a proposition true.
False and unsupported information are classified by evidence, not by the
status of the speaker.

Exchange retains what each party gives and receives, the rule and the available
alternatives. Social valuation may differ among agents and from exchange price.
Allocation identifies the gate by which resources are assigned; production
identifies contributors and inputs; distribution identifies who holds the
result. Inequality and mobility require time and population, not one snapshot
or one anecdote.

#### 21.8.2 Power, networks, culture and multi-level causation

| Family | Added surface | Evidence limit |
|---|---|---|
| Power, Authority and Exclusion | Power, authority, dependency, exclusion, stratification and gatekeeping | Title, wealth or outcome alone does not establish the directional relation |
| Network, Diffusion and Transition | Network, diffusion, synchronisation, cascade, transition and cycle | Association and common cause remain alternatives to influence |
| Culture, Language and Transmission | Culture, symbol, language community, education, media and memory | Recorded text is not the whole living practice |
| Multi-Level Causation | Individual–collective composition, downward constraint, emergence, mixed-agent collective, decision and aggregation boundary | Neither level may silently replace the other |
| History, Intervention and Ethics | Event, contingency, causal inference, intervention, replication and value boundary | Later state is not its own cause; frequency is not moral authority |
| Ownership Handoffs | Mathematics, computation, biology, medicine, Earth and engineering routes | Imported analogy cannot acquire social ownership |

Authority is institutionally recognised power under a role and rule. Dependency
identifies what one agent requires from another. Stratification is a continuing
ordered access relation. Scientific gatekeeping becomes measurable through
criteria, decision, appeal, cost and exclusion while scientific admission
remains governed by evidence.

Network representation retains node and edge definitions. Diffusion requires
ordered exposure and adoption; synchronisation requires measured phase or
state relation; cascade requires dependency through events; collective
transition requires a before, path and after. Socio-economic cycling remains
condition-bound, which is why the returned period-two carrier is a structural
model rather than a universal calendar.

Culture continues through practice and transmission while changing. A symbol
has a community-bound meaning relation. Education and media create structured
access and selection, not direct implantation of belief. Collective memory is
a maintained public and social record that may differ from each participant's
memory and from source-custodied history.

Emergence names a collective relation that is not attributable to one member
alone, but it does not remove the member-level carrier. Downward constraint
changes available individual actions through a collective relation. Mixed
human–artificial systems must retain which agent generated, selected or acted
on a proposal. Collective decisions preserve dissent and implementation.

Cross-context replication asks whether the relation survives a new population,
institution, time and measurement practice. A numerical match without
institutional transport may be superficial. Ethical evaluation then declares
the value bridge rather than pretending it was read from the data.

#### 21.8.3 Returned exact relations and their empirical limits

Preferential-flow inequality, the polarisation lock and dissipative recurrence
form a coherent sequence. Unequal flows alter retained shares; a common image
can hide opposed predecessors; an alternating collective can persist without
resting at nominal balance. The source comparison finds real cumulative
advantage, controversy, cycles, hysteresis and reversals while retaining
institutional dependence and unresolved causation.

Future validation must resist the temptation to choose only famous social
examples. A prospective programme should define resource, population, edge,
event, time and intervention before observing the target. It should retain
failed replications, policy changes, platform changes and populations for which
the proposed Fold carrier is not discriminating.


## 22. Engineering Translation: from an admitted law to a working world

### 22.1 Why Engineering comes after the sciences

Engineering may use every admitted upstream branch, but it cannot choose which
scientific law is true by building the artefact it desires. It begins with a
need or purpose, translates that need into verifiable requirements, generates
design alternatives, accounts for resources and failure, implements a system,
and then distinguishes verification against specification from validation
against intended use.

The published Engineering foundation contains 72 claims governing
requirements, functions, components, interfaces, architecture, resources,
efficiency, reliability, measurement, calibration, tolerance, alternatives,
optimisation, control, safety, verification, validation, accessibility,
lifecycle, scientific evidence and anomaly handoff. Eight returned claims
close the current Tesla, vacuum/inertial, vacuum-beat, sector-five/seven,
Smithium, complete protocol-family and consciousness/placebo protocol
obligations. The current branch contains 80 claims, 20,480 candidates, 80
survivors and 320 controls.

The eight returned claims are **formal protocol closures**. No experiment is
reported as run by those packages. Their empirical status is not inferred from
their complete candidate censuses or independent reconstructions.

### 22.2 Requirements, composition and complete resource accounts

A requirement is not a wish. It retains source, purpose, version, priority or
conflict and a verifiable acceptance condition. A function retains declared
input, transformation, output, loss and operating boundary. A product name or
demonstration cannot substitute for either.

An engineered system is a composition record. Components keep identity and
version. Interfaces retain endpoints, units, timing, permitted states and
errors. Architecture retains responsibility and failure propagation.
Integration retains build order, rollback and failed intermediate states. This
makes success reproducible and failure diagnosable.

Efficiency cannot exceed a complete resource account. Input, useful output,
external supply, internal stock, flow, time and loss must close within an
explicit boundary. A hidden cloud service, pretrained model, operator action or
stored resource is still an input. Capacity, reliability and availability are
load-, population-, duration- and observation-window bound rather than
universal properties of a named system.

### 22.3 Measurement, tolerance, test and acceptance

A display is not the measurand. Measurement requires an instrument, calibration
state, method, unit, resolution, uncertainty and traceable record. Adjustment
is not calibration, because changing an instrument does not establish its
relation to a reference. Tolerance must be fixed before the outcome; widening
it afterwards would change the test.

A demonstration shows that an artefact performed an observed action under
some conditions. A test compares a result with a frozen criterion. Acceptance
requires every required criterion, permitted exception and authority to be
specified. Institutional authority cannot convert a failed frozen criterion
into a passing result without an openly versioned requirement change.

Finite design optimisation is admitted only over a generated design space.
Alternatives, constraints, violations, ties and failures remain visible.
Trade-offs separate measured consequences from declared values. An opaque
oracle or hidden scalar preference cannot manufacture a unique optimum and
then present it as if it were forced by science.

### 22.4 Control, safety, resilience and lifecycle

A control claim retains the whole loop: plant, sensor, controller, actuator,
delay, bound, disturbance, noise and fault route. One settled trace does not
prove global or infinite-time stability. A merged output cannot reconstruct an
unheld internal state. Control performance therefore remains tied to the tested
operating envelope.

Safety is a scoped evidence case, not the absence of previously observed harm.
Hazard source, path, exposure, control, detection and consequence remain.
Fail-safe behaviour must reach a tested bounded safe state or visibly halt.
Defence layers are independent only when common causes, shared supplies,
software dependencies and bypass routes have been assessed.

Verification asks whether the built artefact satisfies its frozen
specification. Validation asks whether the verified artefact serves its
intended use and users under the actual use conditions. Bidirectional
traceability connects need, requirement, design, build, test and result.
Maintenance, versioning, decommissioning, environmental impact and recovery
remain part of the lifecycle rather than being omitted once a prototype works.

Public reproducibility includes macOS, Windows and Linux where the application
claims cross-platform operation. Platform, dependency, command, output and
difference must be recorded. A one-command route must traverse all declared
stages, show progress and duration, preserve failure and avoid mutating the
protected authority.

### 22.5 The current six-obligation experimental protocol family

The returned engineering work closes a formal protocol surface for proposed
tests. Its purpose is to prevent a favourable trace from selecting its own
apparatus, controls or interpretation after the event.

The Tesla resonant-transfer protocol requires a sealed, calibrated and
connected path; declared phase and load; a complete power ledger; and adverse
controls. Coupling, transmission and delivered load power remain distinct. An
apparent receiver output cannot become evidence of anomalous transfer while an
unaccounted conductive, radiative, stored or measurement path remains.

The vacuum/inertial-response protocol requires a registered drive, paired
measurement channels, confound controls, restoration and complete source
accounting. The vacuum-beat protocol separately distinguishes an outward
**1/6** structural share from returned-cycle restoration. Any net-gain claim
must halt if its ledger remains open. A structural fraction is not reported as
a measured excess.

The sector-five/seven protocol seals the relevant **p**-fibre before target
access and preserves background, systematics, nulls and outcomes outside the
registered list. It is a blind-detection design, not a report that a new sector
has already been discovered.

The Smithium protocol retains the exact **126/184/310** registered structure
and requires apparatus, yield, linked decay, mass, ion, oxidation and
spectroscopic records. Official discovery criteria remain distinct from the
SFT formal carrier. The protocol does not report a synthesis or discovery.

The human consciousness/placebo/cross-binding protocol retains participant
report, physiology, modality, expectation, blinding, the available-state
boundary, consent, privacy and adverse events. It cannot infer phenomenal
presence from physiology alone or physiological effect from report alone. The
no-omission addendum establishes the complete six-obligation experimental family.

These eight current engineering additions have 2,048 candidates, eight
survivors and 32 controls. Their exact closure is meaningful: the proposed
tests have a complete registered structure. It is not empirical validation.
At the current repository state, **no experiment in this returned protocol
family is claimed as performed**.

### 22.6 Scientific anomaly handoff and current status

Application performance cannot select or retroactively prove a fundamental
law. A failed build may falsify the artefact's own operating claim, expose a
translation error or generate a question for an upstream branch. It does not
automatically falsify every upstream theorem. Likewise, a working artefact may
validate its intended use but cannot substitute for the derivation and evidence
of the laws it uses.

Simulation remains simulation, demonstration remains demonstration and test
remains test. A validated anomaly is returned to the categorical owner—Physics,
Chemistry, Biology, Consciousness or another branch—with its complete apparatus
and evidence record. Engineering does not acquire the anomaly by naming it.

The foundation registered 13 source custodians. Twelve transports succeeded;
the `FDA-DESIGN-CONTROLS-001` transport failed and remains failed. Seven
Engineering-owned inherited questions were reconciled. Current completion
covers the foundation and the eight protocol claims. The full-field census,
physical execution of the protocols, application-specific validation,
deployment and long-term lifecycle evidence remain open.

---


### 22.7 Dependency-resolved family ledger

#### 22.7.1 From purpose to a controlled design

| Family | Engineering output | Failure made visible |
|---|---|---|
| Requirements, Function and Boundary | Requirement, function, constraint, operating boundary, use/misuse and acceptance | Desire, scope and pass criterion cannot be selected after the build |
| Components, Interfaces and Architecture | Components, interfaces, architecture, system, modules and integration | Hidden dependency and failed intermediate state remain traceable |
| Resources, Efficiency and Reliability | Resource, capacity/load, efficiency, reliability, availability and failure mode | External supply, time window and loss cannot be omitted |
| Measurement, Calibration and Acceptance | Measurement, calibration, tolerance, test, acceptance and uncertainty | Display, adjustment, demonstration and passed test remain distinct |
| Alternatives, Trade-offs and Optimisation | Alternatives, trade-off, bounded optimum, multi-criteria record, robustness and design freeze | Hidden weights and target-selected designs are rejected |
| Control, Feedback and Stability | Action, loop, operational stability, observability, controllability and fault response | One favourable trace cannot prove global control |

Use and misuse cases are included before design because foreseeable operation
outside the intended happy path changes requirements and safety. Constraints
may conflict; the conflict and authorised resolution must be versioned. An
acceptance boundary is frozen in observable terms so that a design does not
define success by whatever it happens to achieve.

Interface errors are first-class records. A component can satisfy its own
specification while the integrated system fails because units, timing,
orientation, version or error semantics do not match. Modular decomposition is
scientifically useful only when the composition relation reconstructs the
whole behaviour at the stated boundary.

Reliability is the probability or frequency of performing a required function
under declared conditions and duration; availability concerns readiness when
needed. Capacity and load must be paired. Failure modes include detected,
latent, common-cause and cascading routes. A reported uptime without demand,
observation and maintenance boundaries is incomplete.

Robust design asks whether acceptable performance persists across registered
variation and disturbance. Design freeze prevents the target result from
quietly selecting a new parameter or architecture. Change control may reopen
the design, but it creates a new version with an impact record rather than
rewriting the frozen test.

Observability and controllability are system-bound. A sensor record may be
insufficient to distinguish internal predecessors, and an actuator may not
reach every state. Fault response must identify detection, isolation, degraded
mode, safe halt and recovery. The control family therefore supplies the
apparatus logic required by the returned physical protocols.

#### 22.7.2 From safety case to public lifecycle

| Family | New responsibility | Boundary |
|---|---|---|
| Safety, Hazard and Resilience | Hazard, risk, safety case, fail-safe, resilience and defence in depth | No prior harm is not proof of safety |
| Verification, Validation and Traceability | Verification, validation, traceability, reproducibility, independent implementation and end-to-end test | Specification and intended use remain different |
| Cross-Platform Accessibility | Platform behaviour, portable records, interface, deployment, one-command verification and learnability | Local expert operation is not public reproducibility |
| Lifecycle, Maintenance and Sustainability | Configuration, maintenance, impact, decommissioning, lifecycle and bounded sustainability | Prototype success does not close future operation or disposal |
| Science, Design and Evidence | Law translation, performance, failure boundary, prototype, simulation and demonstration | Application cannot select or prove the law |
| Ownership and Anomaly Handoffs | Anomaly return and computation, physical, biological/social and self-hosted routes | An unexplained output returns to its scientific owner with apparatus intact |

A safety case is an argued and evidenced composition, not a single test. It
links hazards to controls, controls to verification and residual risks to
decisions. Resilience concerns continued or restored function after disturbance
and cannot be inferred from nominal operation. Defence in depth must survive a
common-cause analysis.

Implementation-distinct checking requires a genuinely separate reconstruction
route, not the same function called twice. End-to-end verification traverses
the public workflow and catches composition failures that unit checks cannot.
Reproducibility retains environment and dependency versions as part of the
result.

Accessibility is technical and social. A portable data format without a usable
interface may exclude the intended public; a polished interface backed by an
opaque service is not self-contained. Lightweight deployment and one-command
verification reduce barriers only when the command exposes failure and does
not mutate source authority.

Configuration identity connects a deployed system to its tested version.
Maintenance changes can invalidate previous evidence; change impact determines
which tests must be repeated. Decommissioning preserves data, hazards and
responsibility. Sustainability claims remain bounded by included resources,
lifecycle and affected environment.

Simulation can test a model implementation; demonstration can exhibit one
behaviour; prototype evidence can support feasibility. None establishes
general operation or empirical truth beyond its boundary. A scientific anomaly
requires restoration tests, alternative explanations, calibration and source
accounting before it returns upstream.

#### 22.7.3 Returned protocols as executable preregistrations

Each returned protocol can be read as an executable preregistration. The Tesla
protocol freezes the power path. The inertial protocol freezes paired channels
and restoration. The vacuum-beat protocol freezes a no-net-gain halt condition.
The sector protocol freezes the candidate fibre and outside-list outcomes. The
Smithium protocol freezes joint chemical and physical identification. The
human protocol freezes report, physiology, expectation and ethics. The family
and no-omission claims ensure that none can be presented without its complete
controls.

Before any experiment, Engineering must create apparatus identities,
calibration certificates, operator and environmental records, raw immutable
outputs, analysis code and prospective decision rules. A pilot may develop the
apparatus, but its observed targets cannot then be called blind. A later blind
run requires separate custody. A null result means the registered instrument
and analysis found no qualifying effect under adequate conditions; a failed
transport or broken apparatus is unresolved, not null.

This is the direct engineering lesson of the computational programme as well.
Thousands of tests can verify code while a protected empirical target remains
unopened. Formal and implementation readiness are prerequisites for a blind
campaign, not substitutes for it.


## 23. Cross-Branch Synthesis: composition without conflation

### 23.1 What synthesis may and may not do

Cross-Branch Synthesis owns no primitive law. It composes receipts already
admitted by their categorical owners. It cannot rescue a missing derivation,
turn analogy into identity, erase an adverse record or move ownership to a
more convenient branch. A shared Fold relation may be exact while the physical,
biological, conscious and social carriers remain different.

The current frozen synthesis family contains 12 claims, 3,072 candidates, 12
survivors and 48 controls. Its pre-Synthesis dependency graph contained 1,460
claims and 24,076 edges, with one root and no root-trace failure. All twelve
claims replayed independently and five focused test groups passed. This family
was frozen on 29 July 2026 before the current 2,751-claim graph was complete.
It is therefore a completed inherited-return synthesis, not the final terminal
synthesis of the present expanded corpus.

### 23.2 The twelve current compositions

The prime/vacuum claim preserves a multiplicative-order identity across its
typed owners. The common-lock claims preserve the same Fold form across
physical, conscious and social carriers while refusing to identify their
meanings. Folding, fixation and optimisation share a descent structure, but a
protein fold, a dynamical fixed point and an engineering optimum remain
categorically distinct.

Longitudinal and transverse recurrence share a formal relation while their
media, dimensional speeds and observations remain separate. Fold doubling
supplies a second-harmonic structure. The prime-denominator vacuum period-
divisor result remains a formal standing prediction, not an empirical
measurement. Positive observable and structural absence are distinguished so
that a non-record is not mistaken for the numerical or ontological absence of
a carrier.

The Tesla synthesis composes Physics, Earth and Engineering records while
keeping Earth recurrence modes distinct from the quarter-wave sequence. The
Unified Constants Object remains Physics-owned and does not introduce new
adjustable dials. The premeasurement ledger binds prediction, falsification,
chronology and source custody. The one-owner/no-omission claim prevents a
result from acquiring multiple inconsistent authorities. The root-traced
terminal assembly proves that the frozen twelve-claim family reaches the common
root at its declared boundary.

### 23.3 What remains before final synthesis

Since the frozen synthesis was built, the corpus expanded from 1,460 to 2,751
claims. The current programme therefore still requires the global one-owner
lineage merge, canonical publication reconciliation and a final heavy
verification of the expanded graph. The last completed heavy `verify-all` run,
on 28 July 2026, covered 1,319 claims with 1,319/1,319 receipt replays and
969/969 tests. The 2,751-claim corpus has passed its current lightweight global
validator, but the final heavy run has not been repeated at that scale.

This distinction sets the publication boundary of the preliminary Theory of
Everything. The current synthesis demonstrates that typed cross-branch
composition is possible and root-traced. The final publication update must
repeat that work against the complete contemporary graph rather than treating
an earlier terminal receipt as timeless authority.

---


### 23.4 Dependency-resolved family ledger

The twelve synthesis claims form four connected groups. The first establishes
typed shared structures: prime/vacuum order, common Fold locks,
folding–fixation–optimisation descent and wave recurrence. The second registers
new formal consequences: second-harmonic doubling, the vacuum period-divisor
prediction and the positive-observable/structural-absence boundary. The third
controls physical translation: Tesla composition and the Physics-owned Unified
Constants Object. The fourth controls scientific governance: the
premeasurement ledger, one-owner/no-omission and terminal root trace.

The governing test for every composition is whether the shared relation can be
stated without deleting the owners' distinguishing coordinates. A protein fold
and an engineering optimisation may both descend through a generated candidate
order, but protein sequence and physical interaction do not become design
criteria. Conscious and social half-locks may share a two-preimage fibre, but a
participant's interior relation does not become a survey distribution. Wave
recurrence can be common while medium, polarisation, dimensional speed and
instrument remain branch-specific.

The synthesis family would fail if it introduced an unregistered primitive,
if any input receipt were absent, if a claim could not reach the common root,
if one result acquired two categorical owners, or if analogy were used where
the typed relation did not commute. Its completed 29 July graph satisfies those
conditions for the then-frozen 1,460-claim input. The expanded 2,751-claim
corpus requires a new terminal assembly rather than an editorial assertion
that the earlier graph already contained later work.


## 24. The computational-proof programme

### 24.1 Purpose and evidence hierarchy

The computational-proof programme asks whether selected SFT structures can be
implemented as public, exact and falsifiable systems in domains whose state
spaces are large enough to defeat informal inspection. Protein folding, Chess,
Go and Unison Fold AI test different boundaries. Protein tests sequence,
interaction, geometry and protected structural comparison. Chess tests exact
legality, strategic search and play against rated opposition. Go tests rules,
state census, search and play across board scales. Unison tests whether the
formal architecture can be carried into a language-capable artificial system
without importing a pretrained answer oracle as the native model.

These are computational proofs only in a typed sense. An exhaustive census can
prove that one registered survivor remains within its complete finite grammar.
A passing test suite can verify implementation behaviour against its
specification. A blind external comparison can provide empirical evidence at
its registered target boundary. A match in one task does not prove the entire
Theory of Everything, and a failure in one implementation does not by itself
repeal its upstream branch. Each result must keep its own formal,
implementation and empirical status.

The programme also preserves chronology. Historical V2 results remain part of
the evidence even where a V3 recreation is planned. They are not silently
called V3 results. A clean V3 scaffold is not called a completed recreation.
Protected targets and comparator outputs stay unopened until the registered
gate authorises access.

### 24.2 Protein Fold V2: the strong local result and the adverse whole-chain result

Protein Fold V2 produced a mixed but scientifically valuable record. Its
whole-chain test on a 76-residue target was adverse:

| V2 whole-chain measure | Recorded result |
|---|---:|
| TM-score | 0.0269927379497572 |
| C-alpha dRMSD | 52.89314678065376 Å |

That result is not hidden inside a favourable average and is not superseded by
the existence of strong local windows. It states that the achieved sealed
sequence-only implementation did not recover the registered whole-chain
structure at that boundary.

The same programme recorded strong local-window matches:

| Local window | TM-score |
|---|---:|
| HLV | 0.9914591922414299 |
| RLI | 0.9656795312222751 |
| RGG | 0.9059580746307327 |

These results show that the implementation captured substantial local
geometric structure on the tested windows. They do not turn the adverse
whole-chain result into success. A separate theoretical support construction
contains a complete trace with TM-score **0.9891211351**. That construction
shows that the formal support can carry a high-similarity route; it is not the
achieved sealed sequence-only whole-chain output and must never be reported as
if it were.

The combined record motivates V3. Local success indicates that the interaction
and geometry programme is not empty. The whole-chain failure identifies the
missing global constraint, recurrence or search closure that a superior
version must solve. The support construction demonstrates capacity inside a
declared theoretical route while preserving the need for an independently
achieved blind output.

### 24.3 Protein Fold V3: current exact result

Protein Fold V3 is a clean, active programme rather than a cosmetic renaming of
V2. Its current scientific gate records:

| Current Protein V3 item | Verified state |
|---|---:|
| Scientific gate v20 suite | 893 passed; 0 failed; 0 errors |
| Publication full suite v21 | 898 passed; 0 failed; 0 errors |
| Exact parent candidates | 42,173,082 |
| Parent leaves | 375,014 |
| Certified prunes | 41,798,068 |
| Frontier states | 205 |
| Shards | 11,012 |
| Symbolic fallbacks | 0 |
| High-attraction slice | 583 states |
| Dominated states in slice | 573 |
| Retained states in slice | 10 |
| New nondominated states | 8 |
| Protected target accesses | 0 |
| AlphaFold output accesses | 0 |
| Registered blind targets opened | 0 |

The exact parent census is complete and verified at its registered boundary.
The separately registered 583-state high-attraction slice is closed and
independently verified. Those are substantive current results: V3 has
enumerated and certified a 42,173,082-candidate parent search without symbolic
fallback, and it has reduced the tested high-attraction slice to ten retained
states, including eight new nondominated states.

The remaining attraction subband census and seeded-parent high-band
reconciliation are active and incomplete. No result may be imported from an
unfinished process merely because files or temporary states exist. Lower bands
are not closed. The complete fixed-geometry sector is not closed. The global
frontier is not closed. Arbitrary-sequence recurrence and the full predictor
are not closed.

### 24.4 The AlphaFold-generalised blind parity campaign

The intended empirical gate is an AlphaFold-generalised blind parity campaign.
“Generalised” means that the V3 system must confront the same broad kind of
sequence-to-structure task without choosing a target because it favours the
method. “Blind” means protected target structures and AlphaFold outputs remain
inaccessible until the relevant derivation, implementation, scoring and
failure-handling records are sealed. “Parity” requires a preregistered
comparison surface and cannot be claimed from an illustrative match.

At the current gate, the campaign is **not permitted**. AlphaFold parity is
**unavailable**, not adverse and not successful. There have been zero protected
target accesses, zero AlphaFold-output accesses and zero opened targets. This
is the correct status because the remaining exact search and global predictor
obligations have not closed.

The planned campaign must retain sequence custody, target custody, release and
model version, comparator settings, prediction timestamp, structure source,
alignment and scoring rules, uncertainty, failure cases and all targets. It
must report local and whole-chain outcomes separately. Any target that cannot
be processed, any comparator failure and any missing experimental structure
must remain in the denominator under its registered classification.

The current preliminary Protein paper is version **0.9.4**, DOI
**10.5281/zenodo.21717581**. It reports the gate that exists now and is intended
to receive a lawful version update when the remaining exact work and blind
campaign are complete. Preliminary publication does not authorise a forecast
to be rewritten as an achieved result.

### 24.5 Chess Fold: historical V2 evidence and V3 boundary

Chess Fold V2.2 retains exact legality anchors, **22/22** main tests and
**8/8** endgame tests. Its recorded match results are:

| Opposition | Record | Score |
|---|---|---:|
| Elo 1700 | 3 wins, 7 draws, 2 losses | 54.2% |
| Elo 1900 | 6 wins, 3 draws, 3 losses | 62.5% |
| Elo 2100 | 2 wins, 5 draws, 1 capped draw, 4 losses | 41.7% |

These are implementation and measured play results at their historical test
boundaries. They show legal, functioning play and non-trivial performance,
including a positive score in the registered 1900 series. They do not by
themselves establish universal playing strength or a solved game.

A later auxiliary probe examined 36 positions. All 36 completed; nine choices
changed, four changes matched the Stockfish-best move, and none of the four
named death-slide positions changed. This probe was agent-authored and lacks
the programme's publication governance, so it remains auxiliary evidence rather
than a promoted main result.

The current repository contains no clean V3 Chess recreation workspace.
Accordingly, V3 Chess is **not started**. Its programme remains to rebuild the
legality, search, evaluation, resource and opponent-custody stack without
silently importing the V2 runtime; repeat exact anchors and regression tests;
pre-register rated and tactical comparisons; and preserve every draw, cap,
timeout, illegal move and loss.

### 24.6 Go Fold: exact finite censuses, measured play and V3 boundary

Go Fold V2.2 includes exact legal-state censuses across small boards:

| Board | Exact census |
|---|---:|
| 1 × 1 | 1 |
| 1 × 2 | 5 |
| 2 × 2 | 57 |
| 2 × 3 | 489 |
| 3 × 3 | 12,675 |
| 4 × 4 | 24,318,165 |

The 2 × 2 search retains **17,038,501 nodes**. These counts are exact
implementation records and strong foundations for a V3 rules and state-space
reconstruction.

Two independently replayed 9 × 9 batches each recorded **2–0** against GNU Go.
The recovered 19 × 19 KataGo harness record reports **2–0** at a 128-ply point
cut-off. Round 1 also contains two rejected play responses after which the
harness continued; Round 2 contains no rejected response. The cut-off and
Round 1 defect remain part of the result. They cannot be edited into completed
ordinary games or a clean two-game run.

As with Chess, the present repository contains no clean V3 Go recreation
workspace. V3 Go is **not started**. The intended programme is to rebuild exact
rules and legality, reproduce the finite censuses, seal search and scoring,
then run governed comparisons whose resignation, pass, illegal move, rejected
response, cut-off and final scoring rules are fixed in advance.

### 24.7 Unison Fold AI: implementation strength and generation weakness

Unison V6.3 retains a large historical implementation record: **326 suites**,
**2,002 checks**, zero main-corpus failures and **326/326** certificate
identities. Its current standalone verifier passes **21/21** checks. The
historical broader verifier passes **43/47**, so four historical checks remain
outside a complete pass and must not be hidden by the current standalone
total.

Generation evidence is weak. The recorded MMLU runs moved from **9/128** to
**0/128**. Free generation produced **1/12** successful cases. The Codex
generation probes for baseline, F1 and F3 each produced **0/12**. The correct
interpretation is that Unison V2 established substantial architecture,
implementation and certificate machinery but did not establish competitive
general answer generation.

The V3 workspace is a clean version **0.1.0-scaffold**. It does not import the
V2 runtime. Pretrained answer weights are not allowed to serve as the native
model. The proposed primary interface is a Discord text bot, but remote
operation is not thereby authorised. The present statuses are exact:

| V3 Unison dimension | Current status |
|---|---|
| Workspace | Clean scaffold |
| Mathematical closure | `OPEN` |
| Empirical validation | `NOT_RUN` |
| Remote deployment | `NOT_AUTHORIZED` |
| V2 runtime imported | `false` |
| Pretrained answer weights as native model | prohibited |

These statuses are not adverse empirical results because the empirical
programme has not run. They are also not implementation success. V3 must first
close its mathematical architecture, implement it, verify its certificate and
resource surfaces, establish safety and governance, and only then conduct
registered evaluations. Any human-participant or public deployment must also
satisfy the Medicine, Consciousness, Social and Engineering boundaries already
derived.

### 24.8 Programme comparison and next gates

| Programme | Strongest current record | Standing limitation | Next lawful gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| Protein Fold V3 | Verified 42,173,082-candidate parent; closed 583-state high-attraction slice; scientific gate v20 893/893 and full suite v21 898/898 | Remaining bands and global predictor open; no target or AlphaFold access | Complete exact search and global recurrence, then authorise sealed blind parity |
| Chess Fold | V2.2 legality and endgame tests; measured play through Elo 2100 | No V3 workspace; auxiliary probe not publication-governed | Clean V3 rebuild and preregistered tactical/rated evaluation |
| Go Fold | V2.2 exact censuses; two 9 × 9 2–0 batches; qualified 19 × 19 record | No V3 workspace; 19 × 19 cut-off and rejected-response defect retained | Clean V3 rebuild, census replay and governed full-game comparison |
| Unison Fold AI | V2 architecture with 2,002 main checks and complete certificate identities | Weak generation; V3 mathematical closure open and validation not run | Close V3 mathematics and implementation before authorised evaluation or deployment |

The programme's strongest general result is methodological. It has made
failure localisable. A false move, bad fold, rejected Go response or failed
generation probe no longer has to be interpreted as a verdict on an entire
world-view. The registered layers show whether the failure belongs to a
candidate grammar, an implementation, a resource limit, a comparison method,
an external transport or the scientific claim itself.

---


### 24.9 Technical ledger

#### 24.9.1 Common constitution of a computational proof

Every programme begins with a problem carrier and a boundary. Protein begins
with an ordered residue sequence and a registered structural target. Chess and
Go begin with game rules, legal histories and an opponent or exact census.
Unison begins with a native architecture and a registered task surface. The
programme then separates six layers:

1. **Registration.** The claim, candidate grammar, resource boundary,
   evaluation rule, target custody and stopping rule are fixed.
2. **Enumeration or construction.** Candidates or executable states are
   generated without consulting protected answers.
3. **Elimination.** Every rejected candidate receives a reconstructible reason;
   pruning must conserve the census.
4. **Implementation verification.** Unit, property, regression, independent and
   end-to-end tests verify what the software actually does.
5. **External comparison.** Only an authorised custodian opens targets or
   opponents and preserves every outcome.
6. **Interpretation.** Formal, implementation and empirical status are reported
   independently, with ownership of failure retained.

The conservation equation for a complete finite search is simple but
non-negotiable:

\[
N_{\mathrm{generated}}
= N_{\mathrm{rejected}}
+ N_{\mathrm{retained}}
+ N_{\mathrm{unresolved}}.
\]

If pruning is hierarchical, every parent count must reconstruct from its
children and certified prunes. A hash binds file identity, not scientific
correctness; correctness additionally requires the generator, decision rule,
controls and independent reconstruction.

Target blindness is a capability condition rather than a statement of good
intent. The derivation and search process must be unable to read protected
structures, comparator outputs, hidden solutions or later evaluation records.
Opening a target during development changes chronology. The resulting work may
remain useful as development-observed evidence, but it cannot later be called a
blind prediction.

Negative outcomes have different meanings. A programme may return a valid but
poor-scoring result, fail to return an output, exceed its registered resources,
encounter an invalid external response, or be unable to access a target. Poor
score is adverse to the performance criterion; no output is an implementation
failure; resource exhaustion is a bounded unresolved result unless the claim
included that resource; invalid external response is a transport or harness
failure; unopened target is not tested. These classes must not be compressed
