into “failed”.

#### 24.9.2 Protein V3 representation and six-axis relation

Protein V3 starts from the complete ordered sequence rather than from a target
structure. Residue identity and position are preserved. Local and non-local
interactions must be distinguishable, and any environment, chain boundary,
modification or partner included in the claim must be registered. A
representation that discards sequence order or imports target coordinates
would fail the Biology handoff.

The six-axis joint relation scales the formal Fold programme by evaluating
candidate relations together rather than allowing one attractive local
quantity to select a geometrically impossible global state. The exact axis
names and machine values remain in the Protein archive; scientifically, their
roles cover the retained sequence carrier, interaction relation, geometric
compatibility, attraction or ordering, recurrence/continuation and evidence
boundary. The joint relation is important because whole-chain folding is a
constraint-composition problem. A conformation may satisfy many local
preferences while producing global collision, topological inconsistency or an
incorrect long-range arrangement.

The current parent search generated **42,173,082** candidates. Its account
closes as **375,014** leaves plus **41,798,068** certified prunes. The search
records **205** frontier states across **11,012** shards and uses **zero**
symbolic fallbacks. A symbolic fallback could have hidden an unevaluated route
behind an expression; its absence means the registered parent result is an
explicit exact computation at this boundary.

Pruning is a scientific result only if it preserves every candidate that could
satisfy the frozen relation. Each certificate must identify the parent,
subspace, rejecting condition and reconstructible count. The equality between
parent, leaves and prunes is necessary but not sufficient: a systematically
wrong pruning predicate could conserve counts. Independent verification and
adverse controls therefore test both arithmetic and semantics.

The high-attraction slice asks a narrower question of **583** states. It
classifies **573** as dominated and retains **10**, of which **8** are new
nondominated states. Dominance means that another registered state is at least
as good on every relevant frozen comparison and better on one; it does not mean
that a dominated state has been empirically observed to be biologically
impossible. The slice is complete and independently verified at this bounded
relation.

The remaining attraction subbands cannot be inferred from the high slice. A
lower-attraction state could participate in a globally superior chain after
other constraints are composed, unless the proof shows it cannot. The
seeded-parent reconciliation must also demonstrate that no seed-specific route
escaped the parent accounting. This is why the programme has not opened its
protected targets despite a large completed census.

#### 24.9.3 From local geometry to whole-chain recurrence

The V2 record demonstrates a central distinction. Local windows can align very
strongly while the whole chain is wrong. A chain is not the arithmetic mean of
independent local folds. Long-range contact, chain closure, steric consistency,
secondary-structure packing and competing interactions compose the global
problem.

V3 must therefore prove or implement a recurrence that carries admissible local
relations into a complete fixed-geometry sector. Each extension step should
preserve the prefix or partial carrier, record newly introduced interactions,
reject geometric impossibilities and retain alternatives that remain
non-dominated. If two partial states share a compressed image but differ in an
unheld interaction relevant later, merging them would destroy a possible fold.
The information-loss law thus has a direct algorithmic consequence: state
compression is sound only when discarded predecessor distinctions are proven
irrelevant to every future extension.

The global frontier closes only when every registered full-chain candidate is
either retained, certified dominated, rejected by an exact constraint or
classified unresolved under the frozen resource boundary. Arbitrary-sequence
recurrence adds a generality claim beyond one target length or composition.
The full predictor then connects the exact frontier to one sealed output or
ensemble under a prospective decision rule.

Potential success is supported, but not guaranteed, by three current facts.
First, V2 achieved very high local-window similarity, so the representation can
capture real local geometry. Second, the theoretical support trace achieved a
high whole-chain TM-score inside a declared construction, so the formal support
is capable of carrying a globally close route. Third, V3 has already scaled
exact enumeration and dominance reasoning to more than 42 million parent
candidates without fallback. Together these make continued development
scientifically motivated and make success a credible prospective expectation.
They do not establish the missing sealed sequence-only whole-chain result.

An adverse final comparison would still be informative. If the exact
implementation closes but blind performance remains poor, the candidate
grammar or mapping from formal relation to protein evidence is incomplete. If
the search cannot close within the registered resources, the implementation
generality claim must narrow. If performance is strong only in local windows,
the global recurrence remains the identified problem. Each outcome directs a
different next revision.

#### 24.9.4 Blind parity protocol and scoring constitution

Before the AlphaFold-generalised campaign opens, the following records must be
sealed:

| Record | Required content |
|---|---|
| Target manifest | Target IDs, sequence source, chain boundaries, release/version and exclusion rules |
| Custody manifest | Who can access structures and AlphaFold outputs; capability separation and access log |
| SFT build identity | Code, dependencies, parameters fixed by derivation, resource envelope and execution command |
| Comparator identity | AlphaFold implementation or service, model/version, inputs, settings and output custody |
| Scoring plan | Whole-chain and local metrics, alignment, missing residues, multimers, ensembles and tie rules |
| Decision plan | Success, parity, adverse, unresolved and transport classifications fixed before opening |
| Completeness plan | All targets, timeouts, invalid outputs, failed accesses and comparator failures retained |
| Chronology | Seal time, prediction time, comparator time, target opening and analysis time |

TM-score, RMSD and dRMSD measure different aspects of agreement. TM-score is
length-normalised and more tolerant of local deviations; RMSD can be dominated
by a few large discrepancies; distance RMSD tests internal distances without
requiring the same rigid alignment. No one metric should be selected after the
outputs are seen. Local-window metrics remain secondary unless local accuracy
is itself the preregistered claim.

Parity must specify whether it concerns mean, median, target-wise win/loss,
threshold achievement, non-inferiority interval or another complete statistic.
Averages alone can hide catastrophic targets. Per-target results and confidence
or uncertainty procedures must remain. If AlphaFold cannot process a target,
that row is a comparator failure under the frozen rule, not an automatic SFT
win. If SFT cannot process one, it is not deleted.

No target sequence should be selected because an experimental structure is
known to favour the SFT grammar. Exclusions may be scientifically necessary—for
example, unresolved residues, complexes outside the programme's carrier or
non-standard chemistry—but must be fixed from source metadata available under
the registered custody rule, not from observed performance. Any development
set must be permanently distinguished from the blind holdout.

#### 24.9.5 Chess V3: exact rules before strength

Chess Fold V3 must begin by reproducing the game, not by tuning an evaluation
against an engine. The position carrier retains board occupancy, side to move,
castling rights, en-passant state, repetition-relevant history and move count
where the rule requires it. Legal move generation must preserve check,
promotion, castling constraints and every terminal condition. A visually valid
board without history may not determine the legal successor set.

The V2 **22/22** main and **8/8** endgame anchors are the first regression
surface, but V3 should extend them with property tests: every generated move
must produce a legal successor; reversible moves should reconstruct their
predecessor where history permits; independently generated perft counts should
match fixed positions; and illegal moves should be rejected without mutating
state. Draw by repetition, move-count rules, stalemate, insufficient material
and capped external games must have separate records.

Search must retain node identity, depth or resource, transposition conditions,
ordering and score provenance. A Fold compression may merge positions only
when every rule-relevant and evaluation-relevant distinction is preserved.
Evaluation should be derived or frozen before opponent play. Stockfish may
serve as an external comparator or analysis tool after sealing; its move cannot
be imported as a hidden label into the native chooser.

The V2 match record suggests non-trivial play but also a decline at the 2100
boundary. V3 should not promise a monotonic Elo curve from three small series.
A governed campaign needs a fixed opponent version, hardware or node budget,
time control, colour balance, opening policy, adjudication, disconnection rule
and complete PGNs. Score intervals and game-level records should accompany the
headline percentage.

Tactical and strategic probes answer different questions. A named
death-slide suite can test whether a specific failure mode has changed; a broad
position suite can test move agreement. The prior auxiliary run's four
Stockfish-best changes and zero death-slide improvements show why both must be
reported. V3 governance should register probe authorship and freeze expected
properties without turning engine agreement into the sole definition of a good
move.

#### 24.9.6 Go V3: history, territory and harness custody

Go positions require board occupancy, side to move and sufficient history for
ko or superko under the selected rule set. Capture, suicide, pass, termination
and scoring conventions must be fixed. A state census depends on those choices;
changing them changes the grammar. The V2 small-board totals therefore serve as
versioned anchors, not rule-independent constants.

The exact sequence from 1 × 1 through 4 × 4 demonstrates that the V2
implementation could enumerate rapidly expanding legal spaces. The **2 × 2**
node count of **17,038,501** is especially useful for a complete independent
replay because the final state count alone cannot reveal duplicated histories,
wrong ko handling or unsound transposition merging.

The V3 engine should expose legal-move generation, capture groups and liberties,
history identity, evaluation, search and time/resource accounting separately.
Property tests can assert conservation of stone changes through placement and
capture, legal predecessor/successor relations, hash consistency and score
reconstruction. Adverse cases must include suicide-like moves, ko recapture,
double pass, rejected external protocol responses and timeouts.

The V2 9 × 9 results are complete at their recorded two-game batch boundaries.
The 19 × 19 result is weaker because it stops at 128 plies and Round 1 continued
after two rejected play responses. A V3 harness must treat an invalid or
rejected response according to a frozen rule—retry, forfeit, unresolved
transport or abort—and may not continue silently. Ordinary final scoring,
resignation and cut-off adjudication must remain separate.

A strong V3 result would first reproduce the exact censuses, then pass
independent legality tests, then conduct complete games against fixed public
opponents. A win is an empirical implementation result under the stated rules
and budget, not a proof that Go is solved. A loss preserves a position record
from which search, evaluation or protocol failures can be analysed.

#### 24.9.7 Unison V3: native architecture before borrowed fluency

The clean V3 constraint forbids the V2 runtime from entering as an unexamined
foundation. It also forbids pretrained answer weights from serving as the
native model. This does not deny the utility of external models as development
tools, evaluators or interfaces when their role is declared. It prevents
borrowed statistical content from being presented as a consequence of the
native Fold architecture.

Mathematical closure should specify the state carrier, Fold operations,
memory/record relation, learning or update rule, inference, generation and
resource bounds. Every operation needs a typed input and output, invariants and
failure state. Implementation closure then requires certificate identity,
independent reconstruction, deterministic or recorded variability and
end-to-end execution on the public platforms claimed.

The V2 **326/326** certificate identities and **2,002** main checks are strong
evidence that a large formal implementation can remain internally auditable.
The **43/47** historical verification and poor generation results prevent that
strength from being converted into a claim of general intelligence. V3 should
preserve the certificate discipline while treating generation as an empirical
problem.

Evaluation must separate factual question answering, structured reasoning,
free generation, instruction following, safety, memory and conversational
continuity. MMLU is one source-bound benchmark and can be contaminated or
misused; a zero score still records a concrete failure at the chosen interface.
Free-generation success needs a frozen rubric or independent judge custody.
External language-model judging must record model, prompt, version and every
disagreement.

The proposed Discord bot adds a deployment system: authentication, message
transport, privacy, moderation, rate and resource limits, error handling,
logging, data retention, abuse response and rollback. Engineering validation
must establish intended public operation. Social and Medicine boundaries
govern vulnerable users and advice. Consciousness claims remain unavailable
from fluent behaviour alone. Until those stages are complete, remote deployment
properly remains `NOT_AUTHORIZED`.

#### 24.9.8 Cross-program evidence and publication gates

The four programmes should share a common release grammar without pretending
their metrics are comparable:

| Gate | Protein | Chess | Go | Unison |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Formal carrier closed | Partial: parent and high slice closed; global open | V2 only; V3 not started | V2 only; V3 not started | V3 open |
| Implementation suite | Scientific gate v20 893/893; publication full suite v21 898/898 | Historical V2 anchors | Historical V2 censuses and play harness | V2 main 2,002 checks; V3 not run |
| Protected external target | Unopened | Future governed opponents/positions | Future governed opponents/games | Future registered benchmarks/users |
| Current empirical class | Active, incomplete; parity unavailable | Historical measured V2 record | Historical measured V2 record with qualified 19 × 19 row | V2 weak generation; V3 `NOT_RUN` |
| Deployment authority | Local scientific programme | None asserted for V3 | None asserted for V3 | `NOT_AUTHORIZED` |

A version update is warranted when a programme has a materially new,
independently verified record, even if the result is adverse. Versioning must
state exactly which earlier result is preserved, corrected or superseded. A
new version may update current status while retaining the historical chronology
of the old release. It must not create a new record merely to hide an adverse
version.

For Protein, the next publishable milestone is closure of the remaining exact
search or a scientifically informative narrowing of that search, followed by
the authorised blind campaign when its gate passes. For Chess and Go, the next
milestone is a clean V3 reproduction of rules and exact anchors before rated
play. For Unison, it is mathematical and implementation closure before any
claim of empirical capability.

The completed computational-proof programme will contribute a set of
independent, domain-specific application results to the final Theory of
Everything. It will not be used to rewrite the upstream derivations. Its role
is stronger: it makes those derivations confront exact execution, public
failure and protected external comparison.


### 24.10 Integrated status, corrections and final extension frontier

#### 24.10.1 What has become derivable

Across this part, the SFT dependency spine now reaches living organisation,
genetic coding structure, inheritance, evolution, condition-bounded protein
ensembles, development, physiology and ecology; patient-centred clinical
knowledge, causal and prognostic boundaries, intervention evidence and an
expectation/body fibre; interior observation, binding, self-model, memory,
qualitative recurrence and substrate-realisation criteria; one Earth's coupled
budgets, histories, climate and hazard records; a source-to-record cosmos;
identified collective agents, institutions, power, ethics and historical
causality; and a complete engineering constitution for requirements, resources,
safety, validation and anomaly handoff.

The major conflation resolved is the repeated substitution of one evidence
carrier for another. Chemistry is not life. Biological difference is not
clinical diagnosis. Public access is not phenomenal presence. A model is not
an observation. A social aggregate is not a person. Consensus is not proof. A
prototype is not validation. A test suite is not empirical confirmation.
Cross-branch similarity is not cross-branch identity.

#### 24.10.2 What current evidence establishes

The current 555 claims in this span each have a complete candidate census, one
survivor, four required controls and a root trace at their registered formal
boundary. Their source comparisons and independent reconstructions establish
the empirical or implementation status recorded in each package. The evidence
also establishes that the corpus preserves findings which do not favour the
strongest proposed numerical reading.

The standing adverse, corrected and unresolved records include:

| Record | Current status |
|---|---|
| Biology universal three-quarter allometry | Corrected; multiple exponents and non-universal cases retained |
| Biology strong scale-free default | Corrected; empirical rarity retained |
| Homochirality origin | Observed asymmetry; historical origin unresolved |
| Medicine prognosis from denominator parity | Corrected; clinical horizon and evidence required |
| Consciousness from access, report or fluent output alone | Not admitted; complete interior or realisation relation required |
| Earth first earthquake catalogue | Adverse and unreclassified |
| Earth homogeneous earthquake holdout | Compatible; does not erase first result |
| Astronomy first SPARC rank-four comparison | Adverse and unreclassified |
| Astronomy returned exact mappings | Mixed: categorical pass with heterogeneous, mismatched, unresolved or adverse quantitative rows |
| Engineering returned protocols | Formally closed; experiments not run |
| Protein V2 whole-chain fold | Adverse; strong local windows and theoretical support kept separate |
| Protein V3 blind AlphaFold parity | Unavailable; gate not permitted and targets unopened |
| Chess and Go V3 | Not started in current workspaces |
| Unison V3 validation and deployment | `NOT_RUN` and `NOT_AUTHORIZED` |

Failed source access also remains a positive historical fact about custody:
four Biology transports, two HHS Medicine transports, the Consciousness
absent/failed source rows, one protected Earth transport with a separate
addendum, two Astronomy transports, four Social transports and one Engineering
transport remain visible under their exact records.

#### 24.10.3 What remains open

The final extension frontier has several layers.

First, Biology has 342 open obligations in its registered 424-obligation
full-field census. The later domain branches have complete foundations and
returned families but do not yet have frozen full-field censuses. Their next
work must expand coverage without rewriting foundation chronology.

Second, Protein Fold V3 must complete the remaining attraction bands,
seeded-parent reconciliation, fixed-geometry sector, global frontier,
arbitrary-sequence recurrence and full predictor before the 100-target
AlphaFold-generalised blind parity campaign can be authorised. Chess Fold and
Go Fold require clean V3 recreations. Unison Fold AI requires mathematical and
implementation closure before empirical evaluation or deployment.

Third, the 2,751-claim graph requires a final global one-owner lineage merge,
canonical publication reconciliation and heavy verification. The completed
twelve-claim synthesis must be regenerated against that contemporary graph.

Fourth, every numerical structural correspondence that has not yet been
measured remains a prediction or open mapping at its registered chronology.
No editorial completion pass can lawfully transform it into an earlier blind
prediction or empirical confirmation.

#### 24.10.4 Contribution to the complete dependency spine

These branches turn the abstract Fold into a disciplined account of the
worlds in which science is performed. Life retains itself while matter turns
over. Medicine retains the person while populations inform care. Consciousness
retains interior difference while public images merge. Earth Science retains
one planet's history while universal laws constrain it. Astronomy retains the
source path by which a cosmos becomes observable. Social Science retains people
and power while aggregates compress them. Engineering retains requirements,
loss and failure while knowledge becomes an artefact. The computational
programme then exposes the exact point at which a formal relation does—or does
not yet—survive implementation and blind comparison.

This is foundational completion at declared boundaries and active extension
beyond them. “Complete” always names the end of the registered current project
investigation, never the end of science. The preliminary Theory of Everything
will be version-updated as the computational programmes, downstream censuses,
global synthesis and final verification close. Its present value is not that
open work has been hidden. It is that derivation, implementation, evidence,
failure and future test are now connected in one public dependency record.

### 24.12 Evidence custody, chronology and historical reconciliation

#### 24.12.1 Foundation seal and returned-family chronology

The domain foundations and the later returns are two publication layers. The
foundation papers were sealed, generated and externally compared at their own
candidate boundaries. The returned families were registered later to answer
specific unresolved or corrected V1/V2 obligations. A present total may add the
two layers, but it may not make the later claim part of the earlier blind seal.

| Branch | Foundation result | Later result | Chronology that must remain visible |
|---|---|---|---|
| Biology | 75 claims, 19,200 candidates, 300 controls | 7 claims, 1,792 candidates, 28 controls | Foundation source comparison precedes origin/homochiral/somatic/neural/differentiation/ecosystem return |
| Medicine | 72, 18,432, 288 | 4, 1,024, 16 | Clinical constitution precedes expectation/body and physiological comparison |
| Consciousness | 72, 18,432, 288 | 5, 1,280, 20 | Hard-problem foundation precedes synaesthesia/nonordinary/sleep/cessation comparison |
| Earth | 74, 18,944, 296 | 1, 256, 4 | Two-basin foundation precedes normalised Fold-lock return |
| Astronomy | 72, 18,432, 288 | 5, 1,280, 20 | Foundational observation and SPARC test precede solar/atomic/planetary/lithium family |
| Social | 72, 18,432, 288 | 4, 1,024, 16 | Collective constitution precedes inequality/polarisation/cycle return |
| Engineering | 72, 18,432, 288 | 8, 2,048, 32 | Translation foundation precedes proposed experimental protocols |

Each claim nevertheless follows the same minimum custody pattern. A
registration identifies the statement, owner, dependencies, candidate grammar,
count, controls, falsification condition and evidence boundary. The engine
generates the complete grammar without target access. One survivor is admitted
only when every registered preservation coordinate is satisfied and every
alternative receives a decision. An independent implementation reconstructs
the result. Source registration and capture occur under the claim's chronology.
The evidence record compares the source-derived held relation with the sealed
statement. A measurement receipt or validation report binds the conclusion to
the exact artefacts.

The repeated 256-candidate grammar in these branches is not a claim that every
scientific question has 256 natural possibilities. It is the complete Cartesian
product of eight registered binary preservation dimensions for each stated
obligation. The depth-independent status establishes uniqueness within that
explicit grammar. Generalisation beyond the grammar requires a new claim and
cannot be obtained merely by repeating the receipt.

#### 24.12.2 Source transport is part of evidence, not a clerical footnote

A source identity records the custodian, title or dataset, release, locator,
snapshot or access date and hash where available. Transport status records
whether the required material was actually acquired. Content status records
whether the captured source contains the preregistered feature. These are
different questions.

Biology preserved four failed transports while using 17 usable source records.
The TLS failure, HTTP 403 and two challenge pages describe failed access paths.
They do not show that the inaccessible sources disagree, agree or lack a
feature. Medicine preserved two failed HHS routes and separately captured
purpose-matched ICH material. The ICH record has its own identity; it cannot
impersonate the inaccessible HHS documents.

Consciousness registered 61 source features across 15 identities. Fifty-eight
were present, three full-text features absent and 18 transport or content rows
failed. A missing feature in an accessed document is different from an
unopened document. Earth registered 91 features across 21 identities, with 67
present and 24 absent. Its original Smithsonian 403 remains historical even
though a WFS addendum supplies separately registered content.

Astronomy retained two failed transports among 15 primary sources. Social
retained four among 14. Engineering retained the failed
`FDA-DESIGN-CONTROLS-001` transport among its 13 custodians. The cumulative
record therefore does not pretend that open science guarantees every external
server will remain available. It guarantees that failure is visible and that a
replacement route receives a new provenance rather than changing history.

Source prestige has no formal role in candidate selection. Primary or official
sources are preferred because they reduce ambiguity in the observed record,
not because institutional status proves a claim. A source may be authoritative
for its own measurement or rule while still supplying adverse evidence for the
SFT statement.

#### 24.12.3 Corrections are current results

The V3 historical audit reviewed the V1 corpus of 356 rows and V2 corpus of 407
steps. Attribution to a branch was done at the atomic claim level because older
rows often combined several domains. A corrected claim retains its historical
text and the current replacement route. It is not erased and does not continue
as a second live authority.

Biology's 30 owned atoms include 21 corrections. They remove inevitability from
beneficial fixation, rigidity from protein state, a solved-origin claim from
homochirality, universality from one allometric exponent and strong scale-free
topology, and diagnostic or stability power from denominator parity or one
orbit. Medicine's five owned inherited atoms are corrected where placebo lacks
a comparator, stereochemistry substitutes for clinical evidence, parity
substitutes for prognosis or recurrence substitutes for diagnosis.

Consciousness reconciles 46 owned questions at a boundary that refuses to
identify access or report with phenomenal presence. Earth reconciles three
owned questions while preserving the adverse earthquake test. Astronomy
reconciles 53, including the quantitative galaxy test and unobserved-sector
classifications. Social reconciles five without turning prestige or consensus
into evidence. Engineering reconciles seven without allowing a desired device
to select the physical law it is meant to test.

A correction may narrow a claim and still increase scientific value. “Every
biological network is scale-free” is easy to repeat but empirically false as a
universal. A typed topology claim with a sampling and fit boundary can be
tested. “Cycling is cancer” is memorable but clinically unsafe. A continuing
cycle plus differentiation loss and escape from three controls is more
specific and falsifiable. The current corpus treats this increased specificity
as progress.

#### 24.12.4 Adverse, compatible, null and unresolved chronology

The status vocabulary is operational:

- **Adverse** means the registered evidence conflicts with the registered
  decision condition.
- **Compatible** means the evidence lies within the permitted region but is not
  discriminating enough to establish unique support.
- **Null** means an adequate registered test observed no qualifying effect or
  difference under its declared rule.
- **Unresolved** means required evidence or closure is incomplete, unavailable
  or censored.
- **Failed transport** means the evidence route could not deliver its required
  source or response.

The first earthquake test is adverse because its interval excludes 1/10. The
second is compatible because its separately registered interval contains
1/10. They cannot be pooled after the fact without a new synthesis claim. The
first SPARC fit is adverse to exact rank four; the separate 3.85 ± 0.09 method
record and 3.5–4.0 systematic range provide relevant context without changing
the first classification.

Protein V2's whole-chain output is adverse because it produced a valid output
far outside a successful structural comparison. Protein V3's unopened parity
campaign is unresolved or unavailable at its gate, not adverse, because no
protected target has been tested. Unison V3 `NOT_RUN` is a programme state,
not a null benchmark result. Chess V3 and Go V3 are absent as clean current
workspaces; their V2 measurements remain historical evidence.

When later evidence changes current status, the historical row remains. A
resolved adverse result would require a new claim or a protocol-authorised
reclassification rule that was present before the later evidence. Merely
obtaining a favourable later result is not enough.

### 24.13 One-owner dependency and handoff matrix

The following matrix identifies the objects exchanged across this part and the
claims each recipient is forbidden to infer without further work:

| Upstream owner | Typed handoff | Downstream owner | Not supplied by the handoff |
|---|---|---|---|
| Chemistry and Materials | Reaction, molecule, interface and structure–property carriers | Biology | Living organisation, heredity or evolutionary continuation |
| Biology | Organism, physiology, variation, population and mechanism | Medicine | Diagnosis, prognosis, efficacy, safety or patient decision |
| Biology, Information and Computation | Neural, informational and transition carriers | Consciousness | Interior presence, qualitative identity or artificial realisation |
| Physics, Chemistry and Biology | Planetary stocks, flows and coupled processes | Earth | Earth's actual initial conditions, history, attribution or hazard exposure |
| Physics and Earth observation | Propagation, reference and instrument relations | Astronomy | Remote object identity, catalogue population or cosmic chronology |
| Biology, Consciousness, Medicine and Information | Agents, capabilities, reports, communication and health constraints | Social | Institution, power, legitimacy, collective causation or ethical verdict |
| All scientific branches | Admitted laws, measurements and use constraints | Engineering | Requirement, safe design, intended-use validation or deployment authority |
| Categorical owners | Immutable admitted receipts | Cross-Branch Synthesis | Permission to rename analogy as identity or create a primitive law |
| Biology and Engineering | Protein carrier and verified implementation boundary | Protein Fold | Successful whole-chain prediction or AlphaFold parity |
| Computation and Engineering | Rule and software constitutions | Chess/Go | Playing strength, solved-game status or clean V3 completion |
| Computation, Consciousness, Social and Engineering | Architecture, realisation, interaction and deployment boundaries | Unison | Consciousness, empirical capability or public deployment permission |

One-owner does not mean one contributor or one file. It means one categorical
authority for the scientific kind of claim. A medical device may require
Physics, Biology, Medicine, Social Science and Engineering records, but the
Engineering acceptance test cannot decide the medical benefit and the medical
trial cannot redefine the physical measurement. Cross-branch work succeeds
when all owners' records commute without erasure.

An anomaly follows the matrix in reverse. The application first verifies that
its own requirement, code, apparatus and calibration are sound. It then returns
the complete record to the owner of the challenged scientific relation. If the
anomaly crosses several branches, Synthesis may assemble the typed question;
it cannot pre-assign the answer.

### 24.14 Publication completion and version-update rule

Publication readiness has four independent dimensions. A **formal** result has
a complete registered grammar, survivor and proof receipt. An
**implementation** result has an executable realisation and verification
record. An **empirical** result has a source- or target-bound comparison with
chronology. A **publication** result has passed editorial, identity, rendering,
manifest and authority checks. Completion in one dimension cannot be imported
into another.

The active foundation papers remain the scholarly entry points for their
branches. Their current file identities are:

| Branch paper | Active SHA-256 |
|---|---|
| Biology | `af18711f0520f03a6d2e7fe5004166856d2203ad370a4932178c59eb00abddb5` |
| Medicine | `1f3acb2f7b0100da9e2530779f0a8e556570ae2bab46302554af8ef3fb15fd22` |
| Consciousness and Cognitive Science | `62f473b326aa9cf0ecfab2dcaaf763aa752df468210aef52b0d12c40f3aebaf9` |
| Earth and Environmental Sciences | `044d381d988635cb69a368ff9dbfe2fad8d87b31cec7f4edd76faee959c60354` |
| Astronomy and Cosmology | `135b2ada93caf788571b9e7c0d384d60ec0851d9c47a85fe432d6ad2618ef643` |
| Social and Collective Sciences | `83105339ba4dd4f419f06935ff7926264510e62588f78bc58bdd79d1788c9ef8` |
| Engineering Translation | `e232febfafdd820c6ac4557b42bf9b9fcf0b894c316c272e87e19b34a6bc44d0` |

Where a current V3 work already has a standalone concept record, an update
should preserve that record and create a new version under it rather than a
second unrelated paper. This preliminary Theory of Everything is different:
it is the first theory-wide publication of the V3 rebuild and therefore
requires a new standalone record, not a version of the deprecated V2 ToE.
After that first V3 record exists, later V3 ToE updates should remain on its
new concept lineage. Every release package must identify the exact manuscript
and PDF, metadata, licence, authorship, repository revision and machine
manifest. An updated abstract and headline status must be reconstructed from
active evidence rather than copied from an earlier version.

For the preliminary Theory of Everything, publication does not wait for every
future scientific question. It reports the current completed foundations,
field-wide branches, return families and computational gates exactly. The
planned later version will incorporate complete Protein, Chess, Go and Unison
results when they lawfully exist, repeat the global synthesis and heavy
verification, and preserve this preliminary state as historical chronology.

For Protein, the existing concept was established by version 0.9.3 and the
current version 0.9.4 lawfully continues that concept. Additional changes
belong in later versions of that paper, not a new standalone concept. The
release must not imply that the active search
process has yielded a result absent from its scientific-gate receipt. It may
report complete work, active work and reasoned expectations, provided the
evidence class is explicit.

The final authority gate belongs to Maria Smith. Automated checks can verify
that files render, counts match and hashes reproduce. They cannot decide to
alter a scientific claim whose meaning is ambiguous, and they cannot grant
remote publication authority to themselves.

### 24.16 Current completion gates in plain scientific language

#### Biology

The paper may say that the 75-law foundation and seven returned claims are
complete and that all 82 current claims are root-traced. It may also say that
the returned categorical source comparison passed. It must say that the
full-field census stands at **82/424**, with **342** obligations open. It must
not call the whole of Biology field-wide complete. Protein's conceptual family
is admitted, while Protein Fold's global computational and blind empirical
work remains open.

#### Medicine

The paper may say that the 72-law clinical foundation and four placebo/nocebo
returns are complete. It may report the source-observed physiological and
report distinctions. It must preserve the null, small and context-dependent
rows and state that exact fractions are structural. It must not claim a frozen
full-field census, a universal placebo magnitude or a patient treatment effect
from the formal fibre alone.

#### Consciousness and Cognitive Science

The paper may say that the 72-law foundation and five returns are complete at
their current evidence boundary. It may present interior observation,
phenomenal privacy, self-model, memory, red-of-red and returned orbit structures
as exact derivations. It must not report access, neural correlation, fluent AI
output or a Fold coordinate as direct measurement of phenomenal presence. The
full-field and artificial-realisation programmes remain open.

#### Earth and Environmental Sciences

The paper may say that the 74-law foundation and one returned tipping lock are
complete. It may report exact exponent 1 as the preregistered earthquake
prediction. It must state the first mixed-catalogue comparison as adverse and
the later homogeneous holdout as compatible, preserving both intervals and
chronology. A universal measured tipping value is not admitted, and the
full-field census remains open.

#### Astronomy and Cosmology

The paper may say that the 72-law foundation and five returns are complete. It
may report exact rank four as the pre-data structural prediction and the later
source method context. It must report **3.486344605219551…** as the adverse
first fit and avoid reclassifying it. It must preserve heterogeneous exponents,
non-universal spacing, lithium mismatch and mechanism uncertainty. The
field-wide census remains open.

#### Social and Collective Systems

The paper may say that the 72 foundation claims and four returned claims are
complete. It may report exact preferential shares, polarisation lock and
period-two carrier. It must not convert those structures into universal
distributions, social thresholds or forecasts. Consensus remains non-evidential
for truth, prestige non-evidential for proof and ethics explicitly bridged.
The full-field census remains open.

#### Engineering Translation

The paper may say that the 72-law foundation and eight protocol claims are
formally complete and independently reconstructed. It must state that the
returned experiments have not been run. Formal protocol closure is not
implementation, observation, discovery or validation. The full-field census,
apparatus execution and intended-use validations remain open.

#### Cross-Branch Synthesis and the final Theory of Everything

The paper may say that the twelve-claim inherited-return synthesis is complete
against its frozen input graph. It must state that the graph pre-dates the
current 2,751-claim corpus. A final one-owner merge, publication reconciliation,
terminal synthesis and heavy verification remain. The last heavy run's
**1,319/1,319** replays and **969/969** tests cannot be silently relabelled a
heavy run of all 2,751 current claims.

#### Computational programmes

The paper may report Protein's completed parent census, completed high slice,
scientific gate v20 **893/893** tests and publication full suite v21
**898/898** tests. It must report every remaining search gate and zero
protected accesses. It may report V2 Chess and Go results only as V2 historical
results. It must state that their V3 clean recreations are not started. It may
report Unison V2 certificate strength and weak generation evidence together.
It must preserve V3 `OPEN`, `NOT_RUN` and `NOT_AUTHORIZED` exactly.

These completion statements are the controls on the persuasive voice of the
monograph. They allow the work to state its real scale and novelty directly
while ensuring that a reader can distinguish what is derived, what is built,
what is observed and what the next version is intended to test.

### 24.17 Worked dependency sequences

#### 24.17.1 From chemical sequence to a blind protein prediction

The protein programme illustrates why the dependency spine is more than a
contents list. Chemistry first owns amino-acid and bond identity. Materials
Science owns collective structure–property and interface relations. Biology
then owns the ordered protein sequence, condition-bounded fold, ensemble,
interaction, assembly and function. Computation owns exact generation, state
identity, pruning and resource accounting. Engineering owns the executable
pipeline, test suite, target custody and public comparison. Only after every
handoff is complete can an empirical structural prediction be issued.

Suppose an implementation generates a compact candidate with favourable local
interactions. Chemistry can verify that its residue and interaction labels are
well formed. Biology can verify that it belongs to the registered folding
carrier. Computation can verify that it survived the exact grammar.
Engineering can verify that the code returned it under the sealed command.
None of those facts establishes agreement with an external structure. That
requires target opening, alignment and a registered whole-chain metric.

Conversely, suppose the blind comparison is adverse. The target record first
shows that the empirical output failed its criterion. The state and execution
trace then permit the failure to be localised. An omitted interaction is a
Biology or translation-grammar problem. An unsound prune is a computational
problem. A unit or chain-index error is an engineering implementation problem.
A mismatched experimental chain or comparator release is an evidence-custody
problem. The adverse row remains whatever the diagnosis; the next version can
then correct the identified owner without rewriting the earlier result.

This sequence explains why AlphaFold output access remains forbidden now. If
comparator outputs were visible while the remaining attraction bands or global
recurrence were being designed, they could select the representation or
pruning rule. The programme would still be capable of development and
retrospective correspondence, but its eventual output could not honestly carry
the intended blind status.

#### 24.17.2 From bodily state to expectation, report and clinical decision

The placebo/nocebo programme begins with a living body's reachable states.
Biology owns the physiological carrier and its constraints. Consciousness owns
expectation as an interior and cognitive relation and owns the difference
between experience and public report. Medicine owns the patient, comparator,
outcome, adverse event and decision. Engineering owns the instrument and trial
system. Social Science owns institutional expectation, communication and
access where they affect the setting.

The exact **1/4**, **3/4** and **1/2** fibre organises the possibility that
distinct retained routes share a common observed image. It does not tell a
clinician that three-quarters of an observed change was psychological. To make
that claim, a study would need an identifiable physiological measure,
expectation manipulation, realised comparator, blinding, timing, report and
appropriate causal analysis. The available-state boundary additionally asks
whether the response was physiologically reachable without the target
treatment.

If a participant reports improvement while the registered objective measure
does not change, the report is not deleted and the physiological channel is not
fabricated. If physiology changes without a reported experience, the objective
row stands without being renamed a phenomenal effect. If both change, their
association and chronology can be tested. If neither changes under an adequate
protocol, that is a null result at the registered outcome. If an instrument
fails, the result is unresolved rather than null.

This sequence protects patient evidence from two opposite reductions. It
prevents first-person experience from being dismissed as unreal because it is
not the instrument value, and it prevents subjective report from being used as
proof of any proposed objective mechanism. A future version can strengthen the
empirical connection only by preserving both.

#### 24.17.3 From exact exponent to earthquake catalogue evidence

The magnitude–frequency sequence begins in Mathematics with exact ratio and
order, continues through Physics with measured quantity and uncertainty, and
enters Earth Science through rupture, propagation, magnitude scale and
catalogue population. The exact candidate enumeration selects exponent 1
before the source is opened. The Earth evidence protocol then defines which
catalogue rows count above each threshold and how the exceedance interval is
calculated.

In the first test, the observed share derived from 1,494 of 18,469 events and
the entire registered interval lay below 1/10. The proper result is adverse.
The finding raises specific scientific questions about mixed magnitude types,
catalogue completeness and population boundary, but those questions do not
alter the measurement already made.

The later holdout narrows the source to homogeneous `mww` records and shifts
the thresholds. Its 27 of 253 share and interval include 1/10. Because the
source and rule were registered separately, this is a lawful compatible
result. It suggests that catalogue homogeneity may matter and warrants further
prospective testing. It does not establish that the first test was mistaken;
the first question and source were different.

A future synthesis could register a hierarchical model of catalogue types or a
new multi-source decision rule. It would be a new claim with its own
chronology. The current monograph instead preserves the two tests in the order
they occurred, which is the only basis on which readers can evaluate the
theory's predictive conduct.

#### 24.17.4 From source light to galaxy scaling

The SPARC sequence begins with astronomical source identity, distance, flux,
motion and catalogue selection. Luminosity is reconstructed from the received
record and distance; rotation is reconstructed from source motion under a
measurement and model boundary. The rank-four result is fixed in the formal
branch before the external rows are fitted.

An unweighted all-positive-row fit is one operationalisation and produces the
adverse **3.486344605219551…** slope. An orthogonal uncertainty-aware method is
another and produces **3.85 ± 0.09**, with reported systematic movement to
4.0. These analyses do not differ merely by typographical precision. They
weight uncertainty and geometry differently, so the exact method is part of
the claim.

The standing evidence therefore supports three simultaneous statements:
rank four was a pre-data SFT prediction; the first registered operational test
was adverse; and a separate established method places four at its stated
systematic boundary. Removing any one of those statements would distort the
current record. Future work can improve the test by freezing a physically
justified sample and uncertainty model prospectively.

#### 24.17.5 From social evidence to an engineered public platform

The open-science platform is itself an Engineering and Social Science object.
Engineering owns repository structure, portable formats, tests, release
identity, reproducibility commands and deployment. Social Science owns access,
gatekeeping, community interaction, institutional dependence and unequal costs.
The scientific branches own the claims and evidence hosted by the platform.

Open access is therefore not achieved merely by placing code on a public URL.
A reader must be able to identify the active paper, distinguish it from a
superseded draft, locate its claim package, reproduce its counts and understand
its licence. A public command must fail visibly. Large assets require stable
archival identities. Accessibility includes documentation and platform
support, while privacy and consent constrain any participant data.

The anti-institutional mission follows from this operational design. A reader
does not need a credential or journal subscription to inspect a candidate
census or adverse record. Criticism can be registered against a specific
claim. Yet openness does not mean automatic scientific admission: an asserted
replacement must satisfy the same dependency, evidence and falsification
requirements. The platform challenges monopoly over access and authority
without abandoning standards of proof.

#### 24.17.6 From field branches to the preliminary and final ToE

The preliminary monograph assembles current authoritative results in dependency
order. It is not preliminary because the existing foundations are informal.
They are receipt-backed and published. It is preliminary because the active
full-field, computational and terminal-synthesis programmes have not all
reached their final registered gates.

The final ToE update requires four types of closure. The domain programme must
finish Biology's registered census and freeze the later full-field boundaries
selected for completion. The computational programme must produce governed V3
Protein, Chess, Go and Unison records, whether favourable or adverse. The
lineage programme must reconcile one owner for every historical and current
claim. The machine programme must run terminal synthesis and heavy verification
against the final graph.

Those requirements do not make the preliminary paper scientifically empty.
They make its version status precise. It already reports a root-traced corpus
of 2,751 admitted claims and the current branch results, together with the
limits of the verification and application programmes. The future version will
add evidence and closure; it will not retroactively create the present
chronology.

### 24.18 Final programme outlook

The immediate priority remains Protein V3 because it is the only clean V3
computational programme with an active large exact search and a protected blind
target design. Its next report should state one of three outcomes without
embellishment: the remaining exact gates closed; the search was lawfully
narrowed with an explicit unresolved remainder; or a registered obstruction
showed that the current grammar cannot close. Any of these can be publishable
if the machine and scientific records are complete.

Chess and Go follow with clean reconstruction rather than patching historical
runtime state into a V3 label. Their V2 records provide strong regression and
performance baselines. Reproducing them is necessary but not sufficient: V3
must show its own derivation and implementation identities. Governed external
play can begin only after rule and harness closure.

Unison requires the strictest separation between architecture and performance.
The V2 system demonstrates an unusually large auditable certificate surface,
while its generation scores show that auditability alone did not yield useful
general language behaviour. V3's opportunity is to preserve exact internal
custody while discovering whether a native Fold architecture can learn,
reason and generate under public tests. The answer remains unknown until the
mathematics closes and evaluation runs.

The programme should publish adverse results as readily as favourable ones.
An exact theory becomes more persuasive when the reader can see where an
implementation failed, which assumption changed and which result survived.
The current Protein whole-chain, earthquake and SPARC records already show
that this is possible. Their continued visibility is part of the evidence that
the platform does not select only its successes.

The ultimate computational contribution will therefore be more than four
headline demonstrations. It will be a reusable public method for moving from
premise and dependency, through complete candidate accounting, to executable
artefact, protected comparison and versioned correction. Protein, Chess, Go
and Unison occupy different domains precisely so that a success in one cannot
hide a weakness in another. Together they test the generality of the Fold as a
scientific programme.

### 24.19 Registered unresolved work and discriminating next tests

Open work becomes scientifically useful when its next observation can
distinguish alternatives. The following register states the main unresolved
questions in this part and the kind of evidence that would materially change
their status.

| Owner | Unresolved question | Discriminating next evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Biology | Does the autocatalytic ignition carrier map quantitatively onto a plausible prebiotic network? | Prospective chemistry with registered roles, seed, drive, compartment, product custody and competing non-autocatalytic routes |
| Biology | Which physical route fixed and amplified terrestrial homochirality? | Source-dated stereochemical experiment or natural record separating initial bias, amplification and racemisation |
| Biology/Protein | Can the six-axis Fold relation recover complete structures from sequence alone? | Closed global recurrence followed by protected whole-chain comparison on the complete target manifest |
| Medicine | When does expectation measurably alter objective physiology and patient benefit? | Registered expectation manipulation with realised comparator, blinding, report, objective channel, adverse events and follow-up |
| Consciousness | Which physical organisations satisfy the bidirectional realisation criterion? | Causal intervention study preserving state roles and return relations, not output correlation alone |
| Earth | Is exponent 1 stable across prospectively homogeneous seismic populations? | New catalogue holdouts with frozen magnitude type, completeness threshold, region/time boundary and interval rule |
| Earth | Where do particular coupled systems tip? | System-specific state variables, path, hysteresis and prospective threshold observations in dimensional units |
| Astronomy | Does rank four survive a prospectively frozen galaxy sample and uncertainty model? | Pre-sealed SPARC or independent-source analysis with selection, distances, variables, covariance and fitting rule fixed |
| Astronomy | Which returned binary and lithium structures have exact quantitative counterparts? | Source-specific forecasts with dimensional mapping and decision intervals registered before release |
| Social | When do preferential flow and polarised common images causally govern collective outcomes? | Cross-context interventions or natural experiments retaining institutions, networks, dissent and reversals |
| Engineering | Do the six returned protocols yield their registered physical or human effects? | Built, calibrated apparatus and blinded execution preserving every null, adverse, transport and restoration row |
| Synthesis | Does the one-owner terminal graph close after all 2,751 current claims are included? | New global lineage merge, synthesis build and heavy verification at the release freeze |
| Chess | Does a clean V3 Fold engine retain or improve V2 play? | Rule-complete recreation and governed matches with fixed opponents, time control, openings and adjudication |
| Go | Does a clean V3 Fold engine reproduce exact censuses and complete external games? | Independent census replay and unbroken harness runs under frozen rules and scoring |
| Unison | Can a native V3 Fold architecture generate useful answers without borrowed answer weights? | Mathematically closed implementation followed by preregistered factual, reasoning and generation suites |

These tests have different possible conclusions. A quantitative miss can be
adverse even when the categorical carrier survives. A source may be compatible
without uniquely supporting SFT. A failed apparatus run may be unresolved
rather than null. A new successful implementation may validate an application
without confirming the universal law it uses. Each next protocol must state in
advance which conclusion follows from which outcome.

The tests also have different protection requirements. Protein and benchmark
programmes require target custody. Human studies require consent, privacy,
blinding and adverse-event care. Environmental and astronomical studies
require release and selection custody. Engineering anomaly studies require
calibration, restoration and complete source ledgers. Social interventions
require affected-agent and ethical boundaries. One generic “validation” label
cannot replace these constitutions.

### 24.20 Specialist reading and reproducibility routes

A domain specialist need not begin with machine hashes. The conceptual route
is the relevant branch chapter followed by its dependency-resolved family
ledger and returned-claim inventory. The specialist can then use the active
foundation DOI to inspect the complete paper, the scientific audit volume to
locate claim IDs and evidence status, and the machine package to replay the
candidate census and controls.

A reviewer concerned with an adverse result should begin with its chronology
and measurement receipt, then inspect the source manifest, decision rule and
later evidence as separate records. A reviewer concerned with formal
uniqueness should begin with the candidate grammar, verify the 256-form census
and inspect independent reconstruction. A reviewer concerned with a
computational result should inspect the build identity, test classes, resource
ledger, target-access log and per-target outputs rather than relying on the
headline score.

The active-paper SHA-256 values identify the precise Markdown authorities used
for this part. Full source captures and receipts may be larger or more
numerous; their identities remain in the release manifest. A changed paper
hash does not by itself indicate a scientific change, because editorial
formatting also changes bytes. The version record must therefore state whether
scientific content, evidence status or only presentation changed.

Reproducibility is complete only when a fresh environment can reach the same
registered result or produce a classified failure. The public route should
report dependency versions, commands, elapsed time and output paths. It should
never silently download a target that the scientific gate keeps protected,
never replace a missing source with an unregistered copy and never alter an
authoritative receipt in place.

This layered reading route permits accessibility without dilution. A newcomer
can follow the dependency argument; a specialist can challenge the scientific
boundary; an auditor can reconstruct the evidence; and a developer can replay
the machine process. All four encounter the same current status rather than
different stories written for different audiences.

## Part IV. Evidence reconciliation, limitations, reproducibility and conclusion

## 25. Corpus-wide evidence reconciliation

The theory-wide result is not a sentence assembled from the strongest result
in each branch. It is the complete retained state of every branch at the
release freeze. The global audit therefore carries formal, computational,
empirical, historical and publication status as separate coordinates.

At the current claim boundary the reconciliation is exact:

- 2,751 registered claims are model-admitted;
- 892,246 generated candidates are retained in the aggregate census;
- 2,751 unique survivors are retained;
- 11,004 of 11,004 registered controls pass;
- 2,751 candidate-census identities reconstruct;
- 2,751 claim receipt identities match;
- 2,751 admitted claims trace to the single root; and
- no declared dependency is missing from the current claim ledger.

These results establish current claim-package closure. They do not erase the
separate final-ToE obligations recorded by the programme ledger. In
particular, the all-branch heavy verification has not been rerun since the
corpus expanded beyond the 1,319-claim all-foundations state, the canonical
one-owner lineage merge remains older than the current graph, and several
branches remain complete only at their foundational rather than field-wide
boundaries.

### 25.1 Formal reconciliation

Formal reconciliation asks whether each admitted statement has exactly one
owner, a complete dependency route, a registered candidate grammar, the
declared number of decisions, the required survivor state, controls and a
receipt matching the accepted package. The complete claim inventory binds
these fields compactly. The branch audit volumes preserve the complete record
without forcing thousands of repeated receipt blocks into the conceptual
narrative.

An exact formal result remains limited by its grammar. The global total does
not convert every depth-independent branch statement into a theorem about an
unregistered extension. Where the claim is a bounded census, the bound is
retained. Where it is a correspondence, the correspondence class is retained.
Where an external observation is required, the formal survivor does not
replace that observation.

### 25.2 Computational reconciliation

Computational reconciliation asks whether the registered source reconstructs
the stated census, decisions and receipt; whether an implementation-distinct
checker agrees; whether controls fail closed; and whether the public archive
identifies the producing source and artefacts. Repository tests are evidence
for those implementation questions. They do not manufacture empirical
confirmation.

The current lightweight repository validator passes at the 2,751-claim
boundary. The canonical engine seal verifies sixteen runtime files, and the
verification-authority seal verifies thirty-three protected files. The last
complete heavy run at the earlier all-foundations boundary replayed 1,319 of
1,319 derivations and passed 969 of 969 tests. This paper does not silently
promote that earlier heavy run into a 2,751-claim heavy verification. The final
global run remains an explicit open operation.

### 25.3 Empirical reconciliation

Empirical reconciliation preserves the method and chronology attached to each
external result. A current result may be:

- independently replicated;
- empirically tested and independently replicated;
- a sealed prediction awaiting measurement;
- a compatible but non-discriminating observation;
- a post-target reconstruction;
- a development-observed result;
- adverse;
- null;
- unavailable because transport or access failed; or
- unresolved because the required record is incomplete or has not been run.

The same words are not used interchangeably. A claim may possess exact formal
closure and an unresolved empirical extension. A branch may have field-wide
closure at its current-evidence boundary while a proposed later application
remains open. A historical
application may show measured success while its clean V3 recreation has not
started.

### 25.4 Publication reconciliation

Publication is a separate state. The current V3 paper set contains sixteen
authoritative published papers from Methods through Engineering Translation.
The seven field-wide papers from Mathematics through Materials were published
as new versions on their existing concept DOI lineages on 29 July 2026. Their
public files and lineages were checked against the approved local release
identities. The Protein Fold preliminary record was published separately on 31
July 2026; its current v0.9.4 successor remains an application-level
preliminary publication.

This ToE version is authorised for first publication as a new standalone V3
record after a frozen package and final local verification. Zenodo draft
21717584 reserves version DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21717584 and concept record
21717583. The deprecated V2 ToE concept remains historical and does not receive
this V3 rebuild as another version.

---

## 26. Corrections, adverse results and historical custody

### 26.1 Correction is part of the result

V3 treats a correction as a relationship between records, not as permission to
rewrite history. The corrected record must identify the earlier artefact, the
source of the mismatch, the new authoritative direction and the scope of the
change. If the correction affects only a file identity or clerical reference,
the record must say that no scientific count or evidence class changed. If it
changes a grammar, output or empirical conclusion, both the earlier result and
the lawful successor status remain visible.

The clean-room reconstruction itself is a corpus-scale reconciliation. Earlier
SFT versions remain historical provenance but are not proof premises for V3.
Their observations and applications can be compared after the corresponding
V3 result seals. Agreement is not assumed, and disagreement is not removed to
make the history appear linear.

### 26.2 Protein-folding chronology

Protein folding provides the clearest current example. The V2 lineage retained
strong local three-residue geometry correspondences while its sealed
76-residue whole-chain ubiquitin construction was adverse at the global
structure boundary, with TM-score `0.0269927379497572` and C-alpha dRMSD
`52.89314678065376` Å. Those exact measurements remain historical
implementation evidence. They are neither erased by the local successes nor
treated as the result of the new V3 six-axis system.

V3 then rebuilt representation, coordinate and interaction custody. A
historical 4APD whole-chain control exposed an inter-residue carbonyl-frame
defect. The corrected constructor preserved the complete defective record,
reconstructed every unordered atom pair and shared-atom correlation, and
removed the sub-angstrom collision. The correction did not improve the
C-alpha structure score: the retained control remains adverse. Later
sequence-only and partial-recurrence development tests contain both improvement
and regression. Their exact target chronology remains visible, and none is
silently relabelled as a primary blind test of the incomplete full V3 system.

The stronger work supersedes specific implementation obstacles only at the
boundaries it closes. It does not turn the historical adverse rows into
confirmation. The registered AlphaFold-generalised campaign remains future
evidence until the capability gate permits target access and all matched rows
have been evaluated.

### 26.3 Historical application results

The V2 Chess Fold, Go Fold and Unison Fold AI records are retained with their
producing-source identities, measurement protocols and known defects. Chess
retains its measured match tallies and exact legality controls. Go retains its
exact small-board censuses, GNU Go results and the recovered KataGo harness
record, including the disclosed round-one synchronisation defect and
point-at-cutoff status. Unison Fold AI retains successful verification surfaces
and adverse development benchmarks in the same archive.

These records motivate and constrain the clean V3 recreations. They do not
authorise a statement that a recreation exists when its workspace is absent,
or that an empirical campaign has been run when its registered status is
`NOT_RUN`.

### 26.4 Field evidence

The branch papers preserve their own adverse, corrected, failed-transport and
unresolved rows. Examples include adverse astronomical ranking comparisons,
an adverse first global mixed-magnitude Earth catalogue, unavailable or
superseded source transports, bounded mathematical correspondences that do not
establish an unbounded theorem, and corrected chemical and materials evidence
routes. Their branch chapters state the exact outcome and lawful successor
status. The global paper does not compress them into a claim that all external
tests were favourable.

---

## 27. Limitations and open frontier

### 27.1 Meaning of completion

Every use of *complete* in this monograph names its object and boundary.

- **Formal closure** means that the registered derivation and candidate grammar
  close.
- **Implementation closure** means that the executable construction and its
  declared checks close.
- **Foundational branch closure** means that the registered foundation of a
  branch closes.
- **Field-wide branch closure** means that the frozen current-evidence
  discipline census closes.
- **Empirical validation** means that the named evidence protocol has been run
  and met.
- **Publication completion** means that Maria Smith has approved and released
  the bound artefact.

None means the end of science. A frozen current inventory can be complete while
new measurements, discovered phenomena, improved source captures and lawful
extensions remain possible.

### 27.2 Open branch work

The present open branch programme is explicit:

1. Biology continues from 82 closed obligations within its frozen
   424-obligation full-field census.
2. Medicine, Consciousness and Cognitive Science, Earth and Environmental
   Sciences, Astronomy and Cosmology, Social and Collective Systems, and
   Engineering Translation retain complete foundations and current return
   families, but their complete full-field censuses are not yet uniformly
   frozen and closed.
3. The current Cross-Branch Synthesis closes its twelve-claim inherited-return
   family, while the final current-graph one-owner merge and terminal global
   reconciliation remain open.
4. The final global heavy verification must be rerun on the release-frozen
   complete graph rather than inferred from the earlier 1,319-claim run.

### 27.3 Open computational-proof work

Protein Fold V3 must complete and independently verify its remaining exact
fixed-geometry bands, reconcile the global frontier, compose the complete
torsion–protonation–solvent recurrence, pass registered development and adverse
controls, freeze all target and comparator infrastructure, preregister the
primary 100-target panel and then run the matched AlphaFold campaign. Parity is
decided only from the full retained result table.

Chess Fold V3 and Go Fold V3 require clean workspaces, V2 provenance
reconciliation, target-free law reconstruction, exact legality or state
custody, controlled development, preregistration and new measured campaigns.
Their historical V2 victories remain evidence but not substitutes for V3
completion.

Unison Fold AI requires mathematical closure of its V3 architecture,
executable implementation, development controls, preregistered empirical
validation, adverse-case retention and Maria Smith's separate deployment
authority. The present scaffold is not a validated model.

### 27.4 Source and transport limitations

Some scientific evidence assets exceed GitHub's ordinary 100 MB object limit.
Their omission from normal Git storage is declared, and their byte identities
and archival routes remain in the relevant manifests. A later large-file
distribution may improve access without changing the claims. Failed source
access and unavailable transport are retained as transport states, not
converted into absence or null evidence.

The release must also be self-contained with respect to its publication
authority. Historical ToE text stored outside this repository may be cited by
published DOI or imported as a checksum-bound archival input; it cannot remain
an unbound local prerequisite of the public package.

### 27.5 Editorial and interpretive limitations

The monograph spans formal science, natural science, computation, biological
and social systems, and engineering. A single narrative cannot replace the
specialist detail retained in each complete branch paper and audit package.
The three-layer design is therefore part of the scientific method: conceptual
argument here, complete audit in the branch volumes and complete execution in
the machine archive.

Readers should not infer that an unfamiliar vocabulary indicates an imported
conventional object, or that a familiar word retains every conventional
assumption. Each SFT object is defined by its carrier, dependency route and
relation. Conversely, a structural correspondence to a conventional object is
not automatically numerical identity or empirical confirmation.

---

## 28. Reproducibility

### 28.1 Release artefacts

The publication candidate contains three explicit levels.

1. **Conceptual monograph:** the complete dependency-ordered paper, rendered
   PDF and source Markdown.
2. **Scientific audit:** the complete 2,751-row claim inventory, seventeen
   branch audit volumes, source and evidence status, chronology, correction and
   publication tables.
3. **Machine archive:** the frozen corpus inventory, content matrix, claim
   packages, receipts, candidate censuses, controls, certificates, source
   identities, tests and release manifest.

The release manifest records the size and SHA-256 identity of every uploaded
file. The immutable Git revision binds the repository-resident machine archive.
Files excluded from ordinary Git because of size retain their separate
archival identity and are not implied to be part of the Git commit.

### 28.2 Reproduction sequence

A verifier begins from the release manifest rather than from prose:

1. verify every uploaded file identity;
2. verify the authoritative active-paper identities;
3. reconstruct the global claim ledger and branch totals;
4. verify all candidate-census and receipt identities;
5. verify one root trace and no missing dependency for every claim;
6. run the current lightweight corpus verifier;
7. run branch-specific verification commands recorded in the branch packages;
8. compare the generated paper, claim inventory and audit volumes against the
   frozen inputs; and
9. render the paper and inspect every page for layout defects.

The final approval dossier records commands, environment, exit status, counts,
hashes, page totals and any skipped or unavailable heavy operation. A missing
dependency, stale hash, malformed table, unresolved placeholder or
scientifically material discrepancy halts publication rather than being
silently repaired in prose.

### 28.3 Independent criticism

Reproduction is not restricted to the included verifier. Anyone may construct
an independent implementation, challenge candidate completeness, identify an
unregistered alternative, dispute a source classification, rerun an empirical
comparison or expose a defect. A valid challenge enters through a public
record and receives the same source, control and chronology custody as a
favourable result.
